# MythScribe architecture (as built)

The per-area notes that used to live in `CLAUDE.md`. Read the bullet for the area you are about to touch; do not read the whole file. Update the bullet in the same change when the code changes (the same rule as `FEATURES.md`).

## Areas

- `src/shared/ipc/contract.ts` — the IPC contract: every channel's zod input/output, main→renderer events, error codes. Add a channel here first; both sides are typed from it.
- `src/main/ipc/registry.ts` — `register(channel, fn)` validates input and wraps errors; throw `AppError(code, message)` from handlers. `emit(windows, event, payload)` pushes events. Handlers live in `src/main/ipc/handlers.ts`.
- `src/main/db/` — `schema.ts` (Drizzle), `migrations/` (generated SQL, numbered, committed, never edited), `migrate.ts` (runner with `schema_migrations` table; `hooks.beforeUpgrade({from, to})` runs once before the first pending migration of a database that already has one, never for a new or refused one), `connection.ts` (`openDatabase(file, { beforeUpgrade })` applies pragmas and migrations; F-8.7: `openProject`'s third argument `backupDirFor` (index.ts: `BackupService.dirForProject`, the default folder before the service exists) makes it call `preMigrationBackup.ts` `writePreMigrationBackup` (`rollbackSnapshot` from `snapshot.ts`, shared with the working copy; `.tmp` then rename; newest 3 kept; a failure is IO and the upgrade does not run); summaries from a newer prompt version count as current (`promptVersionAtLeast` in `src/shared/summary.ts`); `locking_mode = EXCLUSIVE` before WAL, so the WAL index lives in memory and no `-shm` file exists, which fixed "disk I/O error" on Google Drive's H: drive; a second connection to an open project fails with SQLITE_BUSY, so `ProjectManager.closeIfOpen` runs before `project:open` locates a path). Open marker: `src/main/project/openLock.ts` (`OpenLock.acquire` in `createProject`/`openProject`, released by `ProjectSession.close`; refuses with IO naming the other machine).
- `src/main/project/` — `projectStore.ts` creates/opens the `<Name>.mythscribe/` folder; `manager.ts` owns the single open project and notifies on change.
- Story memory switch (F-16.2, story memory P0; no migration): `src/shared/storyMemory.ts` owns `STORY_MEMORY_KEY` (`storyMemory`, a project `settings` row), `StoryMemoryVersion` (`v1 | v2`), the lenient `StoryMemoryState` `{ version, switchedAt }` (a broken field reads `v1`/null), `parseStoryMemoryOverride` (exactly `v1` or `v2`) and `effectiveStoryMemory(stored, override)`. `getStoryMemory(db)`/`setStoryMemory(db, version, now)` in `settingsStore.ts` (a missing or broken row reads v1; the setter's only callers are tests and, later, the F-16.31 switch-over; no IPC channel flips it). `ProjectInfo.storyMemory` is the effective value, filled by `projectStore.ts` `toInfo` on create and open; `ProjectSession.storyMemory` exposes it to main-side feature code. The override comes from `MYTHSCRIBE_STORY_MEMORY`, read in `src/main/index.ts` only when `!app.isPackaged`, handed through `ProjectManagerOptions.storyMemoryOverride` to `createProject`/`openProject`, and never written to the project. Renderer: `features/project/storyMemory.ts` `useStoryMemory()` (hook) and `currentStoryMemory()` (store code), `v1` with no project open. Everything in `FEATURES.md` §2.16 branches on it; installed builds stay on v1 until the author switches over.
- Memory helpers and test book (F-16.3, story memory P0; no AI, no schema): pure helpers in `src/shared/memory/`, shared so the renderer can draw underlines from anchors (P4). `sentences.ts` `splitSentences(text)` → `SentenceSpan { start, end, text }` over `straightenQuotes` text (offsets index the caller's string; abbreviations `SENTENCE_ABBREVIATIONS`, initials, dialogue tags, `?!` runs, closing quotes, ellipses before a capital, speech broken off by a dash; a line break always ends a sentence). `quote.ts` `locateQuote(paragraphs, p, quote)` → `QuoteLocation { p, start, end, sentences }` or `QuoteMiss` (`tooShort | tooLong | notFound | repeated | otherParagraph`, in that order; 3–25 words, case-sensitive, word boundaries, exactly once in the whole window), `normalizeWithOffsets` maps `normalizeForMatch` text back to offsets. `anchor.ts` `sentenceHash` (normalizeForMatch + cyrb53, 14 hex), `sceneSentences`, `anchorFor(sentences, location, namingSentence?)` (the quote's sentences ±1 across paragraphs, plus the naming sentence), `anchorHolds`; names stay in the hash. `sources.ts` `MEMORY_SOURCES`, `meaningOf(source, { order, openOrder })` (happened, later, canon, plan, never), `readerMayRead` (`scene` only). `invariants.ts` `checkMemoryInvariants({ claims, sceneHashes, aliases, citations })` → `anchorMissing | anchorEmpty | unproved | aliasClash | unreadableSource | citesCard` over minimal row types P2a maps onto. The test book is test data in `src/main/memory/testBook/` (never imported by the app): `book.ts` `TEST_BOOK` (17 scenes, about 7,800 words: adapted, disguised excerpts of *Pride and Prejudice* chapters 1–3, 6 and 18 as s01–s05, with every name, place and four facts changed; original hard cases h01–h09; an inactive draft h10, an Idea scene h11 and a scene left out of compile h12), `labels.schema.ts` (zod `TestBookLabels`: records with aliases, expected claims with `expect: literal | verified | reject` and a reason, mentions, twin-gate cases, knows lines, planted C1–C3, negatives), `labels.ts`. `testBook.test.ts` checks the labels with the same helpers (every quote located once in its paragraph, anchors hold, never-read scenes refused, no original name anywhere). P2a's reader and verifier evals, P2b's knows scoring and P4's contradiction scoring read it.
- Story memory v2: records, names and the local index (F-16.4, F-16.5, tables of F-16.7, F-16.10, F-16.11; P1 slice 1, data layer, no UI; P1 as a whole (this bullet and the five after it) verified 2026-10-12 with the full gates, `e2e/memory.spec.ts` (four v2 tests on one launch with Use AI off until slice 5, then a v1 launch for the switch-over) beside the v1 smoke, F-16.4–16.15 ticked; migrations `0029_story_records`, `0030_name_index`, hand-written `0031_memory_passage_fts`, all created empty: nothing is copied from tags, sheets or uploads until the switch-over, P1-D1). **The gate:** `CHANNEL_MEMORY` (`any | v1 | v2`, a `Record` over every channel beside `CHANNEL_ACCESS` in `src/main/ipc/channelAccess.ts`; `v1` = the writes and AI of tags, tag links, sheets, facts, Changes, Organise, sheet sync, Story bible settings, the knowledge conversion, the library's process/review chat/apply, To do, AI tag recommendations and the consistency check; their reads stay `any`; every new channel is `v2`), checked by `createHandler` in `registry.ts` through `assertChannelMemory(channel, memoryOf())` after input validation and before the handler (VALIDATION "This project uses the other story memory"; no project open passes, so NO_PROJECT answers as before). Feature code that branches takes a required `memory: StoryMemoryVersion` (`summarizeScene` derives nothing in v2: no `applyDerivedKnowledge`, so no observed or dated facts, AI records or tags, or Changes rows, while the `scene_summary` row is still written; `runProofread` and `editScene`/`EditPassRunnerDeps.memory` for `projectNameWords(db, memory)`, which reads `alias.text` in v2). `handlers.ts` `memory()` is `manager.require().storyMemory`; on a v2 open it skips `convertKnowledgeIndex`, `convertThreadRecords`, `convertKnowledgeFacts`, `prunePassages`, the `mentions` scan, `todoQueue`, `sheetSync.load` and tag proposals, prunes `pruneMemoryIndex` and queues `queueMemoryIndex` (stale name scans, stale scene passages, one root `passages` job for record pages); `queueTodo` and `publishProposed` are no-ops in v2, the background consistency check runs only in v1, and the conversion dialog event is v1-only (v2 still holds those scenes back). **Shared rules** (`src/shared/memory/`): `records.ts` (`RECORD_KINDS`, `recordKindOf(categoryId)`, origins, `CASE_RULES`, `StoryRecord`/`StoryRecordSummary`/`RecordAlias` schemas, `defaultCaseRule`); `names.ts` (`normName`: NFC, straight quotes, full stops dropped, spaces collapsed, possessive stripped, lower case; `caseForm`; `TITLES` and `gateWords`/`foldTitles` for the gate only; `capitalisedSpans` with link words and a sentence-start flag; `NAME_PART_JOINERS` (of, the); `SENTENCE_OPENERS`); `twinGate.ts` (`twinGate(name, roster, dismissedNames)` → clear or blocked by `alias | subset | prefix | edit | dismissed`, `twinPairs`); `nameScan.ts` (`buildNameIndex`, `scanNames(paragraphs, index, { povRecordId, notMention })` → `NameMention[]` at ProseMirror ranges: whole spans only, spans joined by "of"/"the" in parts (surname particles stay whole), a sentence's first word dropped for certain only when it is a known opener, never a title, and as an ambiguous row when the scene writes it in lower case, "the " + span, plurals to a group or ambiguous, exact-case names and the sentence-start rule, `any` names on word boundaries, shared aliases by the paragraph ±1 or the POV else one ambiguous row per owner with one `spanKey`, Not a mention by `mentionPhrase`); `nameQuestions.ts` (`countNameSpans`: the scan's whole spans per document; questions skip what the scan already finds, a dismissed name first, and title-led nicknames; new name vs nickname through the gate; `NameAnswer` capped like record names, `NameAnswerResult`); `passages.ts` (`PASSAGE_SOURCES`, `passageCanon`: `notCanon` for a marked paragraph and for every never-real source, `passageLabel`, `MemoryOwnerKind`, hit and query schemas); `pageMarks.ts` (`NOT_CANON_ATTR`, `markedParagraphs`, same blocks as `passageParagraphs`); `dismissed.ts` (`DISMISSED_KINDS`, `dismissedKey(input)`); `authorTodo.ts`; `sceneDetails.ts`; `src/shared/storyLabels.ts` (`LABEL_KINDS`, default colours, `labelNameKey`, `labelSteerLines` for slice 5). **Main** (`src/main/memory/`): `recordStore.ts` is the one writer of `record`/`alias` (create runs the gate and answers `{ gate }` unless `force`; an unshared norm another record holds is refused naming the owner; `setAliases` keeps unchanged rows, writes an `alias` dismissal for a removed nickname (never for the old main name: a rename) and clears it when re-added; a name made a record's leaves the `name` dismissals (`forgetNotAName`); `addAlias`, `sameAs` and `splitRecord` into a record refuse past `RECORD_ALIASES_MAX` other names (`RECORD_ALIAS_MAX` equals the record name cap, so a merged name round-trips); `sameAs` moves names and appends pages and re-points `name_mention` (deduped per span), `scene_detail` (POV, place, "also here"), `author_todo` and `dismissed` (keys rewritten, the pair's own split/notSame dropped) in one transaction; `splitRecord` writes a `split` dismissal; `rosterHash`, `invariantAliases` for `aliasClash`); `labelStore.ts`, `dismissedStore.ts`, `authorTodoStore.ts`, `sceneDetailStore.ts` (any node under the manuscript root; record ids must exist; `povOf`); `scanStore.ts` (`memory_scan` hashes per owner); `nameIndexer.ts` (`scanNamesFor` over the active draft of any manuscript document, skipped by `names_hash` = text + roster + Not a mention list + POV + `NAME_INDEX_VERSION`; `staleNameScans`; rows rewritten when any stored field moved; `markNotAMention` refuses a row whose range no longer holds its text, else rescans at once); `passageIndexer.ts` (the one writer of `memory_passage_fts`: `scene`/`ideaScene`/`excludedScene` by the inherited Idea status and compile exclusion, `sceneNote`, `pinned`/`idea` with `notCanon` paragraphs; `passages_hash` skip; `indexStaleRecords` hashes records only; `searchMemoryPassages` with `ftsQuery` and a source filter; `pruneMemoryIndex`, `dropOwner`; Research owners since slice 2, see the next bullet); `questions.ts` (memoised span counts, `answerQuestion`). **Queue:** the mention queue owns `['mentions', 'names', 'passages']` (`IndexQueueOptions.kind` and `listJobs`/`deleteAllJobs`/`requeueFailed` take several kinds); each run keeps to its own memory and drops the other's job quietly; a v2 save touches `names` and `passages`, a notes save `passages`, a page save the root `passages` job (index_job's node foreign key), a roster change, `tree:move`, `tree:duplicate`, an import and a change of Idea status `queueMemoryIndex` (by hash; a no-op in v1). Events `record:changed`, `names:changed`, `questions:changed` (pushed only on a change).
- Research section and the page schema (F-16.8, the Not canon parts of F-16.6; P1 slice 2; migration `0032_research_doc`, additive; story memory v2 only). **The section:** `SECTION_TYPES` gains `research` (`BOOK_SECTION_TYPES`/`isBookSection` in `src/shared/labels.ts` name the book's three; `sectionLabel` gives "Research"); `ensureResearchRoot(db)` (`src/main/project/seed.ts`, idempotent, position after the last root) runs only on the v2 open in `handlers.ts`, before `project:changed`, so a v1 project never gains it. The root takes folders and generic documents only (`canPlaceLevel`), moves stay within the section. **Left out:** compile (`compileSource`/`compileManuscript` read sections by name; the Contents list `includeRows` skips non-book roots), statistics, goals and the word-count report (manuscript only), drafts, voice, summaries, the v1 passage index (`manuscriptDocuments`), snapshots (`projectDocuments` filters `researchSubtree(rows)`; a document snapshot of a Research page is VALIDATION), plan links (`manuscriptRows`), and the chat agent's run (`loadAgentProject` leaves the subtree out, so no ref, outline line, read or edit reaches a page and an open page is not the focus; Ms Scribe reads Research from P3). **One owner of the tree position:** `src/main/research/researchTree.ts` (`isResearchRoot`, `researchRootId(db)`, `isUnderResearch(db, row)` for one node by its parents, `subtreeInOrder(rows, id)` preorder by position, `researchSubtree(rows)`, `researchDocumentIds(rows)`), used by the seed, the Research store, the passage indexer, search, snapshots, the voice profile and the agent. **Taken in:** word search (`searchProject` labels Research documents type `research`, "Source document" via `SEARCH_RESULT_KIND`; no tag filter reaches them; `searchTypesFor(memory)` offers the chip in v2 only and the renderer's `offeredSearchTypes` keeps v1's request exactly as before) and Replace in project (every document, P1-D9; the renderer reloads open pages through `reloadResearchPages`, also after crash recovery's `refreshRewrittenDocuments`). **Documents** (`src/main/research/researchStore.ts`, the one writer of `research_doc`: `node_id` PK cascade, `origin` written|upload, `context_file_id` set null, `file_type`, `converted`, `not_canon`, `created`; a Research node with no row reads as written and canon, `writtenResearchDoc`): text is the node's `content`, saved with `document:save` (no words written: `manuscriptWordCount` is null). `addResearchFiles` takes files through `addContextFileOutcomes` (`libraryStore.ts`, per-file outcomes; `addContextFiles` is the Library's wrapper, unchanged) under the `separate` same-name rule (only the same bytes under the same name are one file, so an original is never swapped under a page); a row a `research_doc` references is Research's own (`researchOriginalIds`): `listContextFiles` leaves it out, the Library's `update` rule never matches it (a same-named Library add or drop gets a row of its own) and a `replaceId` naming it is VALIDATION; a file already linked to a Research page is skipped "Already in Research as …", another type with `RESEARCH_TYPES_MESSAGE`. `researchPage(type, data, text)`: docx via `readDocx`, md `readMarkdown`, txt `readPlainText`, PDF its extracted text through `readPlainText` with `converted`, image `EMPTY_DOC` (the caption page); `blocksToPage` keeps paragraphs as imported (`origin: imported`), headings at 1–3, glyph lines as scene breaks, drops blanks. `researchOriginalPath` goes through `storedPath` (a hand-edited name that leaves the library folder is refused). `copyResearchMeta` pairs `duplicateNode`'s preorder with the source's (Duplicate keeps the original and Not canon). Channels (`v2` in `CHANNEL_MEMORY`): `research:list` (meta in tree order), `research:add` (`paths` or the library's open dialog; answers `{ nodes, docs, skipped }`, null when cancelled), `research:create` (`parentId`, `afterId`, `title`), `research:setNotCanon`, `research:openOriginal`. **Index:** `passageIndexer.ts` owns Research owners (`SOURCES_OF.research` = note|research: a written page `note`, an upload `research`; `notCanon` for a page switched Not canon or a marked paragraph); `indexNodePassages` is what a node's `passages` job runs (Research page, else scene and notes); `stalePassageOwners` lists stale Research pages, `pruneMemoryIndex` and `searchMemoryPassages` keep live ones (`liveOwners`). **Page schema** (`src/renderer/features/editor/pageParagraph.ts`, `buildPageExtensions`/`pageSchema` in `extensions.ts`; `OriginParagraph` moved to `originParagraph.ts` and is re-exported): `PageParagraph` adds `notCanon` (`data-not-canon`), `aiFiled` {doc, para, quote} and `aiWritten` {turn, date} (`keepOnSplit: false`; schemas and lenient readers `aiFiledOf`/`aiWrittenOf`, `PAGE_ATTRS` in `src/shared/memory/pageMarks.ts`); `PageHeadingMarks` gives page headings `notCanon` too (`NOT_CANON_BLOCKS`); `toggleNotCanon` sets attribute steps on every paragraph and heading the selection touches (all marked → unmark); the `clearAiMarks` plugin (`clearAiMarksTransaction`) clears both AI marks (`AI_MARK_ATTRS`) on a block whose words the author's transaction changed: candidates are the marked blocks near each step map's range, and a block keeps its marks when its content maps back through the transaction onto exactly one whole old block with the same words (so Enter at either end or Backspace on the empty line below leaves it); it skips `MEMORY_PROGRAMMATIC_META` transactions and undo/redo (`isHistoryTransaction`) and undoes with the edit. Main applies the same rule to its own author writes with `withoutAiMarks` (`pageMarks.ts`): `replaceInDoc` clears the marks of a block whose own words it replaced. The manuscript and notes schemas are unchanged (pinned in `pageParagraph.test.ts`); `storedContent.test.ts` loads every stored shape with `errorOnInvalidContent`. **Renderer** (`src/renderer/features/research/`): `pageStore.ts` (one autosave instance keyed `research:<node>`, `pinned:<record>`, `ideas:<record>`: document channels and the crash journal for Research, `record:get`/`record:savePage` for slice 3's pages), `researchStore.ts` (meta by node; `add`/`create` merge nodes through `treeStore.mergeCreated`, skipped files toast; only `clear` voids answers in flight, and `load` adds what the store does not hold yet, never overwriting a newer merge), `PageEditor.tsx` (the `NotesEditor` pattern over the page store, `.ms-page`, a toolbar with the Not canon toggle), `ResearchPage.tsx` (title, source line with Open original, the whole-page switch), `ResearchTab.tsx` (Add files…, New note, New folder over `ManuscriptTree roots={[researchRoot]}`), `researchView.ts` (`useResearchDoc` refetches once when a node has no meta yet, `researchTarget`, `selectionNotCanon`). `ManuscriptTree` takes `roots` (default: the book's sections, so the Manuscript tab never shows Research; the Research tree has no tag filter and no compile tick; its "New document" is `research:create`). `App.tsx` opens a Research document as `ResearchPage` and a Research folder as a message (no stack or cork board), gives a Research node no tags column (`taggable`), loads the meta on a v2 open and clears both stores on close; `NotesPanel` shows no notes pad for a Research page (the page is the note). The sidebar's `research` tool (`SIDEBAR_TOOL_IDS`) is listed by `buildSections(…, { research })` in v2 only, first of the tools. CSS: `.ms-editor.ms-page`, `:is(p, h1, h2, h3)[data-not-canon]` muted and hatched with a "Not canon" gutter mark (`--ms-not-canon`, the theme's Idea colour). e2e: `e2e/memory.spec.ts` (launched with `MYTHSCRIBE_STORY_MEMORY=v2`, Use AI off, provider URLs on a closed port) covers the picker, the Manuscript tree, a `.md` upload, editing, a Not canon paragraph and page, search and the compiled preview; the caret helpers live in `e2e/support.ts` for both specs.
- Records UI and labels (F-16.6, F-16.7 UI, F-16.11, the v2 shell; P1 slice 3; no migration; story memory v2 only, v1 unchanged). **Channels** (`v2`, `read`): `label:links` (every `{ labelId, nodeId }`, `listLabelLinks`, by node then label position) and `names:byRecord` (`namedInCounts` in `nameIndexer.ts`: certain mentions per record and scene, manuscript scenes only (a recursive CTE from the manuscript root; rows of a scene placed elsewhere stay, hash and all, should it come back), `NamedInRow` in `src/shared/memory/nameScan.ts`); `research:addData` (a window drop's bytes into Research, the same `uploadResearch` as `research:add`). `record:changed` carries `roster` (true: names or kind moved, from `rosterChanged`; false: an image only); `record:savePage` pushes nothing (the page store holds what it saved). **Sections** (`src/renderer/features/shell/sectionList.ts`): `buildSections(categories, counts, memory)`; v1's list is unchanged and pinned by a snapshot (`SidebarSections.test.tsx`, checked against the previous code); v2's (`buildV2Sections`, P1-D8) is Manuscript, every category (strays included, Threads too) as a record list (`RecordTab`, count `counts.records`), Research, Outline, Edits. **Stores** (one per entity, reset in tests): `records/recordStore.ts` (summaries by id, ids by name, `selectedId` for the main pane, `creating` for the dialog and `picking`, the picker a dialog opened from (POV, Place) that gets the record instead of its sheet opening; merges answers, keeping `byId` as it was when a refetch answers the same, so NameCheck's index stays; `record:changed` refetches only the named ids, NOT_FOUND drops one and lets go of the pane, any other failure toasts; `treeStore.select` clears the selection as it does an entity's, and so do `editPassStore.openWorkspace`/`openReport`; `useOpenDocument` and `SelectionCrumb` treat a selected record like an entity page), `records/nameMentionStore.ts` (`namedIn`, per-node mentions on demand; `names:changed` reloads the counts and the loaded scenes it names; a roster `record:changed` the counts when a row names one of its records and the loaded scenes whose mentions do), `labels/labelStore.ts` (labels in order, `byNode`, one filter per surface `manuscript | research | outline`, `managing`; a delete drops its links and filters, and clears word search's `labelId` when it named the gone label), `editor/sceneDetailStore.ts` (optimistic save, newest answer wins, a refusal restores; the newest save ticket per scene is kept until `clear`, so a read sent before a save never lands after it; a roster `record:changed` rereads the loaded scenes whose details name one of its records, `namesAny`). `App.tsx` loads and subscribes them on a v2 open and clears them on close. **Records UI** (`src/renderer/features/records/`): `RecordList` (thumb, name, nicknames, `namedInLabel`), `RecordCreateDialog` (`record:gate` after `GATE_DEBOUNCE_MS`; `gateWarning` per rule; *Same as* adds the typed name as a nickname unless the rule is `alias`, then opens it; *Create anyway* sends `force`; Create is disabled while warned), `RecordSheet` (PaneContent shows it after an entity page; names through `aliasesInputOf` + `record:setAliases`: case rule `CASE_RULE_LABEL`, *Shared* with `sharedWith`, remove, add (a nickname another record already answers to, `holdersOf`, asks "Share …?" and on Share marks the holder's name shared first, `sharingInput`, then adds it shared); a refused change keeps the typed nickname and puts the Name field back (`run` answers whether it went through); *Same as…* with `mergePreview`/`mergeSummary`, flushing the page store first and reloading the kept pages after; *Split…*; image and category via `record:update`; Pinned and Ideas as `PageEditor inline` over `pinned:`/`ideas:` page keys, journalled under the recovery kinds `pinned`/`ideas` by record id (`recovery:list` titles them by the record's name, `recovery:restore` writes them through `saveRecordPage`, the renderer reloads open pages), Ideas without the Not canon toolbar (`notCanonToggle={false}`: Ideas is never canon); *Named in* in reading order via `listOutline`, a click opens the first certain mention through `openNameMention` (`openPassage.ts`: names as written, never through the tag-name helpers, so "Anne-Marie" stays whole); delete flushes pages first), `recordView.ts` (pure rules and words). Insert › Character/Setting/World opens the record dialog in v2. **Labels UI** (`src/renderer/features/labels/`): `LabelsDialog` (create with kind, rename on blur, `ColourInput` commits on the DOM `change`, kind, move up/down via `label:reorder`, delete with confirm; until slice 5 its words and `LABEL_KIND_HINT` say labels only filter, nothing sent), `LabelPicker` (chips, add, *Manage labels…*), `LabelFilterBar` (its count follows what the view shows: Manuscript every book section, Outline the manuscript, Research its own) + `useLabelFilterView` (`labelFilter.ts`, reusing `tagFilterView` over `byNode`): the Manuscript tab shows it in place of the tag filter, `ManuscriptTree` filters by the surface's label in v2, `ResearchTab` and the Outline (`OutlineTab`, which also hides its Threads view in v2) have their own. Search: `SEARCH_TYPES` gains `record` ("Sheets"; `searchTypesFor('v2')` = document, notes, record, research; v1 unchanged), `SearchRequest.labelId` (optional; the renderer sends it only in v2, so a v1 request is byte-identical), `searchProject` finds records by name, "Nicknames", "Pinned" and "your idea" (`RECORD_SEARCH_FIELD`, through `sheetHit` shared with entities) and filters nodes and their notes by label (records and sheets carry none); a record hit opens its sheet. **Typed details**: `editor/SceneDetailsFields.tsx` in the v2 `MetadataPane` (POV over character records and *New character…*, Place over place records and *New place…* (the creation dialog with `picking`, twin gate included; the record made, or the one *Same as* names, is chosen) plus text, Time, Story year via `parseStoryYear` in `src/shared/memory/sceneDetails.ts`, *+ also here*, labels; outside the manuscript only labels) in place of Location, POV and Timeline. **The v2 editor**: `buildExtensions({ inertTags })` configures `InlineTag` (`inert`: no `#` suggestion plugin, renders the stored name as `.inline-tag-inert` with `data-inert`, copies as the name, `resyncInlineTags` skips it) and `TagRange` (`inert`: no decoration plugin, no Mod+Alt+T) with the schema unchanged; `DocumentEditor` opens no token menu and no tag items in the selection menu (the AI's two remain); `App` hides the tags dock panel and its header toggle and does not mount `ConversionDialog` in v2; View › Tags and Tools › Tags are left out of a v2 menu (`MenuItem.memory: 'v1'`, `menuFor({ devTools, storyMemory })` in main's menu and the in-app bar; `NO_TAGS_MESSAGE` answers only a click that raced the open); the References panel leaves out "In this scene" (slice 4's This scene panel) and its empty state names notes and images only; a file dropped on the window goes to Research (`DropOverlay` branches on `currentStoryMemory()`: `researchStore.addDropped` → `research:addData`, the Research tab shown with the first page open, the bytes read by `library/droppedFiles.ts` `readDropped`, shared with the Library's drop), never the Library's sort; `settingsTabsFor(format, tabs, memory)` drops Story bible in v2; `nameCheck` builds its near-name index from the record store's names and nicknames in v2 (the spellchecker's own list is main's `projectNameWords`, slice 1). `PageEditor` gained `inline` (grows with its text inside a sheet, `.ms-page-inline`). e2e: `memory.spec.ts` slice 3 test (sections, record with nickname, gate warning, Pinned and Ideas saved, Same as and Split, a surname shared by two records, a tone label assigned and filtered by, POV, a Named in click selecting the name, `#` opens nothing, no Tags toggle, a `.md` dropped on the window becoming a Research page).
- This scene panel and Rename in book (F-16.12, F-16.10, F-16.13, the agent in v2 for F-16.4; P1 slice 4; no migration, no prompt change; story memory v2 only, v1 unchanged). **Panel** (`src/renderer/features/thisScene/`): `ThisSceneColumn` renders in the `tags` dock slot in v2 (`App.tsx`'s `render`; the dock id stays, `dockPanelLabel(id, memory)` in `src/shared/dock.ts` names it "This scene" for `Dock.tsx`'s grips and menus; `ThisSceneToggleButton` replaces the Tags toggle in a v2 header, and View › This scene (`toggleThisScene`, `memory: 'v2'`) replaces View › Tags; `menuFor` builds one menu per story memory once, and with no project open shows v1's; the slice 3 filter that hid the slot is gone, so the dock list is v1's again). `ThisScenePanel` has two tabs: **This scene** (for the open manuscript scene, `sceneId` from the main node when it is a manuscript document): *Here* (`PovField` and `AlsoHereField` from `SceneDetailsFields.tsx`, over `useSceneDetailsEditor` in `editor/sceneDetailsEditor.ts`, the one save rule the scene details use too), *Named in this scene* (`nameMentionStore.loadNode`; chips by `namedChips`: certain mentions per record most named first, ambiguous spans one dashed chip each; the why card lists each place with *Go there* (`openNameMention`) and *Not a mention* (`names:notAMention`, the row added to the Dismissed store, the scene's mentions reread) and holds `RenameInBook`), *Needs a look* (`needsALookGroups`: My to-dos (open ones, with the done ones apart behind a collapsed "n done" line; the add form takes "For this scene" and "About a record"), Questions, Notices, a group drawn only when it holds something, the to-do group also while one is typed; `needsALookBadge(conflicts)` counts conflicts only and the panel passes 0 until slice 5; the one notice is `INLINE_TAGS_NOTICE` for a loaded scene with inline tags); **Book** (*Cast*: `castOf`, character records most-named first, a click opens the sheet). Main (`src/main/memory/renameStore.ts`): `checkAliasRename` (`recordStore.ts`, read only; `renameAlias` calls it) runs in the preview and the apply: no letter, the same name, another of the record's names, another record's unshared name, and a shared name another record holds are refused. `previewRename` plans every manuscript document (`manuscriptDocuments`, reading order) and every `draft_text` row with the roster's `nameScanShared` index and each scene's POV; `applyRename` runs one transaction: `takeAutoSnapshot` (a project-scope `auto` snapshot of `projectDocuments`, autos pruned) when any text changes, `saveDocument` for every planned scene but the open one, the `draft_text` rows only when `otherDrafts` (no snapshot holds them), `renameAlias` (the row keeps its id, shared flag, origin and case rule, which follows the new text when it was the default; clears the `name` and `alias` dismissals of the new name), and `moveNotMentions` (a key whose phrase changed is rekeyed, or, while a text still writes the old phrase, gets a new row beside it; an existing key is never deleted); `openId`/`openEdits` come back only when the open scene has places. The undo (in memory for the session, cleared on project change like `replaceStore`'s) restores a scene or draft text only while it reads exactly as the rename stored it, the name through `restoreAliasText` (only while it still reads the new text and no other record took the old norm unshared), the moved Dismissed keys and deletes the added rows. Channels (`v2`): `rename:preview` (read), `rename:apply`, `rename:undo` (write); the handlers end in `documentsWritten` for what main wrote and `rosterChanged`. Renderer: `records/renameStore.ts` (`ask` previews after `flushPendingSaves`, newest ask wins; `apply({ otherDrafts })` flushes, sends the active editor's node as `openId`; once main answers, `undoable` is this rename's before anything else runs; then it reloads what main wrote through `refreshRewrittenDocuments` and rewrites the open scene with `renameInEditor` (`editor/renameInEditor.ts`: main's `openEdits`, each only while `textBetween` still reads its words, through `replaceRange` from `findReplace.ts`; one transaction, the caret mapped, one history step) or, when the editor went away meanwhile, its stored text (`renameStored`, `replaceAtPositions`); a toast with Undo, a warning one when the open scene could not change; `undo` flushes, `rename:undo`, then the open scene through `restoreInEditor` (only while `sameTiptap(editor.getJSON(), after)`; `sameTiptap` in `src/shared/tiptap.ts` compares regardless of key order) or its stored text through `document:get`/`document:save`, naming what it left), `RenameInBook.tsx` (on the sheet under Same as and Split, and in the panel's why card; a shared name is marked "(shared)"; debounced preview with `SampleLine` from `ReplaceDialog.tsx`, the kept line and the "Also rename in other drafts" tick; *Undo rename to “…”* while the session's last rename is this record's). **The agent in v2** (`src/main/ai/agentToolsV2.ts`): `AgentProject.memory` (required third argument of `loadAgentProject`; only a `scene` hit carries `positionNote`, an Idea or left-out scene reads "never happened" and a scene note "a plan"), `todo` (`todoV2`, open to-dos with their scene ref and record) and `cards` by name (`scenesNaming`, whose `real` is false for an Idea or left-out scene by `sceneStatuses`; `lookupV2` counts only real ones and lists the rest apart); `finishAnswer` drops the organise request, counts it and appends `AGENT_V2_NO_ORGANISE`.
