- Story notes block and filing into Pinned (F-16.14, F-16.9, the prose steering of F-16.7; P1 slice 5; migration `0033_research_filed`, additive; story memory v2 only, v1 sends exactly what it sent). **The block:** `src/shared/memory/storyNotes.ts` owns `STORY_NOTES_HEADING` and the pure `renderStoryNotes(facts, maxTokens)` (lines: the heading, `Here: X (POV), Y`, `Named: …`, one line per record `Name (aliases): paragraph. paragraph.` from its Pinned canon paragraphs, each cut to `STORY_BIBLE_VALUE_MAX`, then the tone/content/theme label lines from `labelSteerParts` in `storyLabels.ts`; never Ideas, a Not canon paragraph or an absence; base lines kept with "… and N more", record lines by priority: each Here record's first paragraph (cut to fit when it must, and then no Named line), then the rest of the Here paragraphs, then Named by count in what is left, the first Named record that does not fit ending them; null when there is nothing). `renderStoryNotes(facts, max, { continuity: true })` is the continuity pass's form: `STORY_NOTES_CONTINUITY_HEADING`, no label lines, null without a record line (no pass, no cost). `src/main/memory/storyNotes.ts` gathers the facts (`storyNotesFacts`, `buildStoryNotes(db, { nodeId, maxTokens })`) and `typedSceneMeta` (the v2 scene line from typed details). Budgets: `STORY_BIBLE_TOKEN_BUDGET` 400, ghost text `STORY_BIBLE_GHOST_TOKEN_BUDGET` 150. Each feature (`ghostText`, `rewrite`, `chat` and `agentDraft`, `critique`, `whatNext`, `sceneSuggest` notes, `editPass` continuity) takes `memory` from the handler (`manager.require().storyMemory`), builds the v1 prompt and v1 hash object untouched in v1, and in v2 the new version with the block in the bible's slot and no steer, `...(v2 ? { storyNotes } : {})` in the hash; in v2 the voice profile's POV is the typed POV record's name (the scene line's) and chat sends no `#name` tag notes (`refs: []`). The new versions are thin wrappers (`ghostTextV4Input`, `rewriteV4Input`, `chatV5Input`, `critiqueV3Input`; `editPass.v3` replaces only the continuity task and adds the block to that pass's user turn). `storyNotes:get` (`v2`, read) answers the panel's *What the AI is told* (`ThisScenePanel.tsx`, read on open and when the scene's names, details, labels or records change). **Filing:** pure plan `src/shared/filing.ts` (`FilingReview` and its zod shapes, `parseFilingAnswer`, `filingChunks` over canon paragraphs, `planFiling` (a name one record holds files there, one two records share is dropped (`dropped.shared`); the twin gate's match gets its own undecided card (`sameAs`, *Same as* / *New record* via `filingAsNew`); Dismissed; a nickname another record holds (shared or not) or another card gave, a title (`isTitleAlias`), or no room is dropped, and a new record's name wins over another card's nickname; the quote check; duplicates and updates and conflicts against Pinned through `relateLine`), `decidedFiling`, `applyFilingOps` for the review chat's subset (it refuses a rename or a nickname that `FilingReview.taken` (the roster's norms) or another card holds, and places merged lines against the target's `FilingItem.pinned` again)). Main `src/main/research/filing.ts` (`filingSource` refuses a filed, Not canon or empty page; `estimateFiling`; `fileResearch` one strong request per chunk with `contextImport.v3`, halving a cut-off or unreadable piece up to `CONTEXT_SPLIT_DEPTH` times, proposals recorded; `applyFiling` in one transaction: images first, accepted proposed categories, every new record by its main name first (one whose name is taken by then is skipped and counted), then each card's nicknames one by one (a clash is left out), lines replaced or appended with `aiFiled` (`doc`, `para`, `quote`; an added line Pinned says by then is left out), `setResearchFiled` (`filed_at`, `filed_hash`); answers the project's categories for the renderer's category store; one undo state per session, `undoFiling(db, folder, docId, now)` refusing another page's, cleared on project change; `copyResearchMeta` never copies the filed columns). `src/main/ai/reviewChat.ts` `runFilingChat` (`reviewChat.v3`). `request.ts` `NO_REASONING_PROMPTS` turns reasoning off for both filing prompts on Cloud and own key. `src/main/research/filedLines.ts`: `filedConflicts(db)` (a filed Pinned paragraph whose quote is in no canon paragraph of its page, or whose page is Not canon as a whole (`pageNotCanon`), unless *Keep line*'s `notProblem` dismissal keyed by record, line, quote and the page's closest paragraph's text, so only a change to that paragraph asks again), `keepFiledLine`, `updateFiledLine`. Channels (`v2`): `research:fileEstimate`, `research:file`, `research:reviewChat` (ai), `research:applyFiling`, `research:undoFiling`, `research:keepFiledLine`, `research:updateFiledLine` (write), `research:filedConflicts` (read). Renderer: `features/research/filingStore.ts` (the one owner of a filing: estimating, confirm, running with `library:progress`, review with decisions (a "same as?" card starts `pending`, `filingStartDecisions`) and the chat's undo, Apply settling the proposals, replacing the category store and toasting *Undo filing*, `undoDocId` for the page header's *Undo filing*), `FilingDialog.tsx` on the shared `ReviewDeck` with `FILING_GROUPS` from `library/uploadReview.ts`, *File into sheets* in `ResearchPage.tsx`; `features/thisScene/filedConflictStore.ts` (loaded on a v2 open, re-read 400 ms after a Research or Pinned page save or a page's Not canon switch, `refreshFiledConflictsSoon`); the Conflicts group heads Needs a look and its count is the badge; `editor/filedFrom.ts` draws the "Filed from" mark as a widget decoration (the page schema is unchanged; an author edit clears `aiFiled` through `clearAiMarks`). Gaps: the card's mood and the lasting/transient values wait for P2a/P2b; filing reads a page as stored (an image page is offered to records by its name only), and a page is filed once (filing again after an edit is not offered; its conflicts are).
- Switch-over (F-16.15, P1 slice 6, built and tested on copies; F-16.31 **part A**, plan `docs/research/story-memory-2026-10-11/plans/Switchover.md`, five slices built 2026-10-14; part B, deleting v1, waits for the author's switch of her real project and gets its own plan; no migration, no prompt; v1 unchanged, new projects stay v1, nothing is offered or forced on open). **The way in (renderer, every build, a v1 project open):** Help › *Switch to the new story memory…* (`MENU`'s `switchStoryMemory`, `when: 'project'`, `memory: 'v1'`, `openProjectOnly`, so `menuFor` builds a third menu `none`) and Settings › AI › Story memory (`features/switchover/SwitchoverSettings.tsx`) both call `useSwitchoverStore.open()`. `switchoverStore.ts` owns the flow: phases `idle → intro → (copying | safety) → plan → consent → converting → done`; `target` (`project` | `copy`, from `switchover:trial`, the open project's `switchoverTrial` marker), `source`, `cloud` (for "in Google Drive"), `safety`, `failure` (a `SwitchoverFailure`, shown where it failed) apart from `error` (a thrown channel error), `ask` (the final screen's rebuild ask), `projectId` (the project the flow is for: SW-D5), one generation counter dropping a left flow's answers; `tryCopy` (P1's `switchover:trialCopy`: the copy made, opened and read), `switchThis` (`flushPendingSaves`, `switchover:safety`, then `switchover:plan`), `retry`, `convert` (`{ consent, projectId }`, then `useProjectStore.open(folder)`: the same folder on v2, no restart, SW-D12), `loadAsk`/`rebuildNow` (below), `openMyProject`, `close` (`switchover:leave`; toasts saying nothing changed and where the copies are; leaving a copy the flow made opens `source` again). `switchoverText.ts` owns the words (`whatChanges(onCopy)`, `failureLines`, `countsLine`, `goBackLine` naming the kept backup by date as Settings › Backups lists it, `rebuildLine`); `SwitchoverDialog.tsx` shows the intro (*Try it on a copy first* primary and focused, *Switch this project…*, *Cancel*; a test copy offers only *Switch this copy…*), the safety progress, the dry run (`switchover-yours` on the project itself, with both paths), consent (every inline tag with its editable words; the last line names the backup), and the final screen (counts, `switchover-backup` with *Show backups*, `switchover-dated-copy`, `switchover-go-back`, the rebuild ask; a test copy's `switchover-original` and *Open my project*); `SwitchoverFailure.tsx` (`role="alert"`: what failed, the message, "Nothing was changed", the next step, *Try again*). **The run (main, `src/main/memory/switchover/run.ts`; `handlers.ts` only registers):** `switchoverSafety` (the project itself, before the dry run, SW-D2; VALIDATION on a test copy or a stored v2): `WorkingCopy.syncNow` (step `flush`), `deps.backupNow({ kept: true })` (step `backup`: F-8.4's kept `<Name> before story memory …zip`, never pruned, SW-D3), `saveSafetyCopy` beside the project, so in Drive for a Drive project (step `datedCopy`); its memo `{ session, backup, datedCopy, changes, digest }` is reused only for the same `ProjectSession` while `total_changes()` has not moved or moved only through the derived indexes (`projectDigest` skips `index_job`, `mention_scan`, `tag_mention`, `ai_cache`, the FTS tables) and its files exist; a failed dated copy keeps the backup, so *Try again* makes only the dated copy. `runConvert(deps, { consent, projectId }, memo)`: step `project` (the id, a stored v1, this session's memo), `prepareSwitchover` (the uploads' originals, outside the transaction), the project checked again after that await, then synchronously the copies made current (anything written since is flushed, backed up and copied again, so the record's copies are the project exactly as it was), then `convertSwitchover` (step `convert`), then `deps.close()` (Drive gets the converted database). Every step's failure is `SwitchoverFailure { ok: false, step, message, nextStep }`, the message ending `SWITCHOVER_NOTHING_CHANGED`. A test copy skips the safety step (SW-D4) unless the author has written in it since it was made (`SwitchoverTrial.prose` digest): then a kept backup, no dated copy. `SwitchoverRun` holds the memo between channels: `safety()` first holds v1's writers that touch the digest (`deps.hold()`: the summary, To do, voice and plan-link `IndexQueue`s and the sheet sync, waiting at most `SWITCHOVER_HOLD_WAIT_MS` for a run in flight), `leave()` (`switchover:leave`), `clear()` (every `manager.onChange`, so a successful Convert) and a refused safety step let them go. **The conversion (`convert.ts`, `checkConverted.ts`, `record.ts`; one transaction):** P1's model (below) rebuilt with the consent; inline tags become words in every node's content and notes and every `draft_text`, never `snapshot_text`; records, labels, Research, typed details, dismissals, the ticked merges among those offered, the AI's plan links off; then `checkConverted(tx, takeConvertBefore(tx, tokenText))` (SW-D6: no inline tag left anywhere; every text equal to the original's with each token read as its words, walked over the original document, not the rewrite; every document and record page readable; snapshots byte for byte; no alias clash or nameless record; any problem throws VALIDATION naming up to three and undoes everything); then the switch-over record (`switchover` settings row, `SwitchoverRecord`: `switchedAt`, `trial`, `source`, `backup`, `datedCopy`, `rebuild: 'waiting'`, `rebuildStartedAt`; `getSwitchoverRecord` lenient, `markRebuildStarted` once; SW-D7) and `setStoryMemory(v2)`. **The rebuild after the switch (SW-D8, SW-D9):** `planSwitchover(db, context, sweepCalls?)` prices the rebuild as `rebuildEstimate` does on the converted book (the contradiction sweep with the Contradiction check on; `model.rebuildScenes` counts each scene's converted text; SW-D10). While the record waits, `readerService.ts`'s backlog check queues nothing and raises one stored `switchoverRebuild` notice (`{ switchedAt, scenes, priceUsd, priced }`, priced by `priceRebuild`; shown only while Use AI, the reading switch and a provider allow reading; `settingsChanged` raises or drops it at once; `later`/`dismiss` hide it until the next open); `settle` reads a scene left or paused in only when it was saved from the editor since the open (`editorSaved`); any `rebuild()` that starts marks the record `started` and drops the notice; a rebuild never starts on Cloud without its price (`cloudUnpriced`/`cloudUnpricedRefusal`, `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE`) nor with today's spending cap reached (`BUDGET`); its requests spend as asked (outside the Cloud monthly reading cap), and a daily-cap pause holds it with its progress until Resume or the next open. `switchover:record` (read, v2) answers the record. The final screen reads `memory:status`, `aiSettings:get` and, only when reading may run, `memory:rebuildEstimate`, then asks once (`features/switchover/rebuildAsk.ts`: `RebuildAsk` `reading`, `aiOff`, `readingOff`, `noProvider`, `nothing`, `cloudUnpriced`, `dailyCap`, `offer`, `unknown`; `rebuildAskLine`; one cost wording, `memoryView.rebuildCost`); *Rebuild now* (`memory:rebuild`) closes the dialog and opens P2a's rebuild dialog on its progress, *Stop* and the grouped summary; *Later* leaves the notice (`memoryView.noticeLine`, *Rebuild…* opens the rebuild dialog at `start`). Settings › AI › Story memory shows the record for good (`MemorySettings`' `SwitchoverRecordBlock`). **The test book on v1 and the replays (Switch-over slice 5):** `src/main/memory/testBook/projectV1.ts` `buildTestBookV1Project(dir, now)` builds the test book through the v1 stores (a sheet and tag per labelled record with its names as tag aliases; Mrs. Harrow's sheet untagged, since v1 keeps a name on one tag, so the shared "Harrow" sits on Mr. Harrow's tag and her sheet; the split pair Callum Varrow and a `varrow` tag; inline tags on every third readable scene and in the earlier draft, each on a name standing exactly as its record is named; scene meta POV, location and time; one timeline event a year, the 1811 one over a run of scenes and the flashback; the Captain's Plan sheet holding his Ideas line; an AI-made sheet, an Organise-written field, AI facts, observed facts, summaries; a snapshot; the Idea, cut and inactive-draft scenes laid out by `project.ts`'s shared `layOutTestBook`). `src/main/ai/eval/switchover.eval.ts` (the `eval` project, so `npm run test`): `readyToConvert`/`switchTestBook` (the book in a temp `My Drive` through a `ProjectManager` that sees Google Drive, the safety step, the dry run, the default consent with the Varrow merge ticked, Convert, the reopen and the indexes an open builds), the dry run's lists (the merge list exactly the Varrow pair), the copies and the record, the prose byte for byte in both drafts, the snapshot, no token, the records equal to the labels, the converted pages (the labels' Pinned pages and notes as canon lines, Ideas from Plan sheets, the Filed-by-AI block Not canon), typed details, the price equal to `rebuildEstimate`; `rebuildMemory` (*Rebuild memory* through `createReaderService`: the notice, the ask, the reading, spot checks and sweep until the queue is idle) answered by the oracles behind a fake OpenRouter (`switchoverOracleServer`), whose claims and findings (`memoryLines`, ids named) equal the same rebuild of the v2-built book with its pages and notes (`addTestBookPages`, `addTestBookNotes`; its Research page titled as v1's Project notes converts), the note rules included, the author's pages (`authorPages`: every named record's Pinned and Ideas, converted ones included, and every Research page) byte-identical, the invariants clean with the findings in, the record `started`, the notice gone; the adversarial plant (a lost paragraph refused by the check, the bytes unchanged); and `runLiveSwitchover` (the live run, `-- switchover`, owed by the author: the same switch priced on the run's models, then the rebuild at the app's pace; bars `SWITCHOVER_PASS_BARS`: the actual cost 0.5–1.5× of the dry run's price, the time against its minutes, the planted-error run's bars on the converted book through `plantedScore.ts` (shared with `memory.eval.ts`), the author's pages untouched, no request carrying AI-written sheet text, an Ideas line or a frozen v1 AI row (the AI-made sheet, an old summary); a transient provider error is the queue's to retry, as in the app (`jobOutcome`), and counted; stops at 50¢ with the queue's Stop after the request path refuses as at the daily cap, at a hard provider error (one that pauses the app's queue), at an unknown cost, or after 25 minutes, the report written in each case; the report in `test-results/eval-switchover/`, and one naming the failure when the run fails before its rebuild), tested offline for the report, the stop, a rate limit retried, a hard provider error and an unpriced model. **P1's base (decision P1-D12):** **Shared:** `src/shared/memory/inlineTagReplace.ts` (`replaceInlineTags(doc, replacements)`: each `inlineTag` token becomes its consented words by stored token id, else its stored name with hyphens as spaces; the token's marks are kept and equal-marked neighbours joined; a document without tokens comes back as the same object; `inlineTagWords`, the one word rule, also read by the check; `inlineTagTokens`, `inlineTagSamples`, `tagNameAsWords`), `switchoverMapping.ts` (the one mapping the switch-over and the v0 import in v2 share: `recordCategoryOfTag`/`recordFromTag` (character, setting, world building, plot thread → `character`/`setting`/`world`/`thread`, the name through `tagNameAsAlias`), `labelKindOfTag`/`labelFromTag` (tone, content, custom; colour kept), `pinnedFromFields` (`Label: value` lines), `datedLine`/`sceneReference`, `pageFromLines`/`pageBlock`, `pinnedPage` (the author's lines, then `FILED_BY_AI_HEADING` and the AI's lines, all Not canon, decision 8)), `switchover.ts` (`SWITCHOVER_TRIAL_KEY`, `SwitchoverPlan` (kept, converted, dropped by name; `SwitchoverMerge` with categories; `filedByAi`; notes; `RebuildEstimate`), `InlineTagReplacement`, `SwitchoverConsent`, `SwitchoverResult`, the steps, failures, outcomes and the record). **Main:** `model.ts` `buildSwitchoverModel(db, { replacements })` reads the v1 tables and writes nothing (keys `entity:<id>`, `tag:<id>`, `pov:<name>`; every author sheet a record, a tag linked to it merged in, the first of two sheets sharing one tag taking it; a linked sheet's names are its tag's aliases; the Project notes a Research note; an AI tag dropped; an AI sheet dropped unless the author added a dated line, relationship, picture or Plan/Idea status; an author name or plot-thread tag without a sheet a record, a tone/content/custom tag a label with its author links; AI-written sheet text (`aiWritten`, the paragraph keys every applied `restoreSheet` from `library`/`organise`/`chat`/`sync` runs added, `AI_WRITER_SOURCES`; a sheet such a run made, `AI_RUN_SLACK_MS`) to `filedByAi` on Pinned, or Ideas for a Plan/Idea sheet; write-up paragraphs dropped; dated author lines, relationships and thread events as lines; typed details from scene meta (POV and place by name, alias, kebab words or tag name; a POV no record answers to becomes a Character; the time; a timeline year on the first scene of each run of one event; every event as a line of the Research note `TIMELINE_NOTE_TITLE`; "also here" from author links on scenes the name scan finds no mention in); uploads; dismissals that still apply (`notSame` from kept spellings the twin gate blocks); `twinPairs` over the planned roster with the merge list's rules (SW-D15, slice 5 review: a nickname marked shared is left out of the test, never a pair of two kinds, and no pair of two records that each have a word or title of their own matched only by `alias` or `subset`, `eachHasOwnName` in `twinGate.ts`); norms several records hold shared on each; the consent rows (`inlineTagRows`, the default words the record's name); nesting, shared names and the flashback guess named in the notes). `plan.ts` `planSwitchover`, `estimateRebuild` (one `reader.v2:first` page per 2,500 prose tokens plus a `verify.v2` per page, the 50 fast and 20 strong spot checks and the sweep's `judge.v1` calls, priced with `estimateCost`; Idea scenes and scenes left out of compile are never read), `inlineTagReplacements`. `convert.ts` `prepareSwitchover`, `convertSwitchover` (records through `insertConvertedRecord`, labels through `createLabel`/`assignLabel`, Research through `insertResearchUpload` and `createResearchNote`, details through `setSceneDetails`, merges through `sameAs`, `unshareLoneConvertedNames`, `dropAiPlanLinks`; `ALREADY_SWITCHED` for a stored v2). `trial.ts` `createTrialCopy` (a snapshot into `saveDatedCopy(folder, now, 'story memory test')`, then a new project id, the name and the `switchoverTrial` row with the prose digest), `saveSafetyCopy` (`before story memory`), `switchoverTrialOf`, `trialWrittenIn`. `src/main/project/datedCopy.ts` `saveDatedCopy` is the generalised conflict copy. **Imports in v2:** `importStructure.v2` (no bank, no tags), `importDraft(..., memory)` keeps no tag candidates in v2, `convertLegacyProject(location, now, memory)` writes records and labels in v2, `ProjectManager.newProjectMemory()` for a project an import or a v0 conversion makes; compile's `keepTags` off and hidden in v2. **Channels:** `switchover:trialCopy` (write, v1), `switchover:trial` (read, v1), `switchover:safety` (write, v1), `switchover:plan`, `switchover:consent` (read, v1), `switchover:convert` (write, v1: `SwitchoverConvertInput` → `SwitchoverOutcome`), `switchover:leave` (read, any), `switchover:record` (read, v2). **Tests:** `run.test.ts`, `convertSwitchover.test.ts` (seven faults planted with temporary triggers, each rolled back), `planSwitchover.test.ts`, `record.test.ts`, `switchoverCases.test.ts`, `convert.perf.test.ts` (200 scenes in about 0.7 s, bar 5 s), `readerService.test.ts` "the rebuild after the switch-over", `menu.test.ts`, `SwitchoverDialog.test.tsx`, `switchoverStore.test.ts`, `rebuildAsk.test.ts`, `MemorySettings.test.tsx`, `memoryView.test.ts`, `ThisScenePanel.test.tsx`, the `switchover:*` rows of `handlers.test.ts` and `channelAccess.test.ts`, `inlineTagReplace.test.ts`, `switchoverMapping.test.ts`, `datedCopy.test.ts`, `legacyImport.test.ts` v2, `switchover.eval.ts`; e2e `memory.spec.ts`: P1's copy test through Help › Switch… › *Try it on a copy first* (with Use AI on and an own key since the final verification: the final screen asks once with the price and the copy-only line, nothing read before the answer, and *Rebuild now* runs the rebuild dialog to its grouped summary), and "the switch-over of a v1 project in place, as the installed app runs it" (a v1 project in a temp `My Drive`, a failed backup and *Try again*, the dry run, consent, the final screen's backup and dated copy on disk, the same folder on v2, Use AI off asking nothing, the record in Settings, the notice, *Rebuild…* to the grouped summary), beside the unedited v1 smoke.
- Story memory v2: evidence data (F-16.16 gate, F-16.17 and F-16.18 data; P2a slice 1, no AI, no UI, no channel; migration `0034_story_reading`, additive, created empty; plan `docs/research/story-memory-2026-10-11/plans/P2a.md` §1; P2a as a whole (this bullet and the four after it) verified 2026-10-12 with the full gates, `e2e/memory.spec.ts` (three P2a tests at the fake provider on the v2 launch) beside the v1 smoke, F-16.16–16.21 ticked; the live planted-error run and live fidelity for its seven prompt versions are owed by the author). **Tables:** `reading` (one row per reading, all pages, P2a-D1: purpose, pages, paras JSON, prompt and verify versions, model, tokens, reasoning tokens, `cost_usd` reader + verify, `cloud`, `finish_reason`, the yields `items`/`quotes_found`/`literal`/`verify_sent`/`verified`/`rejected`/`rejections` JSON/`cut`; indexes (model, created) and (node, draft)); `scene_read` (node + draft: `sentences` JSON `[gateHash, normalised text]`, `partials`, `full_at`, P2a-D2); `card` (node + draft: line, gist, `fields` JSON where/when/pov/year/flashback/elapsed each `{ value, quote, p, verified }`, `present` JSON, mood, theme, `anchor`, `seen` (gate hashes of the finished sentences it was made from), `line_hash`); `claim` (record or `pending` JSON name/subject, `subject_text`, slot, value, `value_key`, object, `how`/`from_id` reserved for P2b, voice, speaker, mode, quote, para, `anchor` JSON, proof, change, state, spot fields, created, proved; indexes (node, draft), (record, slot), (state)); `plan_link` (slice 5); `memory_backfill` (slice 3). Every derived row carries its draft; only the active draft's are live (`currentDraftId`, which writes nothing; `activeDraftId` makes the draft row for a writer). **Shared rules** (`src/shared/memory/`): `slots.ts` (closed list with groups fixed/lasting/transient/accumulating/knows/thread, 19 `detail:` labels, `parseSlot`, `SlotSchema`, `slotGroup`, `readerAccepts` refusing knows/thread, `slotLabel`); `claims.ts` (voices, modes, states, purposes, spot verdicts, `Claim`/`Card`/`CardField`/`Reading` schemas, `valueKey`, `setsState` = narration in now mode); `sceneRef.ts` (`sceneRefs`: "14.2", compiled chapters counted across parts, readable scenes numbered, a chapter document its number, a scene outside chapters its title); `gate.ts` (`finishedSentences` with the caret rule, `gateText` replacing roster name spans context-free by `⟨record ids⟩` so a rename never reads as a change while a swap of people does, `readSetOf` re-hashing stored texts under today's roster, `isCosmetic` (punctuation/whitespace, or one unknown word without a digit fixed by ≤2 edits, `editDistance` with transpositions), `readDecision` → nothing (empty/upToDate/cosmetic) / window (changed/stale: unread paragraphs, cosmetic ones included, plus paragraphs of stale claims; `cardStale` makes nothing cosmetic) / full (first/half/partials), `nextReadSet`); `windows.ts` (`planWindows` ±2 extended back to a naming paragraph, merged, split at 1,500 tokens; `planPages` 2,500 a long paragraph cut at sentences, never truncated; `cardDue` against the card's `seen`, `outputCap`); `markers.ts` (`findMarkers` negation/modal/conditional/comparative/simile/pluperfect/reported/question, `REPROOF_MARKERS`/`hasReproofMarker`); `literal.ts` (`LITERAL_PATTERNS` per slot over the quote with the subject masked, kinship included, traits not; modifiers only between a possessive and its cue; ages and deaths end their clause; `literalProof(claim, { paragraphs, p, span }, names)` → value cut to the quote's words or a miss reason: a marker anywhere in the sentence, a quotation open at the quote across the paragraph (`insideQuotation`, carried one paragraph), a kinship object; `cutValue`; `sentenceNames` from the scan's mentions); `anchor.ts` gains `anchorInPlace` (the run consecutive and in order, a pronoun's naming sentence marked `@` and before it); `reproof.ts` (`checkAnchors` → holds / moved / reproved / stale for live claims and subject waits, restored for a stale claim back in place; `locateInScene`, `cardParas`, `cardStale`); `reconcile.ts` (same in place, revive only a stale claim with a record, restated claims kept, drop, merge by containment within one reading, Dismissed `notRight` keys from each item's `stating`, P2a-D23 deletions; subject waits told apart by candidates and name span; shared-alias waits survive a full read); `asOf.ts` (`trueNow` since/last stated, `sheetAsOf` fixed with differing values side by side, lasting, transient, accumulating, others, later count; order inside a paragraph by quote offset then insertion; `ReadingOrder.at` reads an unreadable scene as of the one before it, an unknown node shows nothing as current); `typedVsText.ts` (`compareTyped` per field same/textOnly/typedOnly/differs/none, verified-only for POV and year, a POV whose record is gone as none, place and time only when words of their quote or verified, `effectiveSceneLine`); `views.ts` (the IPC shapes `SceneMemory`, `ClaimView`, `ClaimInfo`, `SheetMemory`, `MemoryStatus`, `MemoryNotice`); `health.ts` (`modelHealth`, `probeDue`); `renameEvidence.ts` (`textEdits`, `rewriteQuote`, `rewriteWords`, `sentencePairs`, `rewriteAnchor`); `quote.ts` gains `locateInText`; `dismissed.ts` keys `notRight` (`recordId|slot|valueKey|statingKey`: the quote's own sentences' gate texts, record tokens folded) and `notProblem` details; `sources.ts`/`passages.ts` gain `card` (meaning `map`: never canon, keeps its label, out of a search unless asked, `DEFAULT_PASSAGE_SOURCES`). **Main** (`src/main/memory/reading/`): `claimStore.ts` (the one writer of `claim`; lenient JSON; `invariantClaims` maps the active draft's claims onto P0's `InvariantClaim` with the scene's source: Idea, left-out and non-manuscript scenes are unreadable); `cardStore.ts` (upsert per scene and draft, the active draft's line and gist indexed as owner kind `card` while the node is a manuscript scene; `moveCardParas`); `readingStore.ts`; `sceneReadStore.ts`; `anchors.ts` (`runAnchors`: one query for a scene without claims or card; a non-readable scene's live claims parked as stale, uncounted; checkAnchors in place, `para` followed, free re-proof, stale claims restored, subject waits staled, P2a-D22 attach of shared-alias waits from a fresh scan, the card's `p` followed, then the invariants with a live violator demoted to stale and its kind answered); `views.ts` (`readingOrder` over readable scenes with refs and `at`, quote offsets for claims sharing a paragraph, `sceneMemory` Here/Mentioned/True now/typed/last read/unread, `sheetMemory`, `claimInfo`); `roster.ts` (`markRosterForRebuild` drops the `memoryRoster` settings row slice 2 builds); `renameEvidence.ts`; `recordEvidence.ts`; `claimFixture.ts` (test support). `passageIndexer.ts` indexes the `card` owner with a scene's text and notes (a scene that never had a card keeps no scan row); `drafts:switch` re-runs `queueMemoryIndex` so the new draft's card lines are found. **Hooks (v2 only):** `JobKind` gains `anchors` (mention queue) and `read`/`rebuild`/`spotCheck` (slice 3); `documentsWritten(db, ids, origin)` touches `anchors` per editor save and queues it at once after a bulk write; `queueMemoryIndex` queues it for scenes whose passages relabel (Idea status, compile inclusion); the queue's `anchors` run counts each healed kind as `memory.invariant.<kind>` (`memoryInvariantCounter`; the Worker's `DiagnosticWireCounter` accepts an unknown `memory.invariant.*` like an unknown `ai.request.*`); `applyRename` rewrites each rewritten scene's claims (quote located in the scene, value, value key, anchor by sentence position), card (quotes and their paragraphs, the POV's quote, line, gist, anchor) and read texts in that draft, the open scene's too, and `undoRename` puts them back with their own text (another draft's only with that draft's) while they still read as the rename left them; `sameAs` re-points claims (subject, object, speaker, source, waiting candidates), card present and POV, back-fill rows and `notRight` keys; `splitRecord` moves claims whose `subject_text` was a moved name and card entries whose quote names the record only by a moved name; `sameAs`, `splitRecord`, `deleteRecord`, `renameAlias` and `restoreAliasText` mark the roster for a rebuild. **Gaps:** an undo refused in the open editor leaves that scene's evidence on the old name until it is read (QUESTIONS). Slice 2's pipeline (next bullet) is the writer that fills `stating`, `seen` and the naming mark.
- Story memory v2: the reader pipeline (F-16.17, F-16.16's Read now; P2a slice 2; plan P2a §2). **Ids and plumbing:** `AI_FEATURE_IDS` gains `reader` (720 out / 6,000 in) and `verify` (400 / 3,000), both in `NO_REASONING_FEATURES` (`reasoning: 'off'` on Cloud and OpenRouter) and `LOWEST_EFFORT_FEATURES` in `request.ts`, which sets `CompletionRequest.lowestEffort`; the OpenAI adapter sends `reasoning_effort` (`lowestReasoningEffort`: `minimal` for gpt-5, `none` for gpt-5.1+, `low` for the o-series, nothing otherwise) only on a direct OpenAI key (not OpenRouter, not a local server), P2a-D15. Cost notes in `AI_COST_NOTES`; the planned `reader.v1:*`/`verify.v1*` rows moved to `SHIPPED_PLAN_ROWS` (design sizes kept), and `switchover/plan.ts` `plannedRow` reads both lists. Data sharing: *Story memory reading* and *Story memory check* rows (`site/public/docs` mirrors them); Settings shows only the open memory's toggles (`MEMORY_ONLY_FEATURES`, `featureToggleItems`; `summary` is v1-only since slice 3 turned it off in v2) with the reader and its check as one **Story memory reading** switch (`FEATURE_GROUPS`, `withGroup`), P2a-D24. **Prompts:** `reader.v1` (one system message: rules ≤ 900 tokens with the opening sentinel the e2e fake reads, then the cached roster; user turn: position, typed details, current card ≤ 200, near facts ≤ 400, anchored facts with `c<n>` ids ≤ 250, the numbered `¶p` window, `Card: yes|no`; near then anchored trimmed over the input budget; `readerRosterLine`/`readerRosterAddition` own the roster format) and `verify.v1` (numbered items: Who (roster lines), Fact or Card POV/year/flashback or On the page, Text; `verifyBatches` splits at 2,500 tokens of items and so the verdicts fit the 400; `verifyMaxTokens(items)` min(400, 20 + 28 per fact + 12 per card or on-page item), the strong spot check 40; a Who line marked `VERIFY_UNNAMED_MARK` may be picked only for a role). `src/shared/memory/readerAnswer.ts` reads both short-key answers leniently (`parseReaderAnswer`, `parseVerifyAnswer`) and salvages a cut-off reply up to its last complete item (`salvageJsonItems`, root keys only). **Pipeline** (`src/main/memory/reading/pipeline.ts` `runReading(db, { ai }, { nodeId, caretPara, requestId, signal?, purpose?, observe? })`): `readerMayRead` and `loadReadingScene` (`readerContext.ts`: paragraphs, sentences, a fresh name scan, typed details) → `readDecision` (stale paragraphs, `cardStale`, `knownWords` = other scenes' words, roster names and the project dictionary) → `planWindows`/`planPages` and `cardDue` → per piece `buildReaderPrompt` (roster from `rosterCache.ts`, near lines from the active draft's live fixed/lasting narration-now facts up to this scene, anchored lines) through `runAiRequest` (context hash = the messages, children `<requestId>:r<n>`/`:v<n>` cancelled through the parent signal) → a reply cut off (`finish_reason: length`) is salvaged and the paragraphs after its last quote read once more (the whole piece when its facts were out of paragraph order, `inOrder`); still cut, or a reply or retry that does not parse, they stay unread (no parsed piece at all writes only the reading row; `cacheIf` keeps unreadable replies out of the cache) → the ladder per item: slot (`readerAccepts`), presence (`locateQuote` in the window sent, then in the whole paragraph), subject (`src/shared/memory/ladder.ts` `resolveSubject`: a certain name pins it, a shared alias waits on its owners, first person goes to the speaker or to the typed POV only with the text's verified POV, anything else to the checker with every character named in the 8 sentences before (`namedAt`, the text from the earliest), the proposed record added only as `unnamed` (a role, "his wife"); a quote with no pronoun and an unknown capitalised name (`unknownName`) is refused; null with one candidate refuses), literal proof in code for a named single-sentence quote, else `verify.v1` batches per page with the card's POV/year/flashback and presence checks (a cut batch re-asks its unanswered items once; an item never answered is `unanswered`, its paragraph left unread, never a rejected quote); stored only when supported with the checker's subject, voice and mode (an answered null on an antecedent with two or more candidates → waiting; first person via a speaker only as dialogue or thought); new names (`n`) through the roster (a known alias is that record's; one two records share runs the ladder on its owners: named, or a shared wait), then `nameAsker` (shared with `nameQuestions`: not scanned, not Dismissed, the twin gate clear or a nickname of one record without a title) and a mid-sentence span in their paragraph (`nameSpans`); their proved facts wait on the name (`pending: { kind: 'name' }`), P2a-D7 → `reconcile` and one transaction (which reloads the scene first and runs `runAnchors` over what it wrote when the text or readability changed while the requests were out): the reading row (yields, cost reader + verify, `finish_reason`), inserts in text order, revivals, deletions, the card (first page's line and gist, each field from the first page whose quote locates in that page's text, presence from every page, anchor = its quotes' sentences, `seen` without the paragraphs a retry-only card never saw), `writeSceneRead` (`nextReadSet`, `full` only for a whole read). Stored claims count as refused only for the very fact (value, voice, mode). A local model's empty answer at its cap → `localCutoff`, nothing written. `rosterCache.ts` (`readerRoster`: the `memoryRoster` row with ids, base, additions, held names and dropped records; appends records and nicknames, rebuilds on a removal, rename, re-kind, removed nickname, new reader version or the 1,200-token cap, least named left out; extra ids for one reading only). `waiting.ts` (`settleWaitingName`: Yes → attached live when the anchor holds in place in a readable scene, else attached stale for the next reading; No → deleted; `settleOwnedNames`, run by `rosterChanged` in v2, settles waits on names a record now holds however it got them, and makes a name two records share wait on them as a subject); `questions.ts` `answerNameQuestion` wraps `answerQuestion` with it, and `listNameQuestions` asks about a waiting name seen once (`nameQuestions(…, { proved })`). **Service and channels:** `readerService.ts` `createReaderService` (Read now: `assertFeatureAllowed` for `reader` and `verify`, `runReading`, `memory:changed`, the questions when a name waits; AI failures as data, a local cut-off as `paused`); `memory:readNow` (`ai`), `memory:scene` (`read`; `SceneMemory.claims` = the scene's claims in text order), `memory:claim` (`read`), all `v2`; event `memory:changed { nodeIds, recordIds }`; `names:answer` pushes it when facts settled. **Eval:** `reader.v1`/`verify.v1` cases from the test book (`ai/eval/memoryFixtures.ts`, live scoring kinds `reader` and `verify`); `ai/eval/memory.eval.ts` (its oracle in `memoryOracle.ts` since P2b slice 3): the oracle replay over `testBook/project.ts` (`buildTestBookProject`, its scene layout `layOutTestBook` shared with the v1 builder `projectV1.ts` since Switch-over slice 5: records with shared surnames and the exact-case Captain, Dismissed names, chapters and typed details, the Idea scene, the scene left out of compile, h10 as h09 in an inactive draft) stores exactly the labels' literal and verified facts; the adversarial replay lets no planted error reach a live narration claim and counts each by reason; the live planted-error run (`MYTHSCRIBE_EVAL_LIVE=1 … npm run eval:ai -- memory`) is owed by the author (FEATURES F-16.17); its bars' measures (`PLANTED_PASS_BARS`, `plantedMeasures`, `plantedFailures`, read from the stored facts and the readings' yields) live in `plantedScore.ts` since Switch-over slice 5, shared with the live switch-over run. `readingFixture.ts` is the tests' fake provider and the prompt readers the e2e fake mirrors. **Gaps (slice 3):** nothing reads on leave or idle yet (only Read now); no queue, backlog, health gate, spot checks, Cloud cap or Rebuild memory; a fact attached stale on a Yes is read again only when its paragraph is next read; `summary.v5` still runs in v2.
- Story memory v2: reading triggers, the backlog, Rebuild memory and the Cloud cap (F-16.16, F-16.19, F-16.17's spot checks and health gate; P2a slice 3; plan P2a §3). **Renderer:** `src/renderer/features/memory/readerTriggers.ts` (started on a v2 open in `App.tsx`, stopped on close): opening another manuscript scene (`isManuscriptDocument`, now exported from `treeStore.ts`) settles the one left (`flushPendingSaves`, then `memory:settle { reason: 'leave' }`); a Research page, a folder, front matter, the chat, the Outline or a sheet is not a leave; `documentStore`'s `onSaved` calls `noteSaved`, which restarts the open scene's idle timer (`AiSettings.reading.idleMinutes`, 2–5, default 3, P2a-D5) that settles with `reason: 'idle'` and `caretParagraph` (the `passageParagraphs` index holding the selection head). Nothing is sent from the renderer; v1 does nothing. **Main:** `reading/readerService.ts` `createReaderService` owns one `createIndexQueue` over kinds `read`, `rebuild`, `spotCheck`, `backfill` (`JobKind` gains `backfill`; request ids `reader-<n>`), `jobs:status|cancel|resume` and `jobs:changed` in a v2 project (the summary queue's `onChange` is silent there). Gates before any request: Use AI and the reading switch (`assertFeatureAllowed`), the request path's provider checks (a key, the source or a model changed lifts a pause through `settingsChanged`), then `gate(db, level, probe)`: `switch` passes *Read now* (`queue.runNow`) and a rebuild's spot checks; `health` adds the health gate for asked bulk work (a rebuild, a confirmed backlog in settings row `memoryAsked`, an asked back-fill); `all` adds a local cut-off, the Cloud cap and the health gate (in that order) for unasked work. A hold throws `JobSkippedError` (`indexQueue.ts`: quiet, the row goes, the queue never pauses), so asked work behind a held background job runs; `publish` notices a hold lifting and re-checks the backlog and the back-fills. Cloud spend is tallied per request through `onSpend` (`runReading`, `runSpotChecks`), so a failed run still counts. `settle` queues with `indexAll(..., { afterRunning: true })`, so a scene left while it is read is read once more. Walks over many scenes share one `readBook(db)` (`readerContext.ts`: rows, roster, sources, known words), passed to `gateScene` and `loadReadingScene`. `settle` asks `gateScene` (the pipeline's gate, now exported) and queues only when something is due; with reading off it answers `off` and queues nothing. **Backlog** (`backlog.ts`: `dueScenes`, `priceReads` from the shipped cost rows, `draftEverRead`, `readableScenes`, `rebuildEstimate`): checked 2 s after a bulk `documentsWritten` (Replace, revert, draft switch, restore, Rename in book, import, and `document:save { outsideEditor: true }`, the chat's new and split scenes) and 2 s after every v2 open (the window's first reads go first); ≤5 due scenes are queued, more raise a `backlog` notice with the price (*Read them* queues all as asked, *Later* drops it), and a draft never read raises `draftUnread` priced like *Rebuild memory* (*Read* starts the rebuild). Turning Use AI on reads nothing by itself (QUESTIONS "P2a slice 3", item 2). **Back-fill** (`backfill.ts`): `record:create`, `names:answer`, `record:setAliases` and `research:applyFiling` call `namesAdded` with the names a record gained; `noteLateNames` writes one `memory_backfill` row per new norm (pages = naming paragraphs ±2 in scenes already read; `done` at once with none; one walk for all names), `backfillsByRecord` is the one rule for asking (a record's due pages together over 3), records at 3 or fewer run as a background `backfill` job (root-keyed) through `runReading(purpose: 'backfill', paras)` with a record's names merged per scene, more raise a `backfill` notice (*Read* → `asked`, *Not now* → `declined`); each row runs once. **Health gate:** `modelHealth` over the fast model's last 20 readings; paused → `settle` and the backlog queue nothing but one probe per 10 due scenes (`purpose: 'probe'`, through the queue, none while the Cloud cap holds); `rebuild()` refuses with the health message and the asked notices' *Read* waits, the notice is computed (*Keep reading anyway* → settings row `memoryHealth`, per model; *Switch to DeepSeek V4 Flash* sets the fast tier of OpenRouter or Cloud in `app-state.json`; *Dismiss* for the session). A local cut-off pauses background reading with one `localCutoff` notice until *Keep reading anyway*. **Spot checks** (`spotChecks.ts`, shared `spot.ts`): after a rebuild (`markRebuilt`, settings row `memorySpot`) 50 live literal claims go fast in batches of 10 (counted on the claim, `setClaimSpot`) and 20 verified ones strong, one per call, ±3 sentences, 40 tokens (a refusal deletes the claim, logged to the developer tools); afterwards the pipeline's `spotEvery` rides 1 literal claim in 20 on its page's check batch and `pickStrongDue` keeps 1 verified in 20 for the next strong batch (run at 5). **Rebuild memory** (`rebuild.ts`): `memory:rebuildEstimate` (P1's `estimateRebuild` over the active draft's readable scenes), `memory:rebuild` (progress row `memoryRebuild`, roster rebuilt, one `rebuild` job per scene through `runReading(purpose: 'rebuild')`, whose reconcile replaces what it did not prove again: `reconcile`'s `window.replace`, P2a-D25; resumed by `queue.load()` after a reopen; `closeRebuild` on every queue change ends it once no `rebuild` row is queued or running, however the last ended, and queues the spot checks when it read a scene; the queue's Stop ends it where it stood, `stopRebuild`; `memory:rebuild` takes no input), `memory:rebuildSummary` (new live lines per record and names left waiting, from the rebuild's readings), `memory:rebuildAction` (`notRightAll` → `dismissClaimsNotRight`, `notNames` → each name answered *Not a name*). **Cloud tally** (`src/main/ai/cloudTally.ts`, `app-state.json` `memoryCloud`, read leniently): Cloud readings and spot checks add their reported cost to `backgroundUsd` or `askedUsd`; background work stops at `capUsd + extraUsd` ($1 default; quarter steps, `cloudCap:get|set`, app-wide `any` channels); the `cloudCap` notice is computed while the cap is reached, until answered or dismissed once that month (*Allow another $1 this month*); `account:getCredits` and an hourly background `credits()` call `reconcileTally` (raises to the server's `reader` + `verify` period spend minus asked spend when the period starts this month; never lowers), P2a-D9, P2a-D10. **Status and notices:** `memory:status` (last reading in the draft, pause reason, today's reading spend, Cloud spent of the limit), `memory:notices`, `memory:answerNotice`, `memory:health` (per model, spot agreement by tier, cut-off rate of the last 100 readings); pushed as `memory:statusChanged`/`memory:noticesChanged` when they move. **`summary.v5` off in v2** (§3.7): `documentsWritten` never touches the summary queue, `backfillSummaries` stops after the voice job (no conversion hold), a v2 open drops leftover `summary` jobs (`dropSummaryJobs`) and does not load the summary queue, `ai:summarize` and `jobs:indexAll` answer `DISABLED` as data; `MEMORY_ONLY_FEATURES.v1` gains `summary`. **Queue fix:** `indexQueue.ts` re-kicks a pump that was just ending when a job was queued (`again`), which had left such a job waiting until the next kick. Until slice 5 the v2 chat reads a stored summary only through `currentSummary` (`summarize.ts`: while its content hash matches). Scenes and sheets are read only while a view shows them (`useSceneMemory`, `useSheetMemory`: watch counts, dropped at zero); `memory:changed` reads again the shown scenes it names or whose Here/Mentioned/True now name one of its records and the shown sheets of its records, `names:changed` its scenes, a roster `record:changed` everything shown; `memory:statusChanged`/`memory:noticesChanged` replace the status and notices. `App.tsx` subscribes and loads it on a v2 open and clears it on close; tests call `resetMemoryStore`. *Not right* takes the line out of shown scenes and sheets at once. Pure words in `memoryView.ts` (lines, notes, notices and their buttons, ⓘ, the cap and estimate lines, `formatCents` in `usageFormat.ts`); test fixtures and a fake main in `memoryFixture.ts`. **Views:** `ClaimLine` (every AI line: ⓘ `ClaimInfoCard` over `memory:claim` with *Go there* through `openPassage`/`locateText`; `NotRightChoices`), `thisScene/TrueNow.tsx`; `ThisScenePanel` reads the open scene's memory: with a card, *Here* shows the text's POV (a verified, quoted card POV) with "you typed: …" dimmed and the on-page characters with where, else the typed POV picker; *Mentioned* (heading *Named in this scene* until a card, P2a-D21) leaves out who is Here; True now shows whenever lines exist. The Book tab adds `RebuildMemory`; `RebuildDialog` (mounted in v2 by `App.tsx`): `memory:rebuildEstimate` → *Rebuild* → progress from `status.rebuild` (`MemoryStatus.rebuild`, pushed with the status) with *Stop* = the indexing store's cancel (`jobs:cancel`) → `memory:rebuildSummary` with `memory:rebuildAction`. The activity bar's kind `rebuild` reads `status.rebuild` (steps = scenes), its done note opens the summary. `records/FromTheBook.tsx` in `RecordSheet` (`memory:sheet`; as of the tree's open manuscript scene, else the end of the book). `editor/CardBlock.tsx` replaces `SummaryBlock` in `MetadataPane` for v2 manuscript documents (so v2 scene details never call `summary:get`). `memory/MemorySettings.tsx` replaces "Summarize all scenes" in v2's AI tab (idle minutes; on Cloud `cloudCap:get`/`cloudCap:set`; `memory:health`). `IndexingIndicator` words via `ai/indexingText.ts` ("Reading 3 of 12 scenes" in v2). **Main:** `memory:sheet` (`views.sheetMemory`); `memory:notRight` → `readerService.notRight` → `reading/notRight.ts`: `wrongValue`/`dismissClaimsNotRight` (moved here from `rebuild.ts`; also the rebuild summary's bulk *Not right*) keep each removed claim row in the settings row `memoryNotRight` by Dismissed id (`readerRows.ts`, typed `StashedClaim`), `restoreNotRight` puts them back on `dismissed:undo` (dropping links to gone readings or records, skipping gone scenes, drafts or subjects), then the handler queues `anchors` for them and pushes `memory:changed`; `notAbout` makes one fast `verify.v1` call (`pipeline.notAboutCheck`: every record named for certain in the quote and the eight sentences before, the picked one marked "not named here" when nothing names it, ±3 sentences; gated like *Read now*, spent as asked), moves a line the checker gives to the picked record (proof `verified`), writes a `notRight` row for the first record and keeps the move in the settings row `memoryNotAbout` (`notAboutMovedTo` → `reconcile`'s `movedTo`, so a later reading, a rebuild too, stores the line for the picked record), dismisses a refused one, and answers `unchecked` (nothing changed) when no check could be built (the quote's sentence changed) or no verdict came back; no `reading` row. A line whose quote is gone (stale) is dismissed with a key on its stored anchor, so *Undo* always works. *Wrong value* and decision 4's *Keep mine* (and closing it) call `access.assertWritable()` in their handlers (they write without a model). **Card values:** the views use `readingFrame.heldCard` (`reproof.holdingCard`: a field or on-page entry stands while its quote is in the scene once and its sentences' hashes are in the card's anchor; nothing stands in an unreadable scene; values the author said are not right are left out) for Here, `SceneMemory.typed` and `detailConflicts` (which reads the scene text only for cards that disagree as stored); `CardView.fields` = shown fields, `CardView.stale` = those no longer standing. `CardBlock` builds each row from `memory.typed` (the text's value only where the comparison counts it; else the typed value with "AI read: …, not checked" or "· re-checking after you pause" dimmed). `memory/CardValue.tsx` (`CardValueInfo`) is ⓘ with *Not right* for card values (Here's POV and on-page chips, card rows) → `memory:cardNotRight` (`write`, `v2`; `notRight.cardNotRight`: key `recordId|card|nodeId|field` or `card|nodeId|field|valueKey`). The `anchors` job (`runAnchors` answers `recordIds` and `card`) pushes `memory:changed` for the scene when a line changed state or the scene has a card, and then `reader.detailsChanged()`; `dismissed:undo` of a `notRight` row pushes for its scene and record and re-publishes the notices. *Update my detail* flushes saves and asks `memory:notices` again before writing. `reading/detailConflicts.ts` computes decision 4's notices from the active draft's cards (`draftCards`) on each look (`computeNotices`), skipping `notProblem` keys; `keepMine`. `sceneDetail:set` in v2 pushes `memory:changed` for the scene and `reader.detailsChanged()`; undoing a `notProblem` row does too. **Gotcha:** the This scene panel and the card block each watch the open scene, so `memory:scene` runs once per open (counted watchers), and again on every `memory:changed` naming it.
