plan `docs/research/story-memory-2026-10-11/plans/P2b.md` §1; P2b as a whole (this bullet and the four after it) verified 2026-10-13 on the reviewed code with the full gates, `e2e/memory.spec.ts` (three P2b tests at the fake provider on the v2 launch) beside the v1 smoke, F-16.22 and F-16.23 ticked; the live knows run and live fidelity for `reader.v2`/`verify.v2` are owed by the author). **Tables:** `topic_quote` (derived, per draft, the one writer `reading/topicStore.ts`: a secret's or thread's reveal, role `defining` or `merged`, the reader's `label`, para, quote, `anchor` and `stating` (gate texts, a Dismissed key's input) JSON, reading, `live`/`stale`; cascades with its record, scene and draft); `knew_before` (author-owned, `reading/knewBeforeStore.ts`: secret + knower, both cascade); `claim.topic_quote_id` (the merged reveal a knows or thread claim came in with, set null with it; stashed Not right rows default it); `scene_read.topics_read` and `settled` (`sceneReadStore.writeSceneRead({ topics, settled })`: a full read by a knows reader sets `topics_read`, its window reads keep it, an older reader's read clears it; `setSceneSettled`, `sceneReadFlags`). **Topics are records:** category `secret` (v2-only built-in, `requireCategoryId` lets it through; `recordKindOf('secret')`) and P1's `thread`, fixed (P2b-D1: `updateRecord` refuses moving into or out of them); their labels are not names (P2b-D2): `rosterOf` (twin gate, name questions), `nameScanShared` and `rosterHash` (so a topic rename rescans nothing), the v2 spellcheck words, the lazy back-fill (`noteLateNames`) and Rename in book (`checkAliasRename` refuses), and the renderer's near-name underline (`nameCheck.ts`) read `NAMED_KINDS` only; P1's thread records stop producing mentions. `recordStore.insertTopicRecord` (no gate; a held label gets " (secret)" / " (thread)", then the scene ref, `uniqueTopicLabel`), `yieldTopicLabels` (a name given later that takes a label's norm, by create, nickname, names list, Rename in book or its undo, makes the label yield the same way inside that write, so a label never blocks a name and `alias_norm_unshared_uq` holds; `withTopicRelabels` tells the handlers which topics changed), `topicLabels` (the name questions and the pipeline's `nameAsker` never ask about a label), `createRecord` for topic categories (exact same-kind duplicate warned as a gate result, `force` adds the suffix; no other names; `RecordCreateDialog` runs no `record:gate` as a topic's label is typed), `pruneTopics` (AI-made topics with no reveal, no claim and no author touch, `recordEvidence.emptyTopics`; run by `deleteRecord`, which answers the pruned ids for `record:delete`'s `record:changed`, after `tree:delete` and `drafts:delete` in v2, and by slice 2's readings), `deleteRecord` of a secret deletes the knows claims about it (`deleteKnowsAbout`), `sameAs` merges only same-kind topics (the kept one keeps its one label; the merged one's `defining` reveal becomes `merged`, so *Split* undoes it) and re-points `topic_quote`, `knew_before` (both ends, twins dropped) and `from_id` (`mergeEvidence`), and rewrites `knows:<id>` / `thread:<id>` in Not right keys; a topic delete or merge leaves the record roster alone. `topicStore`: `createTopic`, `mergeInto` (a `merged` quote; claims re-pointed by slot and tagged), `splitTopic` (P2b-D5: a new AI topic takes the reveal and its tagged claims, `split` Dismissed row), `deleteTopicEvidence`, `listTopics`, `invariantTopics`. **Anchors job:** reveals hold, move, go stale or are restored like claims (never re-proved for free), are parked in a scene that is not readable, and a save leaving a finished sentence unread clears `settled` (`AnchorsResult.topicQuotes`, `unsettled`). **Roster cache:** the `memoryRoster` row's optional `topics` part (`s3 label · secret`, `t2 label · thread`: the author's topics and the AI's the active draft backs, `recordEvidence.topicsInDraft`), appended or rebuilt on its own so the record lines never move (P2b-D10), labels cut to 4 words past 400 tokens, never dropped; `ReaderRoster.topicLines/topicShortOf/topicOf` (slice 2 renders them). **Reconcile:** identity includes `how`; `FreshClaim` carries `how`, `fromId`, `topicQuoteId`; `notRightLine` keys a knows line by `knows:<secretId>` and its how (also used by `notRight.ts`). **Pure rules (`src/shared/memory/`):** `knows.ts` (the ten hows, `knowsLine` counts/partial/other per P2b-D7, a thought only for its thinker, `learnerHere`), `topics.ts` (kinds, categories, `NAMED_KINDS`, steps and `stepRank`, caps, `topicLabel`, `topicTerms` with names folded to `@recordId`, `sameAsCandidates`), `whoKnows.ts` (`checkedUpTo`, a scene with no words passing; `readersSince` with a `stale` mark; `knowsAsOf` with `knownStale` and `touchedIn`; `knowsGrid`; `sceneSecrets` touched by any line or reveal there, tension lines marked `stale`; never a "doesn't know" cell), `threadsAsOf.ts` (status derived from counted steps only; a reveal is the thread's quote, never a status change), `years.ts` (decision 5: `elapsedBreaks` with month counts in words and vague month spans, `yearIn`, `storyYears`, `birthYear` with the Pinned `Born:` / `Born (…):` line, `statedAge`, `hintsSince`, `ageAsOf`, `recordAge` leaving flashback ages out of later scenes); `views.ts` gains their view schemas; `asOf.ts` leaves knows and thread claims out of True now and From the book; `invariants.ts` gains `knowsObject`, `knowsLearner`, `topicEmpty`, `topicName` (`invariantKnows`, `invariantTopics`, `invariantIndexed`). **Test book:** h13 (group learner, a refer, a remembered thread step, no typed year) and h14 ("A year later", the Captain's debts deduced, suspected, mistaken, a rumour, Mr. Harrow at the table), labels `threads`, `years`, `ages`, knows negatives and `addedFor: 'P2b'` records the `reader.v1` eval roster leaves out (its token rows did not move). **Gaps (by design, later slices):** `SceneMemory.secrets` and `SheetMemory` knows/threads/age/topic are added with the views that fill them (slices 4, 5); the gate's `settled` write and the pipeline's `topics_read` came with slice 2 (next bullet).
- Story memory v2: `reader.v2`, `verify.v2` and the pipeline (F-16.22, F-16.23 reading; P2b slice 2; no migration, no channel; plan `docs/research/story-memory-2026-10-11/plans/P2b.md` §2). **Prompts** (`src/main/ai/prompts/`, catalogued after P2a's, `since` F-16.22; `reader.v1`/`verify.v1` stay for their rows and the ledger, and now export their shared helpers `fitLines`/`fitText` and `splitVerifyBatches`): `reader.v2` (same sentinel; one system message: rules ≤ 1,150 with the knows, thread and new-topic rules and "never list who does not know", the record roster (P2a's, ≤ 1,200), then `READER_TOPICS_HEADING` and the topics roster (≤ 400, never dropped, `(none yet)` when empty), so a topic change never moves the record prefix; user turn: head ≤ 200, near ≤ 250 (less whatever the topics roster runs past 400, `trimmed.rosterOver`, counted in the yields as `topicRosterOver`), topic lines ≤ 150 (`Secrets and threads here:`), anchored ≤ 250 (knows lines `c7 r7 knows s3 overheard from r2 ¶3`, steps `c8 t2 thread advances “…” ¶4`, re-checking reveals `s3 revealed “…” ¶2, re-checking`), the window, `Card: yes|no`; over 6,000 near, then topic lines, then anchored go; caps unchanged) and `verify.v2` (item kinds `claim`, `knows` (Who, From, Secret, Fact, Text), `step` (Thread, Fact, Text), `reveals`, `same` (one line, both revealing sentences), `pov`/`year`/`flashback`, `present`; `verifyV2MaxTokens` min(400, 20 + 36 a knows item + 28 a fact or step + 12 the rest), strong 40, 48 for a knows line; `verifyV2Batches`). `readerAnswer.ts`: `ReaderClaim.how`/`fr` (a knows line's value defaults to its how), `ReaderTopic` from `n` items with a kind (`label` null for a listed one's restated reveal), `VerifyVerdict.how`/`fr` read as written. `readerAccepts` is gone: a fact never takes the knows or thread slot, those come as knows lines and steps. **Context** (`reading/topicContext.ts` `topicContext`: the topics as of the scene for one reading: `lines` (the topic lines: secrets whose `topicTerms` the window shares or that someone Here holds, with each holder's earliest counted line, never an absence; open threads it names), `itemLine`, `revealOf`, `termsOf`, `open(kind)` (the Same candidates' pool: the author's topics and the AI's the active draft backs, threads not resolved as of the scene, secrets with their holders); `topicItemLines` for the spot checks and *Not about*). `readerContext.anchoredLine` names knows lines and steps (null for an unlisted topic), `revealLine`. `rosterCache` keys the row on `reader.v2` (a v1 row is rebuilt once). **Pipeline** (`pipeline.ts`, what P2b adds): the gate marks a scene read before `settled` when nothing finished is unread or only cosmetic, and reads a re-checking reveal's paragraph like a re-checking fact's; per reading the records, `topicContext` and the Here set before the reading (`hereChips` over the typed details and `heldCard`); topics first (`NewTopic`, keyed `<piece>:<id>`; a listed one's restated reveal only for a re-checking reveal in the piece: presence, Dismissed (`topic|kind|statingKey`, silent, its facts skipped), same-label fold, caps 3/2 a page (`topicCap`), presence failing → `topic`); knows lines and steps (`topicLine`: the secret or thread a listed one of that kind or a new one of the piece, else `record`; an unknown how → `slot`; a learner a character or group; a dropped topic's facts go with it) through the ladder (`ladder(..., { kind: 'knows' | 'step', topic, how, fromId })`: a group only `named` (`group`), a knows line never waits (shared → `subject`, an antecedent null → `unconfirmed`), a step's subject is its thread, never literal proof); new names' knows lines and steps refused (`name`). The checker batch adds knows items (source candidates: the reader's, the speaker, characters and groups named ±1), step items, an on-page check per page for a named character learner neither Here before nor on this reading's card (P2b-D8), a Reveals item per kept new topic and per restated reveal, up to 3 Same items per new topic (`sameAsCandidates` over `open`). After the verdicts: `hereNow` (+ this reading's verified POV and on-page entries) and `shownHere`; a new topic's Reveals not ok → `topic`, unanswered → `unanswered` and its paragraph unread; else merged into the first Same-ok candidate still there (`topicsMerged`) or created (`topicsCreated`; both counted in the yields' rejections JSON, not rejections); a knows line takes the checker's how (none → `unconfirmed`) and source (only for told/overheard/read and from its candidates), a character learner not Here → `notHere`; a step takes the checker's step; a learner an on-page check showed joins the card's present list (its anchor too). In the transaction: topics made (`createTopic`, origin `ai`) or merged (`mergeInto`, a `merged` reveal) before reconcile, their facts pointed at them (knows → `object_id`, steps → `record_id`) and tagged with the merged reveal; restated reveals revived (`reviveTopicQuote`); re-checking reveals in what was read and not revived deleted; `writeSceneRead({ topics: true, settled })`; `pruneTopics` last. `ReadingOutcome.topicIds` (made, merged into, revived, reveal gone, pruned) → `readerService` `recordsChanged` → `record:changed { roster: false }` (handlers); `recordIds` includes the knows lines' secrets. `LadderReject` gains `notHere`, `group`, `topic`, `topicCap`. `claimCheck`/`notAboutCheck`/`agrees` handle knows lines (a knows item, its how must agree) and steps (a step item; *Not about* does not apply); `notRight` labels a knows tombstone "Tomas: knows the sold map (knew before)"; spot checks and *Not about* send `verify.v2`. `ClaimView` gains `how` and `fromId`. **Costs and sharing:** `reader.v2:first`/`:window`/`:card`, `verify.v2`, `verify.v2:pro`/`:spot` in `SHIPPED_PLAN_ROWS` (plan §6 sizes); the index, session and heavy-month tasks, `switchover/plan.ts estimateRebuild` and `backlog.ts priceReads` read them; `AI_COST_NOTES.reader`/`.verify` and the data-sharing rows name the secrets and threads. **Eval:** `reader.v2` cases h04, h05, h08, h13, h14, a window with topic lines and the maxed input; `verify.v2` typical knows batch, reveals and same, maxed, strong (`memoryFixtures.ts` `MEMORY_V2_EVAL_CASES`, after every other case); live scoring adds knows (learner, secret, how, source), steps and the never-knowing learners (`ReaderKnowsLabel`, `ReaderStepLabel`); the live planted run's spot checks send `verify.v2`. Tests: `reader.v2.test.ts`, `verify.v2.test.ts`, `readerAnswer.test.ts`, `pipelineKnows.test.ts` (21 cases), `roster.test.ts`, `request.test.ts` (reasoning off on Cloud and OpenRouter), `readerService.test.ts` (`record:changed`); e2e `memory.spec.ts` "who knows what and threads are read (P2b slice 2)" (the fake reader and checker read P2b's patterns). **Review fixes (2026-10-13):** `readerV2PageTokens(roster.topicLines)` sizes `planPages`/`planWindows` (their new `pageTokens` argument) so the near lines, then the page, give what the topics roster runs past 400; `rosterCache.buildTopics` stops the roster at `TOPIC_ROSTER_MAX_TOKENS` (1,000) leaving out the least-evidenced AI topics (`TopicsPart.dropped`, `ReaderRoster.topicsLeftOut`, counted `topicRosterLeftOut`); `recordEvidence.topicsInDraft(db, draftId, readable)` weighs evidence in readable scenes only (the roster and `topicContext.open`), and `revealPicker` picks only live reveals in readable scenes (`topicItemLines` too); `verify.v2` reveals items answer voice and mode (28 tokens) and `revealCounts` drops a rumoured, supposed or dreamt secret (`notCounted`, `topicNotCounted`); `ladder.groupHeadIn` lets a group's head noun name it; a rebuild re-checks every reveal in the scene (`rechecks`) and deletes the ones not revived; here checks for every named learner not Here before the reading; `NewTopic.same`/`mergeInto` are `TopicRef`s, so a later page's new topic may merge into an earlier page's (`freshFacets`); a cut reply's dangling new ids (`danglingTopicIds`, `retryAfter`, `withoutLines`; `mergeAnswers` renames every unlisted retry id) and unanswered Same, Reveals or here checks leave their paragraphs unread (`unanswered`); `notHere` and unanswered-here refusals are taken out of `verifySent`; `gateScene` and the reading's `settled` look past the idle caret; `readerService.afterReading` puts `topicIds` into `memory:changed`; `notAbout` refuses a non-knower (VALIDATION), sends `hereCheck` beside the knows item for a character not Here, and re-reads `fromId` from the verdict (`NotAboutCheck.from`). **Gaps:** no view, channel or panel shows knows lines, secrets or threads yet (slices 4, 5); live fidelity for `reader.v2`/`verify.v2` and the live knows run are owed by the author (the replays and the one live key are the next bullet).
- Story memory v2: `reader.v3`, `verify.v3` and the code gates (F-16.17, after the live planted-error run of 2026-10-14; no migration, no channel; QUESTIONS "Live planted-error run of 2026-10-14"). **Prompts**: `reader.v3` is `reader.v2`'s layout and fit (`buildReaderMessages(rules, input)` in `reader.v2.ts`) with new rules: compact facts (voice, mode, sp, o, chg left out at their defaults), the slot list with what each holds, the subject named or pointed at in the quote, the value from the quote; `verify.v3` is `verify.v2`'s items and caps (`buildVerifyV2Prompt`) under rules that refuse one person for several, a want as a thing and a value of the wrong kind; the pipeline, the spot checks and *Not about* send v3. **Sizes** (`reader.v3.ts`): pages and window pieces of 400 prose tokens (`readerV3PageTokens`, less a topics roster's overflow, never under 100), `max_tokens` min(720, 160 + 1.2 × prose + 240 with the card); `windows.ts`'s `PAGE_TOKENS`/`outputCap` stay for v1/v2's goldens. **Cut replies** (`pipeline.ts`): read again from the last quoted paragraph (its facts dropped from the cut reply, `withoutFrom`) unless that is the whole piece, in two halves, each merged with its own id suffix. **Gates** (in the ladder, facts only, after the subject step, before literal proof): `slotFitsKind` (`kind`: places), `valueStated` (`value`), `wishWord` (`wish`), `quotePointsAtSubject` (`subject`); `readSlot` reads a bare detail label. **Prices**: `READING_V3_ROWS` (`aiCostNotes.ts`) feed `plannedRow` for the rebuild (`switchover/plan.ts`, `REBUILD_PAGE_TOKENS` = 400) and the backlog (`backlog.ts`); `SHIPPED_PLAN_ROWS` keep the design's figures for the planned tasks. The roster cache keeps `reader.v2` as its format version (unchanged lines). Offline: `memory.eval.ts` plants each live failure behind a gullible checker.
- Story memory v2: `reader.v4` and the second run's gates (F-16.17, after the second live planted-error run of 2026-10-14, 19:20; no migration, no channel; QUESTIONS "Second live planted-error run of 2026-10-14"). **Prompt**: `reader.v4.ts`, v3's layout, pages and input with the slot list first under its own heading (voice, dialogue, memory… named as never slots; voice/mode/sp/chg under FIELDS) and what each value must be; the pipeline sends v4 (verify stays v3). **Cap**: the reader's budget is 1,000 (`ai.ts`); v4 asks min(1,000, 160 + 1.4 × prose + 240 with the card); v1, v2 and v3 keep their 720 (`FULL_OUTPUT_CAP`, `READER_V3_OUTPUT_MAX`, `buildReaderMessages`'s `ceiling`). **Cut-offs**: `reading.cut` counts every reply cut (reader and checker); `reading.finish_reason` is `length` only when a cut was not recovered (a retry cut again or unreadable, no rest to re-read, a checker batch cut twice: `lost` in `pipeline.ts`), so Settings' cut rate and the live reports' "not recovered" line are final cut-offs; `plantedScore.ts` reports recovered readings and cut replies apart (`cutLines`). **Gates** (`ladder.ts`, facts only, before the checker): `valueOfKind` (`valueKind`: an age is an age, an object no feeling), `absentValue` (`absence`), then after `quotePointsAtSubject`: `possessedOnly` (`possessed`; not for kinship, relation, history, nor a subject that is the relative itself), `locationStated` (`where`), `diesInQuote` (`past`), and one claim one slot (`duplicate`, `sameQuote`: same record, value and paragraph, another slot). `readSlot` also reads a slot's other number ("trait", "roles", "detail:scar"). **Prices**: `reader.v4:first`/`:window`/`:card` in `READING_V3_ROWS` feed the rebuild and the backlog.
- Story memory v2: reader precision (F-16.17, plan `docs/research/story-memory-2026-10-11/plans/ReaderPrecision.md`, after the third live run of 2026-10-14, 21:35; no migration, no channel; QUESTIONS "Reader precision"). **Bar**: `plantedScore.ts` `scoreStored` judges every live fact by the claim label on its sentence and slot, else by a hand verdict (`storedVerdicts` in `testBook/labels.ts`), else counts it wrong as not judged yet; the live report's JSON carries every stored fact (`stored`) so new verdicts re-score it without a run; quotes found counts only `QUOTE_MISSES`, `COST_REFUSALS` are reported, verifier pass has no bar; `MYTHSCRIBE_EVAL_RUNS` (1–3) reads the book that many times (`mergeRuns`). **Prompts**: `reader.v5` (v4's layout and cap; the voice field is `src`, `parseReaderAnswer` reads `src` or `voice`; open-ended value rules), `verify.v4` (v3's items and caps; who did it, what each open-ended slot holds, a report is rumour). **Tiers** (until `ReaderNarrow.md`, next bullet): the pipeline's checker batches split per page by `STRONG_CHECK_SLOTS` (history, traits, relation, kinship, role) into a fast batch and a strong one (`tier: 'strong'` through `runAiRequest`, so routing overrides apply); spot checks and Not right stay on `verify.v3`. **Gates** (`ladder.ts`, facts only, before the checker): for a pronoun subject, `pronounOfOtherGender` (`subject`; gender only from a title, `genderOfNames`), `pronounRivals` (`ambiguous` for two or more, `subject` when the one is someone else: the quote's sentences and the nearest sentence naming someone who fits, possessives and names after a preposition left out); `actsOnSubject` (`actor`); `ageIsSpan` (`valueKind`); `relativeValue` (`possessed`); `relationValue` (`object`); `slotFitsKind`: no location on a place, no location, role, kinship, clothing or objects on a thing. **Prices**: `reader.v5:*`, `verify.v4` and `verify.v4:strong` in `READING_V3_ROWS` (the strong row kept as history).
- Story memory v2: reader narrowed to concrete facts (F-16.17, plan `docs/research/story-memory-2026-10-11/plans/ReaderNarrow.md`, the author's decision of 2026-10-14 after the fourth live run; no migration, no channel; QUESTIONS "Reader narrowed to concrete facts"). **Slots**: `NOT_READ_SLOTS`/`slotIsRead` (`src/shared/memory/slots.ts`): traits, history, relation, role, clothing, objects stay on the closed list but the pipeline refuses a fact in one as `notRead` right after reading its slot (before the quote is looked for); `COST_REFUSALS` reports it; `modelHealth` leaves it out of the items (`HealthReading.rejections`). **Prompt**: `reader.v6` (v5's layout and v4's cap; only the read slots); the checker stays `verify.v4`, every batch on the fast tier (`STRONG_CHECK_SLOTS` and the tier split removed from `pipeline.ts`). **Gates** (`ladder.ts`): `possessiveOwner`/`ownedSlot`/`possessiveOwners` (a his or her before a body or `detail:` value: gender must fit, one owner in its clause else the nearest sentence before; `subject`/`ambiguous`), the item's `owner` (the one person code settled for a pronoun or possessive; the checker's pick must be them, else `subject` after the check), `locationPhrase` (`LOCATION_VALUE_MAX_WORDS` 5 after one leading cue, no clause word; `where`), `bornIsWhen` (in `valueOfKind`), `kinshipStated` (in `valueStated`: a kin word for one person or a marriage word), and plural kin in `relativeValue`. **Scoring**: `scoreStored` judges only read slots (`notRead` counts the rest), a verdict may name a `voice`, literal precision judges an unlabelled literal fact by its verdict; `FINDABLE_CONTRADICTIONS` (`memoryOracle.ts`) leaves out labelled contradictions whose sides are claim labels in slots no longer read (the C7 pair). **Instant cards**: no read-first for a slot no longer read (`instant.ts`). **Prices**: `reader.v6:*` rows; `estimateRebuild` and `priceReads` price one fast check batch per page.
- Story memory v2: suggestions you approve (F-16.17, plan `docs/research/story-memory-2026-10-11/plans/Suggestions.md`, the author's decision of 2026-10-14; no migration; QUESTIONS "Suggestions you approve"). **State**: `CLAIM_STATES` gains `suggested` (`claims.ts`); `isAccepted` (live or stale) is the one rule every reader of memory uses (`asOf.ts` placed lines, `whoKnows.ts` lines, `threadsAsOf.ts` steps, `candidateInputs.ts` and `checkBook.ts`, `scribe/instant.ts` and `scribe/evidence.ts`; `years.ts`, `membership.ts`, `readerContext.ts` near lines and `topicContext.ts` already read `live`). The pipeline inserts `suggested` (pending → `waiting`); `attachClaims` settles a wait as `suggested`; `waiting.ts` deletes a Yes whose anchor no longer holds instead of attaching it stale. **Lifecycle**: `checkAnchors` (`reproof.ts`) treats a suggestion like a live claim (holds, moved, reproved); `runAnchors` deletes a suggestion that goes stale, is parked (unreadable scene) or breaks an invariant (`AnchorsResult.dropped`, counted in `anchorsChanged`), so `stale` is always an accepted line and `reconcile`'s revival and `restoreClaim` bring it back `live`; `reanchorClaim` keeps the state. `reconcile`: a fresh item restating a suggestion keeps it, `same` counts for suggestions, and a rebuild (`replace`) keeps a `live` claim whose anchor holds. The invariants hold a suggestion's anchor like a live claim's. Spot checks pick suggestions (`spotChecks.ts`). **Main**: `reading/suggestions.ts` owns `listSuggestions` (active draft, readable scenes, by node, record (a knows line's secret too) or claim ids; literal first, then reading order; `SuggestionView` = `ClaimView` + name, line, note), `suggestionCount`, `acceptSuggestions` (suggested → live; `undo` live → suggested) and `dismissSuggestions` (`dismissClaimsNotRight`, so Undo in Dismissed restores a suggestion). `readerService` exposes them (pushing `memory:changed` and the status); channels `memory:suggestions` (read), `memory:accept`, `memory:dismiss` (write), all `v2`; `MemoryStatus.suggestions` is the count; `SceneMemory.suggestions` the scene's. `rebuild.ts`'s summary sheets are the rebuild's suggestions still waiting; `RebuildAction` lost `notRightAll`. **Renderer**: `memory/suggestions/` (`suggestionView.ts` words, `SuggestionList.tsx` for Needs a look's "Suggestions from this scene" group (`thisSceneView.ts`), `SuggestionsReview.tsx` the review deck grouped by character (`ReviewDeck` gained `groupAcceptLabel`: "Accept all for Mara (3)"; Accept / Later / Dismiss, applied at the end or with Apply; decisions kept in a ref so the deck's apply-at-end sees the last one), `SuggestionsPanel.tsx` the side work frame); `memoryStore` owns `suggestionsShown`, `acceptSuggestions` and `dismissSuggestions` (toasts with Undo); side work gains `suggestions` (`sideWork.ts` `showSideWork`/`hideSideWork`, `SideWorkStatus`'s "4 suggestions", `AssistantBody`, `SideWorkDialog`); `RebuildMemory`'s summary shows the deck over the rebuild's claim ids. **Prompt**: `reader.v6` again since 2026-10-15 (concrete-only suggestions, the author's decision after the sixth live run): `NOT_READ_SLOTS` is v6's one set (traits, history, relation, role, clothing, objects); prices `reader.v6:*` rows; the cost registry pins the reader row to `reader.v6` (`V1_PRICED` in `costRegistry.ts`). `reader.v7` (v6 + traits and history with v5's value rules) stays catalogued with its golden and eval cases (`labelIsRead(claim, v7)` in `memoryFixtures.ts`), unsent. **Eval**: `PLANTED_PASS_BARS.storedPrecision` 0.85 (suggestions worth accepting); `liveClaims` counts live and suggested; `acceptAll(book)` (`memoryOracle.ts`) accepts every suggestion for the knows, scribe, findings and switch-over replays (the switch-over's `rebuildMemory` then recomputes and judges); the fifth run's verdicts in `storedVerdicts`. **e2e**: `memory.spec.ts` accepts through the status bar's review (P2a) and the rebuild deck (P2a slice 4, the in-place switch-over), and through `memory:accept` where later steps read accepted memory.
- Story memory v2: the knows eval and one live key (F-16.22's merge condition, built for the author; P2b slice 3; no migration, no channel, no prompt change; plan P2b §3). **One key** (`src/main/ai/eval/liveProvider.ts`): `liveSettingsFromEnv(env)` reads `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` first (OpenRouter's base URL, `openRouter: true`, models `MYTHSCRIBE_EVAL_FAST_MODEL`/`MYTHSCRIBE_EVAL_STRONG_MODEL` else `OPENROUTER_DEFAULT_MODELS`), else `OPENAI_API_KEY` with `OPENAI_BASE_URL` (an `openrouter.ai` host is still OpenRouter, `isOpenRouterUrl`), else throws `LIVE_KEY_MISSING` naming both; `liveProviderFromEnv(env, { fetch })` builds the OpenAI-compatible provider and a `describe` ("OpenRouter (OPENROUTER_API_KEY)", never the key) for reports; `liveRequestDeps(live)` is the one request-deps for live runs (in-memory ledger and cache, the Auto routing so reasoning goes off as the app sends it, the reported cost counted, `spent()`, and `costUnknown()` when a charged route answered with no reported cost on a model the price tables lack, so the 50¢ stops stop there; a loopback server, `isLoopbackUrl`, charges nothing); `liveProviderError(err)` is what a live run stops on and reports (an `AiProviderError`'s code and copy; anything else still throws); the OpenAI-itself route passes `openAiURL: OPENAI_API_URL` (`buildOpenAiProvider` sets only the destination, keeping OpenAI's own fields) so a shell's `OPENAI_BASE_URL`, which the SDK reads on its own and the e2e relies on in the app, never moves it; `openRouterKeyFromEnv` is the price probe's key (`OPENAI_API_KEY` only on an OpenRouter base URL). Every live path uses them: `prompts.eval.ts` `runLivePrompts` (now through `runAiRequest`, so each case leaves with its feature's routing and reasoning, and the report's cost is the reported one; `renderLiveReport(…, route)`), `memory.eval.ts` `runLivePlanted`, `knows.eval.ts` `runLiveKnows`, `aiProbe.probe.ts`. Each is driven end to end offline against `fakeChatServer.ts` (a fake OpenAI-compatible server behind `fetch`: answers, reported cost, recorded URL, headers and body) from `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` alone, nothing spent. **The oracle** (`ai/eval/memoryOracle.ts`, moved out of `memory.eval.ts` and shared): `oracleRead` proposes the test book's claim labels, knows lines (`LabelKnows` gained `voice` and `speaker`), thread steps and topics (a topic not on the roster is proposed new with its sentence here; a listed one by its roster id, except at its defining sentence, proposed again under its alias so the Same item merges it), its card carrying the labelled elapsed hint and the scene's learners on the page; `oracleVerify(request, truth?)` answers facts, knows lines (the label with the fact's own how first), steps, Reveals (only a labelled topic by label, or a plant's `OracleTruth`), Same (by label, else by sentence) and on-page checks by the labels; `oracleChatServer` puts both behind the fake server; `readBook(book, ai, { order: 'book' | 'reverse', observe, stopAt, providerErrors })` (`'stop'` in the live runs: a provider error becomes the scene's `error: …` outcome and reading stops; `readingStop` says where and after how many scenes). **`knows.eval.ts`:** `scoreKnows(book)` (knows recall, partial lines, attribution precision over every grid cell (counted and partial lines, a named source checked against the label's; `wrong`, `wrongPartial`, `wrongFrom`), how and from accuracy, steps and each thread's end status, topics mapped by evidence (`topicMap`) with the duplicate rate and the extra ones listed, negatives as grid cells, anything from never-read scenes, yields by reason, cut readings) scores the oracle replays (book and reverse order: perfect, the grid as of h14 (`knowsAsOf`), story years and ages (`storyYears`/`recordAge` over typed details and held cards) as labelled, invariants clean, no `No scene shows`/`checked up to` in any request), the adversarial replay (`plantedRead` + `PLANT_TRUTH`: refused or uncounted, the counted picture unchanged; the h06 dream and h07 town talk as `told` lines on the listed engagement stored with mode `dream` / voice `rumour` in `others`) and the live run (`runLiveKnows`: two passes on fresh copies, book then reverse order, stop at 50¢ over both or at an unknown cost, a provider error stopping its pass with the report still written; `knowsReport` against `KNOWS_PASS_BARS`, P2b-D23; `test-results/eval-knows/knows-<date>.md`/`.json`). **Ladder:** `SubjectInput.sourceId` (the pipeline passes a told/overheard/read line's source): dialogue the source speaks is that source's first person, so the learner is resolved from the names in reach with the source left out, instead of `none`. **Gaps:** the live knows run, the live planted run and live fidelity are owed by the author (`docs/OPERATOR-SETUP.md`, "One key for every live run").

`TopicQuoteView.reading`), `listKnewBefore`, `sceneReadFlags`, and the readable scenes with no words as `empty`; the knows eval reads it too); `storedGrid` (columns by `nameIndexer.namedTotals`, shared with the roster cache's cut), `sheetKnows(knows, recordId)` (a knower's `KnowsSheetView`: its line per secret, the lines that never count apart, `later`), `secretTopic`. `views.ts` composes them without a cycle (knowsViews never imports views): `sceneMemory` adds `secrets: SceneSecrets` (`sceneSecrets` over the scene's Here and POV), `sheetMemory` adds `knows` (characters and groups) and `topic` (a secret; then `deleteTopicEvidence` and `deleteRecord`), and `restoreTopicNotRight` (`dismissed:undo` calls it first: the snapshot comes back through each store's row writer (`restoreTopicRecord` relabels a label another record took since, `insertTopicQuoteRows`, `insertClaimRows`, `insertKnewBeforeRows`, `insertDismissedRows`, `repointTodos`), its sibling rows leave Dismissed, and the anchors job re-checks the scenes). the only place "No scene shows Mara learning this (checked up to 1.2)" and the tension lines are made), `WhoKnowsWhat` (This scene, after True now: touched secrets with ⓘ `TopicInfoButton` and "readers since", holders Here as `ClaimLine`s (Not about offers characters and groups only, `notAboutKinds`; half-knowing ones muted), the muted scoped lines, up to 3 tension lines, "+ N more in Book" switching the panel to the Book tab scrolled to the grid), `TopicInfo` (`TopicInfoCard`: the defining reveal with *Go there*, merged reveals with *Split*, *Not a secret* on AI-made ones), `KnowsGrid` (sticky first column in its own sideways-scrolling box; `KnowsCell` "overheard 9.3", "learns later (17.3)", "knew before the book (you)", "—" titled with the scoped line; a picked cell opens below: ⓘ and Not right, *Remove*, or *Knew before the book* for a character or group), `useNameOf`. `records/AsOfPicker.tsx` + `asOf.ts` (`useAsOf(start?)`, `useManuscriptScenes`) is the one as-of picker (From the book, Knows, The secret, the Book tab, which starts on the panel's scene); `KnowsSection` (a character's or group's Knows) and `TopicSection` (*The secret* in place of From the book, with *+ Someone knew before the book*); `RecordSheet` hides Names, Rename in book, nickname Split and Named in on a secret's or thread's sheet, fixes its category and offers *Same as…* within its kind only; the sidebar's **Secrets** section (`sectionList` `secretsSection`, after the categories, never a stray; `RecordTab` → `SecretList` with "readers since 5.2 · 3 know" and the shared `ai/AiMark`); `SECRET_CATEGORY` in `@shared/categories` names it for `categoryOf` only (not in the library or any picker); `openRecordSheet` is shared by the Cast and the grid. **Absence tests:** `knowsAbsence.test.ts` (the Story notes block at both budgets, `agent.v8`'s v2 `lookup`/`cards`/`find_passages`, the `reader.v2`/`verify.v2` requests of a reading over a book with secrets, knew-before marks and a POV with no line, and every eval fixture) and `storyNotes.features.test.ts` (every v2 prose request) never carry "No scene shows", "checked up to" or a not-knowing line. e2e: `memory.spec.ts` "who knows what in the panel, the grid and sheets (P2b slice 4)" in a new project (closes the slice 2 project, reopens its own after reading so the tree holds the invoked scenes). **Review fixes (2026-10-13):** every shown scene is read again on any `memory:changed` (its secrets, tension lines, "+ N more" and "checked up to" are as of it over the whole book; a sheet when named, or when it carries `knows`/`topic` and a scene moved); `sceneMemory` passes `sceneSecrets` only the Here records of `KNOWER_KINDS` (and the POV only when it is one), and *+ also here* offers no secret or thread; the scoped empty line when neither), `records/ThreadsSection` (a named record's Threads as of [scene ▾], scoped when empty), `TopicSection` takes `kind` (*The thread*: status, `TopicInfoCard`, `ThreadSteps`); `RecordSheet` shows Age for characters and Threads for every named record, and a thread's sheet The thread in place of From the book; the sidebar's Threads list (`RecordTab` → `ThreadList`) notes each thread's status as of the end of the book. The Book tab reads Cast, Who knows what, Threads, Timeline, Rebuild memory. e2e: `memory.spec.ts` "threads, the timeline and ages (P2b slice 5)" (`newV2Project` and `reopenProject` now shared with slice 4; the fake reader reads `<A> was born in <year>.`, `<A> was <number word>.` and a paragraph opening "Three winters later"). **Slice 5 review (2026-10-13):** *cost:* `rowTexts` keeps each document's paragraphs while its stored content is the same string (cleared at 5,000 nodes) and `heldCard` keeps what of a card stands on that text array (`reproof.cardStanding`, by the card's fields, present and anchor; `withoutDismissed` applies the author's *Not right* each time; `holdingCard` is the two together), and `cardStanding` hashes only the paragraphs a quote sits in; `sceneTimes` reads a scene's text only when its card has a year, flashback or elapsed field (and, for Here, a POV or someone on the page): the years, the Timeline and a character's sheet went from about 220 ms to about 10 ms on 90 scenes (`timeViews.perf.test.ts`, budget 50 ms). The anchors job's `result.card` is true only when what of the card stands moved (per draft and scene, `lastStanding`) or its quotes changed paragraph, so a typing pause that leaves a card standing pushes no `memory:changed`. *Fidelity:* `ElapsedHintView` carries the card's `quote` and `para`, `text` is the book's words (`years.hintWords`), and `breaks` reads the value or the quote; `AgeView` gains `bornOnly` (a birth year, the scene's year not known; the Timeline leaves it out) and a stated age's `since` is `AgeSinceView[]` (hints and later typed or quoted years, `changesSince`); `SheetMemory.age` is a `SheetAgeView` (`age`, `checkedUpTo`) so "No birth year or stated age read (checked up to 1.2)" is scoped; `TimelineRow.sources` (the card's year, flashback and elapsed fields the row's year rests on) gives each row ⓘ through `CardValueInfo`; `threadStatusText(thread, checkedUpTo)` never states absence ("revealed in 1.1 · no opening step read yet", "not opened (checked up to 1.2)"), `showsNoSteps` keeps "No scene shows this thread yet" to a thread with no reveal and no step, `noThreadsText` scopes the empty list; `sheetTime` also lists a step about the record in the first person (`ladder.pronounPerson`; the narration or a speakerless thought of a scene whose Here POV is the record, or its own dialogue or thought). *Refresh:* main's `bookOrderMoved` sends `memory:changed` (the scenes, no record) after `tree:create`/`rename`/`duplicate`/`move`/`delete`, an Idea flip in `sceneMeta:set` and a compile exclusion flip in `compileState:set`; `saveRecordPageWith` sends it naming the record when its canon Pinned "Born:" lines (`years.bornLines`) changed; `names:changed` reads the sheets with Threads again; the Timeline's *Ages of* is `memoryStore.agesOf` (outlives the Book tab each scene remounts). **Known gap:** only a verified card marks a flashback; a typed Flashback switch needs an additive column (QUESTIONS, P2b slice 5 review, item 10).

never rules, E0 or the question; the cap is held with the retry turn counted) → one streamed call (`ShownStream` holds the markup back) → a cut-off or unreadable reply asked once more → one Self-Route (`selfRoute.ts` `resolveNeed`: chapter number, Roman or title, scene ref or title, range; `widenKeys`: whole scenes ≤ 8,000 as `W` items, or, when none fits whole, the paragraphs of the first around the turn's hit; nothing new to read (the open scene, nothing fits) ends in `notFoundLine` with no second call; `onStep` "Reading …"; the cost cap is checked first; a second need or an unresolvable one ends in `notFoundLine`) → edits (`offerEdit` from `agent.ts`; `pin` only when Step 0's `cues.sheetWrite` holds, else dropped with `PIN_NOT_ASKED`; `pinOf` resolves the name with `pinRecordOf` (a whole name or nickname, exact case where the alias asks for it, never a part; a name several records hold is the one whose main name it is) and, when the message names records, only one of them (`PIN_NOT_NAMED`) → an `AgentEdit` `sheetLine` with place `pinned`/`ideas`/null; unknown record, secret/thread or already said becomes a sentence in the answer; Plan drops every edit and says so, `PLAN_NO_PIN`/`PLAN_NO_EDIT`) → `found:false` on an answer, lookup or idea turn → the code's Not found line (an edit or draft turn keeps its words, `CHANGE_READY` when it has none) → citations (`parseCitedAnswer`, `quoteHeld`: 3 to 25 words within one of the item's `quotable` stretches, the book's own words: scene paragraphs without `¶n`, a claim's paragraph never its fact line, who-knows quotes never its lines; maps never kept, `markerize`; a sentence naming a record or scene with no kept citation is marked not checked, an uncited first sentence too unless advice or a question (`isAdviceOrQuestion`); on edit and draft turns only the naming sentences that are not about her own change, `I …`) → search misses (`logSearchMiss` for a kept W citation placed in one paragraph outside the navigation's or Step 0's hits whose words the turn's terms do not match, `passageMatches`: a paragraph FTS placed but a ranking left out was found, not missed; slice 6 review). Step 0's `caretParaOf` matches the caret window's blocks as a run (nearest the window's block count from the top for a short window); a knows question naming a knower and what is known runs the ordinal search, so its absence has a scope. Nothing but misses is written: no claim, card or reading. **Renderer:** `applyAgentEdit` applies a `sheetLine` with `record:addLine` behind the page store (`writeBehindPage`: flush, write, reload an open Pinned/Ideas page; typing on that page during the write is saved first and the write run again, at most `SHEET_WRITE_TRIES`, then `SHEET_BUSY` and her words kept), reports a Pinned conflict as the change's notice, and its Undo is `record:removeLine` the same way (main finds the line by its words and the turn's mark, nearest its index); `SheetLineCard` asks "Is this an idea or canon?" (Idea, Canon, Skip; off in Plan) through `assistantStore.pickSheetPlace`; Auto and Apply all skip sheet questions; `filedFrom.ts` labels an `aiWritten` line "Written from the chat, 13 Oct". **Eval:** `scribeFixtures.ts` (14 cases on the test book, scoring kind `scribe`: citations kept, maps refused, absence scoped, pins placed); `prompts.eval.ts` checks each version's own cap (`promptInputBudget`). Rows `agent.v9:open/lookup/need/widened` are shipped in `aiCostNotes.ts`. **Gaps:** the answer UI (`ScribeAnswer`, markers, "not checked") is slice 5; `cite.v1`'s check and the one retry are the next bullet (slice 3); `navigator.v1` (slice 4) is what fills `navigation`.
- Story memory v2: `cite.v1` and the scope rule, Ms Scribe's checks after the code quote check (F-16.25's checks; P3 slice 3; no migration; plan `docs/research/story-memory-2026-10-11/plans/P3.md` §3). **Feature** `cite` (`AI_FEATURE_IDS`; 200 out / 2,500 in; `NO_REASONING_FEATURES` and `LOWEST_EFFORT_FEATURES`, P3-D21; `MEMORY_ONLY_FEATURES.v2`, its own toggle until slice 4's Ms Scribe group; data-sharing row "Ms Scribe’s quote check"; `AI_COST_NOTES.cite`; `cite.v1` moved to `SHIPPED_PLAN_ROWS`). **Prompt** `cite.v1` (`src/main/ai/prompts/cite.v1.ts`, fast, JSON; `CITE_RULES` ≤ 250, cached, with the labels as `agent.v9` reads them: "happened" by Now, "later in the book" after it, notes, source documents and filed lines never make an event happen): the question (1,000 characters) and `Now: <the open scene>`, then each cited sentence (≤ 8, whole with all its quotes, the later ones dropped to fit what the cap leaves; the first always sent, its contexts cut around the quotes (`clipAround`, caps `CITE_FIRST_CONTEXT_CHARS` 450 → 120) until it fits beside a full question and scope item) with each kept quote as `E2 [happened, 9.3 ¶4] “quote” in: <its sentence ±1, or its own sentence when that is too long>`, no sentences at all for an absence checked on its scope alone, then the scope item (`Scope: searched …. The first sentence: “…” It cites 9.3.` and up to 5 passages the search found before it, after it for a last answer; ≤ 400); `citeMaxTokens` min(200, 20 + 10 a sentence + 20 for the scope); `parseCiteReply` reads `{"v":[{"i","ok":"yes|partly|no"}],"scope":"ok|earlier|none"}` leniently (`salvageJsonItems`; `unfence` is exported from `readerAnswer.ts`). `agent.v9` gains the main-claim retry turn (`agentV9UnsupportedTurn(sentence, reason)`, `UNSUPPORTED_REASONS`: noCitation, quotesDoNotSay, earlier, later, notFoundShown, noScope, absence), sent after the user turn (the prompt before it unchanged); `fitScribePrompt` holds the cap with that turn and the cut-off retry counted (`WORST_RETRY`). **Run** (`runScribe.ts` with `src/main/scribe/checks.ts`): the scope rule first (`enforceScope`: a navigation that searched less than every scene up to now, or the whole book when asked, on a first/last/ever/never, knows or learns question gets Step 0's every-earlier-scene search added, its scene hits after the navigation's keys, its search hits first and every one kept, and its scope the answer's, with a "Searching …" step); per answer (`checkAnswer`): code's quote check, then the main claim by code (P3-D14; only on an answer, lookup or idea turn whose question asks for a fact from the book, `asksForFact`: not an opinion, what-if or brainstorm; a Plan run asked for a change or a sheet line is checked as a change turn. The claim is the first sentence, the second after a bare yes or no (`mainClaimAt`); one that names a record or a scene (`needsCitation`) and keeps no citation is `noCitation`, unless it says the search found nothing (`isAbsence`) or is a possibility or an opinion (`isHedged`); someone not knowing said as a fact (`statesAbsenceAsFact`, or a bare "No." to a knows question) without the scope is `absence`, and marks a later such sentence unsupported; an ordinal claim or an answer that found nothing that does not say what was searched (`statesScope`: a search word followed by what it covered) is `noScope`), then, when something was cited (or an answer that found nothing has passages to check) and the main claim stands, one `cite.v1` request (the step "Checking the quotes…", `CHECKING_QUOTES`; request id `citeRequestId`; skipped with the `cite` toggle off or over the cost cap; a failed check keeps the citations quote-checked and notes the request): `citedSentences` (each kept quote in its sentence ±1 across a scene's paragraphs, `quoteContext`), the scope item for an ordinal answer citing a scene (`scopeHits` over the turn's hits in reading order before the cited paragraph, a passage's or claim's own paragraph from its open target, as the scenes read now around the search words) or an answer that found nothing (every hit, the earliest first); none sent with nothing to list), `applyCite` (`no` marks the sentence unsupported, its citations dropped by `markerize`; the main claim `no` (not a hedged one), or scope `earlier`, is the main claim unsupported), `citationLevels` (`cite` only when every sentence that kept it was found `yes`). A main claim unsupported on an answer turn under the cost cap is asked once more (`hooks.reset`, the retry turn quoting the main claim); the second answer is checked the same; still unsupported (or a broken or need reply, or a retry that fails, noted in the developer tools; a cancel still stops the turn), the turn is `notFoundLine` with `notFound` and `retried`, the scenes the answers cited first in its "looked in". Costs and usage are summed over every call; the turn keeps the answer's model. **Eval:** `citeFixtures.ts` (supported, unsupported, partly, scope-earlier, scope-ok, maxed, notes-as-event, later-as-now, absence-scope, maxed-first; scoring kind `cite`, verdicts by index and the scope; `prompts.eval.ts` scores an agent.v9 absence with the shared `statesAbsenceAsFact` and `statesScope`) and `agent.v9`'s `retry-unsupported` case. **e2e:** the slice 2 test checks one `cite.v1` request after a cited answer (quote and sentence checked) and none after uncited ones, then a planted unsupported answer ("Who sold the map?") asked again once and ending "Not found in the manuscript (I looked in the open scene 1.1 …)", two answer calls and two checks (the fake words the retry apart, so its check is not the request cache's). **Gaps:** which questions ask for a fact and which sentences are possibilities is read from their words until `navigator.v1` (slice 4) names the turn's kind; the UI for the marks is slice 5. `src/shared/textClip.ts` (`flatText`, `clipAtWord`) is the one word-boundary clip (cite.v1, filing).

only now's chapter is marked, so the prefix holds within a chapter; ≤ 1,100), all ≤ 3,000; the user turn = Now, the open document, the selection's opening, two turns, the message, the menu (`E3 happened 9.3 ¶4: snippet`, fitted to the input cap); or `NavigateReader.checkAnchors` (the handlers' `checkAnchorsNow`, the anchors job's own run with its counts and `memory:changed`) then *Read now* on the read-first scenes, then the card with the read's cost only if nothing that bears on it is still unread, else a lookup; a stale or contested slot → a lookup answer with the handover keys and Step 0's menu, no navigator call; a slot with no value or a knows question that is not a yes → the navigator, the handover keys on its menu, the read's cost carried) → the navigator off: an answer with no navigation → up to 3 steps (a first step unreadable: no navigation; the Sources with `sourceLabelWords` (`src/shared/scribe.ts`: the prompt's label words, an Idea "your idea, not in the book", "filed from <doc>" from `ScribeCitation.doc`), `shownRef` (paragraphs from 1), the quote, "quote checked" / "quote and sentence checked"; a click opens the scene at the quote (`openScene`), a cited scene note's scene with its Notes panel (`CitationTarget.notes`, `openNotesOf` in `src/renderer/features/editor/openNotes.ts`, search's notes opener too), a Research page with the Research tab (`openResearchPage` in `src/renderer/features/research/openResearch.ts`, shared with search and This scene; no passage jump, the page editor takes none) or the record's sheet (`openRecordSheet`, after leaving focus mode; at its top, no paragraph jump yet); a source drops its title when its reference names it; the scope line; "Read <chapter> too" when widened; a Not found answer in the not-found style); every other turn as before (v1's `QueryAnswer` untouched). An instant card's stored text (`instantCardText`) holds positive lines only, since turns ride along as history. **Cost** (P3-D23): `runScribe` reports the quote checks' share (`ScribeTurn.checkUsd`, lenient); `ai:navigate`'s answer reports the read-first's share apart (`readUsd`); `costPartsOf` (assistantStore) stores the read-first's, the navigator's, the answer's and the check's parts on the turn (`ChatMessage.costParts`, lenient, only parts that cost anything, null under two); `RequestCost` takes `parts` and titles the sum with `describeCostParts` ("read first 0.1¢ · navigator 0.2¢ · answer 1.3¢ · check 0.02¢"). **Steps:** `AgentSteps` folds into `stepsSummary` ("Looked up 2 things · checked the quotes"). **Settings:** Story memory › Details (`MemorySettings.tsx`) holds `src/renderer/features/memory/SearchMisses.tsx` (`scribe:searchMisses`, the count and the five newest with the question, scene ¶, quote and words); the Ms Scribe switch and the `navigator`/`cite` rows are slice 4's, and `nameAssistant` now names a label's "Ms Scribe" too (one pass). **Suggestions** (`src/shared/assistantSuggestions.ts`, `SuggestionContext.memory`): v2 adds four record lookups ("Mara's eyes?", first appearance, what she knows, her ideas) named from the character records; `gateOf` gates a question by `agent` and a routed action by `navigator` in v2 and hides the continuity check until P4. **e2e:** `memory.spec.ts` "Ms Scribe in the chat (P3 slice 5)": three instant cards with no request, a cited "first" answer (chip, "happened", quote and sentence checked, scope line, the cost title), an Ideas answer, a Self-Route turn and Settings' "Search misses: 1"; no request carries an absence or "checked up to".
- Story memory v2: the Ms Scribe eval (F-16.24–F-16.26 final verification; P3 slice 6; no migration, no channel, no prompt change; plan `docs/research/story-memory-2026-10-11/plans/P3.md` §6). **Labels** (test data, `src/main/memory/testBook/`): `TEST_BOOK_LABELS.pages` (the Bell's Pinned note, the Captain's and Lottie's Ideas) and `questions` (`LabelQuestion` in `labels.schema.ts`: `id`, `question` (unique words, the oracle finds a question by them), `asOf` (Now), `kind` of `QUESTION_KINDS`, `plan`, `terms` (a good navigator's search words), `record`, `selection` (the chat's focus, for a route on the selection), an optional late `edit`, and `expected`: `instant`, `lookup`, the settled route (`action`/`pass`/`target`/`chapter`), `scene` + `quoteHas` (+ `also`; `contested` for the other side of a contested value, cited too), `checkedUpTo` (a who-knows card's scoped line), `notFound`, `label` (`idea`/`notes`), `place` + `pinText`, `trap` (words only an Idea scene, a cut scene or an Ideas page holds; kind `trap`)); `project.ts` adds `addTestBookPages`, `editTestBook`, `editedParagraphs`; `testBook.test.ts` checks every kind is asked, each cited scene or page holds its words once (edits applied), and each kind carries what it is scored on. **Oracle** (`src/main/ai/eval/scribeOracle.ts`): reads each request as sent (never the database): `navigator.v1` step 1 the label's route or a search of its terms (`ideas` for an idea), step 2 `use` of the menu lines in the label's scene or page; `agent.v9` cites the label's words where the evidence holds them (`answerEvidence` parses E0/E/W items, fact lines dropped; `quoteAround` copies a 3–25-word quote), says what was searched from the Searched line, answers an absence as "I found no scene where …", `found:false` for not found, a `pin` with the label's place (none in Plan), and asks `{"need":"13.1"}` once when the evidence lacks the scene; `cite.v1` says yes when a quote holds the label's words (or `also`, or the contested side), no to a sentence citing an Idea, Idea scene, cut scene or Not canon item without naming it so (`namesNotTheBook`) and to an absence answer's "knows" claim (`claimsKnows`), and "earlier" when a listed passage is the label's scene and the answer cites another; reader and checker are `memoryOracle.ts`'s. `ScribePlants` replace a question's replies (`cite` too: a fooled checker, so the scoring must catch what passes it); `scribeOracleServer` puts it behind `fakeChatServer`, which now answers `stream: true` as SSE (text in two chunks, finish, usage with the reported cost). **Eval** (`src/main/ai/eval/scribe.eval.ts`, the `eval` project, so `npm run test` runs it): `openScribeSession` builds the book, reads it (oracle via `fakeAi`, or the live reader through the run's deps), writes the pages, makes the edits with the anchors check, indexes scenes and records, Use AI on; `askScribe` runs `runNavigate` then `runScribe` (Ask, or Plan where labelled) with a `NavigateReader` over `runReading`/`runAnchors`, recording each request and reply (`recordingProvider`, memory only); `scoreAsk`/`scoreScribeRun` score routing, instant rate, correctness (the label's scene and words cited), citation validity (made citations with a sent id and held words, `quoteHeld`), cite.v1 agreement with the oracle checker, scope and not-found honesty, absence as fact, an absence answered "knows" (`affirmsKnowing`), what is never the book stated as its fact on any answer (a kept `idea`/`ideaScene`/`cutScene`/`notCanon` citation on a sentence that does not name it so), trap honesty, Ideas labels, sheet-line place and words (one line holding `pinText`), a who-knows card's `checkedUpTo`, both sides of a contested value, widenings and misses, cut-offs, reasoning tokens on answer turns, cost per kind and the open-question average; `scribeReport` holds them to `SCRIBE_PASS_BARS` (P3-D20) with no prompt text and no key; `runLiveScribe` stops at $1 (also during a live reading), at a provider error or an unknown cost. Tests: the oracle replay (every question right, every bar met, nothing absent sent in a user or a system turn past the fixed rules, no claim/card/reading row changed (rows compared whole), misses logged equal to the turns' count and none for a ranking drop, invariants with the answers' citations), the adversarial replay (card and unheld quote dropped, an earlier first retried then Not found with the second check served from the cache, an unasked pin refused, a pin in Plan dropped with `PLAN_NO_PIN`, other words or a second sheet line scored wrong, "yes, he knows" and a cut scene stated as fact refused by the checker then Not found, the same past a fooled checker scored wrong, a second need Not found, navigator prose never forwarded), and the live run end to end against the fake (bodies, cost, the $1 stop, the live-read stop, a 429, an unknown cost); `describe.skipIf(!LIVE)` writes `test-results/eval-scribe/scribe-<date>.md`/`.json`. `memoryOracle.invariants` takes citations. **Owed:** `MYTHSCRIBE_EVAL_LIVE=1 OPENROUTER_API_KEY=sk-or-… npm run eval:ai -- scribe` (`MYTHSCRIBE_EVAL_READ=live` for a live reading first; vitest refuses an unknown `--read` flag). **P3 final verification (2026-10-13, on the reviewed code):** full gates pass (typecheck, lint per folder, `npm run test` 8,415 passed, `eval:ai` 68 passed with the token report unchanged, `test:e2e` 16 passed with no flake); `e2e/memory.spec.ts`'s three P3 tests ("Ms Scribe v9 answers…", "the navigator routes and finds…", "Ms Scribe in the chat") are the v2 path at the fake provider, the v1 smoke is unedited by P3; F-16.24–16.26 ticked, the author's checks in a development build and the live runs owed.

P4 as a whole (this bullet and the five after it) verified 2026-10-14 on the reviewed code with the full gates, `e2e/memory.spec.ts` (four P4 tests at the fake provider on the v2 launch, slices 2–5) beside the v1 smoke, F-16.27–16.30 ticked; owed by the author: the live contradictions run and live fidelity for `judge.v1`). **Tables** (`schema.ts`): `finding` (per draft, cascades with the draft and the record; `key` unique per draft = `rule[:variant]|npKey`; `a`, `b` JSON `FindingSide`s, `extra` JSON `FindingExtra` (C4's year); `verdict_ab`/`verdict_ba`/`why`, `confirm`/`confirm_why`, `state` `unjudged|held|hidden|open|unclear|fine`, `cost_usd`, `shown_at`, timestamps) and `verdict_cache` (not per draft: `key` = sha256 of prompt version, tier, order and item; LRU-pruned at `VERDICT_CACHE_MAX` 5,000 by `used`). **Shared** (`src/shared/memory/`): `findings.ts` (rules, variants, states, `RULE_ORDER`, close reasons, `FindingSide` claim/presence/note, `npKey`/`findingKey`, `findingState`: held and hidden win, two `c` open, any `ok` fine, else unclear; `raisesAlone`); `candidates.ts` (one function per rule over `CandidateInputs`, pure: C1 groups values by agreement with each group's first value (`valueGroups`, so a bridging "grey-blue" never merges "grey" and "blue"; kinship per object, kin to an unnamed relative per kin word), pairs each group with the earliest narration-now group; C2 `dead` pairs a presence with the latest death before it, a living claim cancels, `body` pairs consecutive narration-now hair/scars with no appearance between and no change claim; C3 the first counted tells before a later learns; C4 `birth`/`falls` on a typed or verified quoted year, same calendar, more than one year off; C5 an advance after a resolve in an earlier scene with no opens between; C6 notes via `noteValues.ts` (one representative claim per value group, not per scene; fixed slots any line, lasting/transient only lines dated to the claim's scene, at most 2 sentences; the notes indexed once a run by `noteIndex`, names compiled once per list); `deathWord`/`lifeWord` (a qualified or hedged value is neither); C7 history/detail by shared object or two content words, at most 5 pairs a claim, check only; `backgroundCandidates` = C1–C6 minus Dismissed keys; flashback sides make `hidden`, stale claims `held`); `noteValues.ts` (note sentences, names in notes, label lines, `datedScene`); `membership.ts` (`groupsOf`, for C3's group knew); `looseEnds.ts` (`looseEnds(input)` → `LooseEndsView`: threads, characters 3+ scenes, planned cards, window max(8, a quarter), horizon min(furthest drafted, checked up to), at most 10); `dismissed.ts` `notProblem` `finding`/`loose` keys and `rekeyFinding`; `years.ts` `bornLine`, `yearCalendar`, `ageYears` (years only: months, weeks and days are no age to compare), `birthSource` (what `birthYear` and C4 read); `literal.ts` `patternValue`, `kinWords`; `invariants.ts` `findingUnjudged`, `findingDismissed`, `findingSide`. **Main** (`src/main/memory/findings/`): `candidateInputs.ts` (`findingsFrame`, `candidateInputs` over the active draft's readable claims, slim cards and canon notes (`canonNotes`: FTS5's content table, re-read only when a note owner's passage hash in `memory_scan` moved, live owners only (records that exist, documents in the Research tree), in page order: records, then Research tree order, then paragraph; the same array while neither the rows nor that order moved, so `noteValues.noteIndex` indexes the notes once for many recomputes, keyed on that array and the records' names); `claimStore.draftClaims` reads a draft's claims as plain rows (`CLAIM_FIELDS`, `change` the one column mapped), since the ORM's row mapping cost as much as the read, and `reading.test.ts` holds it to the ORM's; `typedVsText.foldDetail` keeps the values it folded (bounded); `looseEndInputs`, whose deaths skip a living value in a verified flashback); `findingStore.ts` (upsert by key keeping verdicts only while the judged pair is unchanged (`judgedPairMoved`: a side's JSON, whose claim side carries `seen` = `claimSeen` of quote, anchor, voice and mode; the order; `extra`; the flashback), else cleared and the cache asked again; close with a reason, `setVerdicts`, `setConfirm`, `markShown`, `invariantFindings`); `verdictCache.ts`; `recompute.ts` `recomputeFindings(db, now, {cached?})` (a vanished key closes `gone`, a dismissed one `dismissed`; a row no longer produced stays, held, only while one of its claim sides is stale; a C7 row stays while its claims do, restated held while one is stale); `findingsJob.ts` `runFindingsJob` + `findingCounts`. **Wiring** (`handlers.ts`, v2 only): every `memory:changed` goes through one `memoryChanged(payload)` helper that also queues the `findings` job (mention queue kind, keyed to the manuscript root, its 1.5 s debounce); so do a roster change, `drafts:switch`, a record merge or delete, `record:addLine`/`record:removeLine`, `dismissed:undo` of a `notProblem` row and a passages run that re-indexed a record page or a Research document; the job emits `memory:findingsChanged` `{open, unclear, waiting}` when it ran. `memoryChanged.test.ts` proves `memory:changed` is emitted only from that helper. **Tests:** `candidates.test.ts` (each rule's positive and skip cases), `findings.test.ts` (lifecycle), `findings.perf.test.ts` (90-scene book, warm recompute < 50 ms; then with 30 more characters, 300 Pinned paragraphs and a 2,000-paragraph canon Research page, still < 50 ms, the fastest of ten after that setup; about 25–30 ms here since the P4 slice 6 review, in the `perf` project), `ai/eval/findings.eval.ts` (the oracle-read test book: every labelled contradiction a candidate, C7 on a check, h16's hidden, no finding negative but the judge traps, invariants clean, loose ends as labelled). **Test book:** h15, h16, the planned card h17 (`source: 'plannedCard'`, no words; `buildTestBookProject(…, { without: 'P4' })` builds the book as P3 knew it), `addTestBookNotes`, labels `presence`, `notes`, `looseEnds`, `secretsNobodyLearns`; since the P4 slice 6 review also the thread *Mrs. Tull's introduction* (opened and advanced in s02, never touched again: a thread loose end), the secret *Anne's sold pearls* (told the reader in 19.1's last paragraph, learnt by nobody) and two `finding` negatives on the Idea scene h11; P4 records and scenes stay out of the older eval fixtures so the token report is unchanged (the knows replay reads a secret nobody learns as known to readers from its telling scene).

300 out / 3,000 in; `NO_REASONING_FEATURES` and `LOWEST_EFFORT_FEATURES`, so fast and strong calls alike go out with reasoning off; `MEMORY_ONLY_FEATURES.v2`; absent from the Auto routing table: each call names its tier) behind the v2 grouped switch **Contradiction check** (`FEATURE_GROUPS` `contradictionCheck`, `features: ['judge']`); data-sharing row (app and site); `AI_COST_NOTES.judge`; `judge.v1` and `judge.v1:pro` are `SHIPPED_PLAN_ROWS`. **Prompt** `src/main/ai/prompts/judge.v1.ts` (`JUDGE_SENTINEL`; `JUDGE_RULES` and `JUDGE_CONFIRM_RULES` share one core, each ≤ 450 tokens; `judgeQuestion` words each rule's question order-neutrally and positively; `judgeItemText` renders `N. question`, `A [place · relative order · voice, mode · flashback · story year Y] says V: context`, `B …`, `Between: …` (≤ 2 hints), swapped for `ba`, or masked (no number, no book place) for the cache key; `judgeItem` picks the context cap (450 → 160 characters) on the masked text plus a fixed place allowance, so a scene move never changes the words; `judgeBatches` ≤ 10 pairs and ≤ 2,500 tokens; `buildJudgePrompt` (`max_tokens` min(300, 20 + 26n)); `buildJudgeConfirmPrompt` one pair at ±3 sentences ≤ 1,000 in, 90 out, for slice 3; `parseJudgeAnswer` lenient with `salvageJsonItems` for a cut reply, `w` kept only with `c` and cut to 8 words; `parseJudgeConfirm`). **Main** (`src/main/memory/findings/`): `judge.ts` (`judgeContext` over the rules' inputs plus the rows, notes by owner and paragraph, the roster's `NameIndex`, stored cards and readable scenes, each lazy; `resolveSide` (a claim's quote in its paragraph via `quoteContext`, a presence from the held card, a note sentence ±1; null when gone); `judgePair` (question, sides with `says` by rule, flashback, typed or stated year, time-skip hints from held cards' elapsed fields that `elapsedBreaks`); `pairItem` and `pairKey` (`verdictKey` over `gateText` of the masked item, per order and tier); `cachedPairVerdicts` (the recompute's `cached`); `judgeFindings` (cache hits settled free, up to `maxPairs` sent: per batch the A,B call then the B,A call with only the pairs that order lacks, child ids `<requestId>:j<n>`, each answered pair-order cached and the finding's verdicts and cost share stored as each call returns)); `findingsService.ts` (`createFindingsService`: `judge` (background: holds → `JobSkippedError` for the switch or Use AI off, no provider, a running rebuild, 30 pairs a day (`memoryJudgeDay` settings row), the daily cap, the Cloud cap; ≤ 10 pairs a run ordered by `judgeOrder`: the scene just read, `RULE_ORDER`, reading order; `again` while pairs remain) and `checkBook` (asked: every unjudged pair, past the Cloud cap and the day's pairs, never past the daily cap); spend to the Cloud tally; pushes `memory:findingsChanged` and `memory:changed`); `findingViews.ts` (`findingsView`: open and unclear findings with both sides resolved, those touching the asked scene first, counts, waiting, judging; empty with the switch off). `findingsJob.ts` `runFindingsJob(…, { cachedVerdicts: true })` gives the recompute the cache lookup. **Wiring:** `readerService` adds `judge` and `checkBook` to its queue's kinds and only dispatches them (`createFindingsService` inside it), `queueJudge()` (after the findings job, a settings change, an open and a rebuild's close), `judging()`, the scene just read noted in `afterReading`, `findingsChanged` dep; the footer's today and the Cloud tally (`MEMORY_CLOUD_FEATURES`) count `judge`. Channel `memory:findings` (`read`, v2). `readerRows` exports `readRow` and `writeRow`. **Tests:** `judge.v1.test.ts` (golden), `judge.test.ts`, `findingsService.test.ts`, reader service, handler, request, OpenAI, aiSettings, channel access; eval `judgeFixtures.ts` (scoring kind `judge`; `EvalCase.tier` sends the confirm cases on strong); e2e `memory.spec.ts` "contradictions are found in code and judged twice (P4 slice 2)". **Review fixes (2026-10-13):** `finding.rendered` (JSON `FindingRendered`: `inputs`, the stamp of what the pair's rendering reads, and `keys`, its verdict-cache keys) is what the verdicts were given for. `pairInputs` (cyrb53 `quickHash`; per-scene stamps of text hash, the held card's fields/present/anchor and typed year; per-side stamps memoized; the scenes between two book sides from prefix sums over year-crossing time skips; the Not right list) and `readPair` (the stored rendering while its stamp holds, else rendered again) feed `upsertFindings` through a `PairReader` (`pairReader`): a fresh rendering whose keys moved clears the verdicts and asks the cache; an unchanged row is never rendered, and only a fresh rendering asks the cache. `candidateInputs` returns `StoredInputs` (with the cards) so the judge context reuses them. `setVerdicts(…, { rendered, expect })` writes only while the row is as rendered (`FindingAsRendered`), returning the states before and after; `judgeFindings` fills a caller's `JudgeRunResult` (`emptyJudgeRun`; `sent` pairs in answered calls, `settled`, `moved`), skips the second order for a moved pair, and caches a reply only when it answers every item. The service pushes only settled findings, counts a cut-short run's pairs, and makes a background transient failure a quiet `JobSkippedError` (`jobOutcome` from `indexQueue.ts`); the handlers' findings job queues the judge only when the recompute moved something a view shows (`RecomputeResult.changed` leaves out stamp-only writes, `UpsertResult.restamped`). The reader's queue leaves `judge`/`checkBook` out of its status (`IndexQueueOptions.uncounted`); `judging()` keeps its own flag. `findingCounts(db, shown)` is none with the Contradiction check off (the findings job's `shown`, the service, `settingsChanged`). `findings.perf.test.ts` times the job as the app runs it.
- Story memory v2: Check the book and the answers on a finding, main side (F-16.30, F-16.29, F-16.27; P4 slice 3, no UI; plan `docs/research/story-memory-2026-10-11/plans/P4.md` §3). **Check the book** (`src/main/memory/findings/checkBook.ts`): `estimateCheck` (local, nothing written, the cache only peeked with `peekVerdict`: `backgroundCandidates` plus `candidatesC7`, the pair-orders with no remembered verdict give the fast calls through `judgeBatches`, open findings with no `confirm` and no strong-tier verdict (`confirmPair`: `judgePair` at ±3 sentences, keyed on the masked `ab` text for the strong tier) give the confirmations; hidden candidates, `unreadScenes` (due for the gate or not `topicsRead`) and the Cloud flag); `confirmOrder` (`RULE_ORDER`, the open scene first, newest); `confirmFindings` (one strong call a finding, child ids `<requestId>:p<n>`, a remembered strong verdict free, `cacheIf` `parseJudgeConfirm`, `setConfirm` with `expect`: `c` confirmed, `ok` fine, `u` unclear); background judging leaves C7 out. `findingsService.ts` run modes `background` / `sweep` / `check`: `checkBook` sets `progress()` (`MemoryStatus.check`), recomputes with C7, judges every uncached pair (no daily pair limit, asked spend past the Cloud monthly cap, `AiBudgetError` → quiet `HOLD.dailyCap`), confirms, writes the report in `finally` (stopped on a Stop or failure; `AiCancelledError` rethrown); `askedHold` (the start's refusal as data: `NO_KEY`, `BUDGET`, `DISABLED`), `checkAsked(openNodeId)`, `sweepDue`/`waiting`. **Answers** (`findingActions.ts`): `findingNotProblem` (a `notProblem` Dismissed row keyed by `npKey`, labelled "Mara Vell: eyes grey (1.1) and brown (1.2): not a problem" with both quotes; closes with reason `dismissed`; *Undo* is `dismissed:undo` and the recompute's cached verdicts), `findingRight` (the other side through its own path: a claim `wrongValue`, a presence `cardNotRight` `present`; VALIDATION on a note conflict; reason `right`; answers the other side's scene), `notePatch` (`noteEdit` in `findingViews.ts`, the one test behind it and the view's `actions.updateNote`: C6 only, book side narration `now` (`setsState`); the note sentence with the value replaced in code by `patchNoteParagraph`, kinship by `kinWords`; paragraph and result within `NOTE_PATCH_MAX`), `applyNotePatch` (re-checks the proposal: offered, same index and paragraph; `patchedPage` finds the block by `pageTextBlocks` index and text and changes only what changed with `replaceBlockText` (`pageMarks.ts`: common prefix and suffix keep their nodes, marks and `hardBreak`s; new words take the marks where the change starts), drops its AI marks; writes through `NoteWriters` (the handlers: Pinned via `saveRecordPageWith` + `savePage`, Research via `saveFromEditor(…, 'bulk')`), `indexOwner` at once, closes reason `right`), `looseEndsView`, `looseEndNotProblem` (`loose|…` key; NOT_FOUND for a planned card or last scene deleted since). *Undo* of a `notProblem` row adds its key to the handlers' `restoreFindings`, which the next findings job passes as `RecomputeOptions.restore`, so a C7 finding comes back too. `FindingView.actions.right` is offered only when both sides are book passages. **Rebuild:** `closeRebuild` that read a scene marks the sweep due (`memoryJudgeSweep` `{ rebuildAt, pairs }`: `sweepPairs(total)` = min(60, 10 × ⌈scenes / 15⌉), asked) and the review (`memoryRebuildReview`, `noteRebuildDone`: the lines, sheets and names it added, counted once as it closes); `dueSweep` drops a sweep held back when it would run (`askedHold`) or while another rebuild runs, never run later; `estimateRebuild(…, judgeCalls)` adds `sweepCalls(scenes)` (2 × ⌈`sweepPairs` / 10⌉) when the switch is on; the sweep's end reports the open findings made since the rebuild began (`swept` dep → `noteRebuildFindings`, their keys on the review row); `RebuildSummary.findings` and the notice's count are those keys still open (later findings never join); `RebuildAction` `notProblems` answers them with one judge context (`notProblemFor`); the notice is read from the row (no summary on publish). **Notices:** `rebuildDone` (Done dismisses; Review is slice 4's), `findingsWaiting` (pairs the day's limit or the Cloud cap left, priced by `priceWaiting`; dismissed per day). **Elsewhere:** `scribe/instant.ts` `openFindingOn` hands the instant card over (`contested(…, openFinding)`) while an open C1/C6 names the record and slot (for an age, C4 or C1/C6 on `born`), judge switch on;
