- Story memory v2: the contradictions eval (F-16.27–F-16.30 final verification; P4 slice 6; no migration, no channel, no prompt change; plan `docs/research/story-memory-2026-10-11/plans/P4.md` §6). **Oracle** (`src/main/ai/eval/judgeOracle.ts`, test support only): `sentJudge(messages)` reads a `judge.v1` request back from its text (a batch, "Pairs:", or the stronger check, "Pair:"; each item's number, question, A and B lines and time-skip hints; `sideSays` the value code read); `labelledItem(item)` is the labelled contradiction an item is (one label quote on each side, either way round, a question of the label's rule and variant by `questionRules`, naming the label's record); `judgeOracleReply(messages, plant, memory)` answers `c` for a labelled pair and `ok` otherwise (the stronger check likewise), or a `JudgePlant` (`disagree`: `c` the first time a pair is seen, `ok` the second; `unclear`; `allC`; `drop` every second item; `cut` the honest reply halfway with `finishReason: 'length'`; `proOk`); `judgeOracleServer({ plant, costUsd, failAfter })` puts it behind `fakeChatServer` (the reader's and the checker's requests answered by `memoryOracle.ts`, so a live reading runs offline too); `labelMatches`, `negativeHits`, `labelRecord`, `sideIs`, `noteIs` match labels against candidates or stored findings (moved here from slice 1's eval); `FINDING_NEGATIVES`; `statesAbsence(text)` (`statesAbsenceAsFact`, `isAbsence` and event absences such as "no change", "never mentioned") for app-composed text only, never a book quote. **Recorder** (`liveProvider.ts`): `recordingProvider(provider, sink, kindOf)` and `CapturedCall<K>` are shared now (the Ms Scribe run's own copy moved here). **Eval** (`src/main/ai/eval/findings.eval.ts`, the `eval` project, so `npm run test` runs it): `openFindingsSession(live, { read, now, stopUsd })` builds the test book, writes its record pages (P3's Pinned and Ideas) and notes, reads the memory (the oracle through `fakeAi`, or the live reader and checker through the run's deps, stopping at a scene's end), and builds the app's `createFindingsService` over `liveRequestDeps` (Use AI on, the switch on, a `guardedProvider` that refuses the judge's requests with a Stop once the cost cap passes or a cost cannot be known, and keeps the first provider error); `judgeInBackground` runs the `findings` job and judge runs while the service wants them (the recompute after each, as `memory:changed` does); `checkTheBook` runs the asked check and reads its report; `scoreFindings` (recall per rule, planted C1–C6 recall, hidden flashbacks, unlabelled findings with both quotes, traps open, negatives with any finding, order agreement, agreement with the labels, the stronger check on planted and traps, underline precision over every scene's `underlinesOf`, the unclear and cut-off rates, reasoning tokens, the check's cost and pairs); `findingsReport` against `FINDINGS_PASS_BARS` (P4-D24); `runLiveFindings(live, { read, stopUsd })` is the live run (`MYTHSCRIBE_EVAL_LIVE=1 OPENROUTER_API_KEY=… npm run eval:ai -- findings`, `MYTHSCRIBE_EVAL_READ=live` to read the memory live first; 50¢ stop; `test-results/eval-findings/findings-<date>.md` and `.json`, no prompt text, no key). Offline: slice 1's candidate tests; the **oracle replay** (background judging at most 10 pairs a run, every pair in both orders, no C7 in the background; every labelled contradiction open with both verdicts `c` and confirmed after one check, C7 included; no `finding` negative a finding, the traps judged `ok` both ways; every bar met; fast model for batches, strong for the stronger check, reasoning off; a second check priced free with nothing sent; the invariants; the loose ends as labelled (a thread with two steps in s02, three characters, the planned card); the secrets nobody learns as labelled (`secretsNobodyLearns`: Anne's pearls, told the reader in 19.1), each scoped; no absence in an item, a view, the report or an ask; at most 3 underlines a scene, all on labelled findings, none in a paragraph given an unread sentence; h16's hidden pair listed with *Not a flashback?* and judged open after its switch is set to Not a flashback (`setSceneDetails`, 2026-10-14; `findings.test.ts` keeps *Not right* on a card's flashback unhiding a pair)); the parser's and plants' own tests; the **adversarial judges** (disagreeing orders, all unclear, all `c` (open findings exactly code's candidates, and none the labels do not name: no false alarm from an Idea scene, an inactive draft, an Ideas page or a Not canon line), dropped items and a cut reply (unjudged, counted, judged the next run), a stronger check clearing everything); the **live run end to end** against the fake (every bar, a live reading, the 50¢ stop for judging and for reading, before the last scene and during it, a provider error, an unknown cost; traps that became findings listed with both quotes). Owed by the author: the live run (F-16.27's merge condition) and live fidelity for `judge.v1`.
- Story memory v2: the Flashback switch (F-16.11, F-16.23; the author's decision 2026-10-14; migration `0040_scene_flashback`, additive: `scene_detail.flashback` integer boolean, null = follow the book; no prompt change). **One resolver:** `src/shared/memory/flashback.ts` `sceneFlashback(typed, cardField)` answers `{ flashback, source: 'typed' | 'card' | 'none' }`: the author's switch when set (both ways), else the card's flashback field only when verified and standing on the text (`cardFlashback`), else not a flashback. Every reader goes through it: `timeViews.ts` (`flashbackOf` for `storyYears`' rows, so the carry, ages, the Timeline, the sheets and P3's `storedYears`/`storedAge` and `evidence.ts`; `sourcesOf` lists the card's flashback for ⓘ only when `source === 'card'`), `candidateInputs.ts` `sceneFactsReader(…, typed)` (C2–C5 hiding and the loose ends' living values; a switch set to Flashback hides with no card; `TypedScene.flashback` from `allSceneDetails`, the loose ends from `typedFlashbacks`), and through `inputs.scene` the judge's side mark (`judgeSide`), `findingView`'s `notFlashback` and `checkReport`'s hidden list (`typed`: the switch is the cause); the scene's memory via `useSceneMemory` for the book's reading: "AI read: flashback" dimmed when it is what turns the switch on or differs from the author's setting, *Follow the book* sets null); `sceneDetailStore.setFlashback(nodeId, value, expected?)` answers the old value and, with `expected`, writes only while the switch still holds it; *Check the book*'s *Not a flashback?* (`CheckReport.tsx`) sets it to false with *Undo*, which leaves a switch changed since in the scene details as it is (it marked the card not right before). **Switch-over:** `readingOrderRows` moved from the renderer's `timelineView.ts` to `src/shared/timeline.ts` (re-exported there); `buildSwitchoverModel` sets no switch (v1's flag was computed, never typed; review 2026-10-14): years stay on each run's first scene, and a plan note names the scenes v1's reading order flags so the author can turn the switch on. Tests: `flashback.test.ts`, `timeViews.test.ts`, `findings.test.ts`, `judge.test.ts`, `stores.test.ts`, `migrate.test.ts`, `switchoverCases.test.ts`, `sceneDetailStore.test.ts`, `MetadataPane.test.tsx`, `CheckBook.test.tsx`, `findings.eval.ts` (h16's *Not a flashback?* through the switch), e2e `memory.spec.ts` (Use AI off).
- Cloud-folder working copy (2026-10-08, F-8.1; no migration): `cloudFolder.ts` `cloudProviderFor(folder, probe)` (pure; the Windows volume label comes from `vol X:` once per drive). `ProjectManager({ workingRoot })` (index.ts passes userData `working/`; absent in unit tests, so every project opens in place) hands `createProject`/`openProject` a `workingCopy` preparer that runs after the open marker is held. `workingCopy.ts` `WorkingCopy.open|create` keeps `<root>/<sha256(path)[0..32]>/project.db` plus `working.json` (`cloud` fingerprint = size/mtime of the cloud db and `-wal`, `cloudHash` = sha256 of their bytes, `lastSyncedAt`, `dirty`). `dirty` is written true at every open and false only by `finish(true)` after a clean close, so a crash counts as "possibly ahead". Open: no copy or clean+cloud changed → copy in (a cloud `-wal` is checkpointed locally first); dirty+unchanged → open and copy back at once; dirty+changed (or a db with no `working.json`) → a conflict (2026-10-10, the author's decision): without `keep` `WorkingCopy.open` throws `AppError('CLOUD_CONFLICT', …, CloudConflict { folder, provider, computer, cloud: { modifiedAt, bytes } })` before touching anything; `keep: 'computer'` → `saveConflictCopy` of the cloud folder, open the working copy (carried); `keep: 'cloud'` → `saveWorkingConflictCopy` (the working db, `-wal` read in, as a rollback snapshot, plus the folder's assets via `saveConflictCopy(folder, now, database)`), then copy in. `conflictCopyHolds` (`CloudSide`) says which version the copy holds. `ProjectManager.open(folder, keep?)`; `project:open` takes `cloudConflict?: CloudSide`. A changed mtime with the same bytes is no change. Copy back (`syncNow` blocking, `sync` async with a generation check so a slow copy never lands over a newer one): `serialize()` with header bytes 18/19 set to 1 (rollback mode, so the cloud file needs no `-wal`/`-shm` to be read) → `project.db.sync-<uuid>.tmp` in the folder → conflict check → remove any cloud `-wal`/`-shm` → rename (retried at 100/250/500 ms on EPERM/EACCES/EBUSY, Windows refusing while Drive holds the file). `working.json` matches its folder by the same case-folded key as the dir name on Windows. Changes = `total_changes()` against the last copy (0 at open, so an open always copies once). `ProjectSession.close` copies back before closing the database and releasing the marker (every close path: replace, `will-quit`, renderer crash); a failure is logged and leaves `dirty`. `session.localFolder` (the working dir) holds the crash journal; an old `recovery/` in the cloud folder is moved over once. `cloudSyncService.ts` `CloudSyncService` (index.ts; `Schedule` seam): 3-minute timer when changed, retry 30 s doubling to 3 min, `changed()` after imports (`afterImport`), `syncNow()` for the renderer's close flow. Channels `project:cloudSyncStatus`, `project:cloudSyncNow` (read), event `project:cloudSyncChanged` (`CloudSyncStatus` in `src/shared/cloudSync.ts`). Renderer: `features/project/cloudSyncStore.ts` (status, a conflict toast once (wording by `conflictCopyHolds`), `cloudConflictOf(err)` + `chooseCloudVersion(conflict)` (the `dialogs.choose` question, newer version primary; `projectStore.open` re-invokes `project:open` with the answer, Cancel → null; the start-up reopen in index.ts just logs the refusal), `confirmCloudCopy('close' | 'switch')`: Retry / Close anyway after a failed copy, called by `projectStore` before close, window close, open, create, import-create, and restore), `CloudSyncLine.tsx` in the status bar (`status-cloud`). Backups and export already read through the connection (local). Not covered: v0 conversion (`convertLegacyProject`) still runs in place, and `locateProject`'s read-only legacy check reads the cloud db once per open; a moved project folder starts a new working copy and the old one stays under `working/`.
- `src/main/project/seed.ts` — pure `seedSkeleton(format)` (3 sections + Part/Chapter/Scene skeleton, 17 rows) and `seedSettings(format)`; run inside `createProject`'s transaction. `src/main/tree/treeStore.ts` — `insertNodes`, `listNodes` (ordered parent, position, id; roots first), `toTreeNode`; `TreeDb` accepts the orm or a transaction handle.
- `src/shared/labels.ts` — `SectionType`/`HierarchyLevel`/`NodeKind` enums (one owner; Drizzle columns use the same tuples), `sectionLabel(format, section)`, `levelLabel(format, level)`, `skeletonSummary(format)`. Section rows store the canonical key (`front`/`manuscript`/`end`) as `title`; the UI must label roots via `sectionLabel`, never `title`.
- `src/shared/editorSettings.ts` — zod `EditorSettings` and `defaultEditorSettings(format)` (F-3.6 defaults); stored in the project `settings` table under key `editor` as JSON.
- `src/main/appState/` — app-level (not per-project) state in `<userData>/app-state.json`: `appStateStore.ts` reads lazily, validates with zod, writes atomically; `recents.ts` holds the pure recents list operations. Recents are recorded on the `manager.onChange` hook in `handlers.ts`. F-7.9 window state is its `window` key (`WindowState` in `src/shared/windowState.ts`, main-only except `startup:get`/`startup:setReopenLastProject` for the Appearance tab's toggle): the bounds are written on the window's `close` (normal bounds + maximized; fullscreen is never restored) and put back only when `restorableBounds` finds them on a live display; `lastProject` is set on the same `onChange` hook as recents and cleared only by `project:close` (a window close or quit keeps it); `index.ts` opens it after `registerHandlers` and before the window, so the renderer's `project:current` finds it. The e2e's last step relaunches the app on the same userData to check both. `MYTHSCRIBE_USER_DATA` overrides the userData directory (used by e2e for isolation; the single-instance lock also lives there, so e2e and `npm run dev` can run concurrently).
- Autosave (F-3.2, F-3.7): `src/renderer/features/editor/autosaveStore.ts` is the one owner of the machinery: `createAutosaveStore<T, R>({ empty, get, save, onSaved?, delayMs? })` returns `{ useStore, reset }` with `docs[id] = { content, dirty }`, `load/unload/edit/flush/saveNow/clear` (since F-11.1 `load`/`unload` are holder-counted: a second view of the same id shares the first read, and the record is forgotten, its pending edit written, only when the last holder unloads; `reload` re-reads without adding a holder), per-id debounce timers and load tokens, pending jobs keyed per id, one serialized write queue, retry after failure unless a newer edit replaced the job, and its own pending-save registration (first load) and unregistration (clear/reset). `documentStore.ts` (`useDocumentStore`, `resetDocumentStore`, `onSaved` merges the word count into the tree) and `notesStore.ts` (`useNotesStore`, `resetNotesStore`) are its two instances. Notes: `src/main/document/notesStore.ts` (documents and folders; section roots refused by `parentId === null`; shares `parseStoredTiptap` with the document store); `NotesEditor.tsx` takes only `id`; `NotesPanel.tsx` (toggle button in both toolbars' `right` slot, resizable handle with `role="separator"`, width clamped in-file); its open state and width live in the layout store (F-7.2); `App.tsx` `ProjectScreen` renders it as a column for the selected node (a hint while none or an entity page); since 2026-10-06 the column holds the synopsis box, the notes, and the collapsed Scene details (`NotesContent`).
- Sidebar sections (F-7.3 shell, F-9.11): `src/shared/sidebarTabs.ts` holds the persisted vocabulary: `SidebarTabId` is any well-formed section id (a tool id of `SIDEBAR_TOOL_IDS` or a category id), `normalizeSectionId` reads F-9.2's `characters`/`settings` as `character`/`setting`; `Layout.sidebar.tab` and the session persist it. `src/renderer/features/shell/sectionList.ts` builds the sections (`buildSections`, pure: Manuscript, then every category, then the tools, each with `count` and `used`; `useSidebarSections` reads the counts from the tree, entity, tag, timeline, edit-pass, and library stores) and `SidebarSections.tsx` is the picker (button + ARIA listbox, the unused behind a toggle, "New category…") over a `region` panel named after the section. The old `SidebarTabs.tsx`/`sidebarTabList.ts` are gone; `src/main/sourceNames.test.ts` still refuses case-only name clashes (hence `sectionList.ts`, not `sidebarSections.ts`). `ManuscriptTab.tsx` composes the tree and the create bar. `StoredLayout` in `src/shared/layout.ts` is the lenient reader, defaulting to Manuscript.
- Story-bible categories (F-9.11): `src/shared/categories.ts` owns the library (`BUILTIN_CATEGORIES`, ids `character`/`setting`/`world` kept from F-9.1), `StoryCategory`, `categoryOf(id, list)` (project list, then library, then a generic Description+Notes category), id and field-id minting (`customCategoryId` → `c-…`, `fieldIdFromLabel`), `compareCategoryIds` (the sheet order main and renderer share). `EntityKind`/`EntityFieldId` in `entities.ts` are id-shaped strings now; `parseEntityFields` keeps every well-formed key (a write never drops a value outside the template). Main: `src/main/entity/categoryStore.ts` (`listCategories`, `requireCategory`, `createCategory`, `updateCategory`; table `story_category`, migration 0020) — the entity store, continuity, the story-bible prompt context, search, the agent tools, exchange, and the context import all resolve templates through it. Channels `category:list`/`create`/`update`. Renderer: `features/entities/categoryStore.ts` (one owner, stands in with the library until loaded; `useCategory`, `getCategory`; `creating` opens `CategoryCreateDialog`), `categoryIcons.ts`. Observed facts (F-5.16) stay on the three F-9.1 kinds (`OBSERVED_KINDS`); sheets of other categories are listed to the summary prompt as World names. Search files every non-character/place sheet under the World type. The context review carries `categories` (the project's own plus the AI's proposals, `ContextReviewCategory.proposed`); `declineReviewCategory`/`renameReviewCategory` are pure; `applyContextReview` creates the proposals that still hold an included sheet and answers the category list.
- Story-bible settings (F-9.17, F-9.19; no migration): `src/shared/storyBibleSettings.ts` owns `STORY_BIBLE_SETTINGS_KEY` (`storyBible`, a project `settings` row), the lenient zod `StoryBibleSettings { listView: 'list'|'cards', defaultTemplate: EntityTemplate, writeUp: Record<categoryId, WriteUpStyle> }` (every part `.catch`ed to its default; a style under a key that is not a category id is dropped), `WriteUpStyle { length: short|medium|long, roles: Record<fieldId, heading|paragraph> }`, `WRITE_UP_WORDS`, `defaultWriteUpRole` (multiline → heading, one-line → paragraph), `writeUpStyleOf`, `writeUpRoleOf`. Main `getStoryBibleSettings`/`setStoryBibleSettings` in `settingsStore.ts`; channels `storyBible:get`/`storyBible:set` (access `read`: a view preference, like the editor and AI settings). F-9.19: `src/main/entity/categoryFields.ts` `setCategoryFields(db, id, fields)` (channel `category:setFields` → `{ category, entities }`; contract `CategoryFieldInput { id?, label, multiline }`): Threads refused, Notes kept last, ids kept through renames and minted by `fieldIdFromLabel` for new ones, names unique (ALREADY_EXISTS), at most `CATEGORY_FIELDS_MAX` besides Notes; a library category gets or updates its `story_category` row with `fields` (`listCategories` now reads a library row's `fields` as an override, `mergeCategories` takes it); removed fields' text moves into Notes ("Label: text", `joinSheetText`) on every sheet of the category through `writeAuthorFields`, the sheets' own fields untouched; the handler emits `fact:changed` for the moved sheets. Renderer: `categoryStore.setFields` merges the moved sheets into the entity store; `StoryBibleSettingsTab.tsx` (registered as `storyBible`, project scope, after AI): New sheets open in (radios → `defaultTemplate`, read by `EntityCreateDialog` and `EntityQuickAdd` through `storyBibleSettingsNow()`), the Category select, and `CategoryEditor` (keyed by the category's stored fields so a save or another category starts afresh): rows with name, role select (Heading/Paragraph, hidden while unsaved changes are pending), up/down/remove, Notes fixed last, New field + Add field, Save fields (a confirm when removed fields hold text), the Length radios. Renderer `features/entities/storyBibleSettingsStore.ts` (the author-rules store pattern: optimistic, one debounced write, revert and toast, pending-save registration; loaded and cleared in `App.tsx`; `useBibleListView()` answers cards until loaded; `storyBibleSettingsNow()`; `resetStoryBibleSettingsStore()` in tests). `EntityTab` reads and writes `listView`; `entityView.ts`'s `EntityView` is `BibleListView`.
- Sheet views kept true (F-9.18; migration `0026_sheet_sync`; feature id `sheetSync`, fast, JSON, reasoning off, 1,200 out / 6,000 in; prompts `sheetWriteUp.v1` (out `{intro, parts}`, `WRITE_UP_MAX_TOKENS` 400/700/1,200 by length) and `sheetRefile.v1` (out `{edits:[{f,old,new}], add:[{label,value}]}`, `REFILE_MAX_TOKENS` 900)): `src/shared/sheetSync.ts` owns the vocabulary and the pure text rules: `SHEET_SYNC_DELAY_MS` 30 s, `SHEET_EXTRA_FIELDS_MAX` 12, `REFILE_CHUNK_CHARS` 3,000, `SHEET_SYNC_DUE_KEY` (`sheetSyncDue`, a settings row), `SheetSyncState` (`none|synced|pageStale|fieldsStale|both`), `SheetSyncView`/`NO_SHEET_SYNC` (the contract's `Entity.sync`: state, AI-written paragraph count, `writtenUpAt`), `SheetSyncStatus` (`waiting|running|failed` + `JobFailure`), `SheetExtraFields` (the contract's `Entity.extraFields`), `sheetFieldDefs` (template then own), `paragraphsOf`, `pageEdits` (multiset of `paragraphKey`), `chunkParagraphs`, `layoutWriteUp` (intro, then each heading field under its label in field order), `applyRefile` (replace a found `old`, else `joinSheetText`; `add` → own field via `fieldIdFromLabel`, or into an existing field of that label, or Notes past the cap). Main: `entity/sheetSyncState.ts` (`StoredSheetSync { fieldsHash, page, aiParagraphs, writtenUpAt, at }` read leniently from `entity.sync`; `fieldsHashOf` covers labels, order, values, and the category's write-up style; `sheetBasis`, `sheetSyncStateOf` (never synced: filled fields → pageStale, filled page → fieldsStale), `sheetSyncViewOf`; `sheetSyncContext(db)` = categories + story-bible settings, computed once per `listEntities`/`toEntity`). `entityStore.ts`: `rowToEntity` adds `extraFields` and `sync`; `assertFieldsOf`/`collectFields` accept the sheet's own fields; `addEntityField`, `removeEntityField` (text into Notes), `writeExtraFields`, `writeSheetSync` (never stamps `modified`), `sheetSyncRow`. `ai/sheetSync.ts`: `runSheetSync` (gate; hash skip; filing first (`fieldsStale`/`both`), a never-synced sheet keeps its page; write-up when `pageStale` or after a `both` filing; `unchanged` drops an answer the author edited past; lands in every chat mode (author 2026-10-10) through `updateEntity` + `logChanges` (`sheetEditChange`, run `sync:<uuid>`)), `sheetNeedsSync`, `parseWriteUpAnswer`, `parseRefileAnswer`. `entity/sheetSyncScheduler.ts`: one unref'd timer per sheet. `ai/sheetSyncService.ts`: `touch` (only when `sheetSync` is allowed and a provider is ready; status `waiting`), the due list → one `sheetSync` index-queue job keyed to the manuscript root (debounce 0, interval 0) working the list one sheet at a time (`running`, then dropped or `failed` with `failureOf`; a cancel leaves the rest), `runNow`, `load`/`clear` with the project. `changeLog.ts` gained `sheetEditChange` (library Apply uses it too); `CHANGE_SOURCES` gained `sync` ("Sheet sync"). Handlers: `entity:update` with fields (undated) or a page, `entity:create` with text, and the own-field channels touch the service; `onEntity` emits `entity:changed` + `fact:changed`. Renderer: `features/entities/sheetSyncStore.ts` (statuses; `run`; loaded and cleared in `App.tsx`), `SheetSyncBar.tsx` over the fields or the page (out of date + why + Write up now / File now / Try again; AI off → where to turn it on; `sheet-provenance` line), `EntityEditor` shows `sheetFieldDefs` with "this sheet only" + remove and "Add a field to this sheet…" (`entityStore.addField`/`removeField`), the page in the prose font; `entityDraftStore` tracks the parts the author edited and has not had written (`touched`, module state): `draftPatch(draft, stored, edited)` sends only those (own fields included), `adopt(entity)` takes every untouched part of a row main rewrote at once and keeps the touched ones, and a page save carries `baseModified` (the stamp the page was read at). Main: `entity:update` asks `staleWriteUpBase(db, id, baseModified)` (the page is still exactly the last write-up and the base is older; `StoredSheetSync.pageBefore` is the page that write-up replaced), writes the author's page as sent, then `rebaseAuthorPage` (sync page = the page they edited from, fields hash = now), so the next filing reads only the author's edits. Known gaps in FEATURES.md F-9.18.
- Tags (F-4.1): `src/shared/tags.ts` is the one owner of the seven `TAG_CATEGORIES` (camelCase keys), `TAG_CATEGORY_LABEL`, `DEFAULT_CATEGORY_COLOR`, `HEX_COLOR`, `TAG_NAME_MAX`, and `toTagName` (kebab-case; letters and digits of any script are kept, so "Zoë" stays "zoë"). Schema: `tag` (name unique after normalization; `parent_id` self-reference, `ON DELETE SET NULL`) and `document_tag` (node↔tag link, unique pair, cascades from both sides; empty until F-4.4/F-4.6 write it) in migration `0002_tags`. `src/main/tag/tagStore.ts` mirrors the tree store: `listTags`, `getTagWithUsage`, `createTag`, `updateTag` (partial patch; cycle check walks `parentId` up), `deleteTag`; `usageCount` is derived with a `COUNT` join, never stored. Channels `tag:list`/`tag:create`/`tag:update`/`tag:delete` (and the event `tag:changed`, a tag created or renamed by an entity write, F-9.4); errors `VALIDATION` (empty after normalization, self/descendant parent), `ALREADY_EXISTS` (name collision), `NOT_FOUND`. `migrate.test.ts` hard-codes the bundled migration count, so every new migration bumps it.
- Tag Manager (F-4.2): `src/renderer/features/tags/tagStore.ts` is the renderer owner of the bank (`byId`/`ids` ordered by name then id like `tag:list`; generation-guarded `load` on project open and `clear` on close from `App.tsx`, the tag load never blocks the tree; `create`/`update`/`remove` await main and merge the returned row, never re-list; `resetTagStore()` in tests). A tag created through the raw bridge (e2e) is invisible to the store until the next load. `TagsTab.tsx` composes the category strip (ARIA tablist over `CATEGORY_FILTERS` from `categoryFilter.ts`, where `'all'` is a UI-only filter and `defaultCategoryFor('all')` is `custom`), the search, `TagList.tsx` (`role="list"` of button rows; the color dot is the one inline style), `TagForm.tsx` (keyed on the filter so category and color re-default; a hand-picked color survives a category change), and `TagDetail.tsx` (labels stacked above controls so the sidebar minimum never overflows; inline rename keyed on the name so the field re-syncs to the kebab-cased result; color drag steps coalesce to one write in flight plus the latest pick; delete via `dialogs.confirm` danger). Filter, query, and selection are component state. No nesting UI (`parentId` is never sent). The create form's bottom row wraps so the sidebar minimum (15 %) never overflows; check new sidebar content at that width in a 1024 px window (tabpanel `scrollWidth` vs `clientWidth`; the resize handle deliberately straddles the border, so measure the panel, not the `aside`).
- Tag templates (F-4.3): `src/shared/tagTemplates.ts` is the one catalogue (`TAG_TEMPLATE_IDS`, `TAG_TEMPLATES`: Standard Fiction, Mystery, Fantasy, Sci-Fi, each 4–8 kebab-case tags per non-custom category, colors always the category default). `loadTagTemplate` in the main tag store is one transaction that reuses `insertTag`, skips by normalized name across the whole bank, and returns `{ created, skipped }` in template order through `tag:loadTemplate`. The renderer store's `loadTemplate` merges `created` in one `set`; `TemplateLoader.tsx` (select + Load, above the search in the list view only) confirms via `dialogs.confirm` and toasts the counts.
- Custom tag templates (F-4.11; no migration): `src/shared/tagTemplates.ts` also owns `CustomTagTemplate` (`id`, `name` ≤ `CUSTOM_TAG_TEMPLATE_NAME_MAX` 80, `tags`: 1–`CUSTOM_TAG_TEMPLATE_TAGS_MAX` 2,000 `TagExchangeRecord`s, so color, category incl. custom, parent by name, and tracking travel), `CUSTOM_TAG_TEMPLATES_MAX` 50, `customTemplateNameKey`, and `keepTemplateRecords` (nulls a parent outside the kept set). Stored app-wide as `AppState.tagTemplates` (defaulted `[]`). `src/main/tag/customTemplates.ts` holds the pure list edits (`addCustomTemplate`, `updateCustomTemplate` rename/keep, `removeCustomTemplate`; ALREADY_EXISTS by name key, VALIDATION for no tags or the cap, NOT_FOUND). Channels `tagTemplate:list|save|update|delete` (save reads `exportTagBank`, filtered by `tagIds`) and `tag:loadCustomTemplate` (an `importTagBank` of the records, then the same rescan/proposals/spelling follow-ups as `tag:import`). Renderer: `customTemplateStore.ts` (app-level, `ensureLoaded` once, upserts sorted by name; `resetCustomTemplateStore` for tests), `tagStore.loadCustomTemplate` (shares `mergeCreated` with the template load and the import), `TemplateLoader.tsx` (select values `builtin:<id>` / `custom:<id>` under Built-in / Your templates optgroups; Save bank as template…, Manage…), `templateName.ts` (`promptTemplateName`, shared with the bulk bar's Save as template…), `TagTemplatesDialog.tsx` (rename, Edit tags removes one at a time with the last disabled, Delete after a confirm), `loadSummary.tagsLabel`.
- Tag bulk operations and exchange (F-4.9): `src/shared/tagExchange.ts` owns the file (`TAG_EXCHANGE_FORMAT_ID` 'mythscribe-tags', version 1, `TagExchangeRecord` `{ name, category, color, parent (by name), trackMentions }`, `serializeTagBank`, `parseTagBank` — kebab-cases names, drops repeats, throws `TagExchangeError { row }`) and the merge aliases (`TAG_ALIASES_KEY` in the settings KV, `TagAliases` merged-away id → target id, `resolveTagId`, `mergeAliases`, `dropAliasesTo`). Main `tagStore.ts`: `deleteTag`/`deleteTags` share `removeTags` (all ids checked first; AI-made names join the dismissed proposals; aliases leading to a deleted tag are dropped), `recolorTags`, `mergeTags` (links move, a node carrying both keeps one link and `author` wins; dismissals move unless the node carries the target; entities re-point and are announced as `entity:changed`; target keeps name, color, origin), `exportTagBank`, `importTagBank` (skips taken names; parents linked by name in a second pass, a cycle skipped). `src/main/tag/tagExchange.ts` reads and writes the file (`writeTextAtomic`); `dialogs.chooseTagBankFile`. Inline tokens are never rewritten: `tokenTag(id, byId, aliases)` in `InlineTag.ts` resolves them for paint (`resyncInlineTags(dom, byId, aliases)`), `renderHTML`/`renderText`, Open in Tag Manager, and the tag bar's inline counts. Renderer: `tagStore` holds `aliases` (loaded with `tag:list`; every stub answering `tag:list` must answer `tag:aliases`), `recolorMany`/`removeMany`/`mergeInto`/`importBank`/`exportBank`; `TagBankActions.tsx` (Import…, Export…, Select toggle) and `TagBulkBar.tsx` (count, All/None/Done, color on pick coalesced like `TagDetail`, Delete and Merge with danger confirms); select mode prunes the checked set to the visible rows at render and hides the template row; `loadSummary.ts` is shared by the template loader and the import toast.
- Document tag bar (F-4.4): `src/main/tag/documentTagStore.ts` (`listDocumentTags`, `addDocumentTag`, `removeDocumentTag`; idempotent, transactional, documents only via the node row's `kind`, return the tag with its fresh `usageCount`) behind `documentTag:list`/`add`/`remove`. Renderer: `src/renderer/features/tags/documentTagStore.ts` owns `tagIdsByNode` (per-node load tokens, cleared on project close); `add`/`remove` merge the returned tag into the bank (`useTagStore.merge`, re-sorts only on a name change) before splicing the ids, so the Tags tab's counts move without a reload and chips always render the bank's record (rename/recolor shows at once). Since 2026-10-06 the tags live in their own column, not a bar above the editor: `TagsPanel.tsx` exports `TagsToggleButton` (header, `aria-pressed`), `TagsColumn` (the docked column, `data-testid="tags-panel"`, `layout.tags` open/size, `Resize tags` handle; a hint when `id` is null) and `TagsPanel` (`role="region"` "Tags": heading with the link count, Recommend and Add tag, then chips, inline tags, mentions, proposed tags, From the title, stacked for a narrow column). `App.tsx` passes the selected node (`useMainNodeId`: null while an entity page is open; the column also needs a non-root node). `TagPicker.tsx` (searchbox + listbox of unassigned tags, ArrowUp/Down, Enter links, Escape/outside click closes, closes after one pick). View › Tags (`toggleTags`) leaves focus mode first, like References. The old `Layout.tagBar` (height, split) is gone; `StoredLayout` drops the key from an old app-state file. The editor surface (`.ms-editor`) is a flex item that fills its scroll container (no viewport-relative min-height), so chrome above it never forces a scrollbar; stacked regions keep their own `6em` floor.
- Automatic mentions (F-4.12): `src/shared/mentions.ts` owns `MENTION_DEBOUNCE_MS` (1500), `MentionRange`, `TagMentions`, `nameWords`, `MentionCandidate`, and `findMentions(doc, candidates)`, a pure scan over the stored Tiptap JSON that answers ProseMirror `[from, to]` ranges (its own position walk: a container costs 1 before and after its content, text its length, a leaf 1; `LEAF_TYPES` must match the editor schema in `extensions.ts`). Rules are in `FEATURES.md` F-4.12 (proper nouns for `character`, case-insensitive elsewhere, longest first, consumed ranges, inline tokens skipped, one text node per match). Schema (migration `0008_mentions`): `tag.track_mentions` (default on), `tag_mention` (`<tag_id>:<node_id>`, `count`, `positions` JSON, cascades both ways), `mention_scan` (`node_id` PK, `content_hash`). Main: `src/main/tag/mentionStore.ts` (`listMentionsForTag`, `listMentionsForNode`, `replaceNodeMentions` in one transaction, `getScanHash`/`scanHashes`, `deleteMentionsForTag` (answers the affected node ids and drops their scan rows too, otherwise on-again short-circuits on the old hash), `deleteScans`; an unparseable `positions` reads as `[]`), `src/main/tag/scanMentions.ts` (`mentionSource` null for a non-manuscript node; hash = sha256 over the text plus the tracked candidates sorted by id; `scanMentions` throws NOT_FOUND for a non-manuscript node — the queue's quiet path — answers `changed: false` on a hash match or when the rows come out identical, else replaces; `staleMentionNodeIds` in reading order). `handlers.ts` builds `mentionQueue` (`kind: 'mentions'`, `minIntervalMs: 0`, `requested: false`, no `onChange`/`onNodeStatus`; the run emits `mention:changed { nodeIds }` only when rows changed); `document:save` touches it unconditionally; `tag:create`, `tag:loadTemplate` (when it created any), and a `tag:update` that changed name, category, or tracking-on run `indexAll` over `manuscriptDocuments`; tracking-off deletes and emits; `tag:delete` collects the node ids before the cascade and emits after; `manager.onChange` clears, loads, and backfills from `staleMentionNodeIds`. Channels `mention:listForTag` (NOT_FOUND for an unknown tag) and `mention:listForNode` (NOT_FOUND for an unknown node). Renderer: `src/renderer/features/tags/mentionStore.ts` (`byTag`/`byNode`, per-key load tokens, one idempotent `subscribe()` to `mention:changed` opened in `App.tsx` on project open that refetches the named loaded nodes and every loaded tag, a failed refresh keeps the rows on screen; `clear` on close; `resetMentionStore()` for tests). `src/renderer/features/editor/openPassage.ts` holds `openPassage(nodeId, locate)` (extracted from `assistantStore.openScene`, which now wraps it; `PASSAGE_GONE_MESSAGE` and `OPEN_SCENE_TIMEOUT_MS` live here and are re-exported there), `editorFor`, and `openMention(nodeId, range, name)` (the stored range when `textBetween` still reads as the name, else `locateText(doc, words, { ignoreCase: true })` — the one caller of that flag). `TagDetail` shows `Track mentions`, "Mentioned in N documents", and a "Documents mentioning this tag" list in tree order (via `tagFilterView` fed the mention rows as links) with `×count` and a jump; `TagsPanel` shows a `Mentions` list after `Inline tags` (`MentionRow`: dot, name, `×count`, "Jump to first mention of <name>"); both render nothing for a tag the bank does not hold, so a tag created through the bridge only shows once the Tags tab has loaded the bank (the e2e creates its `Rose` through the form for that reason). E2E: after the inline-tag step, `Rose` (character) is created in the Tag Manager, " Rose waited." typed into Scene 1, the tag bar's list reads `rose ×1` with no chip change, both jumps select `Rose`, the detail reads "Mentioned in 1 document" / `Scene 1 ×1`, tracking off reads "Not mentioned" and empties both lists, on again restores them.

Contract: `changes:record` (write) → `ChangeEntry[]`; `changes:undo`/`undoRun` answer `ChangeUndoReply` = result + `entities`/`tags` put back. Handlers: `publishUndo` removes pictures, re-reads restored sheets/tags, emits `entity:changed`/`tag:changed`, rescans and republishes proposals when tags moved; `changes:record` emits `changes:changed`. Renderer: `changesStore.record` prepends the rows; `undo`/`undoRun` flush the open `entityDraftStore` page first and re-open it from a restored sheet; `logAppliedChange(run, change, fallback)` answers the log's undo (null for `none`, `fallback` when the log refuses). `organiseApply.applyOrganiseAction(…, run)` logs tag/sheet/createSheet with inverses and merges, deletions, categories as `none` (run key = the plan's first request id); `agentApply.applyAgentEdit(edit, proposalId, run)` logs `sheet`, `tag`, and sheet/tag `delete` edits (run = the chat message id); `ChangesTab` heads non-reading runs by source and shows "No undo"; `groupRuns` groups by `runId` across the whole held list (a run sits at its newest row; rows stay in main's order, so the page cursor is still the last row). `readRow` safe-parses kind and undo (bad JSON too): `listChanges` reads on past unreadable rows, `undoRun` passes them over, `undoChange` refuses with VALIDATION. Main: `src/main/tag/proposedTags.ts` `listProposedTags(db)` (rebuilds a module-level memo of per-node counts keyed by the document's text on every call, so only a changed document is re-tokenised and a deleted one is forgotten; reads `listTags` and the dismissals each time), `dismissName(db, name)` (kebab-cases, appends once, answers the new list), `resetProposedTagCache()`. `scanMentions` answers `{ changed, scanned }`; `scanned` (the hash moved) is what `handlers.ts`'s `publishProposed()` hangs on: it recomputes, compares the JSON with `lastProposed`, and emits `tag:proposedChanged` only on a difference — also called from `tag:create`/`update`/`delete`/`loadTemplate` and on project open, where `lastProposed` is reset to null with the cache. Gotchas: the F-4.7 `Dismiss` locator needs `exact: true` now; `playwright.config.ts` per-test timeout went 90 s → 150 s because the one smoke test passed 90 s with this step (a step far from the new one then timed out). Auto-tag from titles (F-2.8): `src/shared/titleTags.ts` `titleTagProposal(node, bankNames, dismissed)` gives a part/chapter/scene title's `toTagName` form, or null for a node without a level, an empty name, a bank name, a dismissed name, or a placeholder (every `defaultNodeTitle` of every format and kind, or a `levelLabel` word of any format plus digits or a roman numeral, as the seed names nodes); channel `tag:dismissedNames` (`getDismissedNames(db).names`) feeds `proposedTagStore.dismissed` (loaded with `tag:proposed`, appended by `dismiss`, emptied by `clear`); `acceptTitle(nodeId, name)` = `tag:create` in `custom` + `documentTag:add`; `TagsPanel` renders `TitleTagRow` ("From the title", `#name`, `title-tag`/`title-tag-accept`/`title-tag-dismiss`, `Create tag <name>` / `Dismiss <name>`) last. Known gap: merged-away names (F-4.9 aliases are ids) can be offered again. E2E: the F-2.7 inserted scene renamed "Fallen Creator" → Create → chip, then the chip is removed (the 0-use tag stays). Since 2026-10-08 `PROPOSED_ORDINARY_MIN_MENTIONS` (4) lets a word with lower-case uses through once its mid-sentence capitalised uses reach it; `STOPLIST` and `opensSentence` are exported for `tagTerms.ts`.
- Aliases (F-4.14; migration `0019_aliases`): one alias model. `tag.aliases` and `entity.aliases` are JSON string lists as typed; one owner — a sheet with a `tag_id` reads and writes its tag's list (`entityStore.writeAliases`/`addEntityAliases`, `Entity.aliases` is filled from the tag on every read), only an untagged sheet uses its own column; `removeTags` copies a deleted tag's aliases onto its sheets, `setTagId` moves a sheet's own onto the tag it is linked to. `src/shared/aliases.ts` owns `aliasKey` (= `toTagName`), `normalizeAliases`, `parseAliases`, `findByNameOrAlias`, `tagNameAsAlias`, the limits. Main: `findTagByNameOrAlias` (name first, then alias), `addTagAliases` (skips names another tag owns, never refuses; merge, context import, linking), `updateTag({ aliases })` (refuses another tag's name or alias with ALREADY_EXISTS). `EntityTagChange.aliased` makes `publishTagChange` rescan and announce. Readers: `findMentions` expands each candidate's aliases (`MentionCandidate.aliases`; the scan hash only adds them when present, so an upgrade does not rescan), `suggestItems` (inline `#`), `applyAutoTags`, `recommendTags`, `observedFacts` (`entitiesNamedIn`, `resolveEntity`), `agentTools` (`sheetByName`, tag edits), `planContextReview` (`ExistingSheet.aliases`, matched after names), `applyContextReview` (every record name and alias → `addEntityAliases`), `projectNameWords`, `listProposedTags`. The F-4.9 id redirects (`TagAliases`, settings key `tagAliases`) are separate on purpose: they keep old inline token ids resolving; a merge writes both. Misspellings: `src/shared/misspellings.ts` (`findMisspellings` over `textRuns`, exact mentions consumed first, `isCloseSpelling` = same first letter + OSA distance ≤ 1 (4–5 letters) or 2 (6+), capitalised words only; `isMisspeltName` hides such proposals in `listProposedTags` unless kept). UI: `AliasEditor.tsx` (Tags tab `TagDetail`, `EntityEditor`), `MisspellingList.tsx` in the tags column (live document-store content, deferred; Fix = `tr.insertText` last-to-first on ranges that still read as written), `keptSpellingStore.ts` (read lazily the first time something is offered; cleared on project close). Settings key `keptSpellings` (`tag:keptSpellings`, `tag:keepSpelling`).
- Entities (F-9.1): `src/shared/entities.ts` is the one owner of the three `ENTITY_KINDS` (`character`, `setting`, `world`; `ENTITY_KIND_LABEL`, `ENTITY_KIND_NOUN`), the two `ENTITY_TEMPLATES` (`structured`, `blank`), `ENTITY_FIELD_IDS` and the per-kind `ENTITY_FIELDS` (`{ id, label, multiline }`: character age/born (F-11.2b)/gender/appearance/personality/background/goals/relationships/notes; setting type/description/atmosphere/features/associatedCharacters/notes; world category/description/rules/impact/notes — `name` and `image` are columns, not fields), `ENTITY_KINDS_WITH_IMAGE` (`kindHasImage`), `WORLD_CATEGORY_SUGGESTIONS` (free text; suggestions only), the limits (`ENTITY_NAME_MAX` 200, `ENTITY_FIELD_MAX` 20 000, `ENTITY_BODY_MAX` 200 000), `fieldIdsFor`, `isFieldOf`, `toEntityNameKey` (NFC, trim, whitespace collapsed, lower-cased: the uniqueness key), and `parseEntityFields(raw, kind)` (tolerant: null, bad JSON, unknown keys, non-strings, and `''` all read as no value). Schema (migration `0009_entities`): one `entity` table (`kind`, `name` as typed and trimmed, `template` default `structured`, `fields` JSON default `{}`, `body` nullable, `image` nullable — an asset file name F-9.3 writes —, `tag_id` nullable FK → `tag` `ON DELETE SET NULL` — F-9.4 writes it, so a deleted tag leaves the entity tagless —, `created`, `modified`; index `(kind, name)`; no CHECK on `kind`, like every other enum column). Names are unique per kind on the key, enforced in the store's write transaction, not by an index (SQLite `lower()` is ASCII-only). `src/main/entity/entityStore.ts` mirrors the tag store: `listEntities` (sorted in memory: kind order, then name key, then id), `getEntity` (undefined; the handler raises NOT_FOUND), `createEntity`, `updateEntity` (`fields` merged over the stored map, `''` removes a key, so the column never carries an empty value; `kind` is immutable), `deleteEntity` (the linked tag stays). Channels `entity:list`/`entity:get`/`entity:create`/`entity:update`/`entity:delete`; `fields` is `z.partialRecord(EntityFieldId, …)` so an unknown id is refused at the contract and a known id of the wrong kind is the store's VALIDATION; ALREADY_EXISTS on a name clash within the kind. The image upload channel and `assets/entities/` are F-9.3, the tag link is F-9.4 (`createEntity`/`updateEntity` answer `EntityWrite { entity, tagChange }` since then).
- Entity tabs (F-9.2): `src/renderer/features/entities/entityStore.ts` is the renderer owner of the story bible, the tag store's pattern (`byId`/`ids` in `entity:list` order: kind, `toEntityNameKey`, id; generation-guarded `load` on project open and `clear` on close from `App.tsx`; `create`/`update`/`remove` merge the returned row; `resetEntityStore()` in tests) plus session-only `selectedId` (the picked row; `remove` clears it; F-9.3 opens it in the editor). `entityView.ts` holds the pure helpers `matchesQuery` (name, every field, body), `excerptOf` (first filled template field in order, skipping the World category, else the body; ≤ `EXCERPT_MAX`), and `categoriesOf`. `EntityTab.tsx` (`{ kind }`) is one component for the Characters, Settings, and World tabs, registered three times in `features/shell/sidebarTabList.ts`: search box + `role="group"` List/Cards toggle (`aria-pressed`; F-9.17: one choice for every category, `StoryBibleSettings.listView` through `storyBibleSettingsStore`, see Story-bible settings), a Category `<select>` on the World tab once a category is in use, `EntityList.tsx` (`role="list"` labelled by the kind; the row button toggles the selection with `aria-current`; the trailing "Delete <name>" button confirms through `dialogs.confirm` danger, visible on hover/focus), and `EntityQuickAdd.tsx` (name only, structured template; a refusal toasts and keeps the name). Query and category are component state. The empty-kind message wins over the search's "no match". Sidebar tab bar (F-7.3, changed here): the tablist is a `@container` and each tab is `flex-auto`; labels are `hidden` below `@min-[30rem]` (icons, `title`, and `aria-label` carry the name), so five tabs fit the 15 % minimum; the threshold is in rem so it follows the interface size (F-7.10).
- Double-click rename (2026-10-07): `src/renderer/features/shell/InlineRenameInput.tsx` is the one inline rename field (Enter or blur commits, Escape cancels, empty or unchanged is a no-op, a throw is toasted). The tree's `RenameInput` (`tree:rename`), `EntityList` (local `renamingId`; the open page's name goes through `entityDraftStore.edit` + `flush` so the draft never writes the old name back), and the chat's `ConversationTabs` (`assistantStore.renameConversation`) render it on double-click or F2. A row's `onClick` ignores `event.detail > 1` where a second click would undo the first (entity rows toggle the selection). The Tags tab instead opens the detail with its name field focused (`TagDetail` `focusName` token): the row's first click swaps the list for the detail, so `TagsTab` swallows the second click and treats a double-click within 600 ms of a row opening the detail as the row's.
- Entity editor (F-9.3): `src/shared/assets.ts` is the one owner of the asset scheme (`ASSET_SCHEME`, `ASSET_DIRS` `backgrounds | entities | references` (the last since F-9.6), `IMAGE_EXTENSIONS`, `IMAGE_MAX_BYTES` 20 MB, `imageExtension`, `assetFileName(original, shortId, fallbackStem)`, `assetDisplayName`, `assetUrl(dir, file)`); `focus.ts` keeps its `BACKGROUND_*`/`background*` names as aliases over it, and `entities.ts` adds `ENTITY_IMAGES_DIR` (`entities`) and `entityImageUrl(fileName)`. Images live in `<project>/assets/entities/<stem>.<8 hex>.<ext>` through `imageAssets.ts` (see Backgrounds); `entity.image` is the file name. Main: `setEntityImage(db, id, image | null)` (`VALIDATION` unless `kindHasImage`, stamps `modified`), `deleteEntity` returns the deleted row; channels `entity:setImage { id }` → `Entity | null` (`dialogs.chooseEntityImage()`, single; copy first, row second, the copy is rolled back if the write throws, the previous file deleted only after the write) and `entity:removeImage { id }` → `Entity`; `entity:delete` removes the image file. Renderer: what the main pane shows is `entityStore.selectedId`: non-null → `EntityEditor` (`MainPane` in `App.tsx`, keyed by id) replaces the title block, the document editor, and the notes panel; the tree selection is untouched, and `treeStore.select(id)` with an accepted non-null id calls `useEntityStore.getState().select(null)` (the one cross-store call, so any opener — the tree, `openPassage`, the tag detail, the assistant, import — and a click on the already-selected node return to the manuscript). `entityDraftStore.ts` is the open page's draft (`open(entity)`, `edit({ name? | fields? | body? })`, `flush()` 500 ms debounced through `useEntityStore.update` with only what differs from the stored row — pure `draftPatch`: `''` removes a field, an empty page is `null`, an empty name is never sent —, `close()`, `status idle | dirty | saving | saved`; registered with `registerPendingSave` on open; `ALREADY_EXISTS` toasts and reverts the name, other failures toast and keep the draft; `flush` never rejects; `resetEntityDraftStore()`). `EntityEditor.tsx` (`<article aria-label={name} data-testid="entity-editor">`): Name input, `<noun> · Structured | Blank page` subtitle, `role="group"` Template toggle (`aria-pressed`; switching flushes then `update(id, { template })`; both columns keep their text), `Close <name>`, the image block for character and setting (`<img>` 160 px or an empty frame; `Add image…` / `Replace image…` → `entityStore.setImage`, `Remove image` behind a danger confirm → `removeImage`), then the structured fields (label + input, or auto-growing textarea measured by `scrollHeight`; the world `Category` input has a `<datalist>` of `WORLD_CATEGORY_SUGGESTIONS`, so its role is `combobox`) or the `Page` textarea; foot `role="status"` `Saving…` / `Saved`. A deleted id renders nothing and clears the selection. `EntityCreateDialog.tsx` (mounted in `ProjectScreen` while `entityStore.creating` is a kind; `startCreate`/`cancelCreate`): dialog `New <noun>`, Name, `Template` radios Structured (default) / Blank page, Create → `select(entity.id)`, `setSidebarTab(ENTITY_KIND_TAB[kind])`, opens the sidebar; opened by the `New <noun>…` button beside quick-add's Add and by the Insert menu items `insertCharacter` / `insertSetting` / `insertWorldItem` (`menuActions.ts`, exit focus first). `entityView.ts` adds `ENTITY_KIND_TAB` and `ENTITY_TEMPLATE_LABEL` (non-component exports cannot live in a `.tsx`). Decisions: the body is plain text (a textarea, no Tiptap); autosave, no Save button; the editor shows in focus mode too. Character and setting pages also show the F-11.2c appearance and location logs (`UsageLog.tsx`, see Timeline). The e2e opens Mara from her row, fills Age and Appearance, uploads a 1×1 PNG through the stubbed open dialog, switches templates, renames, reads the row back through `entity:list`, and creates Tomas with the Blank page from the dialog.
- Entity ↔ tag link (F-9.4): `src/shared/entities.ts` adds `ENTITY_TAG_CATEGORY` (character → `character`, setting → `setting`, world → `worldBuilding`) and `entityTagName(name)` (`toTagName`, cut to `TAG_NAME_MAX`, trailing hyphens trimmed; `''` for a name with no letters or digits). Main (`entityStore.ts`): `createEntity` and `updateEntity` answer `EntityWrite { entity, tagChange: EntityTagChange | null }` (`{ tag, created, renamed }`); the private `linkTag` runs inside the create transaction (the `createTag` transaction nests as a savepoint, tested) — `findTagByName` (exported from `tagStore.ts`) hit → link it whatever its category, else `createTag` under the kind's category; `mirrorRename` on a name change: only while `tag.name === entityTagName(old name)` and no other entity shares the tag (`tagIsShared`); a new name that is already a tag relinks, else `updateTag` renames (`renamed: true`); `''` or an unchanged tag name does nothing. `linkEntityTag(db, id)` answers `EntityTagWrite` (non-null `tagChange`), NOT_FOUND / VALIDATION (`''` name), idempotent, no `modified` stamp. `handlers.ts` `publishTagChange` = `rescanManuscript` + `publishProposed` + `emit('tag:changed', tag)` only when `created || renamed` (a plain link is already in every bank); wired into `entity:create`, `entity:update`, and `entity:linkTag` (`{ id }` → `EntityTagLink { entity, tag }`). Event `tag:changed: Tag`. Renderer: `tagStore.subscribe()` (idempotent, module-level `unsubscribe`, `resetTagStore` drops it) merges the pushed tag; `App.tsx` opens it on project open; `entityStore.linkTag(id)` merges the entity and `useTagStore.merge(tag)`. `src/renderer/features/tags/tagUsage.ts` `sceneRowsForTag(index, byId, tagIdsByNode, mentions, tagId)` → `TagSceneRow[]` (`{ id, title, parentTitle, tagged, mentionCount, first }`, one `tagFilterView` walk over the union of links and mention rows); `TagDetail` derives its Documents (`tagged`) and Mentions (`mentionCount > 0`) lists from it, no visual change. `EntityEditor`: `EntityTagBlock` (`role="group"` "Tag": colour dot + `#name` chip + `Open in Tag Manager` (sidebar `tags` + `requestSelection`), else "No tag yet." + `Create tag`) and, for world items only since F-11.2c, `EntityScenes` (`<h3>Scenes</h3>`, "In n scenes" / "Not in any scene yet" (`inScenesLabel` in `entityView.ts`), `role="list"` "Scenes with <name>" of `SceneRowButton` (`SceneRowButton.tsx`, shared with the appearance log: title, folder, `Tag` icon when linked, badges, `×n` when mentioned; mentioned row → `openMention`, otherwise `treeStore.select` which closes the page); `useEntityUsage(tagId)` (`useEntityUsage.ts`, shared too) loads `documentTag:listAll` and `mention:listForTag` on open and when `tagId` changes; without a tag one line "Create the tag to see where <name> appears."). Characters and settings show the F-11.2c appearance log (`UsageLog.tsx`, see Timeline) in its place, a superset of the same rows. `tagUsage.ts` also exports `nodeHeading(id, byId)` (title + non-section parent title, used by `sceneRowsForTag` and the log) and `documentsInTreeOrder(index, byId)`. Decisions: deleting an entity leaves the tag; two entities of different kinds with one name share one tag and a rename then leaves it. E2E: Scene 1 gets ` Mara waited.` before the Characters step and loses it after the jump (every later `SENTENCE`/word-count assertion depends on the original text); a jump from the page then `Control+End` alone does not move the caret — `editor.click()` + `End` first; `mara-vell` and `tomas` stay in the bank after the entities are deleted and no later count assertion sees them.
- Entity export / import (F-9.5): `src/shared/entityExchange.ts` is the one owner of the exchange vocabulary: `ENTITY_EXCHANGE_FORMATS` `json | csv` (`ENTITY_EXCHANGE_EXTENSIONS`, `ENTITY_EXCHANGE_LABEL`, `entityExchangeFormatOf(fileName)`), `EntityExchangeRecord` `{ kind, name, template, fields, body }` (no id, image, tag, or dates), the JSON file `EntityExchangeFile` `{ format: ENTITY_EXCHANGE_FORMAT_ID 'mythscribe-entities', version: ENTITY_EXCHANGE_VERSION 1, entities }` (any mix of kinds: the cross-project library), the CSV layout `ENTITY_CSV_COLUMNS` (`kind,name,template,<ENTITY_FIELD_IDS in order>,body`, every field column for every kind so one reader serves every file; `csvEscape` RFC 4180; rows end `\r\n`; `CSV_BOM` written first and stripped on read; `parseCsv` handles quoted commas/quotes/newlines), `serializeEntitiesJson`/`serializeEntitiesCsv`, `parseEntitiesJson`/`parseEntitiesCsv(text, fallbackKind)` (a CSV without a `kind` column or with an empty cell takes the kind the import was started from; a column not of the row's kind is ignored like `parseEntityFields`; a bad format/version/kind/template or a value over `ENTITY_NAME_MAX`/`ENTITY_FIELD_MAX`/`ENTITY_BODY_MAX` throws `EntityExchangeError { row }` naming the row — never truncated), `toExchangeRecord(entity)`, `ENTITY_IMPORT_ACTIONS` `add | merge | replace | skip`, `EntityImportItem` `{ id 'r<n>', record, existingId, action }`, `EntityImportPlan` `{ source { name, format }, items, duplicates }`, `planEntityImport(existing, incoming)` (match by kind + `toEntityNameKey` → `merge`, else `add`; a later row with the same key is dropped and counted in `duplicates`), `actionsFor(item)` (matched: merge/replace/skip; new: add/skip), `mergePatch(existing, record, action)` (pure: `merge` fills only empty fields and a null/blank body and leaves the template; `replace` overwrites with non-empty values and sets the template; an empty incoming value never erases stored text; the name is never patched), `countImportActions`, `entityExportFileName(project, kind, format)`. Main `src/main/entity/entityExchange.ts`: `readEntityFile(path, fallbackKind)` (extension → parser; unsupported type, unreadable file, parser refusal — with `row` in details —, and zero records are VALIDATION), `writeEntityFile` through `writeTextAtomic` (moved from `handlers.ts` to `src/main/fs.ts`, unchanged; `provenance:export` uses it too), `importEntities(db, items)` in one transaction (`skip` ignored; `add` → `createEntity`; `merge`/`replace` → `updateEntity` with `mergePatch`; an `add` whose name is now taken is ALREADY_EXISTS and the whole import rolls back; a deleted `existingId` is NOT_FOUND) → `{ entities, added, merged, replaced, tagChanges }`. Channels `entity:export { kind, format, path? }` → `{ path, count } | null` (`dialogs.chooseExportPath`, default `<project>-<kind>.<ext>` beside the project folder; no entities of the kind is VALIDATION; counts `export.run`), `entity:importOpen { kind, path? }` → `EntityImportPlan | null` (`dialogs.chooseEntityLibraryFile`, `Entity files (*.json, *.csv)`; writes nothing), `entity:importCommit { items }` → `{ entities, added, merged, replaced }` (one `rescanManuscript` + `publishProposed` after the transaction if any tag was created or renamed, then `tag:changed` per such tag — not `publishTagChange` per row). Renderer `entityStore.ts`: `importPlan`/`importKind` (session state, dropped by `clear`), `exportKind(kind, format)`, `openImport(kind)`, `setImportAction(itemId, action)`, `cancelImport()`, `commitImport()` (generation-guarded: merges the returned rows into `byId`, recomputes `ids`, clears the plan, answers the counts). `EntityTab.tsx`: a `More` button (`aria-haspopup="menu"`) beside the view toggle opens the manuscript `ContextMenu` (now with optional `MenuItem.disabled`; focus and arrow keys skip `button:disabled`) with `Export as JSON…` / `Export as CSV…` (disabled while the kind is empty; success toasts `Exported N <nouns> to <file>`) / `Import…`. `EntityImportDialog.tsx` (mounted in `App.tsx` beside `EntityCreateDialog` while `importPlan` is held; testids `entity-import-dialog|summary|item|action|commit|cancel`): one row per item — kind noun, name, an excerpt through `excerptOf` (its parameter is now `Pick<Entity, 'kind' | 'template' | 'fields' | 'body'>`), `· Existing: <name>` when matched, a `<select aria-label="Action for <name>">` of `actionsFor` — Import disabled when every row is `skip`; Escape/Cancel discard; the commit toasts `Imported N entities (A added, M merged, R replaced)`. Decisions: images and tags are not exported (a project asset file and a project-local id; import creates or links tags through `createEntity`); no File-menu item (File › Export… is F-12.1's); no diagnostics counter for import (the counter set is a closed enum the Worker validates). E2E: `stubSaveDialog` beside `stubOpenDialog`; the step exports Characters as JSON, asserts the file, imports a hand-written library (`Ilse` new, `mara vell` matched: `age` kept, `background` filled), then deletes Ilse so the later "No characters yet." holds. Source gotcha: keep `CSV_BOM` as `'﻿'` and the match-key separator as `'\0'` — a raw BOM or NUL byte in the source makes `grep` and `file` treat the module as binary.
- In this scene (F-9.7): `src/renderer/features/references/sceneEntities.ts` owns the pure `sceneEntityIds(entities, mentions, tagIds)` (entities whose `tagId` is in the scene's `TagMentions` or its tag links; named first by first range start, tagged-only after by name; no tag, no card) and `useOpenSceneId()` (the selected document, else the active editor's node under a selected folder; an open entity page keeps the scene). `InThisScene` in `ReferencePanel.tsx` (`<section aria-label="In this scene">` + `role="list"` of the same name, above the pins in the panel's one scroll column) loads `mentionStore.loadForNode` and `documentTagStore.load` on every scene change (the tag bar is not mounted beside an entity page) and then follows `mention:changed`; entities already pinned are filtered out; not rendered with no scene. `SceneEntityCard` in `ReferenceCard.tsx` is the entity card with `frame: null`: not draggable, no move buttons, `Pin <name>` → `referenceStore.pin`. No request, no channel. Known gaps: updates after autosave + the 1.5 s scan, not per keystroke; the card lists nest observed-fact headings and list items, so tests address cards as `:scope > li` by `aria-label`.
- Context library (F-9.8; migration `0018_context_files`, feature id `contextImport` at Use AI, strong, JSON, 3,000 out / 8,000 in per request, prompt `contextImport.v1`; dependency `unpdf` 1.8.1): `src/shared/contextLibrary.ts` is the one owner of the vocabulary and the pure logic: `CONTEXT_FILE_TYPES` (`docx | md | txt | pdf | image`, `contextFileTypeOf`, `CONTEXT_EXTENSIONS`, `CONTEXT_FILE_MAX_BYTES` 25 MB, `CONTEXT_LIBRARY_DIR` `library`), the derived `contextFileState` (`new | changed | processed | reference | noText`, from `text_hash` vs `processed_hash`), `ContextFile`/`ContextAddResult`, chunking (`splitParagraphs` on blank lines, `changedParagraphs` for an updated file, `chunkParagraphs` ≤ `CONTEXT_CHUNK_CHARS` 16,000 at paragraph boundaries, a lone long paragraph cut at sentence ends), `estimateContextCost`, the review (`ContextRecord` per entity per chunk, `ContextReviewEntity { existingId, include, tag: boolean | null, records, fields: ContextReviewField { upload, existing (null = fill), include, choice }, details, includeDetails, images }`, `ContextReviewNotes`, `ContextReview` with proposal ids and cost), `planContextReview` (union-find over name and alias keys per kind, match an existing sheet by name then alias, fields merged across records — one-line values joined with " / ", long ones as paragraphs —, a value the sheet already holds dropped, a differing one a conflict defaulting to `existing`, details minus what `detailsTarget` holds, images attached by hint or by a name in the file stem to characters and settings only, notes minus the Project notes page; an existing sheet with nothing new is left out), `splitReviewEntity` (one item per distinct record name; the sheet stays with its own name, ids `<item>.<n>`), `canSplit`, `writesField`, `reviewHasChanges`, `recordFields` (only `contextFieldsFor(kind)`: the template minus Notes). Main `src/main/library/`: `extract.ts` (`extractContextText`: Word through the importer's `readDocx`, headings as paragraphs; md/txt as UTF-8 minus a BOM; PDF through a lazy `import('unpdf')`, page texts joined; null when empty — no OCR), `libraryStore.ts` (`addContextFiles(db, folder, sources, replaceId?)`: skips with reasons, a same-name (any case) or `replaceId` row is an update — identical hash is `unchanged`, else the original is replaced and `processed_text` kept —, originals written `wx` under `assets/library/<stem>.<8 hex>.<ext>` via `assetFileName`'s new `ext` parameter; `storedPath` refuses separators; `markContextFileProcessed`), `apply.ts` (`applyContextReview`: texts read and pictures copied (`addImageAsset` now takes the display name) before one transaction that creates (`createEntity(..., { tag })`, the new option) or patches sheets, links tags through `linkEntityTag`, sets pictures, appends to or creates the untagged blank World page `Project notes`, marks files processed; copies removed on failure, replaced pictures after commit). `src/main/ai/contextImport.ts`: `contextWork` (a `changed` file sends only new paragraphs, a `processed` one is read whole again), `estimateContextImport`, `sortContextFiles` (the F-12.3 runner's shape: `assertFeatureAllowed`, serial chunks with `JOB_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`, `<requestId>:c<n>`, one proposal per chunk, rejected on failure, lenient parse, then the planner). Channels `library:list|choose|add|addData|open|estimate|process|apply`, event `library:progress`; `library:choose` needs no project (the wizard); `library:addData` carries dropped files as `Uint8Array`; `library:apply` rescans once and emits `tag:changed` per created tag. Renderer `src/renderer/features/library/`: `libraryStore.ts` (files, `flow` `estimating | confirm | running | review | failed`; `shown` with `show`/`hide` (side work, see Side work; `sort` resets it, `uploadInPanel(flow)` = running, review, or failed); every entry point calls `add`/`update`/`addDropped` then `sort`; `aiAllowed()` loads the AI settings first for a project opened a moment ago; zero chunks skip the confirm; a review with no changes is applied silently; proposals settled accepted / acceptedPart / rejected), `ContextUploadDialog.tsx` (since 2026-10-10 `ContextUploadDialog` is only the estimate's modal, `library-dialog|estimate|confirm|cancel`; `UploadReviewPanel` is the pass, its failure, and the review as side work in the assistant column, `library-panel|progress|stop|error|review-summary|item[data-item-name]|matches|conflict|notes|proposed-category|review-cost|discard`, the deck `compact`; since 2026-10-08 the review is the review deck: `uploadReview.ts` owns `UPLOAD_GROUPS` (categories, new, conflicts, updates, notes), `categoryCardId`/`categoryOfCard` (`category:<id>`), `uploadReviewItems`, and `decidedReview` (a card with no decision counts as accepted since 2026-10-10 (`decisionOf`); unaccepted proposed categories declined, `include` = accepted, notes likewise; the dialog passes `stepEvery`); the flow's review stage carries `decisions`, `libraryStore.decide`, and `apply` sends `decidedReview`; E toggles a sheet card's finer picks or prompts a category rename), `LibraryTab.tsx` (sidebar id `library`, `library-add`, `library-sort-all`, rows `library-file[data-file-name]` with `library-file-state`, Open / Update… / Sort / Sort again), `DropOverlay.tsx` (window capture-phase listeners, only drags carrying `Files`, `library-drop-overlay`), mounted in `ProjectScreen`; the wizard's fifth step (`wizard-context-add`) passes paths to `onCreate`, and `EntityTab` has `upload-context`. e2e: after the F-13.4 step (Use AI on, Mara at 34) two files through the stubbed multi-select dialog, estimate, sort (fake server keys on `CONTEXT_IMPORT_SENTINEL` and the file name), review summary, the age conflict picked, apply, sheets, tags, Project notes, both rows Sorted. Since 2026-10-11: `relateValues` in `src/shared/contextLibrary.ts` decides a conflict (same / covered / extends / empty are not; `differs` is), `mergedValue` collapses values one file repeats or extends; `settleNameClashes` (run by `planContextReview`, `declineReviewCategory`, and the renderer's `decidedReview`) turns an included new item whose `toEntityNameKey` name its category has into an update and joins two items for one sheet or one new name; `keepPicks` moved there from `reviewChat.ts`. `applyContextReview` runs each item under a savepoint (`tx.transaction`): a create refused ALREADY_EXISTS fills the sheet the error names (`clashOf` + `asUpdateOf`), any other AppError leaves the item out (its picture copy removed) and lands in `skipped` (`ContextApplyCounts.skipped`, a warning toast per item in `libraryStore.apply`); a non-AppError still rolls everything back.
- Review chat (F-9.9; feature id `reviewChat` at Use AI, strong, JSON, reasoning per routing, 1,500 out (3,000 for the retry) / 7,000 in, prompt `reviewChat.v1`; no schema): `src/shared/reviewChat.ts` owns the operation vocabulary (`ReviewOp`: `merge | split | kind | toNotes | fromNotes | rename | aliases | include`, kinds read leniently), the caps (`REVIEW_CHAT_*`), `ReviewChatResult`, `itemAliases` (an item's other names: its records' names and aliases minus its own), and the pure `applyReviewOps(review, ops, existing)` (in order, each on the review the one before left; unknown ids and impossible asks skipped with the reason; rebuilds through `buildReviewEntity` (exported from `contextLibrary.ts` with `isEmptyUpdate`) and keeps the author's picks where the same field meets the same values; new items `c<n>`). Main `src/main/ai/reviewChat.ts` (`runReviewChat`: `assertFeatureAllowed`, one request `<requestId>:a`, a cut-off or unreadable answer retried once as `<requestId>:b` with `REVIEW_CHAT_RETRY_TURN` and the larger cap, then PROVIDER; one proposal; `parseReviewChatAnswer` drops and counts bad operations). Channel `library:reviewChat` (`ai`; the parent request id registered for `ai:cancel`). - Review deck (2026-10-08, the author's "one decision at a time"): `src/renderer/features/review/reviewDeckModel.ts` owns `ReviewDecision` (`pending | accepted | skipped | rejected`), `ReviewDeckItem` (`settled` = decided outside the deck: applied, undone, out of date), `ReviewDeckGroup` (`noun` for the heading), and the pure rules (`deckOrder` by rail, `nextOpen` with wrap, `neighbour`, `withDecision`, `deckCounts`, `groupStats`, `groupOpenIds`). `ReviewDeck.tsx` is the one review UI: rail (`review-rail`, `review-group[data-group]`, hidden below the `@2xl` container width or when `compact`, replaced by `review-group-select`), `review-accept-group`, `review-skipped-filter`, `review-progress` (progressbar), the card (`review-card[data-item-id][data-decision]`, `review-position`, `review-decision`, keyed by item), `review-accept|skip|edit|reject|prev|next`, `review-done` (+ `review-review-skipped`), footer (`footer`, `actions`, `review-apply` "Apply N accepted"); keys A / S / E / R and ← → on the deck's own `onKeyDown` (never while typing in a field, never with a modifier); `applyOnFinish` applies when nothing is open and nothing skipped; `stepEvery` (the upload review, whose cards start accepted) moves a decision to the next card in order (`neighbour`), counts progress by position, and applies on finish only after the last card; `onCurrent` reports the card on show; `autoFocus` focuses the deck on mount (so dialogs skip their own focus once the deck shows). The deck holds only where the author is; decisions live in each screen's store. Used by Organise, the upload review, and edit passes. Fixed layout (the author, 2026-10-11: "stay the same size so the buttons don't move"): the deck fills its parent and never sizes to the card; the card is header (`review-position`, `min-h-11`) → `review-card-body` (the only part that scrolls, keyed by item so it starts at the top) → `review-actions` (pinned at the card's bottom). A decision button that does not apply (settled card, the end, nothing to edit) is an invisible `aria-hidden` twin without a test id, so every card and the end (`review-done` renders in the same shell; Previous steps back to the last card) keeps the same slots; the Accept group / Skipped links keep invisible placeholders; the footer is the same for every card (it wraps only in a narrow column) and `review-apply` has `min-w-36` + tabular numbers. Parents give the size: the big dialog `h-[min(85vh,48rem)] w-[min(860px,95vw)]` (`SideWorkFrame`), the edit-pass and To do strips `h-[max(14rem,min(50vh,24rem))]`, the assistant column its own height (compact deck `min-h-56`, the frame scrolls below that).
- Side work (2026-10-10, the author's "long AI work never blocks writing"): Organise and the upload's pass and review never cover the editor. `src/renderer/features/sideWork/`: `sideWork.ts` (`useShownSideWork()` reads which shows from the owners, `organiseStore.open && shown` before `libraryStore.shown && uploadInPanel(flow)`; `showSideWork(work)` shows one and hides the other; `SideWorkFocus` context and `useTakesFocus()`, read once at mount, so a deck arriving while the author writes elsewhere never takes the focus), `SideWorkFrame.tsx` (titled region in the column, `side-work-hide` "Back to the conversation" hides without ending the run, Escape is the screen's own: Stop while running, Close/Cancel after; focuses itself on mount unless the deck did), `SideWorkStatus.tsx` (`SideWorkStatus` in `StatusBar`: `side-work-organise` "Organising…" (`AiWaitText`) → "Organise: ready to review" / "Organise failed", `side-work-upload` "Sorting uploads…" or "Sorting uploads · n of m" → "Upload: ready to review" / "Upload sort failed"; `aria-pressed`; a click calls `showSideWork` + `openAssistant()` (focus mode: the floating assistant), a second click hides; `SideWorkBar` = the same line as a footer, `side-work-bar`, under a main pane with no status bar of its own: `App.tsx` `WithSideWorkBar` wraps a sheet, the cork board, the empty state, the Edits workspace and report). `AssistantBody` renders the shown side work (`OrganisePanel` or `UploadReviewPanel`) under `AiResults` in place of the continuity link, the findings, and the chat. Other `fixed inset-0` dialogs with an AI wait were checked: `ConversionDialog` only backs up before its background pass, `ImportDialog`'s structure detection belongs to the import's own modal review (left as is). F-7.12 adds a second place: `useSideWorkPlaceStore` (`place: 'column' | 'dialog'`, one for both runs since only one shows), `showSideWork(work, place = 'column')`, `useShownSideWork(place = 'column')`; `SideWorkDialog.tsx` (mounted in `ProjectScreen`) renders the shown run with `SideWorkPlaceContext` = `dialog`, and `SideWorkFrame` then frames it as a centred modal (`side-work-dialog`, `role=dialog`, Escape and a backdrop click hide, the button reads "Back to writing", the focus returns to what had it at first render).
- Background activity (F-7.12, requested by the author 2026-10-10): `src/renderer/features/activity/`. `activityJobs.ts` is pure: `ActivitySnapshot` (what the job stores hold), `readJobs` derives the long jobs (`organise` running, the upload's `running` flow with `progress` as steps, each `running` edit pass with `doneNodeIds`/`nodeIds`, `todoStore.checking`, `continuityStore.running`, `conversionStore.rereading` with the index queue's counts; never the quiet summaries or tagging), `crawl`/`jobProgress` (steps done plus a crawl through the current step, or a crawl over the whole job; `CRAWL_CAP` 0.9, `CRAWL_MS` per kind), `combinedProgress` (average), `outcomeOf` (a job's end as a note, or null when stopped), `noteStale` (a note whose result already shows or is gone). `activityStore.ts` holds only the watch's output and what no store owns: `jobs` (the last reading), `timing` (first seen, last step), `finishing` (`FINISH_MS` full before it leaves), `notes` (`parked` in focus mode after `FOCUS_NOTE_MS`, all parked on entering focus mode, all back on leaving); `startActivityWatch` subscribes to the organise, library, edit pass, edit view, To do, continuity, conversion, indexing, and focus stores and runs `syncActivity`; a job starting again clears its kind's notes. `activityRoutes.ts` `openNote` (Organise/upload `showSideWork(work, 'dialog')`, edit pass `openReport`, To do the sidebar's `todo` section (leaving focus mode), consistency the findings view + `openAssistant`) and `retryNote` (organise `start(request)`, upload `sort(fileIds)`, pass `resume`, `runCheck`, `check(nodeId)`, `retryReread`). `ActivityBar.tsx` (`activity-bar[data-placement=header|window][data-state=running|done]`, `role=progressbar`, hover `activity-bar-tooltip`; inside the header, which got `relative`, or `fixed` top in focus mode; CSS `.activity-bar*` in `app.css`, reduced motion drops the pulse), `ActivityDrops.tsx` (`activity-drops`, `activity-drop[data-kind][data-status]`, × is "Dismiss notification" so the e2e's `dismissToasts` clears them too). Source changes: `todoStore.checkResult` (`checkOutcome`) replaces the check's toasts; `conversionStore.rereading` (set on Update now, cleared by its own watch on `indexingStore` once `queueSettled`) and `retryReread`; the edit pass's finish no longer toasts and opens the report only while the workspace shows. e2e: the first Organise run's drop, Open → dialog, Back to writing.
- Quick reference panel (F-9.6): `src/shared/references.ts` owns the pins: `REFERENCE_PINS_KEY` (`references`, a project `settings` row, no migration), `REFERENCE_PINS_MAX` 50, the zod `ReferencePin` union (`{ type: 'entity', id }`, `{ type: 'note', id }` for a node's F-3.7 notes, `{ type: 'image', file }` where `file` is one plain path segment), `ReferencePins { pins }`, the pure list arithmetic both sides run (`pinKey`, `hasPin`, `addPin`, `removePin`, `movePin`, `dedupePins`; each answers the same array when nothing changes), `REFERENCES_DIR` and `referenceImageUrl`. Pinned images live in `<project>/assets/references/` through `imageAssets.ts`; the pin is the file's only record. Main: `getReferencePins` / `setReferencePins` in `settingsStore.ts` (an unreadable row is no pins), `src/main/project/referenceStore.ts` (`pruneReferencePins(db, folder, pins)` drops pins whose entity, node, or file is gone; `addReferenceImage`, `removeReferenceImage`); channels `reference:get` (prunes, and rewrites the row when something dropped), `reference:set` (dedupes, stores, then deletes the files of image pins that left the list — row first, files second) and `reference:addImages` (`dialogs.chooseImages(title)`, the `background:add` shape: refused files and files past the cap are `skipped`; null when cancelled). Deleting an entity or a node does not touch the row; the next `reference:get` does. Renderer (`src/renderer/features/references/`): `referenceStore.ts` (`pins`, generation-guarded `load`/`clear` from `App.tsx` beside the entity store, `pin` — opens the panel, warns at the cap —, `unpin`, `move(from, to)`, `addImages`; optimistic, one `reference:set` per change, only the latest write settles the list, a refused write reverts and toasts; `resetReferenceStore()`), `ReferencePanel.tsx` (`<aside aria-label="References" data-testid="references-panel">` in `ProjectScreen` between the main `<section>` and `AssistantPanel`, so it stays beside a document and an entity page; `ResizeHandle` on its left edge; `Add image…`; the empty line; `role="list"` "Pinned references"; not mounted in focus mode), and `ReferenceCard.tsx` (one `<li aria-label={title}>` per pin with `Move up` / `Move down` / `Unpin <title>` and HTML5 drag and drop; the panel lists only pins whose entity or node is in its store, and the move buttons step over hidden ones). An entity card shows the thumbnail, up to three filled fields or the blank page, `Show more` / `Show less`, and `Open <name>` → `entityStore.select`; a notes card shows `docToText` of the notes — the notes store's copy while an editor has the id loaded (and the last one after it unloads), else one `notes:get` — and `Open notes of <title>` selects the node and opens the notes panel; an image card shows the file at panel width, a click opens a modal viewer, and its unpin asks through `dialogs.confirm` because the file is deleted. Pin entry points: the `Pin to References` / `Unpin from References` button in the `EntityEditor` header and `Pin notes` / `Unpin notes` in the `NotesPanel` heading (both `aria-pressed`).
