Handlers: `ai:ghostText` creates a proposal when `text !== ''` and returns `proposalId` (else null); `ai:recommendTags` always creates one with `content = JSON.stringify(names)` (a historical snapshot, never a bank join) and takes optional `note`/`regeneratedFrom`, which switch `recommendTags` to `prompts/tagsRegen.v1.ts` (wraps `buildTagsPrompt`, one system clause `The writer asked for a different set…`) and join the context hash so a note-less regenerate never hits the cache; `proposal:settle { id, status, note? }`. Renderer: `src/renderer/features/ai/proposalStore.ts` `proposalStore.settle(id, status, note?)` (never throws, toasts on failure, a session-wide `Set` sends each id once; `resetProposalStore()` in before and after each). Ghost text: `GhostState.full` is the original text; plugin state is `{ ghost, exit }` and a plugin view reports each exit exactly once through `editor.storage.ghostText.onSettle(status, consumed)` (`accepted` all of `full` consumed, `acceptedPart` some, `rejected` none; `destroy` is the backstop; a `registerPlugin` reconfigure does not fire); the controller registers the hook per session, stashes `proposalId` per request, and settles with no note. Tags: `TagsPanel`'s `done` state carries `proposalId` and `acceptedCount`; Dismiss settles `acceptedPart`/`rejected`, linking the last chip settles `accepted` (issued inside the `setRecommend` updater, safe under StrictMode because of the per-id guard), Regenerate… uses `dialogs.prompt` (validate refuses over 300 chars) then `askForTags(nodeId, { note, regeneratedFrom })`. The e2e's `TAGS_REGEN_SENTINEL` repeats the clause prefix because the e2e tsconfig cannot import `src/main`. Statuses have no `edited`: editing accepted text is F-14.6's. The target range columns are filled by `ai:rewrite` (F-14.10) as a snapshot of the ProseMirror range at request time; other features leave them null.
- Provenance (F-14.6): `src/shared/provenance.ts` owns `AI_ORIGIN_MARK`, `AI_ORIGIN_KEEP_RATIO` (0.5), `AiOriginAttrs { proposalId, accepted }`, `aiOriginAttrsOf`, `aiOriginStats(doc)` (`aiChars`, `totalChars`, `byProposal`; text-node characters only), and `aiOriginPercent`. `src/renderer/features/editor/aiOrigin.ts`: the `AiOrigin` mark (`inclusive: false`, `span.ai-origin[data-ai-origin][data-proposal-id][data-accepted]`, `parseHTML` for in-app paste), `markAiOrigin(tr, from, to, attrs)` (adds the mark, re-marks an adjacent run of the same proposal so word-by-word acceptance is one span, and sets the `AI_ORIGIN_KEY` meta that vouches for the insertion), and the plugin: state `{ marked }` so unmarked documents pay nothing; `appendTransaction` on a doc change without the vouching meta strips the mark from `insertedRanges(tr)` (each step map's new ranges mapped forward through the later maps; skipped for `history$` undo/redo and `uiEvent` paste/drop) and then clears every span of a proposal whose surviving characters fall under `accepted × AI_ORIGIN_KEEP_RATIO` (max `accepted` across its spans), in one appended transaction so undo restores it with the edit. `ghostText.ts`: `GhostState.proposalId`/`acceptedChars`, `setGhost(text, flagged?, violation?, proposalId?)`, `insertAccepted` marks what Tab/Shift+Tab insert with the running total; a mark-only transaction (Add/RemoveMarkStep only) is neither typing nor a caret move, so the appended strip cannot dismiss a suggestion. `extensions.ts` registers `AiOrigin` with `InlineTag` and `GhostText` (manuscript documents only). `DocumentEditor.tsx` `useLiveDocStats` (one `update` subscription, one `getJSON` per document change, cached by document identity) feeds `StatusBar`'s optional `aiPercent` (`data-testid="status-ai"`, `N% AI`, only when > 0). Main: `src/main/provenance/report.ts` `buildProvenanceReport(db)` (manuscript documents via `manuscriptDocuments`; `documentJson(row)` in `voice/profile.ts` is the one defensive parse, `documentText` delegates to it; `proposals` is the distinct id count) behind `provenance:report`; `disclosure.ts` `renderDisclosure(report, { projectName, date })` is pure Markdown with an inline-snapshot test; `provenance:export` asks `dialogs.chooseExportPath(defaultName, filters, directory?)` (default `<project>-ai-disclosure.md` beside the project folder), writes tmp + rename, returns `{ path }` or null on cancel. Renderer: `provenanceStore.ts` (`load`/`exportReport` flush pending saves first because main reads saved rows; cleared on project close in `App.tsx`; `resetProvenanceStore()`), `ProvenanceSection.tsx` under `VoiceSection` (`provenance-section`, `provenance-percent`, `provenance-empty`, list `AI origin by scene` with `provenance-scene` items whose title button selects the scene, button `Export disclosure report`, toast `Saved to <path>`). The e2e sets a DOM selection inside the span (it can wrap across lines, so a positioned click is unreliable) and deletes 25 of the 41 accepted characters (the join at the caret adds a leading and a trailing space to the continuation) to see the mark go.
- Assistant panel (F-5.4): `src/shared/chat.ts` owns the conversation model and limits (`CONVERSATIONS_KEY`, `ChatMode`, `CHAT_*` caps, `ChatMessage`, `Conversation`, `Conversations`, `parseStoredConversations`, `titleFor`, `parseTagRefs`); conversations are JSON in the project `settings` table (`getConversations`/`setConversations`, `conversations:get`/`set`), main is stateless about them and the renderer sends the last `CHAT_HISTORY_TURNS` as `history`. `src/main/ai/request.ts` has `runAiStream(deps, input, onDelta)` beside `runAiRequest`, sharing `prepare`/`serveCached`/`record` (a cache hit is one delta; a stream without usage falls back to `estimateTokens`; a mid-stream error logs and caches nothing); `StreamChunk.usage?` arrives on the OpenAI adapter's final chunk (`stream_options.include_usage`). `src/main/ai/context/chatContext.ts` `buildChatContext(db, { nodeId, message })` (scene text head-truncated to `CHAT_SCENE_CHAR_BUDGET`, `#name` refs resolved against the tag bank to the notes of the documents linked to that tag within `CHAT_REF_NOTES_CHAR_BUDGET`, unknown names dropped). `src/main/ai/chat.ts` `runChat` (Plan needs `chat` allowed, Agent also `dial >= AI_DATA_SHARING.ghostText.minDial`; the task context is appended to the system turn so history turns are real user/assistant messages; the oldest history is dropped first when over the input budget; Agent answers go through `postProcessChatText` and `checkChatFidelity` with one `chatRegen.v1` regenerate and fall back flagged; every answer is an `ai_proposal` row, Plan ones stay pending). Prompts `chat.v1.ts`/`chatRegen.v1.ts` and, since F-14.3, `chat.v2.ts`/`chatRegen.v2.ts` (Agent mode folds the scene brief in right after the metadata line; Plan mode never carries it) are catalogued with fixture cases (the maxed Agent case sits at 7 003 of 8 000 on v1, 7 406 on v2). Handler `ai:chat` emits `ai:chatDelta { requestId, delta }` per non-empty delta before resolving. Renderer: `activeEditorStore.ts` (`{ id, editor } | null`, set by `DocumentEditor` on ready and focus, released on unmount) is the one way the panel reaches the editor; `assistantStore.ts` (load with the tree, clear on close, the presets write pattern; `send` appends the user turn and an empty assistant turn, subscribes to deltas for its `requestId`, drops stale ones, removes the empty turn and toasts on failure; Agent mode focuses the editor, `setGhost(text, flagged, violation, proposalId)`, records the notice turn, wraps `storage.ghostText.onSettle` for exactly one settlement and restores it; an empty answer or no editor settles `rejected` and toasts); `AssistantPanel.tsx` (`aside[aria-label="Assistant"]`, `data-testid="assistant-panel"`, tablist `Conversations`, log `Messages` with `chat-turn`/`chat-pending`/`chat-turn-cost`, radiogroup `Mode`, combobox `Paragraphs`, textbox `Message` (no Clear conversation since 2026-10-06), `assistant-disabled` below Ask) rendered in `ProjectScreen` after the main section with `Layout.assistant` (`LAYOUT_LIMITS.assistant` 0.2–0.5; `toggle` runs `normalizeLayout`, giving way assistant → notes → sidebar); `AssistantToggleButton` in the header owns Ctrl/Cmd+K. Ghost text: `insertAccepted` splits on blank lines into paragraphs (single newlines are hard breaks) with the mark on each and returns the end position; `.ghost-text` is `white-space: pre-wrap`. The e2e fake server streams SSE for `stream: true` and tells Agent requests by the opening of `AGENT_RULES`. Modes since 2026-10-07 (decided by the author): the panel's modes are Auto / Ask / Plan, one project setting (`AiSettings.chatMode`, previous bullet) instead of a per-conversation mode; `Conversation.mode` is optional, parsed for old rows and never written. `CHAT_MODES` (`query`, `agent` = Author, `plan`) stays only as the label a stored turn carries (`ChatMessage.mode`; a stored Author turn still renders `AGENT_NOTICE`). The panel no longer calls `ai:chat` (no streaming, no ghost placement, no paragraph count; `runChat`/`chat.v5` stay in main), nor subscribes to `ai:chatDelta`. While AI or the `chat`/`agent` toggles are off, `assistant-disabled` says so (`AI_OFF_MESSAGE` names Settings › AI).
- Cancel and activity (F-5.10): `src/main/ai/inflight.ts` is the one in-flight registry (`registerInflight(requestId)` → `AbortController`, duplicate ids refused with `VALIDATION`; `cancelInflight(requestId)` aborts and reports whether it found one but does not release, the request path's `finally` does; `releaseInflight`, `inflightCount`, `regenRequestId(id)` = `<id>:regen`; `resetInflight()` in tests). `AiRequestInput.requestId?` makes `runAiRequest`/`runAiStream` register on entry, pass `signal` to the provider, and release in `finally` on every path; `CompletionRequest.signal` reaches the OpenAI SDK as request options and `mapOpenAiError` turns an abort (`APIUserAbortError` or any `AbortError`) into `AiCancelledError`; the adapter also checks `signal.aborted` inside and after the stream loop because the SDK returns quietly from an aborted stream, and a cancelled stream must never read as a short answer (`CANCELLED`: not logged, not cached, next step `Send it again whenever you like.`; renderers never toast it). Use cases take the id from their input (`ai:ghostText`/`ai:chat` already carried one; `ai:recommendTags` gained an optional one); the fidelity regenerate registers as `<id>:regen` and `ai:cancel` tries both. Renderer: `src/renderer/features/ai/aiActivityStore.ts` (`track(feature, requestId, promise)` adds and removes in `finally`; `cancel(requestId)` invokes `ai:cancel` silently; `resetAiActivityStore()`), `AiActivityIndicator.tsx` in the header beside the Assistant toggle (`role="status"`, `data-testid="ai-activity"`, spinner plus the running features' `AI_DATA_SHARING[feature].label`s (`Assistant chat`, `Tag suggestions`, `Ghost text`), shown 300 ms after the store's `busySince` idle-to-busy transition and kept until nothing is in flight, so cache hits never flicker). Ghost-text request ids are `g-<n>` from a module counter (main refuses duplicate ids, so a per-session counter restarting at 1 would collide); the controller cancels its own stale requests (edit or caret move since send, document switch, mode off, unmount) and treats `CANCELLED` as silent with no back-off; the tag bar's pending state has Cancel; the assistant's Send (`assistant-send`) becomes Stop (`assistant-stop`) while pending; a stop drops the placeholder and its deltas but keeps the user turn and unpends at once, a late reply is ignored (a late ok rejects its proposal), and closing a conversation cancels its request. Toasts stack bottom-right over the panel's composer and intercept clicks for their lifetime (the e2e dismisses them first); moving the toast stack is a follow-up. The e2e fake server delays a request whose last user message contains `SLOW` by 3 s so a Stop can land.
- AI waiting text (2026-10-08, the author's request): every AI wait shows `features/ai/AiWaitText.tsx` (`phrases`, `testId`, `className`, `announce`), muted jade (`--ms-ai-wait` per built-in theme in `tokens.css`, Tailwind `--color-ai-wait`; custom themes keep their base's) and italic, with a slow glint (`.ai-wait`, background-clip text) and a 600 ms cross-fade to the next phrase every `AI_WAIT_ROTATE_MS` (2.5 s); all phrases share one grid cell so the line never changes size; only phrase 0 is in the accessibility tree, so the `role="status"` announces once (`announce={false}` inside a container that is already the status, e.g. `chat-pending`). Reduced motion (`lib/motion.ts` `prefersReducedMotion`, also used by the assistant's suggestion line) sets `data-motion="reduce"`: phrases still change, no fade, no glint. Phrase sets live in `features/ai/aiWaitPhrases.ts` (`AI_WAIT_PHRASES`, 4–6 per task, phrase 0 = the old plain line so tests keep matching): organise, chat/query/whatNext (picked by the turn's routed action), agentInsert/agentReplace, rewrite, brief, tags, synopsis, notes, critique, betaReader, continuity, proofread, editPass (under the workspace's progress bar), contextSort (phrase 0 carries the request count). Lines with live numbers keep their text and take `AI_WAIT_CLASS` only: the header's indexing and AI-activity lines, Summary's "Updating…", Find links, the import detect progress, the upload's estimate and request progress. Non-AI waits stay plain "Loading…". A new AI wait uses `AiWaitText` with its own phrase set.
- Shortcuts (F-2.7): `src/renderer/features/shell/shortcuts.ts` is the one registry of app-level chords (`APP_SHORTCUTS` by `ShortcutId`, `Chord { key, ctrl, shift, alt }` where `ctrl` is Cmd on macOS, `matchesShortcut(event, chord)` exact-modifier and case-insensitive, `formatShortcut`); the Settings and Assistant listeners read their chords from it, and F-7.1's menu and F-7.7's reference must too. `InsertShortcuts` in `App.tsx` (mounted in the header cluster while a project is open) listens on `document` in the capture phase, stops the event so the editor's Mod-Shift-S never fires, checks `resolveCreateTarget(tree, tree.selectedId, level)` (a null target toasts `Select something in the manuscript to insert the <level> after it.`), then `createLevel(level)`. Editor-internal chords stay in the Tiptap extensions.
- Focus mode (F-6.1): `src/renderer/features/focus/focusStore.ts` is the one owner of `active` (`enter`/`exit`/`toggle` invoke `window:setFullScreen { on }` and set `active` from the reply's `on`; `subscribe()` mirrors the `window:fullScreenChanged` event, called once from `App`; `resetFocusStore()` in tests). Main: `createWindow` emits the event on `enter-full-screen`/`leave-full-screen`; the handler flips the first window and reads `isFullScreen()` back (`ClosableWindow` in `handlers.ts` carries `setFullScreen`/`isFullScreen`). `FocusShortcuts` in `App.tsx` is mounted beside `<main>` (the header unmounts in focus mode) with `InsertShortcuts` next to it: F11 toggles; Escape exits only when active and not `defaultPrevented`, which covers dialogs and the context menu but not the editor, because `prosemirror-view` reports every Escape as handled (`captureKeyDown`); inside the editor the `EscapeShortcut` extension (`priority: 50`, below ghost text and the `#` suggestion) calls `escapeFocusMode()` through `buildExtensions({ onEscape })`, manuscript editors only. Closing the project exits focus mode. Known gap: the Settings (Ctrl+,) and Assistant (Ctrl+K) listeners live in header components and are unmounted in focus mode; F-6.5/F-6.6 decide where they go. Hidden while active: the app header, the sidebar, `NotesPanel`, `AssistantPanel`, both toolbars, and the tag bar; `StatusBar` stays; the layout store is never touched. `FocusModeButton.tsx` (`aria-label="Focus mode"`, `aria-pressed`) sits in both toolbars' `right` slot. Fullscreen state is never persisted.
- Typewriter (F-3.9, F-6.7): `src/renderer/features/editor/typewriter.ts` (`Typewriter` extension registered with the manuscript-only extensions; storage `{ enabled }` read at run time; command `setTypewriter(enabled)`; a plugin view's `update` scrolls the container returned by the `scroller` option, default `findScroller(view.dom)` = nearest `overflow-y: auto | scroll` ancestor, by `centerDelta(caret.top, rect)` with `TYPEWRITER_DEAD_ZONE_PX` 8, only when `view.state.doc !== prevState.doc`). `EditorSettings.typewriter` is `z.boolean().default(false)` (so `EditorSettingsInput` is the type callers send and `setEditorSettings` takes it); `DocumentEditor` runs `editor.commands.setTypewriter(focus || settings.typewriter)` in an effect (never mutate `editor.storage` from a component: the react-hooks immutability rule refuses it) and adds `pb-[50vh]` to the column while on. The e2e enables it in Settings → Editor, types 14 lines at the end of Scene 1, and checks the caret sits within a third of the scroller's height of its middle.
- Backgrounds (F-6.2): `src/shared/focus.ts` owns `FOCUS_SETTINGS_KEY` (`focus`), `FocusSettings` (`backgroundId`; every later field is defaulted so old rows parse), `BACKGROUND_EXTENSIONS`, `BACKGROUND_MAX_BYTES` (20 MB), and `Background { id, name, url }`. Files live in `<project>/assets/backgrounds/<stem>.<8 hex>.<ext>` (`backgroundFileName(original, shortId)` sanitizes the stem to `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`, caps it at 40, falls back to `background`; `backgroundDisplayName(fileName)` strips the id, so `Background.id` is `<stem>.<8 hex>` and `Background.name` is `<stem>.<ext>`; `src/main/project/backgroundStore.ts`: `listBackgrounds`, `addBackground`, `removeBackground` deletes `<id>.<ext>`, all three over `src/main/project/imageAssets.ts` — since F-9.3 the one owner of the copy in and out: `assetDir(folder, dir)`, `addImageAsset(folder, dir, source, fallbackStem)` (mkdir, minted id, `COPYFILE_EXCL`, `VALIDATION` for type or size, `NOT_FOUND` for a missing source, answers the stored file name) and `removeImageAsset(folder, dir, fileName)` (a missing file is ignored; a separator or `..` in the name is `VALIDATION`); `settingsStore.ts` has `getFocusSettings`/`setFocusSettings`). The renderer loads them through the privileged scheme `mythscribe-asset://<dir>/<file>` (registered before `app.whenReady`, handled with `protocol.handle` + `net.fetch(pathToFileURL(...))`; `src/main/project/assetUrl.ts` `assetPathFor(root, url)` is the pure resolver for any `ASSET_DIRS` host, null for traversal, an unknown host, or an unknown extension; no project → 404; `index.html`'s CSP `img-src` lists the scheme). Channels `background:list`, `background:add` (`dialogs.chooseImages()`, multi-select; `{ added, skipped } | null`), `background:remove` (clears `backgroundId` when current), `focusSettings:get`/`set`. Renderer: `src/renderer/features/focus/backgroundStore.ts` (presets persistence pattern; `add`/`remove`/`select`; `resetBackgroundStore()`), `BackgroundManager.tsx` (dialog `Backgrounds`: `aria-pressed` tiles, No background first, `<img>` thumbnails, `Delete <name>` with confirm, `Add images…`), `FocusBackdrop.tsx` (`data-testid="focus-backdrop"`, fixed layer with the image and a scrim, mounted by `App` only in focus mode with a selection), and the Focus mode group in Settings → Editor that opens the manager (F-6.5's bar reuses it). The e2e writes a 1×1 PNG, stubs the open dialog, and checks the image loads through the scheme.
- Rotation and overlay (F-6.3, F-6.4): `FocusSettings` in `src/shared/focus.ts` carries `rotation { enabled, intervalMinutes }` (`ROTATION_MINUTES` 1–60, default 5) and `overlay { darkness, width }` (`OVERLAY_DARKNESS` 0–100 default 60, `OVERLAY_WIDTH` 35–100 default 70), both `.default(...)` with the full object (zod 4's `.default` takes the output shape); `clampInt(value, range)` is for the controls. `backgroundStore` has `setRotation(patch)`/`setOverlay(patch)` over one `patchSettings` helper shared with `select` (schema-refused patches are ignored). `rotation.ts` `useBackgroundRotation()` (+ the null component `BackgroundRotation` that `App` mounts only in focus mode): a `setInterval` of `intervalMinutes` that picks the next background in list order, wrapping, through `select`; no timer with rotation off or fewer than two images. `FocusBackdrop` takes `darkness` and sets `--ms-focus-darkness` (the scrim's `opacity`; `--ms-focus-scrim` is now solid black); `DocumentEditor` overrides `--ms-editor-max-width` with `<width>%` only in focus mode. Test fixtures must build focus settings from `defaultFocusSettings()` (a bare `{ backgroundId }` no longer parses as the output type).
- Control bar (F-6.5): `focusStore.panels { notes, assistant }` are session flags (`togglePanel(name)`, both reset on exit) that decide whether `NotesPanel`/`AssistantPanel` render in focus mode; the persisted `layout.notes/assistant.open` keep governing the normal screen. `src/renderer/features/focus/FocusControlBar.tsx` (`role="toolbar"` `Focus controls`, `data-testid="focus-control-bar"`, `data-visible`) is mounted by `App` inside `<main>` only in focus mode; `autoHide.ts` owns the visibility rule (`SHOW_ZONE_PX` 48 from the bottom edge, pointer over the bar or focus inside it keeps it, `HIDE_DELAY_MS` 1 500, `INTRO_MS` 2 000 on entry) with a `pointermove` listener on `document`. Controls: `Backgrounds…` (opens `BackgroundManager`), `Rotate` (`aria-pressed`, `setRotation`), `VibeWriteToggle` with `error={null}`, `Notes`/`AI assistant` (`aria-pressed`, `togglePanel`), sliders `Darkness`/`Width` (`setOverlay`), `data-testid="focus-words"` from `useLiveDocStats(useActiveEditorStore editor)` (the hook now lives in `src/renderer/features/editor/liveDocStats.ts`; blank for a folder stack), and `Exit focus mode`.
- Floating panels (F-6.6): `Layout.floating { notes: Rect, assistant: Rect, references: Rect }` (`references` since 2026-10-07; `StoredLayout` defaults each missing rect) (`Rect { x, y, width, height }` in px; `FLOATING_MIN` 280 × 200; `clampRect(rect, viewport)` in `src/shared/layout.ts` keeps position then size inside the viewport; `StoredLayout` and `defaultLayout` default it so old app-state files load; `layoutStore.setFloatingRect(name, rect)` clamps to `window.inner*`, ignores an unchanged rect, and writes through the same debounced path; `moveFloatingBy`/`resizeFloatingBy` are the keyboard steps of `FLOATING_KEY_STEP_PX` 16). `src/renderer/features/focus/FloatingWindow.tsx` (`role="dialog"` non-modal, `aria-label` = panel name, `data-testid="floating-<name>"`): title bar `role="group"` `<Title> window` (`tabIndex` 0, `data-testid="floating-<name>-title"`) drags from the pointer-down origin with pointer capture, eight resize handles (2026-10-07: edges `floating-<name>-resize-{n,e,s,w}`, corners `-{ne,sw,nw}`, and the bottom-right `floating-<name>-grip` with a visible mark; `data-edge`, pointer-only, `aria-hidden`, cursors ns/ew/nesw/nwse) drag from the pointer-down origin through the pure `resizeRect(from, edge, dx, dy, viewport)` in `src/shared/layout.ts` (only the dragged edges move; each stops at the viewport and at `FLOATING_MIN_SIZE` from the opposite edge), arrow keys move by 16 px and Shift+arrows resize from the focused title bar, `Close <Title>` clears the focus flag, Escape is handled in the capture phase (prevent default and stop propagation, so focus mode stays); it re-clamps on mount and on window resize. `FocusFloatingPanels.tsx` renders the windows from `focusStore.panels` (notes, assistant, and since 2026-10-07 references: `ReferencesBody` with `AddReferenceImageButton` in the title bar, both extracted from `ReferencePanel.tsx`; the control bar has a References toggle) (`FocusPanel` is now an alias of `FloatingPanel`): the notes body for the selected node (a line when none) and the assistant body with `NewConversationButton` in the title bar; `App` renders it in focus mode instead of the docked `NotesPanel`/`AssistantPanel`, whose bodies were extracted as `NotesBody` and `AssistantBody`. `src/renderer/test/setup.ts` stubs pointer capture for jsdom. No click-to-front yet (document order, both under the control bar's `z-20`).
- Menus (F-7.1, F-7.7): the chord registry moved to `src/shared/shortcuts.ts` (`APP_SHORTCUTS` with `save`, `EDITOR_SHORTCUTS`; the renderer's `shell/shortcuts.ts` re-exports it and keeps `matchesShortcut`/`formatShortcut`). `src/shared/menu.ts` owns `MENU` (`MenuItem { id, label, shortcut?, editRole?, when }`, `MenuItemId` and `EditRole` zod enums, `menuItemLabel(item, format)`, `isMenuItemEnabled`, `menuItemChord`, `menuAccelerator`, `DOCS_URL`, `isAllowedExternalUrl`); items for unbuilt features are absent, never disabled placeholders. Main: `src/main/menu.ts` `buildMenuTemplate(menu, { format, platform }, onAction)` is pure and tested (roles for Edit, accelerators, `enabled`, the darwin app menu); `installApplicationMenu` rebuilds on `manager.onChange` and emits `menu:action { id }` to the focused window (replaces the old `Menu.setApplicationMenu(null)`; `autoHideMenuBar` stays). Channels `menu:edit { role }` (`webContents.undo/redo/cut/copy/paste` on the focused window) and `menu:openExternal { url }` (`shell.openExternal`, `https://mythscribe.app/` only). Renderer: `menuActions.ts` `runMenuAction(id)` is the one dispatch (File › New/Open confirm-close an open project first through `closeProjectWithConfirm`, shared with the header button; `insertLevel` is shared with `InsertShortcuts`; View › Notes/AI assistant use the focus panel flags in focus mode and the layout otherwise; Tools › Tags leaves focus mode first; a project item with none open toasts `Open a project first.`); `App` subscribes to `menu:action` once; `MenuBar.tsx` (`menubar` `Application menu`, `data-testid="menu-bar"`, triggers with `aria-haspopup`/`aria-expanded`, popups `role="menu"`, items with `aria-keyshortcuts` and `aria-disabled`) sits in the header after the wordmark. `shellDialogStore` (`settings | shortcuts | about`) owns the three shell dialogs, mounted by `ShellDialogs` before `DialogHost`; `SettingsShortcut` (Ctrl+,) lives beside `FocusShortcuts` at app level; `ShortcutsDialog.tsx` renders `APP_SHORTCUTS` by group plus `EDITOR_SHORTCUTS`; `AboutDialog.tsx` reads the version from `app:info`; `welcomeStore.ts` lets the menu open the create wizard. Known gaps: View items are plain native items (main does not know the focus panels), and macOS accelerators are untested on this machine. The e2e drives the in-app bar and checks the native template through `app.evaluate`.
- Rewrite in my voice (F-14.10): `src/shared/rewrite.ts` owns `REWRITE_TEXT_MIN` (20), `REWRITE_TEXT_MAX` (4,000), `REWRITE_CONTEXT_CHARS` (300), `DiffSegment`, and `diffWords` (word-level LCS over words and the runs between them; `del` before `ins` at a change point; adjacent same-kind segments merged). Feature id `rewrite` in `AI_FEATURE_IDS` (tier `strong` since 2026-10-10 in `rewrite.ts`, the catalogue, and `DEFAULT_ROUTING_TABLE`; was `fast`; `minDial` 2, budgets 1,500 out / 3,000 in); `AiSettings.features` parses as `partialRecord` + defaults so a row stored before a feature existed keeps its dial (`defaultFeatureToggles`). Contract: `ai:rewrite` (the renderer sends the selection as plain text, its ProseMirror range, and the two context windows; `note`/`regeneratedFrom` for an author regenerate) → `AiRewriteResult` (= `AiChatResult`), event `ai:rewriteDelta`. Main: `src/main/ai/prompts/rewrite.v1.ts` (`REWRITE_RULES` opens with `You are the rewrite feature inside a novel-writing app.`, which the e2e fake server keys on; system = rules + voice block + `Scene:` line, user = `Text before`/`Text after`/`Passage to rewrite` + `Rewrite the passage.`; no temperature; `maxTokens = min(ceil(estimate × 1.5) + 40, cap)`), `rewriteRegen.v1.ts` (note clause, then violation clause), `src/main/ai/rewrite.ts` `runRewrite(db, deps, input, onDelta)` (VALIDATION backstop on the limits, `assertFeatureAllowed`, `requireDocument` now exported from `documentStore.ts`, POV from scene meta → `buildVoiceProfile`/`voiceBlock`; the first draft streams through `runAiStream`, then `postProcessChatText` + `checkChatFidelity` from `chat.ts`; one non-streamed regenerate under `regenRequestId`; an author regenerate uses `rewriteRegen.v1` from the start; note, predecessor, and voice version join the context hash), handler `ai:rewrite` (proposal with `targetFrom`/`targetTo`/`regeneratedFrom`), eval cases `fresh`/`full`/`maxed` and the three regen cases. Renderer: `insertProse.ts` (`insertProse(tr, from, text)`: blank lines → paragraphs, newline → hard break; `ghostText.ts`'s `insertAccepted` calls it), `selectedText.ts` `rangeText(doc, from, to, { blockSeparator, hardBreak })`, `rewriteTarget.ts` (`RewriteTarget` extension, manuscript only: plugin state `{ from, to, text } | null` mapped with assoc 1/-1 and nulled on a doc change once `passageText(from, to) !== text`; `.rewrite-target` inline decoration; commands `setRewriteTarget`, `clearRewriteTarget`, `acceptRewrite(text, proposalId)` = delete → `insertProse` → `markAiOrigin(accepted = text.length)` → select end → clear, one transaction; `captureRewriteText(editor)` tightens the selection past edge whitespace and block boundaries and renders `\n\n` blocks, `\n` hard breaks, `#name` tags; `rewriteContext(doc, from, to)`), `rewriteStore.ts` (one session app-wide: `start(nodeId, editor)`, `stop`, `regenerate(note)` (re-reads the mapped target; only while `ready`), `accept(editor)`, `reject`, `dismissFor(nodeId)`; a `transaction` listener flips `ready → invalid` when the target goes; `track('rewrite', …)`; stale deltas dropped; stop clears at once and a late ok settles `rejected`; CANCELLED silent, other failures `status: 'error'` shown in the panel, no toast; `resetRewriteStore()`), `RewriteButton.tsx` (`Rewrite in my voice`, lucide `Wand2`, gated on 20–4,000 captured characters, `isFeatureAllowed(settings, 'rewrite')`, no session; title names the missing condition), `RewritePanel.tsx` (`section[aria-label="Rewrite"]`, `rewrite-panel`, `rewrite-draft`, `rewrite-stop`, `rewrite-diff` with `del`/`ins`, `rewrite-flag`, `rewrite-cost`, `rewrite-accept`, `rewrite-reject`, `rewrite-regenerate` via `dialogs.prompt`, `rewrite-invalid` with `PASSAGE_CHANGED_MESSAGE`, `rewrite-error` (`role="alert"`), `rewrite-close`), mounted by `DocumentEditor` between the tag bar and the content (single-document view, not focus mode; `dismissFor(id)` on unmount or editor rebuild); styles `.rewrite-target`, `.rewrite-diff del/ins` in `app.css`. Known consequences: inline tags go out as `#name` and return as plain text; marks inside the selection are lost on accept; a multi-paragraph selection is joined by the delete and rebuilt from the answer's blank lines; the panel shows one "rewriting" state through the fidelity check because the contract has no end-of-stream marker. The e2e step sits after the assistant-panel assertions (before the dial goes back to Off) because the provenance steps assert exact proposal and span counts. Since 2026-10-06 (all AI in the assistant): `RewriteButton` is deleted: `rewriteReason(settings, length)` in `features/ai/aiActions.ts` is the one gate, used by the selection bubble, the editor's right-click menu, and a routed chat turn; `rewriteStore.start(nodeId, editor, note?)` takes the router's instruction as the note; `RewritePanel` renders in the assistant's `AiResults`, `DocumentEditor` keeps only the `dismissFor` effect. Rewrite in Vibe (notes fixes 2026-10-15, `fixes-1.md` §3): `ai:rewrite` takes an optional `mode` (`AssistantMode`), which `rewriteStore` `send` fills from the AI settings' chat mode; `runRewrite` sends `src/main/ai/prompts/rewrite.v6.ts` (v4's request with `REWRITE_V6_VIBE_LINE` last in the user turn, so the system turn and its cache are v4's) for a first draft in Vibe in a v1 project; every other mode, a v2 project, and both regenerates (the author's and the fidelity check's, `rewriteRegen.v4`) are unchanged; `vibe` joins the context hash only when set. Eval cases `rewrite.v6` fresh / full / maxed (v4's passages).
- Editor's notes (F-14.8), main side: `src/shared/critique.ts` owns the honesty levels (`HONESTY_LEVELS`/`DEFAULT_HONESTY` 'direct'/`HONESTY_LABEL`), `CRITIQUE_CATEGORIES` (+ labels), the caps (`CRITIQUE_TEXT_MIN` 200, `CRITIQUE_SCENE_CHAR_BUDGET` 20,000, `CRITIQUE_NOTES_CHAR_CAP` 500, `CRITIQUE_MAX_NOTES` 8, quote/why/fix maxima), the zod `CritiqueNote`/`CritiqueNotes`, and the matching rules both sides share (`straightenQuotes`, `normalizeForMatch`, `findQuote`). Feature id `critique` (tier `strong`, `minDial` 1, budgets 1,500 out / 8,000 in); `AiSettings.critique = { honesty }` is defaulted so older rows parse. Contract: `ai:critique { nodeId, requestId, note?, regeneratedFrom? }` → `AiCritiqueResult` (`notes`, `truncated`, `dropped`, usage/cost/cached/model, `proposalId`, `requestId`, or the AI failure shape with the echoed id). Main: `src/main/ai/prompts/critique.v2.ts` (F-14.3, the shipped version the feature sends; `critique.v1.ts` stays as shipped and its rules and honesty lines are imported unchanged — the brief replaced the notes in the user turn and the `intent` note is asked against it) over `src/main/ai/prompts/critique.v1.ts` (`CRITIQUE_RULES` opens with `You are the editor feature inside a novel-writing app.`, which the e2e fake server keys on; system = rules + `HONESTY_INSTRUCTION[honesty]` + voice block + `Scene:` line, user = the scene's notes as the brief with the one `intent` instruction, then `Scene text:` and `Give your editor's notes.`; JSON output, `maxTokens = outputBudget('critique')`), `critiqueRegen.v2.ts`/`critiqueRegen.v1.ts` (one clause, `The writer asked for different notes and said: "…".`; no violation clause — a failing fix is shown flagged, never retried), `src/main/ai/critique.ts` `runCritique(db, deps, input)` (`assertFeatureAllowed` first, then the text gate, the brief (F-14.3, `sceneBriefBlock`) / meta / POV context, `fitSceneToBudget` (head cut, then `CRITIQUE_SHRINK_CHARS` 2,000 at a time, floor `CRITIQUE_TEXT_MIN`) for token rule 8, `runAiRequest` with `json: true` on the strong tier; `parseCritiqueAnswer` drops a note with an unknown kind or category, trims and caps the strings, reads a blank fix, one equal to the quote, or a fix on praise as none (praise never diffs), drops every note whose quote `findQuote` cannot find in the text sent (counted in `dropped`, praise included), dedupes by normalized quote, caps at 8, and scores each fix with `checkGhostTextFidelity` (the banned phrases first, F-14.2) when a voice block went out; the note, the predecessor, the honesty setting, and the voice version join the context hash), handler `ai:critique` (proposal with the notes as JSON, `flagged` from any fix, `regeneratedFrom`), catalogue entries for both versions, eval cases `fresh`/`full`/`maxed` plus the two regen cases with the new `critique` scoring kind (parse + every quote found). `headTruncate` is now exported from `src/main/ai/context/chatContext.ts` and shared. Renderer: `locateText.ts` (`locateText(doc, quote)` → `{ from, to } | null`; one entry per character with its position, a boundary space (pos −1) at every block edge and hard break, an inline tag as `#name`, straightened quotes and collapsed whitespace — `normalizeForMatch`'s rules character by character — then `indexOf` on the normalized haystack; the needle is trimmed, so a match never starts or ends on a boundary space), `critiqueStore.ts` (one session app-wide `{ nodeId, requestId, status: pending|ready|error, notes, applied[], stale[], result, error }`: `start(nodeId)` flushes `useDocumentStore.flush()` first (main reads the saved row, as the tag bar does) then `track('critique', …)`; `show(index, editor)` selects the cited passage; `applyFix(index, editor)` refuses while `useRewriteStore` holds a session, then `setRewriteTarget` in its own transaction followed by a chained `acceptRewrite(fix, proposalId)` — two commands, because `acceptRewrite` reads the plugin state the chain started in — and marks the note applied, or stale when the quote is gone; `regenerate(note)` settles `regenerated` and re-sends; `close()` settles `accepted`/`acceptedPart`/`rejected` by the applied count against the notes that carry a fix (a pending one cancels); `dismissFor`; CANCELLED silent, other failures `status: 'error'`; `resetCritiqueStore()`), `CritiquePanel.tsx` (`section[aria-label="Editor's notes"]`, `critique-panel`, `critique-pending`, `critique-stop`, `critique-honesty` (a select of `HONESTY_LEVELS`, shown in every state, writing `update({ critique: { honesty } })`), `critique-note` per note (`data-kind`, category chip, praise tinted apart), `critique-quote` (a button calling `show`), `critique-fix-diff` (`diffWords(quote, fix)` in the existing `.rewrite-diff` styles), `critique-flag`, `critique-apply` ("Applied" once done; the title names why it is off), `critique-stale`, `critique-empty`, `critique-truncated`, `critique-cost`, `critique-dropped`, `critique-regenerate` via `dialogs.prompt`, `critique-close`, `critique-error` (`role="alert"`)), and `CritiqueButton.tsx` ("Editor's notes", lucide `NotebookPen`, gated on `isFeatureAllowed(settings, 'critique')`, `doc.textContent.length ≥ CRITIQUE_TEXT_MIN`, and no session). `DocumentEditor` mounts the button before `RewriteButton` and the panel under `RewritePanel` (single-document view, not focus mode) and calls `dismissFor(id)` on unmount or rebuild. The e2e step follows the rewrite step (its issue note quotes the accepted rewrite's tail) and checks that the fabricated third note never reaches the panel. Since 2026-10-06 (all AI in the assistant): `CritiqueButton` is deleted (Actions › Editor's notes, or a routed chat turn); `CritiquePanel` renders in `AiResults`.
- Story bible (F-14.9): `src/shared/storyBible.ts` owns the budgets (`STORY_BIBLE_TOKEN_BUDGET` 400; `STORY_BIBLE_GHOST_TOKEN_BUDGET` 150), the four story categories (`STORY_BIBLE_CATEGORIES`: character, setting, worldBuilding, plotThread; tone/content/custom are labels, not facts), `STORY_BIBLE_HEADING` (the ground-truth sentence the e2e and the golden tests match on; changing it is a prompt change), the `StoryBibleFacts` shape, and the pure `renderStoryBible(facts, maxTokens)`: fixed line order (heading, categories, this scene, previous, next) but admitted by priority when short (heading and scene line always, then neighbours, then each category cut to "… and N more"; a category that cannot keep one name is dropped), null when the bank is empty and the scene has no neighbour and no tag. `src/main/ai/context/storyBible.ts` `buildStoryBible(db, { nodeId, maxTokens })` gathers the facts (`listTags` filtered to the story categories; for a document under the manuscript root only: `listDocumentTags`, the parent chain nearest first, the position among sibling documents, and the previous/next document in `manuscriptDocuments` reading order (shared with the voice profile and `sceneNeighbours`) with `parseStoredSceneMeta` of their rows; front/end matter and `nodeId: null` get the bank alone). No cache: a few cheap queries per request, and the rendered string is part of every feature's context hash. Every prose prompt carries it in the system turn after the voice block and preset (`ghostText.v3`, `chat.v3` both modes, `critique.v3`, `rewrite.v2`, plus their regen wrappers; each is the previous version plus the bible slot, so a null bible yields the previous version's messages exactly, and the golden tests pin that). It was built against the v1 prompts in parallel with F-14.3 and merged second, hence v3 where F-14.3 had already shipped v2; `AI_DATA_SHARING` names it through `STORY_BIBLE_SENDS`. F-9 entity sheets (for the scene's tags) extend `StoryBibleFacts`, not the prompts. Since F-5.6 each `SceneNeighbor` carries `summary: string | null` (filled from `summariesFor` in one query) and `renderStoryBible` emits `Previous scene summary: …` / `Next scene summary: …` last, each admitted whole or dropped, and only when its neighbour line got in; the v3 prompt golden tests are unchanged because a null summary renders nothing. Notes redesign (2026-10-15, `vibe.md` part A): while the old story bible is archived (`storyBibleArchivedIn(db, 'v1')`), `buildStoryBible` reads no tag, bank or sheet and fills `StoryBibleFacts.notes` with the note pages the scene names (`namedNotePages`: a page's title or a capitalised 4+-letter word of it in the scene's text, or caselessly in its synopsis or scene notes, ranked by count through `scribe2/context.ts` `pagesNamedIn`); `renderStoryBible` admits them as `Note “Title”: …` lines after the sheets and before the neighbours' summaries, each cut to `STORY_BIBLE_NOTE_CHARS` (600) and then to the room left (the ghost budget gets first lines), dropped under `STORY_BIBLE_NOTE_MIN_CHARS` (40). `buildSceneSteer` is null while archived too. Same heading, no new prompt versions; the block is in each feature's context hash. Tests: `src/main/ai/archivedBible.features.test.ts` (the six features) over `context/archivedBibleFixture.ts`.
- Scene steer (F-14.13): the bible lists the scene's tags as unlabelled facts; the steer is the instruction to write by them. `src/shared/sceneSteer.ts` owns `SCENE_STEER_CATEGORIES` (tone, content, plotThread, custom; custom reads as `Themes`, since F-4.13 files themes as custom), `SCENE_STEER_LABEL`, `SCENE_STEER_NAMES_MAX` 6 per line (`… and N more`), `SCENE_STEER_TOKEN_BUDGET` 120 (every line keeps fewer names, down to one, rather than overspend; sized so the ghost-text maxed case still fits 2,200), `SCENE_STEER_HEADING` (`Scene tags (let them steer the writing):`, matched by the tests), and the pure `renderSceneSteer(tags)`: heading, one `Label: a, b` line per present category in that order, one closing sentence built from the categories present (`Write in this tone, keep to what the content tags allow, and keep these threads and themes in view.`); null with no steer-category tag. `src/main/ai/context/sceneSteer.ts` `buildSceneSteer(db, nodeId)`: null for a null, unknown, or section-root node (guarded before `listDocumentTags`, which throws there), else the node's tags in `listDocumentTags` order. Task-specific context, not the stable prefix: `ghostText.v4` (user turn, after the brief, before the notes), `chat.v4` (Agent mode only, in the system-turn context after the brief and before the active scene; Plan mode ignores it and `runChat` passes null), `rewrite.v3` (first in the user turn), `whatNext.v2` (user turn, after the brief, before `Text so far:`; v1's rules and sentinel imported), plus `ghostTextRegen.v4`, `chatRegen.v4`, `rewriteRegen.v3`. Each is the previous version plus the slot, so a null steer yields the previous version's messages exactly (golden tests pin it). Each caller adds `steer` to its context hash. `AI_DATA_SHARING` unchanged: `STORY_BIBLE_SENDS` already names the scene's tags. Eval: fresh/full/maxed per version (the `chat.v4` maxed case drops the oldest history turn to fit, as `runChat` does). Known gap: no e2e step sends a prose request while a scene carries a steer tag (the F-4.13 `stormbound` tone is removed before any later prose request), so the caller unit tests cover it.
- Scene summaries (F-5.6): `src/shared/summary.ts` owns the limits (`SUMMARY_TEXT_MIN` 200, `SUMMARY_SCENE_CHAR_BUDGET` 20,000, `SUMMARY_MAX_CHARS` 600, key points ≤ 4 × 140, characters ≤ 8 × 40, `SUMMARY_BANK_NAMES_MAX` 40, `SUMMARY_DEBOUNCE_MS` 3,000), the zod `SceneSummary` / `StoredSceneSummary` (+ `contentHash`, `promptVersion`, `model`, `truncated`, `createdAt`), `SummaryStatus` (idle / pending / failed), `SceneSummaryState` (`available`, `summary`, `stale`, `status`, `error`) and `UNAVAILABLE_SUMMARY`. Not a proposal: derived index data with no accept step, stored in `scene_summary` (migration `0006_scene_summaries`; `node_id` PK with cascade) through `src/main/document/summaryStore.ts` (`getSummary`, `upsertSummary`, `deleteSummary`, `summariesFor`; a corrupt row reads as none) and costed in the ledger only. Feature id `summary` (tier `fast`, JSON, `minDial` 1, budgets 300 out / 8,000 in). Prompt `src/main/ai/prompts/summary.v1.ts` (`SUMMARY_RULES` opens with `You are the scene-summary feature inside a novel-writing app.`, the e2e sentinel; system = rules + the bank's character names + the `Scene:` metadata line, user = the scene text + `Summarize the scene.`; catalogued with fresh/full/maxed cases, maxed 5,506). `src/main/ai/summarize.ts`: `summarySource(db, nodeId)` is the one owner of the content hash (scene text as sent, location/POV/timeline, bank names; the brief is deliberately outside it) shared with `summary:get`'s `stale`; `summarizeScene` gates on the dial, requires a manuscript document (VALIDATION otherwise), deletes the stored row and refuses under `SUMMARY_TEXT_MIN`, answers from the stored row without a request when the hash and prompt version match, else runs the fast tier, `parseSummaryAnswer` (summary required, lists lenient: non-strings dropped, trimmed, capped, deduped) and upserts. Since F-5.13 the debounce, the runs, and the node statuses belong to the index queue (`src/main/jobs/indexQueue.ts`, its own bullet); `summaryScheduler.ts` is gone. Wired in `registerHandlers`: `document:save` → `queue.touch('summary', id)` behind `isFeatureAllowed` (every node; the pane hides the block outside the manuscript), `summary:get` (state + `queue.nodeStatus`/`nodeError`), `ai:summarize { nodeId, requestId }` → `queue.runNow`, event `ai:summaryChanged { nodeId, status }` from the queue's `onNodeStatus`. `staleSummaryNodeIds(db)` in `summarize.ts` is what "Summarize all scenes" queues: manuscript documents over `SUMMARY_TEXT_MIN` with no stored row, a different `summarySource` hash, or an older prompt version, in reading order. Renderer: `src/renderer/features/editor/summaryStore.ts` (`byNode`, `load`, `summarize` through `useAiActivityStore().track('summary', …)` after a document flush, CANCELLED silent, `clear` on project close, `resetSummaryStore()` in before and after each; one `ai:summaryChanged` subscription refetches) and `SummaryBlock.tsx` under the Brief row of `MetadataPane` (a disclosure that grows the tag bar like Brief; `summary-status` "Updating…" / "Out of date"; `summary-refresh` "Summarize now" gated on the dial, `SUMMARY_TEXT_MIN` live characters, and not pending; `summary-text`, `summary-key-points`, `summary-characters`, `summary-error` (`role="alert"`), `summary-empty`; hidden unless `available`). The e2e unchecks the Scene summaries toggle the moment the dial leaves Off (background runs would break the exact request counts) and re-checks it for its own step at the end of the AI section.
- Scene mood and theme (F-5.6, the author 2026-10-10; no migration, no channel): prompt `prompts/summary.v5.ts` = v4 + one clause (deduce mood and theme, ≤ `SCENE_MOOD_MAX` 60 chars, "your reading, not tags") + top-level `"mood","theme"` keys after `card`; same output cap. `parseSummaryAnswer` keeps them on the card (`cleanCard(card, mood, theme)`, a card with only a mood is still a card). `AiSceneCard.mood/theme` are `.default('')`, so v4 rows and an older build read fine. `SUMMARY_CURRENT_MIN_VERSION` (= `summary.v4`) in `summarize.ts` is what `summaryStaleness`, the cached-row return, and so the F-9.14 conversion compare against: bumping the prompt re-reads nothing; raise it only for a reading old rows cannot do without. `SceneCard.mood/theme` ('' for none) come from `sceneCardFor`; `isEmptyCard` counts them; `renderCard` adds `Mood: …; Theme: …` after Changed (the chat agent's card reads). `SceneCardView` shows Mood and Theme with the AI mark (`AiLine`), not in the compact Outline row. Edits: `renderSceneMood(mood, theme)` (shared, pure) → `Scene mood: …` / `Scene theme: …` / `Keep the edit in line with them|it.`; `ai/context/sceneMood.ts` `buildSceneMood(db, nodeId)` reads the stored card. `rewrite.v4`/`rewriteRegen.v4` wrap v3 with the block joined after the steer (`rewriteV3Input`); `editPass.v2` wraps v1 with the block first in the user turn for `EDIT_PASS_MOOD_TYPES` (developmental, line, custom) only. Both put the block in the context hash. Not yet: the chat agent's own prose edits (merge note for agent.v8: pass `buildSceneMood` for the open scene into its context), ghost text, What next.
- Observed facts (F-5.16; since F-9.13 the table is frozen and the facts live in `fact`, see Dated facts above; the renderer's `observedFactStore`/`ObservedFacts.tsx`/`observedFactView.ts` and the `observedFact:*` channels are gone): `src/shared/observedFacts.ts` owns `OBSERVED_ATTRIBUTES` per kind (attribute id = sheet field id), `isObservedAttribute`, `observedAttributeField`/`Label`, the caps (value and quote 160; `SUMMARY_FACTS_MAX` 6 in `summary.ts`), zod `ObservedFact` / `ExtractedFact`, `factKey`, the pure `groupFacts(facts, readingOrder)` (hidden skipped; equal keys merge with one source per scene; `differs` on every row of an entity + attribute with two or more values), and the dismissed-names list (`OBSERVED_DISMISSED_KEY`, a project `settings` row). Schema: migration `0010_observed_facts` (`observed_fact` cascaded from entity and node; `entity.origin` `author | ai`). Not a new feature id: facts ride `summary` (one request, one toggle, label "Scene summaries and story bible", output 600). `prompts/summary.v2.ts` (v1's sentinel sentence unchanged) sends only the entity and unlinked-tag names that occur in the scene (`knownNames` in `src/main/ai/observedFacts.ts`), and the content hash covers only those, so a new entity does not stale other scenes. `summarize.ts`: `parseSummaryAnswer(text, sceneText)` drops and counts a fact with a bad shape, an attribute outside its kind, or a quote `findQuote` cannot find in the text as sent; `applyObservedFacts` resolves the name (kind + name key, then any kind, then a linked tag), creates a missing entity as `ai`/`blank` with its tag unless dismissed, and `replaceSceneFacts` — all in one transaction with the summary upsert. A run that created entities stamps the row with the post-run names so it does not stale itself, but only when the names had not moved while the request was out. `observedFactStore.ts`: tombstones are entity-wide (a hidden fact's key is never re-inserted for that entity from any scene; restore lifts it); `replaceSceneFacts` returns the affected entity ids. `entityStore.ts`: `createEntity(db, input, origin)`; a patch with name, fields, or body flips `ai` → `author`; `deleteEntity` records the dismissal for an AI-made entity or one with facts; an author create clears it. Channels `observedFact:listForEntity`, `observedFact:setHidden`; events `entity:changed`, `observedFact:changed { entityIds }` (`publishObservedFacts` in `handlers.ts`, plus `tag:changed`). Context: `StoryBibleFacts.entities` (`storyBibleEntities` in `context/storyBible.ts`: structured sheet fields, or a blank page as one `Notes` entry, then facts for attributes the sheet does not fill; sheets admitted before neighbour summaries, facts last) for the scene's tagged entities, no new prose prompt versions; `query.v2` adds a `Story bible:` block (`QUERY_BIBLE_ENTITIES` 6, `QUERY_BIBLE_TOKEN_BUDGET` 400; orientation only, never citable) for entities the question names and `QUERY_WEIGHTS.fact` 5 boosts their source scenes; a question naming no entity yields v1's messages. Renderer (`features/entities/`): `observedFactStore.ts` (by entity, re-reads held entities on the event, `resetObservedFactStore()`), `observedFactView.ts`, `ObservedFacts.tsx` (`role=region` "From the manuscript", `data-testid="observed-facts"`; `Go to passage in <scene>`, `Add to sheet` through the open draft store — `; ` in one-line fields, a new line in multiline ones, `Label: value` on a blank page —, `Hide` over every `factIds`, `Show hidden (n)` / `Restore`; `compact` in `ReferenceCard` = 3 rows and jumps only; not rendered with no facts), "Added by AI" in `EntityList` and the editor subtitle. Known gaps: the dismissal is keyed on kind + exact name key; the e2e covers only AI-created entities (the author-entity path is a handler test); live fidelity for `summary.v2`/`query.v2` not yet run.
- Background AI tagging (F-4.13): tags ride the summary request (no new feature id; toggle label "Scene summaries, story bible, and tags"; `FEATURE_BUDGETS.summary` 800). `src/shared/summary.ts` owns `SUMMARY_TAGS_MAX` 8, `SUMMARY_NEW_TAGS_MAX` 3, `SUMMARY_BANK_TAGS_MAX` 40, `AUTO_TAG_CATEGORIES` (character, setting, worldBuilding, tone, plotThread, custom — themes are `custom`; `content` is linked from the bank, never added to), `AUTO_TAG_BANK_CATEGORIES`, zod `ExtractedTag`. Schema (migration `0011_auto_tags`): `document_tag.source` and `tag.origin` (`author | ai`), `document_tag_dismissal` (`node_id` + `tag_id` PK, cascades both ways). `tag.origin` is main-only: the contract's `Tag` is unchanged; `documentTag:list` answers `DocumentTag` = `Tag` + `source`. Prompt `prompts/summary.v3.ts` = v2's rules with the tags clause before the reply shape, a `Tag bank:` line (tone, content, plotThread, custom names, most used first) between the known names and the scene line, v2's user turn; the bank line is outside `summarySource`'s hash on purpose (a new tone tag stales no scene). `src/main/ai/autoTags.ts`: `bankTagNames`, `applyAutoTags(tx, nodeId, tags, sceneText)` — a bank name is linked whatever category the model gave; a missing one is created with origin `ai` only in an `AUTO_TAG_CATEGORIES` category, at most 3 per run, never a name in the F-4.12b dismissed names, and a character/setting/worldBuilding name only when `sceneNamesTag` (`observedFacts.ts`; proper noun for a character) finds it in the scene as sent; no entity is created. `documentTagStore.ts`: `replaceAutoTags` makes the node's `ai` links exactly the answer (author links untouched, dismissed pairs skipped, answers the moved tag ids); `removeDocumentTag` records the dismissal for any link that existed; `addDocumentTag` lifts it and turns an `ai` link into the author's. `tagStore.ts`: `createTag(db, input, origin)`; an edit of name, category, color, or parent flips `ai` → `author`; `deleteTag` of an AI-made tag appends its name to `DISMISSED_NAMES_KEY`. `summarize.ts` applies the tags in the summary transaction after the facts (a created name tag restamps the row's hash like a created entity), clears the `ai` links under `SUMMARY_TEXT_MIN`, and reports through `onTagsChanged`; `handlers.ts` `publishAutoTags` rescans, republishes proposals, and syncs spelling when a tag was created, emits `tag:changed` per moved tag and `documentTag:changed { nodeIds }`. Renderer: `documentTagStore.ts` holds `aiTagIdsByNode`, strips `source` before the bank merge, and has one `subscribe()` (opened in `App.tsx`) that reloads loaded nodes; `TagsPanel.tsx` chips carry `data-ai="true"` and the `tag-ai-mark` Sparkles ("Added by AI"). Known gaps: each run replaces the scene's AI links, so a tag the model stops naming goes; AI-made tags are not marked in the Tags tab; live fidelity for `summary.v3` not yet run; the e2e covers create, mark, remove, and no re-apply (the caps and the dismissed-name path are unit tests). Tag rule (2026-10-08): `src/shared/tagTerms.ts` owns `TermVerdict` (`name | term | unusual | ordinary | absent`), `TERM_PHRASE_MIN_REPEATS` 3, `termUses` (the name's words in order joined by space/apostrophe/hyphen, any case; a use is capitalised when every non-linking word opens with a capital; `opensSentence` from `proposedTags.ts` splits mid from start), `classifyTagTerm`, `mayAutoCreateTag` (name, term). `applyAutoTags` reads `manuscriptTexts(tx, { nodeId, text: sceneText })` (main `tag/proposedTags.ts`) lazily, only when a missing name is answered, and drops anything not `mayAutoCreateTag`. Since F-9.13 the call comes through `applyDerivedKnowledge` (`knowledge/derive.ts`), and the same rule decides whether a sheet `applyObservedFacts` creates for a new name gets its tag (`createEntity(..., { tag })`; a tag the bank already has is always linked).
- Consistency checker (F-13.4; feature id `continuity`, since F-14.7's voice report owns `consistency`): `src/shared/continuity.ts` owns the caps (`CONTINUITY_TEXT_MIN` 200, scene 20,000 chars, 6 findings, quote 240 / why 300 / fix 600, `CONTINUITY_REFS_TOKEN_BUDGET` 1,500, ref value 200), `ContinuityRef` (`sheet | fact | timeline`), `ContinuityFinding` (status `open | dismissed | applied`, origin `background | request`), and `continuityDedupeKey` (node, ref kind, entity, attribute, folded value). Tier `strong` on demand, `fast` in the background (tier is in the context hash); budgets 6,000 in / 800 out. Schema: migration `0012_continuity_findings` (`continuity_finding`, cascaded from node and entity; `ref_node_id` and `proposal_id` set null). Contract: `ai:continuity { nodeId, requestId }` → `AiContinuityResult` (`references: 0` = nothing to check against, no request), `continuity:list`, `continuity:settle { id, status: dismissed | applied }`, event `continuity:changed { nodeIds }` (also emitted by `tree:delete` and `entity:delete`, whose cascades remove findings). Main: `prompts/continuity.v1.ts` (sentinel `You are the continuity feature inside a novel-writing app.`; system = rules + the voice block (`voiceBlock` for the scene's POV; a fix is prose, AI rule 2), user = the scene brief (`sceneBriefBlock`), numbered references, the scene's timeline, the scene text; the hash covers brief and voice version; JSON `{ findings: [{ ref, quote, why, fix }] }`); `src/main/ai/continuity.ts`: `continuityRefs` (entities named in the scene via `entitiesNamedIn` or linked tags; sheet fields first, then `groupFacts` groups of other scenes, then the previous scene's timeline; dismissed references are not sent; facts are dropped first when over budget; a blank-template page is one `notes` reference; F-11.2b: for a structured character with a whole-number `born` in a scene whose linked event (`SceneMeta.eventId` on `getProjectTimeline`, never the free-text field) has a year, the `age` sheet reference is the computed `computedAgeValue` `"<age> at this scene (born B, scene year Y)"` ("not born yet …" when negative), replacing the static sheet age or added where it is blank, so other scenes' age facts are left out too; `ContinuityRefs.ages` carries the number), `localCandidates` (a fact of this scene whose entity + attribute differs in another scene or is filled on the sheet; with a computed age, an `age` fact is a candidate iff its first whole number differs from it or it has none), `candidateContext` (only the paragraphs holding candidate quotes), `parseContinuityAnswer` (a bad reference number, an unlocated quote, or a dismissed key is dropped and counted), `runContinuity` / `runBackgroundContinuity`, `storeContinuityRun` (on demand replaces every open row of the scene; the background run replaces only open `background` rows and removes rows whose quote left the scene), `settleContinuityFinding` (main is the one owner of the run's proposal: `accepted` / `acceptedPart` / `rejected` with its last open finding; the renderer never settles it). `continuityFindingStore.ts` holds the rows. The background hook sits after `summarizeScene` in the summary queue's `run` in `handlers.ts`, with a per-session memo of the last context hash per node (cleared on project change) so a save that changes no candidate paragraph asks nothing; every error of the check there is swallowed except a cancel (the summary is already stored, and the memo is not set, so the next run retries). `settleFinding` settles only an `open` row (NOT_FOUND otherwise), so a stale window cannot lift a tombstone. Fixes are scored with `scoreFix` (exported from `critique.ts`). Renderer: `features/ai/continuityStore.ts` (open findings by id, refetched on `continuity:changed`; `check`, `stop`, `settle`, `apply`), `ContinuityPanel.tsx` (`ContinuityButton` with the quiet count in the assistant header and the focus floating assistant; `ContinuityView` replaces the chat while pressed and shows the pending/Stop/error/outcome lines; the on-demand check is started by the Check consistency quick action, F-5.17, which replaced its `Check this scene` button), and `features/editor/applyFix.ts` + `FixDiff.tsx`, extracted from the critique panel and shared with it. Known gaps: a scene cut under 200 characters keeps its open findings until the next check; a card marked "passage has changed" stays so until a re-check; the candidate gate fires for almost any filled free-text sheet field (one ~400-token `fast` request per change to those paragraphs).
- Proofread (F-14.12; feature id `proofread`, tier `fast`, `minDial` 1, budgets 2,000 out / 8,000 in; no schema): `src/shared/proofread.ts` owns `PROOFREAD_KINDS` (+ labels), the caps (`PROOFREAD_TEXT_MIN` 20, `PROOFREAD_CHAR_BUDGET` 20,000, 30 fixes, quote 160 / fix 200, `PROOFREAD_MAX_CHANGED_WORDS` 6, `PROOFREAD_KEEP_WORDS_MAX` 200), zod `ProofreadFix` and `ProofreadScope` (`scene | selection`). Contract: `ai:proofread { nodeId, requestId, selection? }` → `AiProofreadResult` (`fixes`, `scope`, `truncated`, `dropped`, usage/cost/cached/model, `proposalId`). Main: `prompts/proofread.v1.ts` (sentinel `You are the proofreading feature inside a novel-writing app.`; system = rules + voice block + `Keep as written:` paragraph; user = the brief as context only, `Text to proofread:`, `Proofread the text.`), `src/main/ai/proofread.ts` `runProofread` (gate, NOT_FOUND/VALIDATION as critique, the selection or the saved scene through `fitSceneToBudget` with proofread's caps; hash covers text, scope, brief, keep words, voice version) and `parseProofreadAnswer` (drops and counts: fix equal to quote, quote not in the text sent, quote not exactly once in the saved scene — so the renderer's first-match `locateText` is right —, an overlap with an earlier fix, more than 6 changed words by `diffWords`, and a change that only removes keep words; sorted by position; each fix scored with `scoreFix`). Keep words: `src/main/spellcheck/projectWords.ts` (`projectNameWords` = entity and tag name words, `projectSpellingWords` = those + the dictionary; `syncSpelling` uses it too); the prompt lists names first, case-insensitively deduped, capped at 200, while the drop rule checks the whole list. Handler `ai:proofread` always creates one proposal (content = fixes JSON, flagged from any fix), even for an empty result. Renderer: `features/editor/proofreadStore.ts` (one session app-wide; `start(nodeId, editor)` sends `proofreadSelection(editor)` (a selection of 20+ characters through `captureRewriteText`, cut to the budget locally with `truncated` set) after a document flush; per-fix state `open | applied | rejected | stale`; `accept` through `applyFixToPassage`, `busy` toasts `REWRITE_BUSY_MESSAGE`; `acceptAll` stops at the first refusal; the proposal settles once — when no fix is open or on close — `accepted` / `acceptedPart` / `rejected` by the applied count; `resetProofreadStore()`) and `ProofreadPanel.tsx` (`section[aria-label="Proofread"]`, testids `proofread-*`, `FixDiff`/`OffVoiceFlag` reused), mounted by `DocumentEditor` under the critique panel (single-scene view only, not in focus mode). The pass is started by the Proofread quick action (F-5.17; the toolbar `ProofreadButton` is gone). Known gaps: a quote that crosses an inline tag may not locate and is dropped; live fidelity for `proofread.v1` not yet run.
- Edit passes (F-14.15; migration `0017_edit_passes`, feature id `editPass` at Ask, budgets 4,000 out / 7,000 in per request, prompt `editPass.v1` for every pass type): `src/shared/editPass.ts` owns the types (`EDIT_PASS_TYPES` developmental / line / copy / proofread / continuity / custom, labels, descriptions, `EDIT_PASS_TIER`: strong for developmental, line, custom), the caps (piece 12,000 chars, 40 items per request, quote 600, replacement 1,200, instruction 1,000, 2,000 scenes, 50 passes kept, 20 presets), `chunkText` (paragraph breaks, then sentence ends), `estimateEditPass` (chunks per scene from words × 5.7 chars, tokens × 1.35 + 1,200 overhead per chunk, output by `EDIT_PASS_OUTPUT_RATIO` capped per chunk, `priceFor`; no editor-fee comparison since 2026-10-07, AI-BILLING-SPEC C1), the built-in presets (`presetInstruction`), `EditPassPreset(s)` under settings key `editPassPresets`, and the zod `EditPassSummary` / `EditChange` / `EditPassDetail`. Main: `src/main/editPass/editPassStore.ts` (rows; `createPass` evicts beyond 50 but never a running pass, `interruptRunningPasses` on project open turns `running` into `cancelled` "Stopped when MythScribe closed.", `settleChanges` moves only pending rows, `proposalChangeCounts`, `passDetail` with titles, presets get/set); `src/main/ai/editPass.ts` `editScene` (saved scene as paragraphs joined by blank lines (`sceneTextOf`), voice block for line/copy/proofread/custom, keep list (`keepList` from proofread) for copy/proofread, `continuityRefs` lines for continuity — none means no request — pieces fitted to the input budget by halving, one request per piece with retries on RATE_LIMIT/NETWORK/PROVIDER after `JOB_BACKOFF_MS`, `parseEditChanges` (quote in the piece, exactly once in the scene, no line break in quote or replacement, no overlap across the scene's pieces, a proofread only `isCorrection`) / `parseEditNotes` (cited), `scoreFix` on every non-empty replacement, one proposal per scene) and `createEditPassRunner` (one pass at a time per session, reads `db()` per scene and stops silently when the project changed, gate re-checked before every scene, scene rows + progress written in one transaction per scene, cancel aborts the request in flight, `resume` runs the scenes not in `done_node_ids`). Handlers in `handlers.ts` (`editPass:*`; `editPass:settle` settles a scene's proposal once none of its changes is pending: accepted / acceptedPart / rejected, a stale change counts as not taken; `editPass:changed` to every window). Renderer: `features/editPass/editPassViewStore.ts` (the main-pane view, workspace or report; no imports, closed by `treeStore.select` and `entityStore.select` like the entity page), `editPassStore.ts` (passes, the open report's detail, pending changes per scene, presets; `start` flushes pending saves; a pass this window started opens its report when it finishes; `accept` applies through `applyEditChange` in the scene's registered live editor (one transaction per change, AI-origin marked from the change's proposal) or, with the scene closed, through `documentStore` load / edit / unload with `applyChangesToDoc`, then settles accepted or stale; never touches a scene in the running pass; `jump` selects the scene and the hook highlights the change or selects a note's passage), `useTrackedChanges.ts` (per editor: fetch the scene's pending changes, draw them unless locked, route the inline buttons, settle orphaned ones stale), `TrackedChangesBar.tsx` (lock banner with Stop, or the count with Accept all / Reject all), `EditPassTab.tsx` (sidebar tab `edits`), `EditPassWorkspace.tsx`, `EditPassReport.tsx`. `src/renderer/features/editor/trackedChanges.ts` is the Tiptap extension (manuscript documents): `setTrackedChanges`, `focusTrackedChange`, inline `.tracked-del` + widget `.tracked-ins` with `.tracked-accept` / `.tracked-reject`, found again with `locateAll` (one scan) on every document change, each orphaned id reported once through `storage.onStale`. `DocumentEditor` sets the editor read-only (`setEditable(…, false)`, no update event) while the scene is in the running pass. Known gaps: the lock is the renderer's (another window or a project-wide replace can still write the scene; the change is then out of date, never misapplied); no entry from the AI assistant panel; live fidelity for `editPass.v1` not run. One at a time (2026-10-08): `editPassStore.review` (`EditReviewSession { changes (pending tracked changes only, a snapshot whose statuses `applySettled` keeps current), titles, skipped, currentId, nodeId }`), `startReview(changes, titles)` (a toast when none is pending), `reviewAt(id)` (the deck's `onCurrent`: `jump(change, { quiet: true })`, so `useTrackedChanges` selects and scrolls without focusing the editor), `skipInReview`, `endReview`; `TrackedChangesBar` renders `EditReviewStrip` (testids `edit-review`, `edit-review-change[data-status]`, `edit-review-diff`, `edit-review-close`) instead of itself when `review.nodeId` is its scene (a deck per mounted scene, `initialId` = `currentId`), and gains `tracked-changes-review`; `EditPassReport` gains `edit-report-review`. A on the deck = `accept` (applies at once), R = `reject`, S = `skipInReview`.
- What comes next / quick actions (F-5.17), main side (feature id `whatNext`, tier `fast`, JSON, `minDial` 1, budgets 300 out / 4,000 in; no schema): `src/shared/whatNext.ts` owns the caps (`WHAT_NEXT_TEXT_MIN` 40, `WHAT_NEXT_CHAR_BUDGET` 6,000, 3 directions, title 80 / text 300) and zod `WhatNextDirection`. Contract `ai:whatNext { nodeId, requestId, before? }` → `AiWhatNextResult` (`directions`, `dropped`, usage/cost/cached/model, `proposalId`). Main (since F-14.13 the feature sends `whatNext.v2`, v1 plus the scene steer): `prompts/whatNext.v1.ts` (sentinel `You are the what-comes-next feature inside a novel-writing app.`; system = rules + story bible; user = the brief, `Text so far:`, `Suggest three directions for what comes next.`; no voice block: directions are advice), `src/main/ai/whatNext.ts` `runWhatNext` (gate first, NOT_FOUND/VALIDATION via `getDocumentContent`, VALIDATION outside the manuscript or under 40 chars; `before` (text up to the selection's end) or the saved scene, `tailText` cuts from the front at a word boundary behind `…`, `fitTailToBudget` shrinks 500 chars at a time; hash covers text, brief, bible) and `parseWhatNextAnswer` (PROVIDER for non-JSON or no `directions` array; trims, cuts to the caps, drops and counts the wrong shape, blanks, and past the third). Handler `ai:whatNext` creates one proposal per run (content = directions JSON, pending, never flagged). Eval cases fresh/maxed under scoring kind `whatNext`. The e2e fake in `e2e/smoke.spec.ts` recognises the sentinel.
- Quick actions, renderer (F-5.17): `src/shared/quickActions.ts` owns the table (`QUICK_ACTION_IDS`, `QUICK_ACTIONS` label/feature/tier/description), `recapQuestion`, `WHAT_NEXT_QUESTION`, `directionMessage`, and `quickActionReason` (dial → toggle → scene → length → busy). `features/ai/QuickActions.tsx` is the row (`group[aria-label="Quick actions"]`, testids `quick-action-whatNext|proofread|continuity|recap`, the tier as a suffix, the reason in `title`, `quick-action-reason` under the row only when all four are off), mounted by `AssistantBody` above both the chat and the Continuity view; `useOpenScene` (live active editor, manuscript document, `useEditorState` length) moved here from `CheckScene`. Busy: proofread running, focus mode, or the active editor not the selected node (a stacked region has no proofread panel); continuity running; the active conversation pending for whatNext/recap. Clicks: Proofread → `useProofreadStore.start`; Check consistency → `useContinuityStore.check` + `setViewOpen(true)`; the other two close the Continuity view and call `assistantStore.whatNext()` / `recap()`. `assistantStore`: `send(message, override?: { mode?, pinActive? })` (the override mode is this turn's only; `pinActive` rides on `ai:query`), `whatNext()` (flushes the document store, `before` = `textBetween(0, selection.to)` tail-sliced to `WHAT_NEXT_CHAR_BUDGET` only with a selection; tracked as `whatNext`; the assistant turn is mode `plan` with `content = directionsText(...)` and `directions`; failures settle like `sendQuery`), `recap()` (Query turn, `recapQuestion(selection)`, `pinActive: true`), `writeDirection(d)` (Author send of `directionMessage(d)`); `finishTurn` fills the last assistant turn for all paths. `AssistantPanel.tsx` `Directions` renders `what-next-direction` cards with `what-next-write` (Write this), off with the reason when the chat toggle is off, the dial is below `ghostText.minDial`, there is no editor, or the conversation is pending. Since 2026-10-06 (all AI in the assistant): `QuickActions.tsx` is deleted: its table and rule live on in `features/ai/aiActions.ts` (`AI_ACTIONS`, ten actions, `aiActionReason` over `featureActionReason` from `src/shared/quickActions.ts`, `useOpenScene`, `openSceneNow`, `startSceneAction`, `openAssistant`, `openNotes`) and `AiActionsMenu.tsx` (`ai-actions` button in the panel header, the items through the shared `ContextMenu`, whose `MenuItem` gained `title`); the busy rules dropped the focus-mode and stacked-region cases, since the results now render in the assistant.
- Chat router and side-panel suggestions (F-5.19, F-5.20), main side (no schema; the renderer is the next bullet): feature ids `route` ("Assistant routing", `fast`, JSON, Ask, 120 out / 1,200 in), `synopsis` (350 / 4,000) and `notesSuggest` (600 / 6,000), both Ask, `fast`, JSON. `src/shared/assistantRoute.ts` owns `ROUTE_ACTIONS` (`chat | query | rewrite | critique | betaReader | proofread | continuity | whatNext | synopsis | notes`), the caps (message 1,000, 2 turns × 300, selection preview 200, title 100, instruction = `PROPOSAL_NOTE_MAX`), `settleRoute` (rewrite needs a selection, every scene action an open document, else `chat`) and `localRoute` (no letters/digits → `chat`; exactly an action or quick-action id, any case, trailing punctuation ignored, `recap` → `query`; no request). `src/shared/sceneSuggest.ts` owns the suggestion caps (text min 100, scene 12,000 chars, 8 points × 200, focus 500, current notes 1,500) and the panel caps the chat/query read (`PANEL_SYNOPSIS_CHARS` 1,000, `PANEL_NOTES_CHARS` 1,500). Contract: `ai:route { nodeId|null, message, history, selection: {text ≤ 200}|null, requestId }` → `AiRouteResult` (`action`, `instruction|null`, `routedBy: local|model`, usage/cost/cached, `model|null`; no proposal); `ai:suggestSynopsis { nodeId, requestId }` → `AiSuggestSynopsisResult` (`synopsis`, `truncated`, usage/cost/cached/model, `proposalId`); `ai:suggestNotes { nodeId, requestId, instruction? }` → `AiSuggestNotesResult` (`points`, `dropped`, `truncated`, …, `proposalId`). Main: `prompts/route.v1.ts` (system = rules with the action list, the stable prefix; user = recent turns, `Open document: <level> "Title"`, the selection opening, the message), `src/main/ai/route.ts` (`runRoute`: gate first, then `localRoute`, then the request; `parseRouteAnswer` never throws: unreadable or unknown → `chat`, case-insensitive ids, instruction trimmed and capped); `prompts/synopsis.v1.ts` (user = stored summary + key points, the scene) and `prompts/notesSuggest.v1.ts` (system = rules + story bible; user = brief, summary, current notes, scene, focus); `src/main/ai/sceneSuggest.ts` (`runSuggestSynopsis` / `runSuggestNotes`: gate, manuscript document with ≥ 100 chars, `fitSceneToBudget` with the suggestion caps; `parseSynopsisAnswer` / `parseNotesSuggestAnswer`, PROVIDER on a bad shape). The handlers record one pending proposal each (content = the synopsis / the points JSON) and write nothing to the panel. The side panel in prompts: `src/main/ai/context/scenePanel.ts` `buildScenePanel(db, nodeId)` (null for no node, a section root, or an empty panel) feeds `chat.v5` / `chatRegen.v5` (the panel block right before `Active scene:`, both modes; v4 exactly with no panel; `renderScenePanel` and `SCENE_PANEL_HEADING` live there) and `query.v4` (the block plus `QUERY_PANEL_RULE` between the bible and the scenes; never a citation source; v3 exactly with no panel). Both callers add `panel` to the context hash; `AI_DATA_SHARING.chat` and `.query` name it. Eval: fresh/full/maxed per new version, scoring kinds `route` (expected action), `synopsis`, `notesSuggest`. Known gaps: live fidelity not run. Since P3 slice 4 `ai:route` serves story memory v1 only; v2 routes through `ai:navigate` (`navigator.v1`, the story-memory bullet above).
- All AI in the assistant, renderer (F-5.19, F-5.20; 2026-10-06): the toolbar's only AI control is `VibeWriteToggle`. `src/shared/chat.ts` adds `ChatMessage.action` (`RouteAction | null`, defaulted); its 2026-10-06 per-conversation `CONVERSATION_MODES` are legacy since 2026-10-07 (parsed, ignored), and it owns the mode policy: `agentAccessFor(mode)` (Plan read, else write), `appliesEditsItself(mode)` (Auto only), `PLAN_ROUTE_ACTIONS` (chat, query, whatNext, betaReader) and `routeActionFor(mode, action)` (any other pick in Plan becomes chat). `src/shared/assistantRoute.ts` adds `ROUTE_ACTION_LABEL` and `routeActionFeature(action, memory)` (the feature a pick runs as; `ROUTE_ACTION_FEATURE` is module-private since F-16.25). `assistantStore.send` routes every turn in the mode read at send time (`chatModeNow`; an override `{ agent: access }` from the recap or Write this skips the router): `routeTurn` (track `route`, `ai:route` with the open scene, the last `ROUTE_HISTORY_TURNS`, and the selection's first `ROUTE_SELECTION_PREVIEW_CHARS`; DISABLED → chat, CANCELLED silent, other failures settle like chat) → `dispatchRoute` on the same turn pair (`patchLastTurn` sets `action` and `mode`; chat/query/whatNext get a fresh request id through `nextRequest` and run `runAgentTurn` (chat with `agentAccessFor(mode)`, query read-only) / `runWhatNext`; rewrite → `rewriteReason` then `rewriteStore.start(..., instruction)`; the scene actions → `aiActionReason` then `startSceneAction`, whose notice is the turn's text with the router's cost). `attachment` / `attach` (trimmed, cut to `ATTACHMENT_MAX` 1,000) / `detach`: a plain send consumes it through `withAttachment` (`message\n\nPassage:\n"…"`, within `CHAT_MESSAGE_MAX`). `AssistantPanel` (polish, 2026-10-06): the header is the dock controls and the title only (`AiActionsMenu` and the header Continuity button are gone; `useAiActionReasons` went with the menu); `ConversationTabs` ends with `NewConversationButton` (the floating window no longer carries title-bar actions) and each tab's close button is `opacity-0` until hover or keyboard focus; the Composer is `SuggestionLine` (`assistant-suggestion`; `src/shared/assistantSuggestions.ts` `assistantSuggestions(context, seed)` is the pure picker, `SUGGESTION_ROTATE_MS` 6 s, 300 ms fade, held while the draft is non-empty or the line is hovered or focused, no timer under prefers-reduced-motion where each message-box focus advances it; reads the active editor's selection through `useEditorState`, the held `sceneMeta`/`notes` records, and the entity bank), then the message box with Send/Stop inside it, then the compact `Mode` radiogroup (`MODE_RADIO`); `ContinuityLink` (`continuity-button`, `continuity-count`) renders under `AiResults` only while findings exist or the view is open; `BriefDraftButton` (`draft-brief`) beside the Brief disclosure starts `briefDraftStore` and opens the assistant. `AssistantBody` renders `AiResults` (rewrite, critique, proofread, beta reader, brief draft panels for their session's node, the active editor when it holds that node, `max-h-[55%]` scrolling, `ai-results`) above the chat or the Continuity view; `Turn` shows `chat-turn-action` for a non-chat action; the Composer shows `assistant-attachment` with Remove passage and focuses the message box when a passage arrives. Editor: `SelectionBubble.tsx` (`selection-bubble` toolbar, `selection-rewrite`, `selection-ask`; shown while the editor is focused and editable with a non-empty selection; positioned each render from `coordsAtPos(from)` above the line, under it near the window top, clamped into the window; re-rendered on scroll), `selectionActions.ts` (`selectionOffer`, `rewriteSelection`, `askAboutSelection`), and `DocumentEditor`'s range menu gains Rewrite / Ask AI.
