Notes column: `sceneSuggestStore.ts` (per node and kind, `Suggestion<T>` pending/ready/error, `suggestSynopsis`, `suggestNotes(nodeId, instruction)`, `cancel`, `acceptSynopsis` through `patchSceneMeta`, `addNotes(nodeId, picked)` flushes the notes store, reads `notes:get`, saves `appendNotePoints` ("• " paragraphs; no lists or AI-origin mark in the notes schema), and `reload`s a loaded record; `dismiss`), `SceneSuggestions.tsx` (`SuggestButton` with `suggest-synopsis` / `suggest-notes`, `compact` in the Notes heading; `SynopsisSuggestion` under the Synopsis box; `NotesSuggestion` over the notes with `suggested-note` checkboxes); `sceneMetaStore.patchSceneMeta(id, patch)` edits a held record or loads, edits, and releases one. e2e: the fake server answers `route.v1` (critique for `ROUTE_CRITIQUE_MESSAGE`, else chat), `synopsis.v1`, and `notesSuggest.v1`; the steps send exact action names in Ask (`sendRouted`: "What next?", "Proofread", "Beta reader", "Continuity", routed locally with no router request), click the suggestion line once, use the bubble, Draft beside the Brief, and one model-routed Auto turn; the typewriter step checks the caret after every line.
- "Make this scene better" routes to edit passes (F-5.26, 2026-10-11; plan `plan-improve-scene.md`; migration `0027_edit_pass_excerpt`): the router is `route.v2` (`src/main/ai/prompts/route.v2.ts`, v1's user turn under new rules; proofreading left the action list, it is the proofread pass now). `src/shared/assistantRoute.ts` adds the `editPass` action and `RouteEdit` (`pass`: `ask` | an `EditPassType` | `dialogue`; `target`: selection / scene / chapter; `chapter`: the name the author gave); `settleRoute` completes it (missing → ask on the selection or scene; selection without one → scene; custom without instruction → ask; no document and no named chapter → chat) and turns a `rewrite` without a selection into an edit pass on the scene. `parseRouteAnswer` (`src/main/ai/route.ts`) reads the extra fields leniently; `ai:route` answers `edit`. `src/shared/editRequest.ts` owns the chat's choices (`EDIT_ASK_CHOICES` Tighten prose = line, Pacing & structure = developmental, Dialogue = custom with the built-in dialogue preset, Proofread, Editor's notes = critique), `choicePass`, `findChapter` (title, or number as reading-order place), and `EditTurn`, stored as `ChatMessage.edit` (defaulted). Renderer: `features/ai/editRequest.ts` resolves the target (selection ≥ `EDIT_PASS_TEXT_MIN` and ≤ `EDIT_PASS_EXCERPT_MAX` else the scene; a chapter's documents via `descendantDocuments`), gates a choice (`featureActionReason('editPass')` + one pass at a time; critique through `aiActionReason` on its open scene), quotes it, and starts it through `useEditPassStore.start` (with `excerpt` for a selection) or `startSceneAction('critique')`. `assistantStore.dispatchRoute` case `editPass` → `runEditRequest` (ask: the question + chips; specific: starts at once unless blocked or over the hosted confirm threshold); `pickEdit` runs a chip (refused in Plan; Plan never reaches it from the router since `editPass` is not in `PLAN_ROUTE_ACTIONS`). `EditRequestCard.tsx` renders the chips with each pass's estimate (`features/editPass/passQuote.ts`, shared with the Edits workspace: `usePassModel`, `quotePass`, `estimateLine`, `confirmLine`), then the pass's status and Review the changes (`editPassStore.reviewPass` → the review deck) or Open the report for a developmental pass. Main: `edit_pass.excerpt` (nullable) holds the selected passage; `editScene` cuts the pieces from it (quotes must still occur once in the whole scene); `EditPassSummary.excerpt` says so. Known gap: the chat agent (agent.v8) still answers improvement requests the router calls chat with scattered edits; teaching it to hand them over waits for agent.v9 (QUESTIONS.md). In story memory v2 `navigator.v1`'s first step carries the same action and edit-pass fields (`ROUTE_V2_ACTION_LINES`, `ROUTE_V2_EDIT_FIELDS`), settled by the same `settleRoute`/`routeForMemory`, so the quick picks behave the same (P3 slice 4).
- Chat agent (F-5.22; no schema; feature id `agent`, strong, JSON, Ask, 1,500 out / 12,000 in per step): `src/shared/agent.ts` owns the caps (`AGENT_MAX_STEPS` 6, `AGENT_COST_CAP_USD` 0.5, `AGENT_READ_CHARS` 6,000, `AGENT_RESULT_CHARS` 6,000, `AGENT_SEARCH_RESULTS` 8, `AGENT_CARET_CHARS` 1,500, `AGENT_SELECTION_CHARS` 2,000, `AGENT_NOTES_CHARS` 1,500, `AGENT_MAX_EDITS` 8, `AGENT_MAX_CITATIONS` 6), `AgentAccess` read | write, `AgentTool`, `AgentStep { tool, label }`, the resolved `AgentEdit` union (text, insert, synopsis, notes, sheet, create, rename, move, split, merge, tag, delete), `AgentChange { id, edit, status pending|applied|skipped|undone|failed, violation, error }`, `AgentTurn { access, steps, changes }` (on `ChatMessage.agent`, defaulted null), `isDeletion`, `editProse`, `describeEdit`, `AgentFocus`. Main: `prompts/agent.v1.ts` (`AGENT_RULES` opens with the e2e sentinel; `AGENT_EDIT_RULES` for write runs; system = rules, edit rules, voice block (write only), `renderAgentFocus` of the open document or "No document is open."; then history, the message, each step as the model's call (assistant) and `Result of <tool>:` (user); `AGENT_FINAL_TURN` after the caps), `agentTools.ts` (`loadAgentProject` refs `n1…` in tree order, `nodeByRef`, `sheetByName`, `runAgentTool` (never throws on the model's mistakes), `occurrencesOf`, `resolveAgentEdit` against the project now: a passage must occur exactly once by `normalizeForMatch`), `agent.ts` (`runAgent`: gate, focus, loop of `runAiRequest` steps registering `agentStepRequestId(id, n)` under the run's own inflight id so `ai:cancel` stops the step in flight, `fitAgentPrompt` drops the oldest results then history, `parseAgentReply` lenient, `finishAnswer` keeps verified passage and sheet citations (`QueryTurn`), strips dangling markers, resolves and voice-checks edits). Handler `ai:agent { nodeId, message, history, access, focus, requestId }` → `AiAgentResult`, emits `ai:agentStep { requestId, step }` per lookup, one pending `agent` proposal with `{ answer, edits }`. Renderer: `assistantStore` subscribes to `ai:agentStep` (`agentSteps` by request id, not persisted), `runAgentTurn(id, requestId, access, autoApply, ask)` for the router's chat (`agentAccessFor(mode)`) and query (read) actions, the recap (read), and Write this (write, refused in Plan with `PLAN_NO_EDITS_MESSAGE`), auto-applies when the turn was sent in Auto every edit that is not a deletion and not flagged, `applyChange` / `applyAll` (never deletions) / `skipChange` / `undoChange` (undo closures in a module map, cleared with the store; `canUndoChange`), settles the proposal once no edit waits (all applied accepted, some acceptedPart, none rejected). `agentApply.ts` `applyAgentEdit(edit, proposalId)` applies through the owners (text via `editor/agentEditing.ts` in the live editor it opens with `editorFor`: `replacePassage`/`undoReplacePassage`, `insertParagraphs`/`undoInsertParagraphs`, `paragraphsFrom`/`cutFromParagraph`/`appendBlocks`, every change one transaction, new prose AI-origin marked; synopsis `patchSceneMeta`; notes read-modify-write with `appendNotePoints`; sheets `entityStore.update`; structure `treeStore.createIn` (new) / `rename` / `move` / `remove`; tags `documentTagStore` and `tagStore`). `locateUniqueText` in `locateText.ts`. `AgentTurn.tsx` (`AgentSteps` live or folded, `AgentChanges` cards with `FixDiff`, `agent-change` / `-apply` / `-skip` / `-undo`, `agent-apply-all`, `agent-step`). e2e: the fake answers `agent.v1` through `chatAgentReply` (search, read_scene, read_sheet, then the answer; a Write this direction gets one insert at the end of Scene 1); Plan questions check the agent requests carry no edit rules; the step after the sheet question applies and undoes an edit in Ask and in Auto. Known gaps: live fidelity not run; Undo is gone after a restart; `ai:query`/`query.v4` are no longer called by the panel. 2026-10-07 (insertions as ghost text, `plans/plan-ai-insert-speed.md`): the features send `agent.v2` (`prompts/agent.v2.ts`: no voice block; `AGENT_EDIT_RULES_V2` asks for a `brief` (+ `words`) instead of prose for `insert` and `text`; `AGENT_RETRY_TURN`; `AGENT_STEP_MAX_TOKENS` 1,500, `AGENT_RETRY_MAX_TOKENS` 3,000 = the feature cap). `runAgent(db, deps, input, onStep, hooks)` sends every step through `runAiStream`; `AnswerStream` pulls the `answer` string out of the streaming JSON for `hooks.answer` (→ `ai:agentDelta { requestId, delta, reset }`), `readAgentReply(text, finishReason)` tells a broken reply (`cutOff` | `unreadable`) from a plain-prose answer, one retry per step (`agentRetryRequestId`), then `AGENT_CUT_OFF_MESSAGE (Request <id>)`; `hooks.note` annotates the inspector. `resolveAgentEdit` keeps a drafted insert's `after` even when the scene lacks it; `draftWords` clamps 20–900. `AgentEdit.insert/text` gained `brief` (and `words`), `AgentChange` gained `proposalId`, `notice`, and the statuses `writing`/`shown` (`settledAfterReload` turns them back to pending), `isDraftIntent`/`isRewriteIntent`. `agentDraft.ts` `draftForAgent` behind `ai:agentDraft` (streams `ai:agentDraftDelta`): an insertion through `runChat` Author mode with `sceneText` (the text before the anchor) and `streamDraft`, a rewrite through `runRewrite` with the brief as the note; an empty draft is `AiCutOffError` / `AiFallbackError`; one proposal (`chat` or `rewrite`). Renderer: `draftLanding.ts` (`resolveInsertAnchor`, `landDraft` opens the scene with `openEditor`, pins the ghost (`setGhost(…, { at, pin })`, `streamGhost`), listens on `storage.ghostText.listeners`, auto-accepts in Auto unless flagged, settles the draft proposal; `acceptLanding`/`dismissLanding` for the card; `draftReplacement` for rewrites; `reportAiNote` → `devtools:aiNote`), `assistantStore` `writeIntents`/`writeIntent` after the turn (one at a time, not awaited by `send`), `agentAnswer`/`drafts` live state, `acceptChange`; `AgentTurn.tsx` `DraftCard` (Accept/Dismiss or Stop, `agent-change-accept`, `agent-change-draft`), `agent-change-brief`, `agent-change-notice`; the panel shows `chat-streaming` in place of Thinking…; Undo of an accepted insertion is `removeInsertedProse`. e2e: the fake streams agent steps and drafts; Write this lands at the caret and Tab accepts; an insertion after a named sentence (`AGENT_INSERT_MESSAGE`, `insertAnchor`) lands there, in the normal view and in focus mode.
- Story time (F-5.23; no schema, no channel): `src/shared/storyTime.ts` owns the progress rule (`SCENE_PROGRESS`, `sceneProgress(wordCount, status)`: no words = planned, Revised/Final = revised, else drafted; `folderProgress`), now (`resolveNow(documents, activeDocumentId)` → `{ nowId, basis: open | latest | none }`, `positionIn(order, nowId, id)` → earlier / now / later / null, `STORY_POSITION_NOTE`), and the map (`StoryMapItem`, `STORY_MAP_HEADING` — the golden tests and the e2e match it, and it contains "▶ NOW" itself, so match `] ▶ NOW` for the marker —, `STORY_MAP_LATEST_NOTE`, `STORY_MAP_NOTHING_WRITTEN`, `mapSummary` (first sentence, 120 chars), `renderStoryMap(items, now, maxTokens)`: every line first, then summaries now → earlier nearest → later nearest; over budget a window around now with "… N earlier/later scenes"; budgets `STORY_MAP_TOKEN_BUDGET` 1,200 (agent) and `STORY_MAP_SMALL_TOKEN_BUDGET` 400 (whatNext)). Main: `src/main/ai/context/storyTime.ts` `manuscriptRows(rows)` (needs the root rows: pass `listNodes`, never `nodesInTreeOrder`, which drops roots), `storyTime(db, activeId)` (a selected chapter → its last document), `positionNote`, `storyMapItems` (fulfilled planned scenes left out), `buildStoryMap`. `AgentProject.time` (`loadAgentProject(db, activeId)`); the tools label results: search lines `ref title (position): …` and `Sheets (SHEETS_ARE_PLANS)`, `read_scene` head `ref title (position), characters …` (same line, the e2e fake slices from line 2), `read_summary` head, `read_notes` head `NOTES_ARE_PLANS`, `read_sheet` first line, `outline` adds progress and `▶ NOW`. Prompts: `agent.v3` (`AGENT_TIME_RULES`; system = rules, time rule, edit rules v2, map, focus; `fitAgentPrompt` is generic over the input type and defaults to v3), `continuity.v2` (`continuityRefLineV2(ref, n, later)`: "sheet (notes and plans)", "an earlier scene" / "a later scene"; `continuityRefs` budgets on the v2 line; the hash includes the later flags), `whatNext.v3` (`WHAT_NEXT_TIME_RULE`, bible, small map), `notesSuggest.v2` (`NOTES_SUGGEST_TIME_RULE`); the last two render the bible with `STORY_BIBLE_PLANS_HEADING` (`renderStoryBible`/`buildStoryBible` take an optional heading). Data sharing: `STORY_MAP_SENDS` in the agent and whatNext entries. Eval: agent.v3 has a "before the war" case scored by `storyTime { forbidden }` (the answer must not state a death only the sheet plans). Not changed: `editPass.v1`'s continuity pass, the prose prompts, `query.v4` (unused).
- Lookup ladder (F-5.24, P4 of the knowledge model; no schema, no channel; prompt `agent.v6`): `src/shared/agent.ts` freezes each version's tools (`AGENT_TOOLS_V1` for v1–v4, `AGENT_TOOLS_V5` + `todo`, `AGENT_TOOLS_V6` = lookup, cards, find_passages, outline, read_scene, read_notes, read_sheet, list_sheets, tags, todo); `AgentTool`/`AGENT_TOOLS` is the union, so steps stored with older turns (`search`) still parse; caps `AGENT_LOOKUP_CHARS` 1,200, `AGENT_CARDS_MAX` 8, `AGENT_PASSAGE_RESULTS` 8. `runAgentTool(project, activeId, name, args, tools = AGENT_TOOLS_V6)` refuses a tool outside the list as an unknown tool; `runAgent` passes `AGENT_TOOLS_V6` and builds with `buildAgentPromptV6` (`fitAgentPrompt`'s default). Tools in `agentTools.ts`: `lookup` (`recordByName`: `sheetByName`, then the tag by name/alias → its record; head `Name (noun) [status]; also …; as of nX (now)`; fields via `sheetAt` at now with `entity.fields` as baseline, values that differ from it with their refs and `[plan]`/`[idea]`; relations from `listFactsForEntity` up to now both ways (`RELATION_LABEL`/`RELATION_INVERSE_LABEL`); threads via `threadsAtNow` (`deriveThreads` over facts at or before now); `Named in N scenes (M up to now): first nX ¶p, last …` from `listMentionsForTag` + `mentionParagraphsForTag` (new in `mentionStore.ts`); `Last seen` = `renderCard` of `sceneCardFor` the last naming scene up to now; `headTruncate` to the cap), `cards` (ids array or string, else the scenes naming a record in reading order; `renderCard(sceneHead, card)` or the summary's head or "No card yet"; `…and N more`), `find_passages` (`searchPassages(db, q, 8)`, `nX ¶p (position): snippet` with whitespace collapsed), `read_scene {para}` (`readParagraphs`: `passageParagraphs(documentJson(row))` from `para`, whole paragraphs up to `AGENT_READ_CHARS`, `(continues; read on with "para":n)`), `statusMark` (`[canon]`/`[plan]`/`[idea]`) on `read_sheet`'s head (`entity.status`) and `read_notes`'s head (`SceneMeta.notesStatus`). `SHEETS_ARE_PLANS` stays for v5's `search` only; `NOTES_ARE_PLANS` is gone. Prompt `agent.v6.ts`: `AGENT_RULES_V6` (v1's opening sentence, the e2e sentinel; the v6 tool list), `AGENT_STATUS_RULES` (v3's NOW rule with the status marks), `AGENT_LADDER_RULES` (the ladder, the snippet-quote rule, v5's todo rule), then v4's organise rule; order rules → status → ladder → organise → edit rules (write) → map → focus. Renderer: `NotesStatusSelect` (MetadataPane.tsx, `aria-label` "Notes status") beside the Notes heading in `NotesPanel`, through `useSceneMetaStore`. Eval: agent.v6 fresh / lookup / fact / plan (`storyTime` forbidden) / maxed / retry, and an agent.v5 `fact` baseline. e2e: the fake agent's general first step is `find_passages`; `LOOKUP_QUESTION` runs lookup → find_passages → an answer citing the snippet (`lookupQuote`), and the citation's click selects it. Known gaps: live fidelity for agent.v6 not run; the savings are fixture numbers (answer step −44 %, three-request run −25 %), not the ledger's.
- Clear the story bible (F-5.25; no schema; channel `bible:clear` (write); prompt `agent.v7`): `src/shared/bibleClear.ts` owns `ClearGroup` (sheets | tags | library | notes), `ClearOption` (one card line: group, id, label, count, the `ids` it covers (≤ `CLEAR_IDS_MAX` 5,000), checked), `ClearSelection` (ids per group: main deletes only what the card listed and still exists, never what was added since), `clearSelectionOf`, `tickedCounts`, `describeClearCounts`, `clearLabel`, and `ClearSnapshot` (rows per table of `CLEAR_TABLES` as SQLite holds them, `sheetTags`, `tagParents`, `entityRefs`, `notes`, `tagAliases`, `counts`). `AgentEdit` gains `clear { options }` (`isDeletion`, so it asks in every mode and is never in Apply all); `changes.ts` gains kind `clear` and inverse `restoreCleared { counts, snapshot? }` (only main writes it; `RECORDED_UNDO_OF.clear` is empty; `UNDO_ORDER.clear` 5, last, so what the turn did after the clear unwinds first; `pruneChanges` never deletes an applied `clear` row; `listChanges` reads `LISTED_COLUMNS`, which strips `$.snapshot` in SQL (`json_remove`, guarded by `json_valid`), so a page never parses a snapshot; `undoRow` refuses a `restoreCleared` without one). The chat's `tagMany` logs a tag it had to make as kind `tag` with inverse `removeMadeTag { tagId, modified }` (`changes:record` checks `noteCreatedTag`, called by `tag:create`, and the stamp; the undo refuses an edited or still-used tag), so "clear the tags, then tag every scene #draft" undoes whole. Main: `agentTools.resolveClear` reads `sheets`/`tags` ("all" or names, lenient: id, name, noun, plural) and `library`/`notes` (true) into `ClearWanted`; `knowledge/bibleClear.ts` `clearOptions(db, wanted)` (each kind the project has, empty ones left out, ticked where named; the `CLEAR_OPTIONS_MAX` cap falls on sheet categories only, so tags, Library, and notes lines always show) and `clearStoryBible(db, selection, runId, now)` (one transaction: capture the rows (`clearSnapshot.captureRows`, generic `SELECT *` with `IN` chunks of 500), `deleteEntity` per sheet, `deleteTags`, delete `context_file` rows, `saveNotes(EMPTY_DOC)`, put the dismissed lists back as they were, log one row via `changeLog.logChangeEntries`). `knowledge/clearSnapshot.ts` `restoreCleared` (called by `undoRow`): `assertRestorable` refuses whole when a tag name, a sheet of the same kind and name key, or an upload name exists again, or a cleared document has notes; `PRAGMA defer_foreign_keys`; columns filtered by `pragma_table_info`; a row whose scene is gone is skipped, a reference to something gone is nulled (`REFS`); then sheet tags, tag parents, log/To do references, notes, merge aliases. Handler `bible:clear` calls `backups.backupNow()` first, then emits `mention:changed`/`documentTag:changed` (link nodes), `continuity:changed`, `fact:changed`, `changes:changed`, `publishProposed`, `syncSpelling`, `queueTodo`; `UndoOutcome` gains `restoredLibraryIds`/`restoredNotesNodeIds`, and a restore adds its sheets to `entityIds` (so `fact:changed`). Renderer: `agentApply` `clear` flushes the sheet page and notes, invokes `bible:clear` with the message id as run, closes a removed sheet's page, `forgetRecords` + `refreshLibraryAndNotes` (both in `changesStore.ts`, also run by any undo of a `clear` row), and answers the log's undo; `assistantStore.tickClearOption`; `AgentTurn.tsx` `ClearCard` (`agent-clear-option` checkboxes grouped by `CLEAR_GROUPS`, Delete with the ticked counts, disabled with none, Keep everything). Prompt `agent.v7`: v6's rules + `AGENT_BULK_RULES` (delete/clear/remove/wipe all → one clear; story bible / start fresh = sheets, tags, library; without the edit list say what would go; scenes and chapters never in bulk; unclear noun → ask) + `AGENT_ORGANISE_RULES_V7` (judgment tidying only, never bulk deletes) + `AGENT_EDIT_RULES_V7` (v2's + the clear line). Eval: agent.v7 clear (scored `agentEdit` expected `clear`), clearPlan, fact, maxed (110 tokens of headroom), retry. Audit fixes 3, 4, 6 in the same version: `AgentEdit` gains `tagMany { nodes, tag, add }`, `moveMany { nodes, parentId, parentTitle }`, `tagRename`, `sheetPatch { sheets[{entityId,name,kind}], rename, to, toName }`, `sheetMerge` (in `isDeletion`), `sheetCreate`, capped by `AGENT_BULK_MAX` 200 (the edit cap counts proposals, not items). Resolvers in `agentTools.ts`: `tag` with `ids` ("all" = the manuscript's documents via `listNodes`, since the project's rows drop the section roots; a folder ref = its documents; only documents the tag would change) → `resolveTagMany`; `move` with `ids` or `from` → `resolveMoveMany` (never a folder into itself); `rename_tag`, `rename_sheet`, `recategorise` (`sheets` or `from`, `to`; refuses a name clash in the target), `merge_sheets` (`withText` from `organiseProject.isEmptySheet`; `organiseProject` imports `agentTools` too, function-only cycle), `create_sheet`; `list_sheets` with `field` (+ `empty`) → `listSheetField` (`AGENT_RULES_V7` = v6's rules with that line). Renderer `agentApply`: `tagMany` (each link through `documentTagStore`, put back on a mid-way failure, logged in one `changesStore.logAppliedChanges` call (pages of 100), Undo = each row's undo newest first), `moveMany` (each to the end of the folder, Undo moves them back newest first; a failure part-way moves back what moved, then rethrows; `sheetPatch` likewise runs the collected undos), and the story-bible kinds through `organiseApply.applyOrganiseAction` (`tag`, `sheet` per sheet, `mergeSheets`, `createSheet`; `agentApply` ↔ `organiseApply` is a function-only import cycle); `AgentTurn` `NameList` (`agent-change-items`, first 8 names). Known gaps: pictures and uploaded originals of cleared sheets and uploads stay on disk (for the Undo) and are never cleaned up; the Undo is refused once anything of the same name exists again; live fidelity for agent.v7 not run.
- Chat tools, agent.v8 (F-5.25 audit fixes 5, 7, 8; no schema; no new channel): `src/main/ai/prompts/agent.v8.ts` is v7 with only the write edit list changed (`AGENT_EDIT_RULES_V8`: the notes line gains `"text":""` clears, plus `AGENT_EDITS_V8`), so a read run is v7's bytes; `runAgent`/`fitAgentPrompt` build v8. `src/shared/agent.ts` `AgentEdit` gains `notesClear`, `status` (`target` record | facts | notes, `items` = each target's status before), `todo` (`items` id + subject, `status` done | dismissed), `undoTurn`, `summaries`, `open` (`dialog` upload | library); `isDeletion` adds `notesClear` and `undoTurn`; `isOpenAction`. Main `agentTools.ts`: the `todo` tool numbers the open items across the whole list (`t1`…; `AgentProject.todo` caches the order for the run, `todoInOrder`), `resolveTodoSettle` reads `t2`, `T2`, or `2`; `resolveStatus` (no `name`: a document's `notesStatus`; no `field`: the record; else the visible field facts of that attribute, or relationship facts whose type is the field word, narrowed by `value` words, only those that would change); `notes` with `text:""` resolves to `notesClear` (refused on empty notes). `src/shared/changes.ts` adds the undo `restoreStatus { entityId, facts: [{id, before}], before, after }` (kinds `record` with no facts, `fact` with facts); `changeLog.ts` checks it landed (`statusOfFact`) and undoes only while every target still reads `after`, else VALIDATION. Renderer `agentApply`: `notesClear` through `rewriteNotes` (Undo while still empty), `status` (`applyStatus`: notes via `patchSceneMeta`, record via `entityStore.update`, facts via `factStore.setStatus`, both logged), `todo` via `todoStore.settle` (Undo `todoStore.reopen`, which skips `c:` contradictions), `summaries` via `indexingStore.queueAll` (the toast-free `indexAll`; throws the gate's message), `open` (`openPlace`: leaves focus mode, Library section or `libraryStore.add()` unawaited); `undoTurn` is the assistant store's own (`undoLastTurn`: the latest earlier turn with applied changes that still have in-memory undoers, newest first; none throws). `runAgentTurn` applies `open` changes at once in Ask too. Known gaps: undo-the-last-turn cannot reach turns from before a restart (the undoers live in memory); live fidelity for agent.v8 not run.
- Ms Scribe in story memory v1 (notes redesign phase 1, slice 2; plan `docs/research/notes-redesign-2026-10-15/phase1.md`; no migration; feature id `scribe`, strong, JSON, 3,000 out per step (6,000 the retry), 140,000 in; prompt `scribe.v5` since 2026-10-16 (notes structure part C, `structure.md` §C: v4's request with `SCRIBE_V5_STRUCTURE_RULES` after v4's rules and `SCRIBE_V5_MODE_RULES` naming them, built from v4's by swapping the rules and the mode line), `scribe.v4` since the notes fixes of 2026-10-15 (`fixes-1.md` §2: v3's request with one rule line, `SCRIBE_V4_TEMPLATE_RULE`, built from v3's by swapping the rules), `scribe.v3` before (`vibe.md` part B: v2's rules plus every tool in all modes, the mode's line last in `# Now`, so switching mode keeps the provider's cached prefix; `scribe.v1`–`scribe.v3` kept catalogued)). Templates known before she writes: `source.ts` `folderTemplatesOf` (each `templatedFolders` entry with its path and headings: a built-in's, or `templateSections` of the author's page) → `ScribeMaterial.folderTemplates` → `buildScribeContext`'s `folderTemplates`, which `folderTemplatesBlock` writes once at the end of the notes block ("Folder templates:" then `Characters → template: Role, …`; at most `SCRIBE_TEMPLATE_FOLDERS_MAX` folders and `SCRIBE_TEMPLATE_SECTIONS_MAX` headings each); the edit flow's notes block (`buildNotesBlock`) does not carry it; `create_note`'s layout stays the backstop. Modes: `src/shared/aiSettings.ts` `ASSISTANT_MODES` are Brainstorm / Write / Vibe; `AssistantMode` reads a stored `plan` as brainstorm and `ask`/`auto` as write (`migrateAssistantMode`, in code); `agentAccessFor` (brainstorm read), `appliesEditsItself` (vibe only: Organise, plan links, the v1 agent's Write this and story memory v2 keep Auto's behaviour there); picking Vibe in `AssistantModeControl` also sets `ghostText.enabled`. `src/shared/scribe2.ts` owns the caps (`SCRIBE_WHOLE_BOOK_TOKENS` 60,000, `SCRIBE_PICK_BOOK_TOKENS` 24,000, `SCRIBE_FORCED_BOOK_TOKENS` 80,000, `SCRIBE_NOTES_TOKENS` 30,000, …), the tools, `scribeAccess(mode, message)` (Brainstorm writes notes only when `wantsSave`, never prose), `scribeProseMax(mode)` (`SCRIBE_VIBE_PROSE_MAX` 3 in Vibe, else `SCRIBE_MAX_WRITES`) and `ScribeSources`. Main `src/main/scribe2/`: `source.ts` (`loadScribeSources`: notebook pages, then, only while the old story bible is switched back on (fresh start), story-bible sheets and Library uploads as read-only pages, refs `p1…`; manuscript scenes in reading order under the agent's `n…` refs; names), `context.ts` (`nameCount`/`namesIn` and `pageNames`/`pagesNamedIn`, the one name matcher, shared with the v1 story-bible block; pure `buildScribeContext`: notes block (past the budget the page list and the pages named first), book map (synopsis, else the stored summary; notes), the whole book when the whole request is estimated under the line or Whole book is on, else `pickScenes` (question names, full-text hits, neighbours, names in the open scene, within the budget), the moment), `tools.ts` (`search_book`, `open_scene`, `list_notes`, `open_note`; `edit_note` whole/section/append and `create_note` write through `notebookStore` with `'ai'` (the version kept is the Undo), `propose_prose_edit` through `offerEdit`, refused past `ScribeRun.proseMax`; changes become `AgentEdit` `notebook` (applied) or `text` (pending)), `run.ts` (`runScribe2` over `runAgentLoop`, the chat agent's loop extracted in `agent.ts` and shared by `runAgent`; `answerSources` keeps each source's quotes (`scribe.v2`: `{"ref","quote"}`) that `quotes.ts` `checkQuote` finds in the scene or page text, normalized as `locateText` matches, and drops the rest; `readableAnswer` writes refs cited together as places: a scene as its chapter ("the chapter Blades of Alandor", a folder called "Chapter 3" as itself, a scene outside a chapter by its title), one chapter cited twice once, a page by its title, a bracketed ref after its own full title replacing it). Channel `ai:scribe` (v1 only) emits `ai:agentStep`/`ai:agentDelta` and `notebook:changed`, one `scribe` proposal. Renderer: `dispatchRoute` sends a v1 `chat`/`query` pick to `runScribeTurn` (recap and Write this still run the agent); prose suggestions never apply on their own; a `notebook` change's Undo is stored with it (`undoNotebookEdit`: `notebook:restoreVersion`, or `notebook:delete` for a page she made), so it outlives a restart; `ChatMessage.sources` draws `ScribeSourceList` (a scene opens in the editor at its first quote through `openScene` → `openPassage(…, { centre: true })`, the passage selected and centred two frames on (`centreOnceLaidOut`, `centreCaret`'s `at`); further quotes as numbered links; a note page through `openNotebookPage(id, quote)` in `src/renderer/features/notebook/openPage.ts`, which `NotebookPageView` selects and centres once loaded (`takeNotebookQuote`); a sheet on its page); `WholeBookToggle` beside the modes (session only). e2e: `e2e/scribe.spec.ts`. Vibe (`scribe.v3`): she proposes up to three prose changes unasked, each a pending card in the chat, each with its reason in the answer; Write proposes only when asked; Brainstorm's write tools are refused in `tools.ts`. Ghost text keeps its length cap in Vibe (decided by Claude: a longer continuation is Draft with AI's job). Prose suggestions as tracked changes (notes fixes 2026-10-15, `fixes-1.md` §1; no migration): `src/main/scribe2/tracked.ts` `trackScribeProposals` writes a turn's waiting `text` suggestions as pending `edit_change` rows (rationale `SCRIBE_TRACKED_RATIONALE`, the turn's proposal for provenance) of one hidden `edit_pass` row per turn (`editPassStore.ts` `createScribePass`: `flow` = `SCRIBE_PASS_FLOW`, status done; `isScribePass`; left out of `editPass:list`, `flowRows` and `evictPasses`' count, own cap `SCRIBE_PASS_RETENTION_MAX` 200); the handler returns each change's `trackedId` (`AiScribeResult`, stored on `AgentChange.trackedId`). The editor draws them through `editPass:changes` like any pass change; `editPass:settle` does not settle the turn's proposal for her rows and emits `scribe:trackedSettled` instead, which `assistantStore.ts` `onTrackedSettled` maps onto the cards (accepted → applied, rejected → skipped, logged "Rejected:", stale → failed with `TRACKED_TEXT_CHANGED`). The card's Accept / Reject go through `useEditPassStore` `accept` (which now answers `{ applied, stale }`) and `reject` (`acceptTracked`, `rejectTracked`); a turn nobody waits for rejects its rows. An accepted one gets the card's Undo for the session (`rememberTrackedUndo`: `undoReplacePassage` with the suggestion's own words; none for a cut). e2e: `e2e/scribe.spec.ts` (accept in the text marks the card), `smoke.spec.ts` (the card's Accept clears the drawn change; `documentTextWithoutGhost` drops `.tracked-change` widgets). Structure tools (`scribe.v5`, 2026-10-16; no migration; contract additive: `AgentEdit` kinds `notebookFolder`, `notebookMove` (each item's `fromParentId`/`fromPosition`), `notebookRename` (old `title`), `notebookDelete`, and `create.moveIn`): `src/shared/scribe2.ts` `SCRIBE_STRUCTURE_TOOLS` (Notes: `create_folder`, `move_note`, `rename_note`, `delete_note`; book: `create_chapter`, `create_scene`, `move_scene`, `rename_scene`, `split_scene`, `merge_scenes`, `delete_scene`), `ScribeAccess.structure` (Write and Vibe; Brainstorm refuses them even when saving; the edit flow's notes run leaves it out), `SCRIBE_MAX_STRUCTURE` 40 a message, each item moved counted, apart from `SCRIBE_MAX_WRITES` (which now counts note writes and prose only). Main `src/main/scribe2/structure.ts` (`STRUCTURE_TOOLS`, dispatched from `runScribeTool`): Notes changes go through `notebookStore` at once (`findFolder` by path or shallowest name, `ensureFolder` makes missing folders, each a `notebookFolder` change; `create_note` with `structure` makes a missing folder too; `list_notes` ends with a `Folders:` line), `delete_note` is a pending `notebookDelete`; the book's tools name chapters by ref or title (`nodeArg`; an unknown name answers with the chapters' refs) and resolve the old agent's edit through `resolveAgentEdit` (`folderOrRoot`: `"manuscript"` is the Manuscript root, named "Manuscript"), never writing scene text; a `move_scene` into a chapter made earlier in the message joins its `moveIn`. Renderer: `runScribeTurn` applies `isTreeEdit` changes (create, rename, move, moveMany, split) at once through `applyChange` → `applyAgentEdit` (session Undo; `create` with `moveIn` makes the chapter then `moveToEnd`, shared with `moveMany`); merges and deletions wait (`isDeletion` includes `notebookDelete`; the card's buttons read Delete / Keep). `isNotebookEdit` changes keep their Undo stored with the turn (`undoNotebookEdit`: delete the folder, `notebook:move` each item back newest first, `notebook:update` the old title, `notebook:restore` a confirmed deletion); `undoAll` and the card's *Undo all* (two or more with an Undo). e2e: `e2e/scribe.spec.ts` ("put Tash in a Characters folder", Undo all; a delete waits for Delete). Known gaps: a confirmed manuscript deletion or merge has no Undo (as the old agent's); her manuscript changes' Undo is this session only; a stopped turn's note edits stay applied (the page keeps their versions); a sheet or Library source opens without its quote; live fidelity for scribe.v1 to scribe.v5 not run.
- Edit flow (notes redesign phase 2; plan `docs/research/notes-redesign-2026-10-15/phase2.md`; migration `0042_edit_flow`, additive; feature id `editFlow`, Quick `fast` / Deep `strong` per request (`EDIT_FLOW_TIER`, so not in `DEFAULT_ROUTING_TABLE`), 8,000 out, 140,000 in; prompts `editFlow.v1` (stages and checks) and `editNotes.v1` (Update notes), one shared system prefix). `src/shared/editFlow.ts` owns the categories (stages developmental / line / copy / proofread, checks continuity / plotHoles / voice / lineSpelling, `updateNotes`), their labels and help (the author's stage definitions), the families (each stage alone, the checks together, notes apart), depth, scope, `scopeKey`, `flowCategoryForPass` (the chat's router passes), the caps, and the zod `EditFlowSpec`, `EditFlowPlanResult`, `EditFlowRun`, `EditFlowIssue` (= `EditChange` + `flowCategory` + `textChanged`), `EditFlowDetail`. Data: a run is an `edit_pass` row with `flow` (JSON: spec, scope title, requests done/total), `overview` and `note_edits` (JSON `EditFlowNoteEdit[]`: Ms Scribe's `notebook` `AgentEdit` + `undone`); `type` is `custom` and unused; its issues are `edit_change` rows with `flow_category` (`kind: 'change'` = a fix, drawn in the editor and settled through `editPass:changes`/`editPass:settle` like any pass change; `kind: 'note'` = an issue without one). `editPass:list` leaves flow rows out; `editPass:settle` emits `editFlow:changed` for them. Ms Scribe's hidden rows (`SCRIBE_PASS_FLOW`) share the `flow` column and are not runs: `flowRows` skips them. Main `src/main/editFlow/`: `plan.ts` (pure: `sliceScenes` Quick a chapter a slice ≤ 16k tokens, Deep ≤ 60k across chapters, a long scene in `chunkText` pieces; `flowFamilies`, `planRequests` slice-major), `parse.ts` (`parseFlowIssues`: each quote checked by `scribe2/quotes.ts` `checkQuote` and kept as the text has it, a fix through the edit passes' own `changeSpan` (exactly once, one paragraph, no overlap per scene across the run, proofread `isCorrection`), a failed fix keeps the issue without it, an overlap or unfound quote dropped and counted; `parseFlowNotes`), `flowStore.ts` (rows; `createFlowRun` deletes the finished runs of the same scope first, the latest per scope kept, then `evictPasses`; `flowDetail` sorts by the scope's reading order then place and marks `textChanged` against the scene now), `run.ts` (`prepareFlow` over `loadScribeSources`: scope, slices, the notes block and book map from `scribe2/context.ts`, the Style guide page, and on Deep for a scene or chapter the rest of the book (`referenceBook`) in the prefix; `countFlow` for `editFlow:plan`; `createEditFlowRunner`: one run at a time, requests through `requestWithRetry`, each answer written at once in one transaction (issues + one proposal per request, overview, note edits applied through `runScribeTool` `edit_note`/`create_note` with `'ai'` versions, the notes proposal settled accepted), stop keeps what came back, cleared on project change; `interruptRunningPasses` covers its rows). Channels (AI area of the contract): `editFlow:plan`, `editFlow:start` (`EditFlowStartResult`), `editFlow:cancel`, `editFlow:list`, `editFlow:get`, `editFlow:noteUndone`; event `editFlow:changed`; plan/start/noteUndone `v1` only. Renderer `src/renderer/features/editFlow/`: `editFlowStore.ts` (panel open, choices, `chapterId` a chat-named chapter, kept runs, shown run's detail; `specFor` resolves the scope from the open scene; accept/reject through `useEditPassStore`; jump through `openPassage(…, { centre: true })`; `undoNote` = `undoNotebookEdit` + `editFlow:noteUndone`; every `editFlow:changed` reloads the shown scenes' tracked changes), `EditFlowPanel.tsx` (`EditFlowButton` in the assistant header, v1 only; the set-up priced through `editPass/passQuote.ts` `useFeatureModel` + `quoteEstimate` with the hosted confirm; results: overview, note edits with `NotebookChange` and Undo, issues grouped by category). `AssistantBody` shows the panel in the chat's place while open. Chat: in v1 a specific router request whose pass has a flow category opens the flow pre-filled (`editFlowOpenedText`; a selection becomes its scene; a named chapter through `editChapterId`); custom, dialogue and Editor's notes keep their pass. Phase 3 (notes updates, plan `phase3.md`, no migration, no prompt change): the step `openNotesUpdate(scope, nodeId)` / `openNotesUpdateHere()` / `notesUpdateTarget` in `editFlowStore.ts` pre-fills Update notes (Quick, Deep for the book; `sceneId` beside `chapterId` for a tree-named scene) from the tree menu (`contextMenuItems.ts` `update-notes`, `notesUpdateScopeOf`), the Notes tab's Update notes… and, in Brainstorm only, a chat message matched whole by `notesUpdateAsk` (`assistantStore.ts` `routeTurn`, before `ai:route`); all hidden while the edit flow cannot run. Automatic after a chapter: `chapterTriggers.ts` (v1; the chapter of the last manuscript scene opened, chapters written in via `documentStore` `onSaved` → `noteChapterSaved`; opening a scene elsewhere flushes saves and calls `editFlow:chapterLeft`; started/stopped in `App.tsx`, which also loads the flow's runs for v1); main `autoNotes.ts` `createAutoNotes` decides (switch `notesAutoUpdate` and state `notesAutoUpdate.state` in the settings KV via `settingsStore.ts`; Use AI, a run going, `chapterTextHash` vs the hash at the last automatic run, the daily cap by local `dayOf`), starts `runner.start(autoNotesSpec(id), { automatic: true })` and records count and hash at start; skips go to `console.warn` (not `off`). `automatic` rides in the `flow` column and on `EditFlowRun`; `scopeKey(scope, automatic)` keeps it apart from the author's runs. The runner emits `editFlow:changed` as a run starts. Activity kind `notesUpdate` (`activityJobs.ts`, snapshot `editFlow`): the drop "Notes updated from <chapter>: N pages" (`notesUpdatedPages`) opens the run in the panel; a failure says its cause, never retried. Settings › AI `NotesAutoSection.tsx` (v1): the switch, the price of a chapter (the book's Quick Update notes over its requests), the cap 1–50. Channels `editFlow:autoSettings`, `editFlow:setAutoSettings`, `editFlow:chapterLeft` (`v1`). e2e: `e2e/editflow.spec.ts`. Known gaps: Proofreading reads the text and titles, not the compile format; a run is not resumable (stop keeps what came back); the voice block is not sent (fixes are voice-checked locally with `scoreFix`); live fidelity for `editFlow.v1`/`editNotes.v1` not run.
- Index job queue (F-5.13): `src/shared/jobs.ts` owns `JOB_MIN_INTERVAL_MS` 500, `JOB_BACKOFF_MS` [5,15,45] s, `JOB_MAX_ATTEMPTS` 3, the zod `JobKind` (`summary` | `mentions`; a new kind is a new member and either a branch in a `run` or, when its failure policy differs, its own `createIndexQueue` instance with `kind` set — F-4.12's mention scan is the second instance, silent and never paused), `JobStatus` (`queued` | `failed`), `JobFailure { code, message, nextStep }`, `IndexQueueStatus { queued, running, failed, done, paused }` and `IDLE_INDEX_QUEUE`. Table `index_job` (migration `0007_index_jobs`): id `<kind>:<node_id>`, `node_id` cascaded, `attempts`, `last_error` JSON; only `queued` and `failed` rows are persisted — a running job is memory-only, so a crash leaves it `queued` and reopening resumes it, and a finished job leaves no row (the `scene_summary` and the ledger row are its record). `src/main/jobs/jobStore.ts` (`listJobs` oldest first, ties by rowid so one "Summarize all" keeps reading order; `getJob`, `enqueueJob` (a queued row is left in place, a failed one returns with attempts 0), `bumpAttempts`, `markFailed`, `deleteJob`, `deleteAllJobs`, `requeueFailed`; an unknown kind/status row is skipped and a corrupt `last_error` reads as a generic PROVIDER failure). `src/main/jobs/indexQueue.ts` `createIndexQueue<T>({ kind?, db, run, cancelRequest, now, onChange, onNodeStatus, debounceMs, minIntervalMs, backoffMs, maxAttempts, requestIdFor })` (`kind` narrows every `listJobs`/`deleteAllJobs`/`requeueFailed` call to that kind, so two instances over one table never pick, cancel, or requeue each other's rows; `jobStore` takes the same optional last parameter): one serial lane for foreground and background, `touch` (debounce → enqueue, node pending at once, a save during a run keeps the row for exactly one more run), `runNow` (joins the run in flight or waiting — handing it the author's request id — else jumps the queue and deletes the row), `load` (resume), `indexAll`, `cancelAll`, `resume`, `clear` (generation guard; rows stay), `status`, `nodeStatus`/`nodeError`. Failure policy: transient (RATE_LIMIT/NETWORK/PROVIDER/unknown) retries with backoff then `failed`; hard (NO_KEY/INVALID_KEY/QUOTA/BUDGET/DISABLED) pauses the whole queue with the job untouched; VALIDATION/NOT_FOUND are quiet (row deleted, node idle); CANCELLED records nothing; the one exception is a Summarize now the dial refuses, which leaves the node quiet and the queue unpaused because the channel answers it as data. Timers are unref'd and nothing throws into one. Contract: `jobs:status`, `jobs:cancel`, `jobs:resume`, `jobs:indexAll` (→ `JobsIndexAllResult`, gated by `assertFeatureAllowed(settings, 'summary')`) and the `jobs:changed` event carrying the whole status. Handlers build the queue where the scheduler was, with `db: () => manager.current() === null ? null : manager.require().connection.orm`, `run` = `summarizeScene` reporting `requested: !result.cached`, `cancelRequest` = `cancelInflight`, and `manager.onChange` → `clear()` then `load()`. Gotcha: a background job's request id is `job-<n>` from a module counter, so `ai:cancel` can reach it and main's duplicate-id guard never fires. Renderer: `src/renderer/features/ai/indexingStore.ts` (`status`, `load` opens the one `jobs:changed` subscription, `cancel`, `resume`, `indexAll` toasting "Queued N scenes for a summary." / "Every scene is up to date." / the refusal with its next step, `clear` on project close, `resetIndexingStore()` in before and after each) and `IndexingIndicator.tsx` in the header beside `AiActivityIndicator` (`indexing`, `role="status"` named Indexing; "Indexing {done + running} of {done + queued + running + failed} scenes", "Indexing paused · {message}" with the next step as its `title`, "{n} scenes could not be summarised", `indexing-resume` Retry when stalled, `indexing-cancel` always). "Summarize all scenes" (`summarize-all`) sits under the dial section in `AiSettingsTab`, disabled unless `isFeatureAllowed(settings, 'summary')`. No provider batch API at launch (see `FEATURES.md` F-5.13): do not add a `batch?` to `Provider`. The e2e step follows the F-5.6 one: it edits Scene 1 with the toggle off so exactly one scene is stale, clicks the button, and checks the toast, the single request, and the ledger; the paused, failed, and Cancel paths are covered by the unit tests and are a manual check in the running app (turn the key off and click Summarize all scenes: the indicator pauses with "No API key is saved." and Retry). Backfill (2026-10-06): `backfillSummaries(resume)` in `handlers.ts` waits `SUMMARY_BACKFILL_DELAY_MS` (5 s, one timer, coalesced, dropped on a project change by `cancelBackfill`) and then, when `summary` is allowed and `ai.get(sourceOf(db))` has a provider, `queue.indexAll('summary', staleSummaryNodeIds(db))`; `resume` (from `aiSettings:set` and `ai:setKey`) also lifts a pause. Called from the project-open block, `aiSettings:set`, `ai:setKey`, and `import:commit` (which also queues the mention scan). `staleSummaryNodeIds` reads the tree once (`sourceOfRow`). Cloud sign-in does not trigger it; the next open or settings change does.
- Beta reader (F-14.11): `src/shared/betaReader.ts` owns the five categories (`BETA_READER_CATEGORIES` knows / believes / expects / confusion / droppedThread, + labels), the caps (`BETA_READER_TEXT_MIN` 200, `BETA_READER_SCENE_CHAR_BUDGET` 20,000, `BETA_READER_SHRINK_CHARS` 2,000, `BETA_READER_SCENE_CHAR_FLOOR` 4,000, `BETA_READER_MAX_ITEMS` 12, quote 240 / note 300), the zod `BetaReaderItem { category, scene (1-based into the list sent), quote, note }` and `BetaReaderScene { nodeId, title, current }`. Feature id `betaReader` (tier `strong`, `minDial` 1, budgets 1,200 out / 12,000 in); the honesty setting is `AiSettings.critique.honesty`, shared with F-14.8. Contract `ai:betaReader { nodeId, requestId, note?, regeneratedFrom? }` → `AiBetaReaderResult` (`items`, `scenes` in the order sent with the current one last, `truncated`, `skipped`, `missing`, `dropped`, usage/cost/cached/model, `proposalId`, `requestId`, or the AI failure shape). Main: `src/main/ai/prompts/betaReader.v1.ts` (`BETA_READER_RULES` opens with `You are the beta-reader feature inside a novel-writing app.`, the e2e sentinel pinned by the golden test; system = rules + `BETA_READER_HONESTY[honesty]` (reader-flavoured lines, not the critique ones), user = `Scenes read so far, in order (summaries):` with `[n] Chapter › Title\nsummary\n- key point` per earlier scene or `No earlier scenes.`, then `[N] Title (this scene, full text):` and `Report as the reader.`; no voice block, no story bible, no brief by design) and `betaReaderRegen.v1.ts` (the note clause appended to the user turn, so the cached system prefix is byte-identical); `src/main/ai/betaReader.ts` `runBetaReader(db, deps, input)` (`assertFeatureAllowed` first, the text gate, VALIDATION for a node outside `manuscriptDocuments`, `summariesFor` over every earlier manuscript document (no summary → `missing`, staleness not checked), titles as `Chapter › Scene` unless the parent is a section root, `fitReadThrough` (shrink the scene to the floor, then drop the farthest scenes as `skipped`, measured as `runAiRequest` measures), `runAiRequest` JSON on the strong tier with the hash over the scene text, the ordered `(nodeId, contentHash)` of the summaries, missing/skipped, honesty, note, predecessor; `parseBetaReaderAnswer(text, texts)` drops and counts a bad shape, a scene number out of range, a blank quote or note, and a quote `findQuote` cannot find in that scene's text as sent (`summary + key points` for an earlier scene), dedupes by scene + normalized quote, caps at 12), handler `ai:betaReader` (one proposal per report with the items as JSON, never flagged), catalogue entries for both versions with eval cases fresh/full/maxed (20 earlier scenes at every cap sit at 11,599 of 12,000; 25 would not fit) plus the two regen cases under scoring kind `betaReader { texts }`. Renderer: `betaReaderStore.ts` (one session app-wide like `critiqueStore` minus Apply: `start` flushes the autosave then `track('betaReader', …)`; `show(index, editor)` only for an item whose scene is the current one, `locateText` or mark stale; `regenerate` settles `regenerated`; `close` settles `rejected` (a pending one cancels); `dismissFor`; `resetBetaReaderStore()`), `BetaReaderPanel.tsx` (`section[aria-label="Beta reader"]`, `beta-reader-panel`, `-pending`, `-stop`, `-honesty` (the `HonestySelect` shared with the critique panel, `testId` prop), `-group` per non-empty category in order with an `h3`, `-item` (`data-category`), `-quote` (a button for this scene, a `p` for an earlier one), `-scene` chip with the earlier scene's title, `-stale`, `-empty`, `-truncated`, `-skipped`, `-missing`, `-cost`, `-dropped`, `-regenerate` "Read again…" via `dialogs.prompt`, `-close`, `-error` `role="alert"`), and `BetaReaderButton.tsx` ("Beta reader", lucide `Glasses`, gated like `CritiqueButton`). `DocumentEditor` mounts the button after `CritiqueButton` and the panel under `CritiquePanel` (single-document view only) and calls `dismissFor(id)` on unmount or rebuild. The e2e step follows the F-5.6 summary step (Scene 1 has a summary; Opening before it is under the floor, so `missing` is 1 and the user turn says `No earlier scenes.`) and checks the fabricated quote and the item naming an unsent scene never reach the panel. Since 2026-10-06 (all AI in the assistant): `BetaReaderButton` is deleted (Actions › Beta reader, or a routed chat turn); `BetaReaderPanel` and `ProofreadPanel` render in `AiResults`.
- Story Intelligence query mode (F-5.7): `src/shared/query.ts` owns the limits (`QUERY_FULL_SCENES` 3, `QUERY_SUMMARY_SCENES` 10, `QUERY_SCENE_CHAR_BUDGET` 12,000, `QUERY_SHRINK_CHARS` 2,000, `QUERY_SCENE_CHAR_FLOOR` 3,000, `QUERY_MAX_CITATIONS` 6, `QUERY_QUOTE_MAX` 240, `QUERY_ANSWER_MAX` 2,000, `QUERY_ALSO_MAX` 8), `QUERY_NOT_FOUND`, the zod `QuerySceneRef { nodeId, title }`, `QueryCitation` (+ `scene` as sent, `quote`), `QueryTurn { found, uncited, citations, also }`, `CITATION_MARKER` (`[n]`), `citedSceneNumbers`, `stripDanglingMarkers`. `CHAT_MODES` gained `query` (label Query) and `ChatMessage.query: QueryTurn | null` (zod-defaulted to null so stored rows parse). Feature id `query` (tier `strong`, JSON, `minDial` 1, budgets 600 out / 12,000 in). Contract `ai:query { nodeId | null, message, history, requestId }` → `AiQueryResult` (`answer`, `found`, `uncited`, `citations`, `also`, `dropped`, usage/cost/cached/model, `proposalId`, `requestId`, or the failure shape). Main: `src/main/ai/context/queryContext.ts` (`queryTerms`: lowercase unicode tokens ≥ 3 chars minus a stopword list, plus `parseTagRefs`; pure `scoreCandidate` with `QUERY_WEIGHTS` title 4 / tag 4 / metadata 3 / summary block 2 / body 1 per distinct term + 0.1 × min(occurrences, 10), a `#name` ref matching a document tag + 6, word-prefix match through a unicode lookbehind, not `\b`; `rankCandidates(db, { question, nodeId })` over `manuscriptDocuments` with `listAllDocumentTags` (one query, new in `documentTagStore.ts`), `parseStoredSceneMeta`, `summariesFor`, and `documentText`, ties by reading order with the active scene first, score 0 excluded, fallback to the active scene then reading order when nothing scores; `sceneTitles` (`Chapter › Scene`) moved here from `betaReader.ts`), prompt `src/main/ai/prompts/query.v1.ts` (`QUERY_RULES` opens with `You are the Story Intelligence feature inside a novel-writing app.`, the e2e sentinel pinned by the golden test; system = rules + `Scenes (full text):` `[n] title` blocks + `Other scenes (summaries only):`, numbers continuing; history as real turns; the question last; no voice block, bible, or temperature), `src/main/ai/query.ts` `runQuery` (`assertFeatureAllowed` first, VALIDATION when no manuscript document has text, `fitQueryPrompt` in the order shrink the longest full scene to the floor → drop summaries lowest-ranked first → drop the oldest history down to `QUERY_HISTORY_KEEP` 2 → drop the lowest-ranked full scenes keeping one → the rest of the history, measured as `runAiRequest` measures; the hash covers the question, history, `(nodeId, text)` of the full scenes and `(nodeId, contentHash)` of the summaries; `parseQueryAnswer(text, scenes)` drops and counts a citation naming a summary-only or unsent scene, a blank quote, or one `findQuote` cannot find in the text sent, dedupes, caps at 6, strips dangling markers; `found: false` discards citations uncounted, `found` with none left is `uncited`; `also` = ranked minus cited, first 8), handler `ai:query` (proposal `query` with `{ answer, citations }` as JSON, pending, never flagged), catalogue `query.v1` with eval cases fresh/full/maxed (the maxed case is built by the real fit and sits at 11,552 of 12,000) under scoring kind `query { texts }`. Renderer: `assistantStore.ts` `send` routes a `query` conversation to `ai:query` (tracked as `query`, not streamed; the turn gets the answer, cost, proposal, and `query`), `openScene(ref, quote)` (`useTreeStore.select`, then the registered editor or a one-shot `useActiveEditorStore` subscription with `OPEN_SCENE_TIMEOUT_MS` 3 s, then `locateText` + `setTextSelection().scrollIntoView()`; a gone quote toasts `PASSAGE_GONE_MESSAGE`); `AssistantPanel.tsx` `Query` radio (gated on `isFeatureAllowed(settings, 'query')` on top of the chat gate; Paragraphs stays Agent-only), `QueryAnswer` (`query-not-found`, `query-uncited` with `QUERY_UNCITED_WARNING` only when the turn's `mode` is `query` (`warnUncited`; a Plan chat answer is never warned, author 2026-10-10), live `[n]` markers as `query-cite` buttons, Sources as `query-citation` buttons, Also mentioned in as `query-also` chips). The e2e step follows the beta reader step: it asks about the storm on the ridge so Scene 1 ranks `[1]`, checks the fabricated quote and the `[3]` marker never reach the panel, and clicks the citation to select the passage. No split view: the app has none. query.v3 (2026-10-06): `runQuery` builds `query.v3` (`QUERY_RULES_V3` keeps v1's first sentence for the e2e sentinel; heading `QUERY_BIBLE_HEADING_V3`); the bible renders through `renderStoryBibleEntities(entities, QUERY_V3_BIBLE_TOKEN_BUDGET 1500, QUERY_V3_BIBLE_VALUE_MAX 600)` (the value cap is now a parameter, default `STORY_BIBLE_VALUE_MAX`); `sentSheets` lists the entities whose own sheet went out (`StoryBibleEntity.entityId`, optional, set by `storyBibleEntities`), `parseQueryAnswer(text, scenes, sheetsSent)` keeps the model's `sheets` names that match them (`toEntityNameKey`), and `uncited` needs no citation and no sheet. `rankCandidates` falls back to `sceneEntities` (entities the top full scenes name, a character by first name of 3+ letters too, most-named first, `QUERY_SCENE_ENTITIES` 3) when the question names none. `QueryTurn.sheets` (`QuerySheetRef`, defaulted `[]` for stored turns) rides `AiQueryResult`; `AssistantPanel` lists them under "From your notes" (`data-testid="query-sheet"`, opens the entity through `useEntityStore.select`). `send` and `sendQuery` flush the document store before the request.
  - F-5.17: `ai:query` takes an optional `pinActive`; `runQuery` passes it to `rankCandidates`, which then puts the active scene (when it has text) first whatever it scores, the rest in their usual order, so the What happened here? recap always sends the open scene as `[1]`.
- Ghost text (F-5.3): the renderer owns the trigger and sends only the caret window. `src/renderer/features/editor/ghostText.ts` is the `GhostText` Tiptap extension (always in the manuscript schema; the suggestion lives in plugin state and renders as a widget decoration `.ghost-text` at the caret, never in the document; the anchor maps with `assoc = -1` so a keystroke at the caret consumes rather than clears; Tab accepts all, Shift+Tab one word plus its space, Escape clears; a matching typed character (space included) consumes, anything else, caret movement, blur, or `compositionstart` clears; accepted text is inserted as plain text with the caret's marks through the normal editor transaction so it autosaves and undoes as one step). `ghostTextController.ts` (`useGhostTextController` in `DocumentEditor`) applies `shouldTrigger` (idle delay from `AiSettings.ghostText.idleMs`, `GHOST_MIN_NEW_CHARS` 12, no request while pending or a ghost is visible, `GHOST_MAX_PER_DAY` 200 in memory), sends `ai:ghostText { nodeId, before (≤ 500), after (≤ 100), requestId }`, drops stale answers (newer id, document switched, text changed), turns the mode off with one toast on DISABLED/NO_KEY/INVALID_KEY, and backs off `GHOST_BACKOFF_MS` (30 s) after other failures; `resetGhostTextController()` in tests. `VibeWriteToggle.tsx` sits in the single-document toolbar (`aria-pressed`, disabled below Suggest with the reason). `AiSettings.ghostText = { enabled, idleMs }` (defaulted so old rows load; the idle-delay row, 0.5–5 s, lives under the dial section). Main: `src/main/ai/ghostText.ts` `generateGhostText` (dial gate first, then notes ≤ 500 chars, scene metadata, `resolvePreset` for temperature, max tokens ≤ 60, the style instruction and the new-elements rule; `fast` tier; context hash over window + notes + meta + preset) with `postProcessGhostText` (trim, strip wrapping quotes, cut to two sentences, drop a leading repeat of the text before the caret, join spacing); `src/main/ai/prompts/ghostText.v2.ts` (F-14.3; `ghostText.v1.ts` stays as shipped: rules → voice block (F-14.1) → preset → user turn: meta, the scene brief, notes, the window; the golden tests pin 707 of the old 1 500 budget for v1, 1 093 of the old 2 000 for v2 with a brief at every line cap, and 1 243 of 2 200 for v3 with the story bible at its 150-token ghost budget on top, F-14.9, and 1 362 for v4 with the scene steer at its budget too, F-14.13; the features send v4). `Provider.complete` takes `temperature`. The e2e fake chat endpoint branches on `response_format`: JSON → tags, text → the canned continuation. Ghost text does not stream (one ≤ 60-token `complete`, idle-triggered, so nobody waits on it); `PLAN.md` §3's "streaming everywhere users wait" applies to chat and rewrites (F-5.4, F-14.10). 2026-10-07: an answer that is empty with `finishReason: 'length'` (all reasoning) throws `AiCutOffError` (`cutOff`, code PROVIDER) and the handler annotates the inspector row; `GhostState.pin` marks a chat insertion (F-5.22): placed with `setGhost(…, { at, pin })`, grown with `streamGhost`, kept through caret moves, edits elsewhere (mapped; ends when its anchor is deleted), and blur; reported to `storage.listeners` (every exit, with its pin) rather than `onSettle`; the controller never sets or clears over a pinned ghost (`ownGhostShowing`). The widget draws `\n` as `<br>` (`white-space: normal`).
- Website (F-15.10): `site/public/` is the static site (plain HTML + one `styles.css`, no build step, no scripts; landing, `privacy.html`, `terms.html`, `docs/index.html` for the AI settings, `404.html`, `_headers` with a strict CSP; no `_redirects`: Workers assets refuse absolute sources, so `www` → apex is a zone Single Redirect rule created through the API, see below). It deploys as a Cloudflare Worker serving static assets (Pages is folded into Workers now; `wrangler pages` commands delegate and fail without a config): `site/wrangler.toml` pins the account, `workers_dev = false`, and the two `custom_domain` routes, so `npm run site:deploy` (`wrangler deploy --config site/wrangler.toml`, wrangler pinned as a devDependency) creates the DNS records and certificates itself. The `www` → apex 301 is a zone-level redirect rule (`http_request_dynamic_redirect` phase, expression `http.host eq "www.mythscribe.app"`), created once through the API with a token that has Zone → Single Redirect → Edit; the Worker's `www` route still exists so the record and certificate are managed with the rest. The token lives in the gitignored `.env` (`CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN`, `CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID`); `wrangler whoami` fails under it, real commands work. `api.mythscribe.app` is reserved for F-15.4: nothing is created for it. `src/shared/site.test.ts` (`npm run site:check`) pins the docs page to `AI_DIAL_LABEL`/`AI_DIAL_MEANING`/`AI_DATA_SHARING` cell by cell and checks every page's links, assets, and structure, so a copy change in `aiSettings.ts` fails until the docs page is edited by hand. Since the 2026-10-07 revamp: fonts are self-hosted woff2 in `site/public/fonts/` (Fraunces, Inter, OFL texts beside them) so the CSP stays `'self'`; download buttons are disabled `<button>`s reading "Coming soon" (no script, no link); the pricing section is one block between `PRICING BLOCK` comments in `index.html`. The product shots (`img/shot-*.webp` at 1600 and 800 wide, PNG fallback at 1600) come from `scripts/site-screenshots.mjs`: after `npm run build`, run it under `xvfb-run -a -s "-screen 0 1600x1000x24"` with `WAYLAND_DISPLAY` unset; it builds the demo novel "The Lantern Ferry" over IPC, answers AI from a loopback fake OpenAI (no key, no network), captures at 1440×900 ×2, and resizes in the app's own Chromium (no image dependency). Its fake server matches the system-prompt openings, so a prompt whose opening changes needs the script updated. The edit-pass report and the estimate are deliberately not captured (they compare with a human editor's rates). Recapture when the UI changes.
- Demo video (Lemon Squeezy store verification and the website; author decisions 2026-10-08): `npm run demo:video -- [--out <dir>] [--skip-build | --edit-only]` runs `scripts/demo-video.mjs`, which builds, re-runs itself with `--record` under `xvfb-run` on a 1920×1080 screen, then edits with ffmpeg from the pinned `ffmpeg-static` devDependency (electron-builder ships only `out/**` and production dependencies, so it never reaches the app bundle); each Node-heavy step goes through `flock /tmp/mythscribe-heavy.lock` where `flock` exists. The demo novel, the fake OpenAI (now with `agent`/`prose`/`delayMs`/`proseChunkMs` options for scripted chat steps and streamed drafts), `seedLanternFerry`, and `showSection` (the F-9.11 section picker) live in `scripts/lantern-ferry.mjs`, shared with `site-screenshots.mjs`. The recording is Playwright's `recordVideo` of a 1280×720 window at `--force-device-scale-factor=1.5` (a sharp 1080p); a drawn cursor, a hover outline, and a shortcut badge are injected into the page because recordings show no cursor. Sections are cut by wall-clock marks against the webm's `creation_time` (Playwright stamps it at recorder start); title cards are HTML in a second BrowserWindow (`capturePage`) with the site's fonts and palette. Outputs go outside the repo (default `<tmp>/mythscribe-demo/`): `mythscribe-demo.mp4` (H.264 yuv420p, ~90 s), `mythscribe-hero.webm` (VP9 yuv420p 720p loop, ~20 s, no cards), `stills/`, `raw/`. Its fake answers match system-prompt openings like the screenshot script's, so a prompt whose opening changes needs `lantern-ferry.mjs` updated.
- MythScribe account (F-15.2): `src/shared/cloudApi.ts` is the wire contract shared by the app and the Worker (`CLOUD_API_URL`, `EMAIL_MAX`, `LOGIN_ATTEMPT_TTL_MS` 15 min, `SESSION_TTL_MS` 90 days, `POLL_INTERVAL_MS` 3 s, `START_RATE_LIMIT` 3, the zod bodies of `/auth/start|poll|me|signout`, `CloudSession`, `CloudApiError` + `CLOUD_ERROR_STATUS`); `src/shared/account.ts` owns `AccountStatus` (`signedOut | pending | signedIn`, the token never crosses IPC) and `cloudApiUrl(env)` (`MYTHSCRIBE_CLOUD_API_URL` overrides the default for dev and e2e). Main: `src/main/account/cloudAuthClient.ts` is the typed fetch (15 s timeout, `AccountError { code, message, nextStep }`); `accountService.ts` is the one owner of the state machine and the 3 s poll timer (injected `Schedule`), persists the session as JSON in `AiKeyStore` under `SecretId` `'cloudSession'` (same `ai-keys.json` and safeStorage as the OpenAI key; no safe storage → IO before any request), maps errors to `AppError` (`INVALID_EMAIL` → VALIDATION, else IO with the next step in the message), and emits `account:changed` via `onChange` (constructed in `index.ts`, disposed on `will-quit`). Channels `account:getStatus|requestLink|cancelLink|signOut|refresh` in `handlers.ts`. Renderer: `src/renderer/features/account/accountStore.ts` (status, busy, inline `error`; `subscribe()` mounted once in `App.tsx`) and `AccountSettingsTab.tsx` (the Account tab; dev link shown as `account-dev-link` with Copy when the Worker runs the `log` transport; `refresh()` on mount when `since` is null). The Worker lives in `cloud/` (`src/index.ts` router + `handleRequest(request, deps)` seam, `auth.ts` pure handlers, `store.ts` `Store` with `d1Store`/`memoryStore`, `email.ts` `resendMailer`/`logMailer`, `pages.ts`, `crypto.ts`; D1 `mythscribe` bound as `DB`, migrations under `cloud/migrations/`, `worker-configuration.d.ts` generated by `npm run cloud:types` and ignored by eslint/prettier); only SHA-256 hashes of the poll secret, link token, and session token are stored; links are single use, the session is handed to `/auth/poll` exactly once, and an unknown attempt id and a wrong secret answer identically. Scripts: `cloud:dev` (needs `cloud/.dev.vars` with `EMAIL_TRANSPORT=log` and a `--local` migrations apply), `cloud:migrate` (remote), `cloud:deploy`, `cloud:types`; the vitest `cloud` project and `cloud/tsconfig.json` are in the gates. Operator state 2026-09-19: live. The `.env` token has Account → D1 → Edit, migration 0001 is applied remotely, the Worker is deployed at `api.mythscribe.app`, Resend has the domain verified and `RESEND_API_KEY` is set (a new secret takes a few seconds to reach the running Worker; a 503 `NOT_CONFIGURED` right after `secret put` is propagation, not a bug). Gap: Google/Apple sign-in is F-15.12.
- Cloud credits (F-15.3): every balance and charge is an integer in micro-USD (`MICROS_PER_USD` in `src/shared/cloudRates.ts`, which also owns `CLOUD_RATE_MULTIPLIER` (2x `MODEL_PRICING`), `cloudRateFor`, `cloudChargeMicros` (ceil, min 1, throws for an unpriced model), and the published `CLOUD_RATES` table with the default tiers; the Worker imports the same module, so there is one rate). Wire shapes in `cloudApi.ts`: `CreditPack`, `CreditSpendRow`, `CreditsResult` (`GET /credits`), `CheckoutBody`/`CheckoutResult` (`POST /billing/checkout`, the Worker builds the Lemon Squeezy URL with `checkout[custom][user_id]` and `checkout[email]`; the app only names a `variantId`), `isCheckoutUrl` (https on `*.lemonsqueezy.com`), error codes `BAD_SIGNATURE` 401 and `INSUFFICIENT_CREDITS` 402. Worker: migration `0002_credits.sql` (`credits` balance per user, `credit_events` signed ledger with `order_ref` UNIQUE), `store.ts` `getBalance`/`applyCreditEvent` (one D1 `batch` = one transaction; a UNIQUE violation answers `'duplicate'` and nothing moves; `memoryStore` mirrors it)/`spendByFeature`; `credits.ts` `handleCredits`, `handleCheckout`, `handleLemonSqueezyWebhook` (raw body, HMAC-SHA256 `X-Signature` compared timing-safe via `hmacSha256Hex` in `crypto.ts`; `order_created` with status `paid` credits `priceCents` of the configured pack, `order_refunded` debits it, idempotent on `<event>:<order id>`; unreadable, unknown-user, unknown-variant, and unrelated events are a logged 200 because Lemon Squeezy retries every non-2xx), and the meter F-15.4 calls: `hasCredit` (balance > 0, the pre-flight gate) and `meterRequest` (charges after the answer, unconditionally, so the last request may overdraw slightly). `authenticate()` in `auth.ts` is the shared bearer check. Vars: `LEMONSQUEEZY_PACKS` (JSON `[{ variantId, url, priceCents }]`, validated with `ConfiguredPacks`; malformed → `[]` and one `console.error`; unset until the products exist) and the secret `LEMONSQUEEZY_WEBHOOK_SECRET`; `cloud:types` now writes `cloud/worker-configuration.d.ts` explicitly. The operator recipe (test-mode store, packs, webhook URL and events, the curl replay against `wrangler dev`) is in `cloud/README.md`. App: `cloudAuthClient.credits/checkout`, `accountService.credits()/checkoutUrl()` (signed out → IO with the sign-in step; UNAUTHORIZED forgets the session like `refresh()` and pushes `account:changed`; unknown pack → VALIDATION), channels `account:getCredits` and `account:buyCredits` (the handler refuses any URL `isCheckoutUrl` rejects, then `openExternal`; `menu:openExternal` stays mythscribe.app-only), `accountStore` `credits`/`creditsBusy`/`creditsError` (cleared whenever the status is not signedIn), and `CreditsSection` in `AccountSettingsTab.tsx` (balance `account-credit-balance`, `Buy $5.00` buttons or "not on sale yet", Refresh, spend table via `featureLabel` now in `ai/usageFormat.ts`, the rates table). The e2e fake Cloud API answers both routes. Gaps: auto-top-up was dropped (a subscription, later); `order_created` arriving unpaid (a delayed payment method) is ignored and Lemon Squeezy sends no later paid event, so such an order is credited by hand if it ever happens.
- Cloud proxy (F-15.4): one route, `POST /ai/complete`, wire in `src/shared/cloudApi.ts`: `AiCompleteBody` (`feature: AiFeatureId`, the resolved `model`, `messages`, `maxTokens`, `json?`, `temperature?`, `stream`; caps `AI_COMPLETE_MAX_TOKENS` 4 000, `AI_COMPLETE_MAX_MESSAGES` 64, `AI_COMPLETE_MAX_CHARS` 200 000), `AiCompleteResult` (`text`, `model`, `usage`, `chargeMicros`, `balanceMicros`), and for `stream: true` a 200 `AI_STREAM_CONTENT_TYPE` (`application/x-ndjson`) body of `AiStreamEvent` lines (`delta`s, then exactly one `done` with the usage and the charge, or one `error` when the upstream failed after the headers went out). New codes `BAD_REQUEST` 400 (unreadable body, over a cap, or a model outside `CLOUD_RATES`) and `UPSTREAM` 502. Worker: `cloud/src/ai.ts` `handleAiComplete(request, deps: AiDeps)` (`AiDeps extends CreditsDeps` with `upstream: Upstream | null`, built from the secret `OPENAI_API_KEY`; null → 503 `NOT_CONFIGURED`) in the order authenticate → validate → `hasCredit` (402) → upstream → `meterRequest` (the model that answered when it has a rate, else the requested one) → answer; `cloud/src/openai.ts` is the upstream seam (`openAiUpstream(key, fetch?, baseUrl?)`: Chat Completions over plain `fetch`, no SDK in the Worker bundle, `stream_options.include_usage`, the `sseLines` reader, `UpstreamError` kinds `rate_limit → 429 RATE_LIMITED`, `auth`/`network`/`other → 502 UPSTREAM`, never a body in an error). A client that stops reading a stream aborts the upstream and is charged nothing (the tokens already generated are bounded by `maxTokens`, the operator's cost); a stream that ends without a usage chunk still gets one `done` (zero usage, the 1-micro minimum); a store failure while metering (after the provider answered) ends the stream with one `INTERNAL` error event, the cause in the log, nothing charged; writes after a client cancel are dropped rather than thrown. One `console.log` per answered request (request id, user id, feature, model, token counts, charge, status; never content), allowed for `cloud/src/**` by an eslint override. App: `src/main/ai/providers/cloud.ts` `buildCloudProvider({ baseUrl, fetch, token, onSessionEnded, resolveModel, credits })` is the `Provider` with `id: 'cloud'`: bearer from the live `token()` (null → `AiSignedOutError`), `feature` required on the request, `CLOUD_COMPLETE_TIMEOUT_MS` 15 s on the non-streamed call, `ndjsonLines` over the web `ReadableStream` (partial lines, cancel on early exit), and one mapping: `UNAUTHORIZED` → `onSessionEnded()` + `SIGNED_OUT`, `INSUFFICIENT_CREDITS` → `NO_CREDIT`, `RATE_LIMITED` → `RATE_LIMIT`, everything else → `PROVIDER` (`MythScribe Cloud reported a problem (<code>).`), fetch failure → `NETWORK`; `price = cloudPriceFor` (`src/shared/cloudRates.ts`, `priceFor`'s shape at the Cloud rate, never throws); `testConnection` = `credits(token)` (balance ≤ 0 → `NO_CREDIT`) answering the fast model. `Provider.price?` and `CompletionRequest.feature?` are the interface additions (`providers/types.ts`, with `AiSignedOutError` and `AiNoCreditError`); `runAiRequest` uses `provider.price ?? deps.price` for both the daily-cap estimate and the ledger row, so every cost line under a proposal is at the Cloud rate with no change to the feature stores. Where a project's requests go is `AiSettings.source` (`AiSource = ownKey | cloud`, `AI_SOURCE_LABEL`/`AI_SOURCE_MEANING` in `src/shared/aiSettings.ts`, defaulted to `ownKey` so stored rows parse): `AiProviderRegistry.get(source)` answers the one lazily built Cloud provider (4th constructor argument, wired in `index.ts` with the same `createCloudAuthClient` the account uses, `account.sessionToken()`/`account.sessionEnded()`, and `models.cloud[tier]`) or the key path as before; `handlers.ts` binds `providers.get` to `ai.get(sourceOf(db))` per project and `ai:testConnection` reads the open project's source (none → `ownKey`). `SIGNED_OUT` and `NO_CREDIT` are hard codes in `indexQueue.ts` and turn-off codes in `ghostTextController.ts`. AI tab: an "AI source" radiogroup (`ai-source-ownKey`/`ai-source-cloud`) writes the setting; Cloud hides the key form and shows `ai-cloud-account` (signed in as … / not signed in), the Models group edits `status.models[source]`, Test connection needs a key or a session by source, the data-sharing table's Provider column follows the source, and `aiStore.test()` awaits `flushAiSettings()` first (main reads the source from the saved row, so a click inside the debounce answered for the old source). e2e: the fake Cloud API answers `/ai/complete` (JSON or NDJSON) behind the bearer, records `cloudAiRequests`, and a streamed Plan-mode chat runs through it with the fake OpenAI seeing nothing and the cost line at 2x `priceFor`. Operator state: `OPENAI_API_KEY` not yet set on the live Worker (`npx wrangler secret put OPENAI_API_KEY` from `cloud/`, then `npm run cloud:deploy`); until then the route is 503. Gaps: no per-user rate limit on the proxy; a cancelled stream is unmetered. The balance in `done` reaches the app through `onBalance` (F-15.5).
- Cloud billing (AI-BILLING-SPEC slice B2, 2026-10-07; supersedes the balance and metering parts of the two bullets above on the Worker side): migration `cloud/migrations/0005_billing.sql` adds the append-only `ledger_entries` (types `topup | trial_grant | charge | refund | adjustment`, unique `idempotency_key`, charge details incl. cached tokens, provider cost, `markup_bps`; triggers refuse UPDATE/DELETE; seeded from `credit_events`, while `credits`/`credit_events` stay unread), `holds` (`active | settled | released`, prices locked per hold, unique per account + `Idempotency-Key`), `billing_config` (one JSON value per key over `DEFAULT_BILLING_CONFIG` in `cloud/src/config.ts`), `rate_limits`, `access_tokens`, and the sign-in code columns. Available balance = ledger sum − active unexpired holds (`Store.getBalance`); the balance check and the hold are one conditional `INSERT … SELECT … WHERE available >= amount` (`PLACE_HOLD`, `cloud/src/store.ts`); a Cron Trigger (`*/5`) runs `runScheduledSweep`. Pricing math is integer/BigInt in `src/shared/cloudBilling.ts` (shared with the app: `lockPrices`, `chargeMicros` = ceil(cost × (1 + markup)), `holdMicros`, `inputTokenUpperBound` = UTF-8 bytes + template), price table ids are OpenRouter's (`openai/gpt-5.4-mini` …, bare names accepted as aliases). Gateway: `openRouterUpstream` (`OPENROUTER_API_KEY`) else OpenAI direct with `openAiModelId`. New routes: `GET /pricing` (public `PricingResult`), `GET /usage` (paged ledger), `POST /auth/refresh` (15-min access tokens from the session = refresh token), `POST /auth/verify` (6-digit code); `/credits` adds `heldMicros`; `/ai/complete` adds `Idempotency-Key` and codes `REQUEST_TOO_LARGE` 413, `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE` 422, `DUPLICATE_REQUEST` 409; the webhook adds `STALE_WEBHOOK`, partial refunds by `refunded_amount`, and the $30 app license (`LEMONSQUEEZY_APP_LICENSE`, same license row as the Supporter). Tests run the production SQL on `node:sqlite` with every migration applied (`cloud/src/testing/sqliteD1.ts`; the in-memory store is gone). Known gap until slice B3: the app still prices Cloud at `cloudRates.ts` (2x), sends no `Idempotency-Key`, and uses the session token as the bearer (still accepted).
- Starter pack and refunds (author 2026-10-08): migration `cloud/migrations/0006_starter_refunds.sql` adds `users.email_verified_at` (backfilled from `created_at`; `completeSignIn` sets it, `Store.markEmailVerified` for an unverified row), `starter_purchases` (PK `user_id`, UNIQUE `order_id`, never deleted), and index `ledger_entries_user_order`. `cloud/src/credits.ts`: `Variant.kind` `pack | starter | license`; the starter (`LEMONSQUEEZY_STARTER`) skips `min_pack_usd`, `starterRefusal` gates checkout and `/credits.starter`; the webhook credits a starter through `Store.creditStarter` (one batch: claim + conditional top-up → `applied | duplicate | refused`), and a refused one is refunded in full via `cloud/src/lemonSqueezy.ts` (`lemonSqueezyApi(LEMONSQUEEZY_API_KEY)`, injected as `deps.lemonSqueezy`, outcome `refunded | refused | unknown`). `handleRefund` (`POST /billing/refund`): `findOrder` → repeat answer from `findRefundHold` (`refund:<order>` key in `holds`, feature `refund`) → window → amount = floor-to-cents(min(ledger − held − `grantedMicros`, min(paid, top-up) − refunds)), where paid is `ledger_entries.paid_micros` (0006; the webhook records `total_usd − tax_usd` via `Store.creditOrder`/`creditStarter`; NULL = the credit; `OrderRow.creditedMicros`/`paidMicros`) → `placeRefundHold` (SQL `REFUND_LIMITS` re-checks both limits) → API → `settleRefund` (the webhook's `order_refunded:<order>:<cumulative cents>` key, `REFUND_ORDER` caps at the order's top-up) / `releaseHold` on 4xx / hold kept `REFUND_HOLD_MS` (24 h) on no answer. `refundOrder` (webhook) now caps at the order's top-up, scopes by account, ignores orders with no top-up, and settles a running refund hold for the order in the same batch only when the order's refunds reach `holds.refund_base_micros` (the order's refunds when the hold was placed, 0006) + the hold, so a replayed or smaller refund webhook leaves it running. `lemonSqueezyApi` times out at 12 s (`REFUND_TIMEOUT_MS`), under the app's 15 s `CLOUD_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. `/ai/complete` refusals carry `CloudApiError.offer` (`CreditOffer`: starter | packs + negative). Shared: `RefundableOrder`, `RefundBody`/`RefundResult`, `CreditsResult.starter|refunds` (defaulted for older Workers), code `NOT_ELIGIBLE` 409, `starterOfferText`/`noBalanceText` in `src/shared/hostedPricing.ts`. App: `CloudAuthClient.refund` (server copy passed through, `NOT_ELIGIBLE` always), `AccountService.refund` (`NOT_ELIGIBLE` → VALIDATION), channel `account:refund` (`read` access, works read-only), the cloud provider maps the offer into `AiNoCreditError`'s message; `BalanceSection` shows `account-starter` and the `account-refunds` list with a two-step Refund button; `accountStore.refund` reloads credits. Tests: `cloud/src/refund.test.ts`, `lemonSqueezy.test.ts`; e2e checks the offer and one refund.
