- Hosted UX and client switch-over (AI-BILLING-SPEC slice B3b, 2026-10-07; supersedes the app-side pricing, wording, and bearer parts of the bullets above and below): **one pricing shape**, `PricingResult` (`GET /pricing`, `src/shared/cloudApi.ts`); B1's `CloudPricing` is gone, `CloudPricingCache`/`AiModelChoice.cloudPricing` hold `PricingResult`, `hostedAutoTable` (`aiRouting.ts`) turns its `routing.features` into the Auto table. `src/shared/cloudRates.ts` is deleted (`MICROS_PER_USD` now in `cloudBilling.ts`, which also owns `DEFAULT_BILLING_TERMS` spread by the Worker's config). `src/shared/hostedPricing.ts` is the app side: `bundledPricing()` (the Worker's defaults until `/pricing` answers), `hostedPriceFor` (exactly the Worker's `chargeMicros`), `hostedQuote` (uncached charge × safety factor, `needsConfirm` above the threshold), `multiplierLabel`, `hostedModelFor` (a tier left at `HOSTED_DEFAULT_MODELS` or `LEGACY_HOSTED_DEFAULT_MODELS` follows `routing.tiers`), `wordsCovered` (null while the constant is). Defaults approved 2026-10-07: `deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash` / `-v4-pro` for `OPENROUTER_DEFAULT_MODELS`, `HOSTED_DEFAULT_MODELS`, `DEFAULT_HOSTED_MODELS` (OpenAI models stay on sale with multipliers), priced in `OPENROUTER_PRICING`. Tokens (A5, S6): `src/main/account/accessTokens.ts` — `CloudAccessTokens` (in-memory access token per session, minted by `cloudAuthClient.refresh`, renewed 60 s before expiry, concurrent calls share one refresh, NOT_FOUND → the session token for an older Worker) and `withAccessTokens(client, tokens)`, which `index.ts` wraps around the client so `AccountService` keeps passing the session token unchanged (one retry with a fresh token on a 401; sign-out still sends the session token, revoking all). The Cloud adapter takes `bearer`/`invalidateBearer`/`idempotencyKey` (`Idempotency-Key`, same on the retry) and maps the new codes (`AiTooLargeError`, `AiModelUnavailableError`; `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE` stops the index queue and ghost text). Keychain-only session: `AiKeyStore.setKey('cloudSession')` refuses the plain fallback (`NO_KEYCHAIN_SESSION_MESSAGE`) and `requestLink` checks `canStoreProviderKey()` first; `MYTHSCRIBE_E2E_PLAINTEXT_KEYS` is honoured only when `!app.isPackaged`. Channels `account:getUsage` (`UsageResult`, cursor) and `account:getPricing` (refreshes when an hour old via the optional `cloudPricing` handler dep; null before any answer). Renderer: `accountStore` `pricing`, `usage`/`usageCursor`/`usageBusy`/`usageError`, `loadPricing`, `loadUsage(more)`, a window-focus refresh of the balance and table (at most every 10 s, one listener at a time, removed by `resetAccountStore`); `features/account/hostedPricing.ts` (`useHostedPricing`, `hostedPricingNow`, `useHostedSource`); `BalanceSection.tsx` (the Account tab's balance: `account-balance`, `account-words-left`, `account-balance-warning`, `Add $10.00` packs, `account-pack-example-<variant>`, `account-privacy`, `account-terms`, spend table without tokens, `MythScribe Cloud models` with `account-model-price-<id>`, `account-usage-history`); `BalanceNotice.tsx` (was `CreditNotice`; `balance-notice`, always on Cloud, `data-warning` when warning); copy in `creditMeter.ts`; `ai/RequestCost.tsx` renders `describeRequest(request, !hosted)` under every answer; `EditPassWorkspace` quotes hosted passes and confirms above the threshold (`edit-pass-quote`), `importStore.estimateFor` and the `library:estimate` handler quote hosted jobs. Gaps: the Worker still accepts the session token as a bearer (S6 not fully in force until it stops); single requests and background indexing are never quoted; a stored plain-text session from before is still read.
- Usage meter and caps (F-15.5): `src/shared/cloudUsage.ts` is the one owner of the period and the warnings, imported by the app and the Worker: `USAGE_PERIOD_DAYS` 30 (a rolling window; prepaid credits have no billing cycle), `LOW_BALANCE_MICROS` (1.00 USD), `RUN_OUT_WARNING_DAYS` 3, `periodSpentMicros`, `projectedDaysLeft` (whole days at the pace since the first charge in the period, 1 to 30 active days, so a new account is not averaged over thirty; null with no spend, 0 at a balance ≤ 0), and `creditWarning` (`empty` → `low` → `runOut`). Hard caps are what already existed, nothing new: the Worker's `hasCredit` refusal at a balance ≤ 0 and the app's daily USD cap in `runAiRequest`, which prices Cloud requests at the Cloud rate; there is no server-side author cap, no migration, no new route. Wire: `CreditsResult` gains `periodDays`, `periodSpend`, `periodFirstChargeAt` (zod defaults, so the app still reads a Worker deployed before them; `spend` stays lifetime). Worker: `store.spendByFeature(userId, since = 0)` and `store.firstChargeAt(userId, since)` (d1 and memory, covered by the `(user_id, created_at)` index), `handleCredits` windows on `deps.now() - USAGE_PERIOD_MS`. Live balance: the Cloud provider's `onBalance?` option fires from `complete` and the stream's `done` (never on a failure), `index.ts` emits the event `account:balanceChanged { balanceMicros }`, and `accountStore.subscribe()` (one unsubscribe for both account events) replaces `credits.balanceMicros`, or loads credits once when none are held and the account is signed in; `creditsAt` is the store's "now" for the projection (eslint's `react-hooks/purity` refuses `Date.now()` in render). UI: `features/account/creditMeter.ts` owns the copy (`creditWarningText`, `runOutText`); `UsageMeter` in the Account tab's Credits section (`account-period-spent`, `account-run-out`, `account-credit-warning`, the per-feature table on `periodSpend` captioned "Last 30 days", `account-all-time` for the lifetime total); `CreditNotice.tsx` is the status-bar button (`credit-notice`, rendered last in `StatusBar`), shown only when the project's `AiSettings.source` is `cloud`, the account is signed in, and a warning applies; it loads credits once on mount and opens Settings on the Account tab through `useShellDialogStore.show('settings', 'account')` (`settingsTab`, cleared on close; `SettingsDialog` `initialTab`, ignored when that tab is not visible). e2e: the fake Worker keeps a balance (`CLOUD_BALANCE_MICROS` 1 003 000, so one charged chat crosses 1.00 USD) and the notice appears with no Settings visit and no Refresh. Gaps: only the balance is live, `periodSpend` is as old as the last `/credits` load (the Account tab reloads on mount); the live Worker serves the period fields only after the next `npm run cloud:deploy`.
- AI source choice (F-15.11): the source itself is F-15.4's `AiSettings.source` and the AI-tab picker; this feature added the two other places it shows. `project:create` takes an optional `aiSource` (`AiSource`); the handler writes it into the new project's AI settings row right after the synchronous `manager.create` and before it answers, so the renderer's first `aiSettings:get` already reads it (no renderer-side write, no race with the store's load). `projectStore.create(name, format, directory?, aiSource?)` passes it through. `CreateProjectWizard` is four steps since F-5.18 (`STEPS`: name, format, source, dial; the header reads `Step n of 4`; Back follows `PREVIOUS`): step 3 lists `AiSource.options` with `AI_SOURCE_LABEL`/`AI_SOURCE_MEANING`, defaults to `ownKey`, and a hint (`wizard-source-hint`) says what the choice needs next (the key under Settings › AI, or sign-in and credits under Settings › Account; `signedInEmail` comes from `WelcomeScreen`, which reads the app-wide `accountStore`) and that it can be switched later. In `AiSettingsTab`, `ModelField` renders `CloudRateLine` (`ai-model-rate-<tier>`) only while the source is `cloud`: the saved model's rate from `cloudRateFor` (the one table in `cloudRates.ts`), or a warning for a model outside the table, which the proxy refuses (`UNKNOWN_MODEL`). Cloud gate (decided by the author 2026-10-07, own key until Cloud launches): `CLOUD_AI_AVAILABLE` in `src/shared/cloudApi.ts` (false until the Worker with `/ai/complete` is deployed and the store exists). While false: `CreateProjectWizard` and `AiSettingsTab` (both take an optional `cloudAvailable` prop defaulting to the flag, so unit tests can drive both states) render the Cloud option disabled with `CLOUD_COMING_SOON` (`wizard-cloud-coming-soon`, `ai-source-cloud-coming-soon`); a project still stored on `cloud` keeps it (never rewritten) and the AI tab shows `ai-cloud-unavailable` (`CLOUD_UNAVAILABLE_NOTICE`) instead of the account line, with Test connection disabled; `CreditNotice` renders nothing; `buildCloudProvider` (`available` option, default the flag) throws `AiCloudUnavailableError` (`CLOUD_UNAVAILABLE`, a hard code in `indexQueue.ts` and a turn-off code in `ghostTextController.ts`) from `complete`, `stream`, and `testConnection` before any fetch. Regardless of the flag, a Cloud `NOT_FOUND` maps to `CLOUD_UNAVAILABLE` ("isn’t reachable for AI yet") instead of `PROVIDER (NOT_FOUND)`. The e2e creates the smoke project on the own key and checks Cloud disabled in the wizard and the AI tab (signed in too); the streamed Cloud chat step and the Cloud rate lines are out of the e2e until the flag flips (the fake Worker's `/ai/complete` stays, `cloudAiRequests` is asserted empty).
- Local models (F-5.15; no migration): `AiSource` gains `local` and `AI_PROVIDER_IDS` gains `local` (the ledger's `provider` column is a Drizzle text enum, so no SQL change); `providerForSource` maps a source to its model map/ledger id (used by the AI tab, the dial's data-sharing table, and the import estimate). `src/shared/ai.ts` owns `LOCAL_DEFAULT_MODELS`, `defaultModelsFor`, `LocalAiBaseUrl` (http/https, ≤ 300), `LocalAiSettings`, `isLoopbackUrl`; `AiModels.local` and `AppState.localAi` are defaulted for older files; `AiStatus.local` carries the address. `buildOpenAiProvider` takes `baseURL`/`id`/`label` (a compatible server gets `max_tokens`; `mapOpenAiError(err, label)` names it; a 404 there says to pull the model). `registry.ts` `buildLocalProvider` (placeholder key, `price` always $0) is built by `AiProviderRegistry.get('local')` from the live `local()` settings and rebuilt when the address changes. `ai:setLocalEndpoint` stores the address without a trailing slash and runs `backfillSummaries(true)`. Renderer: `aiStore.setLocalEndpoint`, `AiSettingsTab` `LocalEndpointForm` (Server address, Save, `LOCAL_QUALITY_WARNING` as `ai-local-warning`, an off-machine alert), the privacy line by source, Reset to defaults per provider; the wizard's hint for the local source. E2E: the local source pointed at the fake provider answers Test connection and a Plan turn at $0.0000.
- Assisted first run (F-5.18): the wizard's fourth step lists `AI_DIAL_LEVELS` with `AI_DIAL_LABEL`/`AI_DIAL_MEANING`, an explainer of the background work (`wizard-dial-explainer`), and preselects `RECOMMENDED_DIAL` (`AI_DATA_SHARING.summary.minDial`, i.e. Ask) with a Recommended badge; Off is one click. `project:create` takes an optional `aiDial` written in the same settings write as `aiSource` (omitted keeps Off, so `project:open` and every older caller are unchanged). `App.tsx`'s `onCreate` opens the assistant panel (`layoutStore.toggle('assistant')`) when the chosen dial reaches `query`'s `minDial` and the panel is closed; the layout is app-wide, so this is the only layout write and opening an existing project never touches it. Query mode and the quick actions need nothing new: a fresh project has no conversations, a new one starts in `query`, and `QuickActions` always renders. App tests' `fillWizard` picks Off unless told otherwise, and the e2e picks Off so the rest of the smoke run starts at dial 0 as before. Since 2026-10-06 (all AI in the assistant): the row is the Actions menu (`AiActionsMenu`).
- Updates (F-15.7): `src/shared/updates.ts` owns `UPDATE_CHANNELS` (`stable | beta`, `updaterChannelFor` → `latest | beta`), `UpdateSettings` (`channel`, `autoCheck`, `installedNotes`, `lastSeenVersion`; stored in app-state.json under `updates`), the `UpdateStatus` union (`unsupported | idle | checking | upToDate | downloading | ready | error`), `UpdateState`, `releaseNotesText` (GitHub's HTML → plain text, capped at `RELEASE_NOTES_MAX`; notes are never rendered as HTML), and the check timing (10 s after start, then every 6 h). Main: `src/main/updates/updateService.ts` is the one owner, built like `AccountService` (injected `AppUpdaterLike`, `Schedule` from `src/main/schedule.ts`, `onChange` → `updates:changed`, disposed on `will-quit`); `autoUpdater.ts` is the only file that imports `electron-updater` (a named import: the package has no default export). `index.ts` passes `updater: null` with a reason for an unpackaged build, `NODE_ENV=test`, and a Linux build that is not an AppImage. It sets `channel` and then `allowDowngrade = false` (assigning a channel turns downgrades on), downloads in the background, never restarts by itself (`autoInstallOnAppQuit`), keeps `ready` until the install instead of checking again, stores the downloaded version's notes so the next run can show "What's new" (`unseenNotes` is derived, cleared by `markSeen`), and shows only the first line of an updater error. Channels `updates:getState|check|setChannel|setAutoCheck|markSeen|install`; `updates:install` refuses while a project is open, so the renderer's `installNow()` goes through `closeProjectWithConfirm` first (the installer starts before the process exits). Renderer: `src/renderer/features/updates/` has `updateStore.ts` (`subscribe()` mounted once in `App.tsx`), `UpdatesSettingsTab.tsx` (the app-scope Updates tab, also on the welcome screen; `ReleaseNotesView`), and `UpdateNotice.tsx` in the header (`update-notice`: update ready, or what's new once after an update). Help › Check for updates… opens the tab and checks. Packaging: `electron-builder.yml` publishes to GitHub as a draft with `generateUpdatesFilesForAllChannels`, mac `dmg` + `zip` with the hardened runtime; Windows signing values arrive only as `-c.win.azureSignOptions.*` flags from `.github/workflows/release.yml`, which creates the draft from `docs/release-notes/<tag>.md`, builds the three platforms, verifies the Authenticode signature and the notarization staple, and publishes the draft last (`--prerelease` for a `-beta.N` tag). Recipe: `docs/RELEASING.md`. Known gaps: the real download → restart → install path and the workflow have never run (no signing accounts yet); the e2e covers the unsupported-build tab and the stored channel.
- Diagnostics (F-15.8): opt-in, off on install, no install ID, no Sentry, no Electron `crashReporter` (minidumps hold process memory and cannot be scrubbed). `src/shared/diagnostics.ts` owns the counter enum (`DIAGNOSTIC_COUNTERS`: `app.launch`, `project.create|open`, `export.run`, `focus.enter`, `proposal.accept|reject`, `ai.request.<AiFeatureId>`; a name outside it cannot be stored or sent; F-16.2: `DiagnosticCounterTally` drops an unknown counter or a bad count on read instead of failing, and `AppState.diagnostics` `.catch`es to the defaults, so a retired `ai.request.*` id never resets app-state.json), `CrashReport` (`main | renderer | processGone`), the scrubbers (`scrubMessage`: quoted text, URLs, emails, paths → placeholders, 300 chars; `scrubStack`: app-relative frames under the given roots, everything else `<external>`, function names dropped, 20 frames), `DiagnosticsSettings` (`enabled`, `counts` per day, `queue` ≤ 10, `lastSentDay`; app-state.json under `diagnostics`), and `DiagnosticsState` (`pending` is the pretty-printed JSON that would be sent: `diagnostics.ts` must not import `cloudApi.ts`, which imports it for `DiagnosticsBody`). Main: `src/main/diagnostics/diagnosticsService.ts` is the one owner, built like `UpdateService` (injected `send`, `Schedule`, `onChange` → `diagnostics:changed`): nothing is recorded while off, switching either way clears counts and queue, `count()` emits no event, `flush()` (30 s after start, then every 6 h) sends only completed days plus the queue and keeps everything silently on failure, counts older than 14 days are pruned. `diagnosticsClient.ts` is the typed `POST /diagnostics` (no auth header, 15 s timeout, trims to `DIAGNOSTICS_BODY_MAX` oldest rows first; the sender is null under `NODE_ENV=test` unless `MYTHSCRIBE_CLOUD_API_URL` is set); `crashHandlers.ts` reports `uncaughtException`/`unhandledRejection` and then shows Electron's error box itself (a listener suppresses the default one). Count hooks are one line each: `app.launch` and `focus.enter` (the `enter-full-screen` listener) in `index.ts`; create/open, `export.run`, `proposal:settle`, and the ledger insert (so a cache hit counts, like F-5.14) in `handlers.ts`. Channels `diagnostics:getState|setEnabled|reportRendererError`. Renderer: `src/renderer/features/diagnostics/` (`diagnosticsStore.ts` subscribed once in `App.tsx`, `DiagnosticsSettingsTab.tsx` the app-scope Diagnostics tab with the "See exactly what would be sent" preview, `rendererErrors.ts` the `window` `error`/`unhandledrejection` listeners called from `main.tsx`, capped per session). Worker: `cloud/src/diagnostics.ts` (`handleDiagnostics`: unauthenticated, 32 KB cap, zod body, per-counter clamp, 204; the body's `DiagnosticWireCounter` in `cloudApi.ts` also accepts `ai.request.<CloudFeatureId>`, so an older Worker counts a newer app's feature instead of refusing every report) upserts aggregates only into `diagnostic_counts` and `diagnostic_crashes` (grouped by `crashFingerprint` = SHA-256 of name + top 5 frames + version; migration `0003_diagnostics.sql`); no IP is stored and there is no code-side rate limit (zone rate limiting is the dashboard option). The operator reads reports with the two `wrangler d1 execute` queries in `cloud/README.md`.
- Developer tools (2026-10-07, `plans/plan-devtools.md`): an app-wide switch, `devTools` in app-state.json (off, read leniently), in Settings › Advanced (`AdvancedSettingsTab.tsx`, tab id `advanced`). `src/shared/devtools.ts` owns the vocabulary: `DevLogEntry` (`error|warn`, source `main|renderer|ipc|ai`), `DevAiRequest` (feature, provider, model, tier, prompt version, status `running|ok|cached|failed|cancelled|skipped`, `waitMs`/`firstTokenMs`/`totalMs`, tokens in/out/cached/reasoning, cap, cost, finish reason, answer length, error code, `note`), `GHOST_SKIP_REASONS` with labels, caps `DEV_LOG_MAX` 500 / `DEV_AI_MAX` 200 / `DEV_TEXT_MAX`, `DEVTOOLS_CHORD` (Ctrl+Shift+D), `redactSecrets`/`redactSettings`, and the pure `formatDiagnosticsReport`. Main `src/main/devtools/devToolsService.ts` is the one owner of the switch and both ring buffers (memory only; switching off drops them; every recording call is a no-op while off): `record`/`recordError`, `ipcFailure` (every error envelope: `createHandler`/`register` in `registry.ts` take an optional `FailureObserver`, `handlers.ts` passes it; the `[ipc]` console line, `IPC_LOG_PREFIX` in `errors.ts`, is skipped by the console hook), `hookConsole` (main's `console.error/warn`, installed once in `index.ts`), `observer` (the request path's `AiRequestDeps.observe`: `runAiRequest`/`runAiStream` call `start → prepared → sent → firstToken → done|failed`; the prompt and raw answer are held beside the row for `devtools:requestText`), `ghostSkip`, `ghostEmpty` (the `ai:ghostText` handler notes an answer post-processing emptied, with raw length, finish reason, output tokens against the cap, and reasoning tokens), `openChromium` (refused while off). Providers report `finishReason` and `usage.reasoningTokens` (`completion_tokens_details.reasoning_tokens`) for it. `handlers.ts` wraps `aiFailure` to log DISABLED, which never reaches the path. Channels `devtools:getState|setEnabled|snapshot|requestText|log|ghostSkip|clear|openChromium|report` (all `read`); events `devtools:changed|logAdded|requestChanged` (pushed only while on); `index.ts` rebuilds the native menu on change (`installApplicationMenu` now takes `devTools` and returns `{ dispose, rebuild }`). Menu: `menuFor({ devTools })` appends `DEVELOPER_MENU_ENTRIES` (`openDeveloperTools`, `openChromiumDevTools`) to Help; `MenuBar.tsx` and main both use it. Renderer `src/renderer/features/devtools/`: `devToolsStore.ts` (mirror of main's buffers plus local `open` and revealed `texts`; subscribed once in `App.tsx`), `DevToolsPanel.tsx` (a bottom drawer over the layout, tabs AI requests / Log, filters, clear, Copy diagnostics via `navigator.clipboard`, Chromium DevTools), `rendererDevLog.ts` (`listenForDevLog` from `main.tsx`: window errors, rejections, console wraps; `reportGhostSkip`, which `ghostTextController.ts` calls once per change of reason from `throttleReason` in `aiThrottle.ts` and its own checks). `DevToolsShortcut` in `App.tsx` binds the chord only while on. E2E: the step before Flexible nesting turns it on, opens the panel from Help, and fails a ghost-text probe on the fake server (`DEVTOOLS_FAIL_SENTINEL` → HTTP 500 → PROVIDER) through the local source.
- Supporter license (F-15.9, absorbs F-15.6): `src/shared/license.ts` is the one owner of the token format and the extras, imported by the Worker, main, and the renderer: `LICENSE_GRACE_DAYS` 90 since 2026-10-10 (`LICENSE_GRACE_MS`), `LICENSE_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS` 24 h, `LICENSE_PUBLIC_KEY_JWK` (Ed25519 public JWK; a documented placeholder that verifies nothing until the operator pastes the real one; `licensePublicKey(env)` takes `MYTHSCRIBE_LICENSE_PUBLIC_KEY` first, for dev and e2e), `LicenseClaims` `{ v: 1, sub: userId, iat, exp }` (epoch ms), the base64url helpers, `encodeLicensePayload` / `formatLicenseToken` / `parseLicenseToken` (`base64url(JSON claims).base64url(signature)`; parsing never verifies), `ACCENTS` (`default | ember | sky | rose | gold | violet`, the three `--ms-accent*` values each), `SupporterSettings` (`token`, `refreshedAt`, `accent`; in `app-state.json` under `supporter`, plain JSON because the token is signed) and `SupporterStatus` (`licensed`, `since`, `validUntil`, `offline`, `product`, `accent`; the token never crosses IPC). Wire: `LicenseResult` (`GET /license` → `{ token | null, product: CreditPack | null }`) in `cloudApi.ts`; the Supporter product is bought through the existing `POST /billing/checkout` by its variant. Worker: migration `0004_supporter.sql` (`supporter_licenses` one row per user, `order_ref` UNIQUE, `granted_at`, `revoked_at`), `store.ts` `grantSupporter` (`'applied' | 'duplicate'`; a new order after a refund re-grants) / `revokeSupporter` / `findSupporter`, `license.ts` `handleLicense` (bearer; signs a fresh token per call with `importSigningKey`/`signEd25519` in `crypto.ts` (Web Crypto `Ed25519`); `token: null` without an active license; 503 `NOT_CONFIGURED` only for a licensed account with no key), `credits.ts` resolves a variant from packs ∪ supporter, so the webhook grants on a paid `order_created` and revokes on `order_refunded` for that variant and never books a credit event for it; the checkout message is `UNKNOWN_VARIANT` now. Vars: `LEMONSQUEEZY_SUPPORTER` (`{ variantId, url, priceCents }`) and the secret `LICENSE_SIGNING_KEY` (private JWK); `npm run cloud:license-keygen` (`cloud/scripts/licenseKeygen.mjs`) prints both halves; the operator recipe is in `cloud/README.md`. Main: `licenseVerifier.ts` `verifyLicenseToken(token, jwk, now)` (`node:crypto` `createPublicKey({ format: 'jwk' })` + `verify(null, …)`; expired, forged, malformed, or unusable key → null); `cloudAuthClient.license`; `AccountService` owns the license because it follows the account: `supporter()` (cache only; a token counts only when it verifies *and* its `sub` is the signed-in `userId`; `offline` when no refresh reached the Worker within the interval), `refreshLicense()`, `supporterCheckoutUrl()` (the variant from the last `/license` answer, kept in memory), `setAccent()` (VALIDATION for a non-default accent while the extras are locked, see F-15.13), a quiet refresh on construction when signed in, after `signIn`, and every `LICENSE_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS` on the injected `Schedule` (a token that does not verify is refused and the cached one kept; an unreachable Worker leaves the cache), `forget()` (sign-out, 401) clears the token and pushes `account:supporterChanged` (the accent choice stays stored, reported as `default` while the extras are locked). In-flight answers are matched to the session by token (`sameSession`), not by object: `refresh()` replaces the state object, and a `/license` answer racing the Account tab's mount-time `refresh()` was dropped before that (the e2e caught it, failing on the badge about half the time). Channels `account:getSupporter|refreshSupporter|buySupporter|setAccent` in `handlers.ts` (buy refuses any URL `isCheckoutUrl` rejects, then `openExternal`), event `account:supporterChanged`. Renderer: `accountStore` `supporter`/`supporterBusy`/`supporterError` (loaded by `App` at start, signed out too; not cleared by the store on sign-out, main pushes that), `SupporterSection` in `AccountSettingsTab.tsx` (`account-supporter-badge`, `account-supporter-buy`, a Refresh named "Refresh license", the offline line), `AccentPicker.tsx` (`account-accent-<id>`, non-default swatches disabled while the extras are locked), `App` sets `document.documentElement.dataset.accent` (deleted for `default`), and `tokens.css` holds the `:root[data-accent=…]` blocks (hexes duplicated from `ACCENTS`; change both). e2e: the fake Cloud API signs with `e2e/fixtures/license-test-key.json` (throwaway; never the Worker's key) and the app is launched with that public half. Operator steps: keygen → `wrangler secret put LICENSE_SIGNING_KEY` → paste the public JWK into `license.ts` → the $39 product and `LEMONSQUEEZY_SUPPORTER` → `cloud:migrate` (0004) → `cloud:deploy`; until then `/license` answers `{ token: null, product: null }` (404 on the live Worker before the deploy) and the tab says the license is not on sale yet.
- Document zoom and interface size (F-7.10): two app-wide view settings, both in `appState.view` (`ViewSettings` in `src/shared/zoom.ts`: `editorZoom: ZoomFactor` default 1, `uiScale: UiScale` default `medium`). `zoom.ts` also owns `ZOOM_STEPS` (0.67–2, ten steps), `nextZoom` (snaps an off-table value to the nearest step first, stays put at the ends), `formatZoom` (`'125 %'`), `UI_SCALES`/`UI_SCALE_FACTORS` (`small` 0.9, `medium` 1, `large` 1.15), `UI_SCALE_LABELS`. Main owns the values: `view:get`, `view:zoomDocument { step }` (steps and persists, touches no window), `view:setUiScale { scale }` (persists, then `setZoomFactor` on every live window) in `handlers.ts`; `createWindow(uiScale)` in `index.ts` applies the factor on `ready-to-show` before `show()`. Renderer: `features/shell/viewStore.ts` (`load()` at App mount, `zoomDocument(step)` invokes and toasts `Document zoom 110 %`, `setUiScale` invokes silently, `useEditorZoom()`); `features/shell/zoom.ts` is the pure input side (`zoomStepFor(event)` for Ctrl+= / Ctrl+- / Ctrl+0 and `+`; `wheelZoomStepFor(event, now, lastAt)` for Ctrl+wheel, Ctrl only, `deltaY` sign, one step per `WHEEL_ZOOM_COALESCE_MS` 150 ms burst so a wheel notch or a trackpad pinch is one step); `ZoomShortcuts` in `App.tsx` (always mounted, capture phase; `keydown` and a non-passive `wheel` listener, both `preventDefault` + `stopPropagation`, so the editor never sees the chord and Chromium never zooms the window itself). The page scales through `editorStyle(settings, zoom)` in `features/editor/column.ts` (`maxWidth × zoom`, `fontSize × zoom`; line height is unitless and the spacings are em, so they follow), read by `DocumentEditor` and `StackedEditor`; in focus mode the `%` max-width override stays and only the font scales; the notes editor and the Settings preview are chrome and never zoom. Interface size scales everything (the page included; document zoom multiplies on top). Shortcuts `zoomIn`/`zoomOut`/`zoomReset` in `APP_SHORTCUTS` and the three View items (`when: 'always'`) carry the chords as native accelerators, the fallback when the page does not claim the key; `runMenuAction` routes them above the project gate. Settings › Appearance (`AppearanceSettingsTab.tsx`, app scope): the three-size radiogroup `appearance-ui-scale-<id>` and the document zoom row `appearance-document-zoom` with − / + / Reset. Fit at Large in 1280 × 720 (CSS viewport 1113 × 626) was measured with a scratch Playwright script and fixed where it failed: labelled buttons in the toolbar, tag bar, metadata pane, and summary block are `shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap` (a label never breaks mid-word; rows wrap between buttons), the metadata pane's Brief and Summary rows are `flex-wrap` (the pane used to scroll sideways at Medium too), the toolbar's right cluster wraps, and the settings tab strip wraps (six tabs overflowed the 640 px dialog at every size). Known and left: the sidebar's create bar (`CreateNodeBar.tsx`) wraps its three labels at narrow widths; making them nowrap turns the bar into two rows even at the default window, so it needs a design call. e2e: Ctrl+= → font 17.6 px and column 770 px with `getZoomFactor()` still 1, Ctrl+wheel → one more step, Appearance → Large → `getZoomFactor()` 1.15 with the document untouched, Ctrl+0, and `view: { editorZoom: 1, uiScale: 'medium' }` in app-state.json.
- App license and trial (F-15.13, AI-BILLING-SPEC M1, slice B3a 2026-10-07): `src/shared/appAccess.ts` owns `APP_TRIAL_DAYS` 30, `TRIAL_ENDED_MESSAGE`, `AppTrial` (`startedAt`, `lastSeenAt`; `trial` in `app-state.json`, `.nullable().catch(null)`), `AppAccess` (`state: trial | licensed | expired`, `trialEndsAt` ISO, `daysLeft`), and the pure `trialEndsAt` (local midnight 30 calendar days after the start day), `touchTrial` (starts the clock, only moves `lastSeenAt` forward), `appAccessFor` (license wins; the clock is max(now, lastSeenAt), so a clock turned back gains nothing), `canWrite` (null = writable until main answers), `extrasUnlocked` (Sepia, custom themes, accents: on in `trial` and `licensed`, off when `expired` or unknown; changed by the author 2026-10-10, was license-only). Main: `src/main/account/appAccess.ts` `AppAccessService` (built in `index.ts` after the account; `licensed` = `account.supporter().licensed`, i.e. the F-15.9 token; caches the state, recomputes on its `Schedule` at most hourly and exactly at the trial's end, and on `refresh()`, which `onSupporterChange` and the `account:refreshSupporter` handler call; pushes `app:accessChanged` on change, and `index.ts` pushes `account:supporterChanged` with it because the reported accent follows the access; `writable()`, `assertWritable()` → `AppError('VALIDATION', TRIAL_ENDED_MESSAGE, { code: 'TRIAL_ENDED' })`). `enforced` is false for a build that cannot verify a license and under the developer exemption `devLicenseExempt(process.env)` (`MYTHSCRIBE_DEV_LICENSE` set to the author's secret, checked against the SHA-256 `DEV_LICENSE_SHA256` so the code holds no usable value, read once in main at launch; no setting, menu, or channel touches it; the author's setup is in `docs/PERSONAL-USE.md`), so the state is always `licensed`. The extras gate: `view:setTheme`/`view:saveCustomTheme` and `paintWindows` in `handlers.ts` ask `extrasUnlocked(access.status())`; `AccountService` takes an optional `extrasUnlocked` (main passes the same; default the license) for `setAccent` and the reported `accent` (the stored pick, `default` while locked). The offline grace is `LICENSE_GRACE_DAYS` 90 (changed by the author 2026-10-10, was 14; tokens signed before the Worker's deploy keep their 14-day `exp`). The gate: `src/main/ipc/channelAccess.ts` `CHANNEL_ACCESS: Record<Channel, 'read' | 'write' | 'ai'>` (a new channel does not compile until classified; its test checks it equals `channels`); `registerHandlers` wraps `register` so every `write` channel asserts first; `ai` channels pass and the request path refuses instead (`providerFor` in `handlers.ts` throws `AiTrialEndedError`, code `TRIAL_ENDED`, before any text is built; the summary backfill and the voice job's `providerReady` check `access.writable()`), so AI failures stay data. `read` includes export, compile, provenance/tag/entity export, backups and restore, the account and purchase, app settings, and the project's view preferences (layout, session, focus, editor and AI settings, goals, backgrounds, reference pins, conversations). Channel `app:getAccess`, event `app:accessChanged`, `AiErrorCode` `TRIAL_ENDED` (in the index queue's hard codes and VibeWrite's turn-off codes). Renderer: `features/account/appAccessStore.ts` (`useAppAccessStore`, `useCanWrite`, `useExtrasUnlocked` for `ThemePicker`, `AccentPicker`, and App's theme paint; `viewStore`'s `switchTheme` reads `extrasUnlocked` directly; loaded and subscribed once by `App`), `TrialBanner.tsx` under the header (`read-only-banner` role alert after the trial, `trial-banner` in the last `TRIAL_BANNER_DAYS` 7, `trial-buy` opens Settings › Account), `DocumentEditor` and `NotesEditor` add `useCanWrite()` to `editable`; the Account tab's license section (`LicenseSection`, region "MythScribe license", test ids still `account-supporter-*`, `account-trial-status`) replaces the Supporter section. Known gaps: other editable surfaces (sheets, metadata fields, tree rename) stay interactive and their saves fail with the trial message; the e2e has no read-only step (the smoke run holds a license), so the manual check is a scratch Playwright script on the built app: write `trial: { startedAt: now − 40 days, lastSeenAt: now }` into the userData `app-state.json`, launch, and expect `read-only-banner`, a `contenteditable=false` scene, `document:save` refused, `backups:now` ok. With the placeholder `LICENSE_PUBLIC_KEY_JWK` no build verifies a license, so every install turns read-only 30 days after its first launch of this version until the operator steps are done.
- Themes (F-7.8): `src/shared/themes.ts` owns the ids (`dark | light | high-contrast | sepia`), `BUILT_IN_THEMES` (label, `scheme`, `supporter`, the eight `THEME_TOKENS` hexes, preview accent), `CustomTheme` (`custom-<12 hex>`, name ≤ 40, `base`, all eight colours), `CUSTOM_THEMES_MAX` 8, and the pure `resolveTheme(settings, licensed)` / `availableThemes` / `nextTheme` / `themeNeedsLicense` / `themeBackground`. Gating (decided 2026-10-05): Dark, Light, High contrast free; Sepia and custom themes are Supporter extras; a locked or missing choice stays stored and paints Dark, like the accent. Persisted in `ViewSettings.theme` / `customThemes` (both `.catch` to Dark / `[]`, so a bad hand edit never resets the whole file). Channels `view:setTheme`, `view:saveCustomTheme` (creates or replaces, then selects it), `view:deleteCustomTheme` (current one falls back to its base; allowed unlicensed); handlers check `account.supporter().licensed` and call `setBackgroundColor` on every window (`ClosableWindow` grew it); `index.ts` creates the window with `themeBackground(view)`. Painting: `tokens.css` holds one `:root[data-theme=…]` block per built-in (dark is `:root`); the eight hexes are duplicated in `BUILT_IN_THEMES` and `themes.test.ts` compares them, so change both. `App.tsx` sets `data-theme` (absent for dark), `data-scheme`, `color-scheme`, and for a custom theme the eight variables inline on `<html>`. Light-scheme themes get darker Supporter accents (`:root[data-scheme='light'][data-accent=…]`); High contrast keeps its yellow over any accent. `viewStore`: `setTheme` (silent), `switchTheme` (View › Switch theme, toast `Theme: <label>`, cycles `nextTheme`), `saveCustomTheme`, `deleteCustomTheme`. UI: `shell/ThemePicker.tsx` (`appearance-theme-<id>` cards, `-new`/`-edit`/`-delete`, locked cards disabled) and `shell/ThemeEditor.tsx` (`theme-editor`) at the top of Settings › Appearance, with the `AccentPicker` moved there from the Account tab (test ids unchanged). Known gaps: the shell paints the theme after `view:get` answers, so a non-dark theme can show one dark frame at launch (the window background already matches); an accent error lands in `supporterError`, shown only on the Account tab.
- Page edges (F-7.11): the third app-wide view setting, `ViewSettings.pageEdges` (default `true`; an app-state.json from before it parses as on). Main persists it through `view:setPageEdges { on }` (answers the `ViewSettings`) and does nothing else with it; the renderer applies it. `viewStore`: `setPageEdges(on)` silent (Settings › Appearance checkbox `appearance-page-edges`), `togglePageEdges()` toasts `Page edges shown/hidden` (View › Page edges, `when: 'always'`, no chord, routed above the project gate in `runMenuAction`), `usePageEdges()`. Drawing: `editor/column.ts` `columnClass(sheet)` = `COLUMN` plus `SHEET` (`.ms-sheet` in `app.css`: `--ms-sheet` background, `--ms-line` hairline, soft shadow, 2 px radius) and `deskClass(sheet)` = `bg-desk px-4 py-6` for the scroll container; tokens `--ms-desk` (= `--ms-bg`, so Off is pixel-identical to before) and `--ms-sheet` (one step lighter, same hue; a theme sets both, sheet lighter). The column's own `px-6` is the margin, so toggling never moves the text. Single document: the `EditorContent` wrapper is the sheet, its scroll container the desk; `sheet = pageEdges && !focus` (focus mode keeps F-6.4's surface). Stacked view: the `<section>` of each `Region` is now the column (moved off the `h2` and the toolbar-less `EditorContent`, which renders `py-6` only) and becomes the sheet with `my-6`; the stack's scroll container is the desk (`bg-desk px-4`); separators stay on the desk between sheets; `EmptyFolder`, notes, and the Settings preview are untouched. The desk's side gap is 16 px on purpose: it comes out of the pane width, and the e2e's F-3.6 check (a 900 px setting yields a column wider than 700 px) has only ~742 px of pane at that point. e2e: the sheet class on the column, View › Page edges hides it with a toast and writes `pageEdges: false`, the Appearance checkbox restores it.
- Assistant name (F-7.13): `src/shared/assistantName.ts` owns `DEFAULT_ASSISTANT_NAME` (Ms Scribe), `ASSISTANT_NAME_MAX` 24, `StoredAssistantName` (blank, overlong, or unreadable parses as the default), `normalizeAssistantName`, and `nameAssistant(text, name)`, which renames "AI assistant" / "the assistant" / "Assistant" in a shared string. The name is the fourth app-wide view setting (`ViewSettings.assistantName`); main stores it through `view:setAssistantName { name }` and calls `HandlerDeps.onAssistantNameChanged`, which `index.ts` wires to the menu's `rebuild` (`InstallMenuDeps.assistantName`, `MenuContext.assistantName`, `menuItemLabel(item, format, name)`). The renderer reads it with `useAssistantName()` (components) or `currentAssistantName()` (`featureLabel`, stores) from `viewStore`; `AssistantNameField.tsx` heads Settings › AI. Shared labels and help texts (`AI_DATA_SHARING`, `USE_AI_MEANING`, `ASSISTANT_MODE_MEANING`, `DOCK_PANEL_LABEL`, menu and shortcut labels) keep the generic word and go through `nameAssistant` where they are rendered; main's messages, the cost registry, and prompts never do.
- Sections (F-7.14): `tokens.css` derives `--ms-surface-panel` (side panels), `--ms-surface-panel-title` (title strips), `--ms-surface-page` / `--ms-surface-sheet` (the writing pane and sheet), and `--ms-surface-gap` (between stacked panels) with `color-mix` from the theme's own colours and per-theme amounts (`--ms-panel-shade`, `--ms-title-tint`, `--ms-page-lift`, `--ms-gap-shade`) in each theme block, so custom themes (inline colours on <html>) derive theirs. Tailwind: `bg-panel`, `bg-panel-title`, `bg-page`, `bg-gap` (the old `bg-desk` is `bg-page`). Every docked side panel root is `bg-panel` and its heading row `PANEL_TITLE` (`Dock.tsx`); `DockColumn` is `bg-gap` with `gap-1.5`, and `DockSlot` lost its `first` prop (no border between stacked panels). `EditorColumn` paints `bg-page` except in focus mode, where the backdrop shows through. `themes.test.ts` keeps body and secondary text at AA on every new surface for each built-in theme.
- Settings dialog (F-7.5): `src/renderer/features/shell/settingsDialogTabs.ts` is the tab registry (`SETTINGS_DIALOG_TABS`, Editor and AI (F-5.1) with `scope: 'project'`, Appearance (F-7.10), Account (F-15.2), Updates (F-15.7), and Diagnostics (F-15.8) with `scope: 'app'`; the tab strip wraps since F-7.10; `settingsTabsFor(format)` keeps only the app-wide tabs when no project is open, which is when the welcome screen's header button, Ctrl+,, and Tools › Settings open it; never a placeholder) and `SettingsDialog.tsx` the modal (role `dialog`, ARIA tablist like `SidebarTabs`, Escape/X/backdrop close, initial focus on the active tab, no Tab trap, same as `DialogHost`). It is not routed through `dialogStore` (whose modals are confirm/prompt-shaped). `SettingsButton` in `App.tsx` owns the open state, the Ctrl/Cmd+, `keydown` listener, and the dialog mount; it renders only while a project is open, so the shortcut cannot fire on the welcome screen. The active tab is local state, reset on open; a caller may name the tab to open on (`show('settings', tab)`, F-15.5). Fixed size since 2026-10-07 (decided by the author): `h-[min(640px,85vh)] w-[min(640px,90vw)]` on every tab (`data-testid="settings-dialog"`), only the tab panel scrolls; the e2e compares the bounding box on the Editor and AI tabs.
- Layout (F-7.2): `src/shared/layout.ts` owns the panel limits (`LAYOUT_LIMITS`), the zod `Layout` (`sidebar`/`notes` with `open` and `size` as fractions of the window width), `defaultLayout()`, and the clamps (`clampPanel`, `clampForEditorMin`, which keeps the editor at 30 % by clamping the dragged panel). App state stores it (`layout` field, defaulted so old files load); `layout:get`/`layout:set` read and write it. `src/renderer/features/shell/layoutStore.ts` is the renderer owner (loaded at app start, optimistic with one debounced write, registered with the pending-save registry; `resetLayoutStore()` in tests); `ResizeHandle.tsx` is the one drag handle (`role="separator"`, aria values in percent, px deltas that callers turn into fractions); widths render as `vw` so panels follow window resizes with no listener. The sidebar toggle lives in the top bar; the notes toggle in the editor toolbars. Since F-5.4, F-9.6, and the tags column (2026-10-06) there are five `LAYOUT_PANELS` (`sidebar`, `notes`, `tags` 15–35 % closed at 0.2, `assistant` 20–50 %, `references` 15–35 %, closed at 0.22 by default); `StoredLayout` defaults the later ones so an older app-state file loads. Five panels at their floors no longer leave the editor its 30 %, so `normalizeLayout(layout, keep)` first shrinks in the order assistant, references, tags, notes, sidebar and then closes in `CLOSE_ORDER` (tags, references, notes, assistant, sidebar), never `keep` (the panel being opened). The e2e reopens the tags column with `showTags()` where a step needs it. Dock (layout 3c): `src/shared/dock.ts` owns `DOCK_PANELS` (the five side panels plus `editor`), `defaultDock()`, `isValidDock`/`normalizeDock` (the contract refuses an invalid `layout.dock.columns`; `StoredLayout` repairs or defaults it, so old files keep their widths), and the pure moves `movePanel` (`beside` = new column, `stack` = into a column; never stacks the editor; returns the input itself for a no-op), `stepPanel` (menu Move left/right; skips columns whose panels are closed), and `dropTargetAt` (`DOCK_EDGE_PX` edge strips open a column, halves stack; the editor only takes beside drops). `layout.ts` counts the editor minimum per column: `columnWidth` (first open panel's size), `columnLimits` (tightest bounds of the open panels), `withColumnSize` (resizing sets every stacked panel), `clampForEditorMin` and `normalizeLayout` work on columns. The store adds `movePanel`/`stepPanel`/`resetLayout` and `isColumnShown`. Renderer: `features/shell/Dock.tsx` (`DockColumn` = width, border, handle on the edge facing the editor, labelled after its first open panel; `DockSlot` = drop zone, capture-phase listeners active only while `dockDragStore` holds a dragged panel, indicator `dock-drop-indicator`; `DockPanelControls` = grip `Move <Panel>` plus menu `<Panel> panel options` on the shared `ContextMenu`); panels render content only (their headers carry the controls) and `App.tsx` `ProjectScreen` maps the shown columns, one keyed list in and out of focus mode so the editor never remounts. Panel button names now overlap (`Move Assistant`), so e2e queries for the header toggles use `exact: true`. The references toggle (`ReferencesToggleButton`) sits in the top bar beside the assistant toggle; View › References (`toggleReferences`) leaves focus mode first, then toggles.
- Resume where you left off (F-1.7): `src/shared/session.ts` owns `ProjectSession` (every field defaulted; `parseStoredSession` is lenient, cutting an over-long `positions` list; `withPosition` keeps the most recent `SESSION_POSITIONS_MAX` = 200 first), stored under the settings key `session` (`getProjectSession`/`setProjectSession` in `src/main/project/settingsStore.ts`; channels `session:get`/`session:set`). `src/renderer/features/project/sessionStore.ts` holds only what no other store owns (`positions`, `sceneDetailsOpen`, the one-shot `caretFocusId`) and snapshots the rest from its owners when it writes (tree `selectedId`/`collapsed`/`tagFilter`, entity `selectedId`, layout `sidebar.tab`, outline `folderView`, focus `active`). `App.tsx` calls `load(Promise.all([tree, tags, entities]))` on project open: it restores only ids that still exist, then subscribes to those stores (slice comparisons, so word counts are not moves) and registers its flush with the pending-save registry; `clear()` runs first on close so leaving focus mode and emptying the tree are not recorded. `DocumentEditor` (single-document view only) records the caret on `onSelectionUpdate`, restores it with `setTextSelection` once ready, and takes the focus (`focus(null, { scrollIntoView: false })`) when `takeCaretFocus(id)` says so; `editor/scrollMemory.ts` `useScrollMemory(ref, id, ready)` restores and records the scroller's `scrollTop` for the document and, keyed by folder id, for `StackedEditor` once every region has loaded. `NotesPanel`'s Scene details state moved into the session store. The e2e checks it across the F-7.9 relaunch (same scene, caret, folded folder, sidebar tab).
- `src/renderer/features/project/pendingSaves.ts` — the pending-save registry: `registerPendingSave(flush)` returns an unsubscribe; `flushPendingSaves()` runs every flusher and rethrows the first failure. The project store awaits it before create/open/close and in `closeWindow()`. F-3.2 autosave registers here.
- Window close and quit: with a project open, main prevents the window `close`, emits `window:close-requested`, the renderer flushes and invokes `window:close`, and main closes the project and windows. `manager.close()` runs on `will-quit` (not `before-quit`) so the database outlives the flush. A failed flush leaves the window open with a toast and invokes `window:close-cancelled`, which resets main's `quitRequested` so an abandoned Cmd+Q does not make the next plain window close quit the app (macOS; manual check only, the e2e does not drive Cmd+Q).
- Crash safety (F-8.3; no migration): WAL in `src/main/db/connection.ts`; close/quit flush above; `src/renderer/features/project/saveOnBlur.ts` `installSaveOnBlur()` (mounted in `App.tsx`) runs `flushPendingSaves` on window `blur` and on `visibilitychange` to hidden, toasting a failure. Recovery journal: `src/shared/recovery.ts` (`RECOVERY_KINDS` `document | notes`, `RECOVERY_STASH_MS` 250, `RecoveryItem`, `RecoveryRestored`); `src/main/project/recoveryJournal.ts` keeps `<project>/recovery/<kind>-<id>.json` (atomic through `writeTextAtomic`, no fsync — an app crash keeps the page cache; ids outside `[A-Za-z0-9-]` refused; malformed, misnamed, and `.tmp` files deleted on read). Channels `recovery:stash|clear|list|restore|discard`; `list`/`restore` drop entries whose node is gone or whose content equals the stored one, and `restore` saves documents through `saveFromEditor` in `handlers.ts` (the same path as `document:save`: word log, summary/mention queue). `createAutosaveStore` takes an optional `journal { stash, clear }`: a per-record trailing stash timer starts on the first edit, a save with nothing newer pending clears the entry and `reload` clears dropped drafts, both only for ids stashed this session (a save that beat the throttle sends no IPC); journal failures are swallowed (the real save reports disk trouble). Only the document and notes stores journal; scene metadata and entity drafts do not. Each stash sends the whole document JSON. `features/project/recovery.ts` `offerRecovery()` runs after `init` (project already current) and `open`, never `create`: Recover → flush, `recovery:restore`, `reload` the open editors, merge word counts, refresh goals; Discard… asks again (`danger`), and "Keep for later" leaves the journal for the next open. A dead renderer makes main close the project (`render-process-gone`), so the usual path is the prompt on reopen.
- Automatic backups (F-8.4; no migration, no dependency): `src/shared/backups.ts` owns `BACKUP_INTERVALS` (15/30/60/120 min), `BACKUP_KEEP_CHOICES` (5/10/20/50), `BackupSettings` (app-state `backups`, all defaulted: on, 30 min, on close, keep 10, `folder` null = default), `BackupSettingsPatch` (`folder` accepts only `null`: a path comes only from main's `backups:chooseFolder` dialog), `BackupEntry`, `BackupState`, the name helpers (`<Name> YYYY-MM-DD HHmmss.zip` in `<Name> (<id8>)/`; a renamed project keeps its subfolder by the id suffix). `src/main/backups/zip.ts` is our own stored/deflated zip writer/reader on `node:zlib` (no zip64, no encryption; size and CRC checked, inflate capped). `backupArchive.ts`: `createBackupArchive` (a `VACUUM INTO` snapshot as `project.db` plus the folder minus `-wal`/`-shm`, `recovery/`, `*.tmp`, and a backup folder nested inside the project; written to `.tmp` and renamed), `listBackups`/`pruneBackups` (only names matching the pattern; `listBackups` marks a kept backup, `pruneBackups` deletes only rolling ones), `extractBackup` (relative names only, 2 GB cap, SQLite header check, never into an existing folder). `backupService.ts` `BackupService` (built in `index.ts`; default folder `Documents/MythScribe Backups`, or `<userData>/backups` under `MYTHSCRIBE_USER_DATA`): change detection by `SELECT total_changes()` against a baseline taken on open (its `onChange` listener is registered after the main one in `handlers.ts`) and after each backup; the timer runs through the `Schedule` seam; `manager.onBeforeClose` (new; listener errors are logged and the close proceeds) runs the on-close backup. Everything is synchronous on main (fine at novel sizes; large image assets pause main briefly). Automatic failures are kept as `lastError`, pushed on `backups:changed`, and toasted once by `features/backups/backupStore.ts`. Channels `backups:get|setSettings|chooseFolder|now|reveal|restore`; `restore { file? }` takes only a listed backup of the open project (else NOT_FOUND) and extracts beside it, or with no file asks for a zip and a parent folder; the copy is `<Name> (restored YYYY-MM-DD HHmm).mythscribe` (` 2`, ` 3` on collision), opened through `manager.open` (migrations run), removed again if it fails to open. A restored copy keeps the project id, so its backups share the original's subfolder. Renderer: `BackupsSettingsTab.tsx` (app scope, after AI), `projectStore.restoreBackup(file?)` (flush first, no recovery offer: the journal is never in a backup). Kept backups (F-16.31, Switch-over slice 1): `KEPT_BACKUP_LABEL` (`before story memory`), `keptBackupFileName`, `isKeptBackupName`, `BackupEntry.kept`; `backupsToPrune` skips kept entries and does not count them toward `keep`; `BackupService.backupNow({ kept? })` answers `{ state, file }` (`backups:now` answers the state) and writes a kept name through the same `createBackupArchive`; the tab marks the row "(kept: before story memory)"; a restored kept backup opens as `<Name> before story memory (restored …)`. A project whose own name ends in the label has its rolling backups read as kept: retention can only keep too much.
- Drafts (F-8.5; migration 0014): text-only drafts. The tree, titles, notes, scene metadata, tags, and entities are shared; only the text of manuscript documents (`manuscriptDocuments` from `voice/profile.ts`) differs. Node ids never change, so everything keyed by node id keeps working. The active draft's text is the live `node.content` (no other read path changed); an inactive draft keeps `draft_text` rows (`draft_id`/`node_id` cascade, `content` null = empty, `word_count`). The one rule (`textOf`): a draft reads a document as its row, or as the live text when it has none, so a scene added after a draft was left reads the same there until edited there. The active id is the settings KV `ACTIVE_DRAFT_KEY` (`activeDraft`). `src/shared/drafts.ts` owns `DRAFT_NAME_MAX` (60), `FIRST_DRAFT_NAME`, `DraftName` (trimmed), `DraftInfo`/`DraftList`/`DiffSegment` (`same|add|del`)/`DraftDocDiff`/`DraftComparison`/`DraftChange { list, changed }`; `src/shared/textDiff.ts` (own code, no dependency) owns `diffWords(a, b)` (word+whitespace tokens, merged runs, `DIFF_MAX_EDITS` 2000) and `diffWordCount`. `src/main/draft/draftStore.ts`, one transaction each: `ensureDrafts` (creates "Draft 1", active, on first use; repairs a dangling active id), `listDrafts`, `switchDraft` (live text into the left draft's rows, the target's rows into live through `saveDocument`, target rows deleted), `duplicateDraft` (textOf the source; does not switch), `renameDraft`/`deleteDraft` (VALIDATION for a taken name case-insensitively, the active draft, the last draft), `compareDrafts` (plain text via `docToText`, tree order, ancestor `path`), `revertDocuments(fromId, nodeIds?)` (into the active draft only). Channels `drafts:list|switch|duplicate|rename|delete|compare|revert`; the switch and revert handlers call `documentsWritten` (summary/mention re-queue) and never `recordWriting` (not words written). Renderer: `features/drafts/draftStore.ts` (list, `activeId`, `busy`; every mutation flushes pending saves first, a failed flush aborts it; errors toast; `nextDraftName`, `draftNameProblem`), `DraftsDialog.tsx` (shell dialog `drafts`, Tools › Drafts…: list with Active marker and counts, Switch, Compare with current, Duplicate…, Rename…, Delete… with a danger confirm, disabled for the active one), `DraftCompare.tsx` (From/To pickers, per-scene inline diff in the `.rewrite-diff` styles with long unchanged runs collapsed by `diffContext.ts`, Revert this scene / Revert all with a confirm, offered only when one side is active), `DraftStatus.tsx` (`status-draft` in the status bar once there are two drafts; a click opens the dialog). Switch and revert refresh through `editor/rewrittenDocuments.ts` `refreshRewrittenDocuments(changed, { countsAsWriting: false })`, shared with crash recovery (which passes the default `true`); `false` uses `treeStore.rebaseWordCount`, which moves the session baseline with the count so the status bar's session delta does not jump. The list loads on project open and clears on close (`App.tsx`). Known gaps: formatting-only differences read as unchanged (compare and revert's change detection by plain text vs. stored JSON differ: revert copies any JSON difference); a manuscript document cannot leave the manuscript (moves stay within a section, deletes cascade its rows), so `draft_text` rows never go stale.
- Snapshots (F-8.6; migration 0015): text-only frozen copies of one document or of every document in the project (`projectDocuments` in `voice/profile.ts`: front, manuscript, end, in tree order; it shares `documentsInTreeOrder` with `manuscriptDocuments`). The text taken is the live `node.content` (the active draft's); `draft_name` records the active draft without creating one (`activeDraftName` in `draftStore.ts`, null before drafts exist). Tables `snapshot` (`kind` manual|milestone|auto, `scope` document|project, `node_id` cascade for a document snapshot, `note`, `created`) and `snapshot_text` (`snapshot_id`/`node_id` cascade, `content` null = empty, `word_count`); a deleted document drops out of every snapshot, a document snapshot goes with it. `src/shared/snapshots.ts` owns the limits (name 80, note 2000, `SNAPSHOT_AUTO_KEEP` 20), the input/output schemas, `defaultSnapshotName`, and `autoSnapshotName`. `src/main/snapshot/snapshotStore.ts`, one transaction each: `listSnapshots` (newest first, rowid tie-break; docCount/wordCount summed from `snapshot_text`, `nodeTitle` joined), `takeSnapshot` (document scope needs a document: NOT_FOUND/VALIDATION), `updateSnapshot` (`milestone: true` flags; `false` turns a milestone into manual, an auto stays auto), `deleteSnapshot`, `compareSnapshot(id, againstId?)` (docs either snapshot holds, a side without a copy reads current text; reuses `diffDocument` from `draftStore.ts`), `restoreSnapshot(id, nodeIds?)` (docs whose live JSON differs from the snapshot's row are first kept as an `auto` snapshot "Before restoring …" (document scope when one, else project), written through `writeDocumentText` in `documentStore.ts` (shared with draft switch/revert), then autos beyond 20 are pruned oldest first; nothing differs, no auto). Channels `snapshots:list|take|update|delete|compare|restore`; the restore handler calls `documentsWritten` for the changed ids and never `recordWriting`. Renderer: `features/snapshots/snapshotStore.ts` (list newest first, `busy`; take/update/remove/restore each flush pending saves first, a failed flush aborts; errors toast; restore refreshes through `refreshRewrittenDocuments(changed, { countsAsWriting: false })`; loads on project open, clears on close in `App.tsx`), `SnapshotsDialog.tsx` (shell dialog `snapshots`, Tools › Snapshots… after Drafts…: Take snapshot form with This document, the open document (tree selection of kind `document`, none while an entity page is open) or Whole project, name prefilled with `defaultSnapshotName`, note, Milestone; list filtered All / This document (document snapshots of the open one) / Milestones; rows with Milestone and Auto badges, scope, date, words, note; Compare with current, Restore… and Delete… behind danger confirms, Edit… inline, sending only changed fields so an unflagged auto stays auto), `SnapshotCompare.tsx` (snapshot picker and Against: Current text or another snapshot; per-document `drafts/DocDiff.tsx`, shared with `DraftCompare`; Restore this document / Restore all… only against the current text; asks again after a restore).
- `src/main/lifecycle.ts` — `installSingleInstance` requests Electron's single-instance lock and focuses the existing window on a second launch; `index.ts` quits when the lock is refused.
- `src/main/project/legacy.ts` — `isLegacyDatabase` opens a database read-only and detects the v0 layout (`documents` table, no `node` table); `locateProject` in `projectStore.ts` uses it so a v0 file is refused with a clear message instead of being migrated by accident.
- v0 import (F-1.6; no migration): `project:open` runs `locateProject` first; a `legacy` location asks `dialogs.confirmLegacyConversion(source, legacyBackupPath(...))` (a native message box, Convert/Cancel) and then `convertLegacyProject` before `manager.open`. `legacyImport.ts` reads the v0 database read-only (missing tables and later-v0 columns read as empty), builds the v1 project in a hidden sibling `.<name>.converting-<id>` with `createProject(..., { skeleton: false })` (sections only), and only then renames the original to `<Name> (v0 backup YYYY-MM-DD[ n]).mythscribe` (with `-wal`/`-shm` for a single file) and the new folder to the original path; a failed second rename restores the original. What comes across: the tree (`legacyTree.ts` `planLegacyTree`: section by v0 `section`, null to the manuscript, top-level `doc_type = 'note'` rows into an end-matter "Notes" folder, orphans and cycles to the top level, `novel` level to a plain folder, siblings by position then created, location/POV/timeline to `scene_meta`), content and notes (`legacySlate.ts` `slateToTiptap`: paragraph, heading 1–3, blockquote, sceneBreak, align; bold/italic/underline/strike/code; inline tag marks, sizes, colours dropped; unreadable values become plain paragraphs), `editor_*` settings field by field (`legacyEditorSettings`), tags (`legacyTagRecords` → `importTagBank`; `plot-thread` → `plotThread`, bad colours to the category default, parents by name) and their document links (`addDocumentTag`, non-taggable targets skipped), and `reference_docs` as blank-template entities (`slateToText`; a duplicate name is skipped). Left in the backup: scene summaries, background images, v0 tag templates, AI presets, per-document formatting presets. `manager.open` and `openProject` still refuse a v0 location (the startup reopen never converts silently). Test fixture: `legacyFixture.ts` (`writeV0Project`, v0's own schema) and the checked-in `e2e/fixtures/v0-project.mythscribe` made from it.
- `src/preload/index.ts` — exposes `window.mythscribe` (`invoke`, `on`), restricted to contract channels. Sandbox and context isolation are on.
- `src/renderer/lib/ipc.ts` — `ipc().invoke(channel, input)` typed client that validates outputs; `setIpcClient` for tests.
- `src/renderer/features/manuscript/` — `treeStore.ts` is the one owner of tree state: normalized `byId`/`childrenOf`/`rootIds`, derived `sectionOf` and `wordCountRollup` (rebuilt once per `load()` from `tree:list`), plus `selectedId` and in-memory `collapsed`. F-2.2–2.4 mutate it by updating the changed records, never by reloading the world. `ManuscriptTree.tsx` is an ARIA tree (roving tabindex, arrow keys); sections are labelled via `sectionLabel` and are not selectable; front/end-matter documents carry `data-matter` and the gold `text-matter` color. Per-level colors are tokens (`--ms-level-*`, `--ms-matter`); each theme (F-7.8) sets its own. `App.tsx` loads/clears the store on project open/close and renders the fixed-width sidebar + main pane (F-7.2 adds resizing).
- Tree mutations: main validates structure inside a transaction in `src/main/tree/treeStore.ts` (`createNode`, `renameNode`, `duplicateNode`, `deleteNode`, `moveNode`; section roots are refused with `VALIDATION`, sibling positions stay contiguous: gap-close the old siblings, then shift the new ones, then land the row) and returns the row(s); the renderer store merges the returned records with pure, tested helpers (`insertIntoIndex`, `duplicateIntoIndex`, `removeFromIndex`, `moveInIndex`, `planRemoval` for the post-delete selection fallback) instead of reloading; `expandAncestors` opens the path to a new node. The structural rule (what level fits under which parent) has one owner, `canPlaceLevel` in `src/shared/labels.ts`, used by main and by `resolveDropTarget`/`resolveMenuCreateTarget` in `placement.ts`. Flexible nesting (the author, 2026-10-07): upward only, a level sits at its own level or any higher one (scene under the root, a part, or a chapter; chapter under the root or a part; part under the root). Hierarchy consumers must not assume scene → chapter → part: `compiledBlocks` heads a scene whose parent is the root or a part with its title at chapter level (no chapter numbering exists; headings are node titles), export `title` blocks carry `inPart` so EPUB's contents keep a root-level chapter out of the part above it, a single scene exported alone prints no heading, `chapterGroups` lists chapter-level scenes, and the import commit keeps nodes the outline does not show (generic, or a loose scene/chapter) in front of their container's outline children when they were in front. `tree:move`'s `afterId` is tri-state: `undefined` appends, `null` inserts first, a string inserts after that sibling. Delete confirms through `dialogs.confirm` in `ManuscriptTree.tsx`. Drag and drop is native HTML5 (no dependency); the transient drag state and the indicator live in `ManuscriptTree.tsx`, not the store. In e2e, `locator.dragTo()` on an expanded folder row needs an explicit `sourcePosition` inside the header row, or it grabs the centre of the whole subtree. `placement.ts` is the one place that turns "selection + level" into `{ parentId, afterId }` (bottom bar, Insert menu/shortcuts, and the empty-folder invitation use `resolveCreateTarget`, which appends along the bottom of the outline and stops where the last leveled child cannot hold the level; the right-click menu uses `resolveMenuCreateTarget`, which creates right inside a clicked root, part, or chapter, via `createLevel(level, id, { direct: true })`). `contextMenuItems.ts` returns plain `{ id, label }` data so F-7.1 can render the same items natively; front/end matter rows get the section's template items (`template:<id>`, parsed back with `templateIdOf`, never cast) after the generic pair (F-2.6). `src/shared/matterTemplates.ts` is the one owner of the 14 templates (Tiptap JSON inside the editor schema); `tree:create` takes an optional `template`, main fills title, content, `matterType`, and `wordCount` and refuses a template outside its section; the renderer's `createFromTemplate` selects the new document without inline rename and adds its words to every ancestor rollup. Default titles come from `defaultNodeTitle` in `src/shared/labels.ts`.
- `src/renderer/features/editor/` (F-3.1) — `extensions.ts` is the one owner of the document schema: `buildExtensions({ sceneBreak, onSave })` returns StarterKit trimmed to the spec (no lists, links, code blocks, horizontal rules, or trailing node; the trailing node made a freshly applied heading read inactive after select-all), `TextAlign` on headings and paragraphs, and the custom `SceneBreak` atom block (`<div data-scene-break>` with the text as an extension option, not stored in the node, so restyling applies to old breaks; `insertSceneBreak` opens a paragraph after it). `documentStore.ts` owns the loaded document (`id`, `content`, `dirty`; generation guard) and autosave (F-3.2): `edit(json)` records the latest editor state and restarts a 1 s debounce; `flush()` writes the pending job through `document:save`, waits for any write already on the wire (writes are serialized, so close/quit cannot outrun a save that already left), keeps the job for retry on failure unless a newer edit replaced it, and merges the returned word count into the tree via `useTreeStore.setWordCount` (delta walk up the ancestors, no reload); `saveNow()` is Ctrl+S (the `SaveShortcut` extension, `Mod-s`); `load(id)` flushes the outgoing document without waiting and registers the flusher with the pending-save registry once, `clear()` unregisters. Pending jobs (a map keyed by document id, so a failed save for one document survives edits to another), the timer, and the in-flight promise are module state; tests must call `resetDocumentStore()` in `beforeEach`. `src/shared/wordCount.ts` is the one word counter (main caches it on `node.word_count`).
- Stacked editing (F-3.8, F-2.5): `documentStore.ts` holds every loaded document in `docs: Record<id, { content, dirty }>` with `load(id)`/`unload(id)`/`edit(id, json)`, per-id debounce timers and load tokens, and one serialized write queue (`drain`). `DocumentEditor.tsx` is the region: it owns the load/unload effect, the Tiptap instance, an optional own toolbar, and an `onFocus(editor)` callback; `EditorPane.tsx` is the single-document wrapper. `StackedEditor.tsx` renders `descendantDocuments(index, folderId)` (pure, in `treeStore.ts`) as `<section aria-label={title}>` regions with one shared `Toolbar` targeting the last-focused region (tracked as `{ id, editor }` and dropped when the region leaves the stack); the separator is decided per folder from `sectionOf` (scene-break text for manuscript, `<hr>` for matter). The empty-folder invitation creates through `createLevel`/`createGeneric` with `{ keepSelection: true }` so the stack stays. `App.tsx` `MainPane` renders `StackedEditor` for folders and `EditorPane` for documents. The book-like column (F-3.4) and formatting (F-3.6) have one owner, `column.ts`: `COLUMN` is the utility string every editing surface uses and `editorStyle(settings)` sets the `--ms-editor-*` variables (max width, font size, line height, paragraph spacing, first-line indent; token fallbacks in `tokens.css`, consumed by `.ms-editor` rules in `app.css`) on the pane; this is the one sanctioned inline style in the editor. Settings: `src/main/project/settingsStore.ts` reads/upserts the `editor` row (falls back to `defaultEditorSettings(format)` on a missing or invalid row); `src/renderer/features/editor/settingsStore.ts` is the renderer owner (`load` with the tree on project open, `update(patch)` optimistic with one 150 ms debounced `editorSettings:set`, revert and toast on failure, registered with the pending-save registry, `clear` on close; `useEditorSettings(format)` falls back to the format default; tests call `resetEditorSettingsStore()`). `EditorSettingsTab.tsx` hosts the controls plus a live preview (a static sample styled with `editorStyle`, `.ms-editor.ms-preview` drops the editor's min-height) inside the Settings dialog (F-7.5). Changing the scene-break text rebuilds the editor instance (the text is an extension option); `documentStore.edit()` keeps `docs[id].content` at the latest JSON so the rebuild starts from unsaved typing. The empty state (F-3.5) is the `empty-state` block in `App.tsx`. Word counts (F-3.3): `StatusBar.tsx` is presentational (`status-words`, `status-delta` test ids; formatters in `wordFormat.ts`); `DocumentStatusBar` inside `DocumentEditor.tsx` counts the editor live with `useLiveWordCount` (a `useSyncExternalStore` on Tiptap's `update` event, snapshot cached by document identity so caret moves never recount; saved count while loading), the stack shows the folder's `wordCountRollup` with no delta (folder rollups move with structure); the session delta comes from `sessionBaseline` in the tree store (a copy of the rollup taken in `load()`, cleared in `clear()`; `sessionDelta(state, id)`), so nodes created this session count as all new. `Toolbar.tsx` snapshots its `editor` prop inside `useEditorState`'s selector because Tiptap 3 does not refresh the selector when the editor prop changes. `EditorPane.tsx` loads by id and mounts a fresh editor instance per document, keyed by id and load state and constructed with the content, so loading never enters the undo history and undo cannot cross documents; while loading the editor is read-only and the toolbar gets `editor={null}`. `Toolbar.tsx` reads active state through `useEditorState` and exposes it as `aria-pressed`. `src/shared/tiptap.ts` holds the recursive zod `TiptapNode` schema and `EMPTY_DOC` for main, renderer, and later importers. jsdom lacks what ProseMirror needs to mount, so `src/renderer/test/setup.ts` polyfills `Range.getClientRects`/`getBoundingClientRect` and `document.elementFromPoint` once for every renderer test.
- Compiled preview (F-3.12): no migration, no AI. `src/shared/compile.ts` owns zod `CompiledSceneMeta` (`SceneMeta` picked to `{ location, pov, timeline }`, trimmed; `compiledSceneMeta()` answers null when all are empty, so the brief never crosses IPC), `CompiledTag { id, name, color }`, `CompiledEntry { id, kind, level, depth, title, meta, tags, content }`, `CompiledManuscript { entries }`. Main `src/main/document/compileStore.ts` `compileManuscript(db)`: `listNodes` once, a preorder walk of the manuscript root's descendants by position, `documentJson` (unreadable = null), `parseStoredSceneMeta`; tags from `listAllLinkedTags` in `documentTagStore.ts` (one query, `{ id, name, color }` per node; `listAllDocumentTags`, the F-5.7 ranker's read, now derives from it). Channel `manuscript:compile`. Renderer `src/renderer/features/compile/`: `compiledBlocks.ts` (pure reading-order rules: heading / meta / break / text; a generic folder prints nothing) and `CompileDialog.tsx` (shell dialog `compile`, View › `openCompile` `Compiled preview`, built on `StatsFrame`; flushes the document and scene-meta stores, then asks once; loading, error `role="alert"`, empty `Nothing to compile yet.`; text is `<article class="ms-editor ms-preview">` with `editorStyle(settings)`, each document rendered by `Node.fromJSON(manuscriptSchema(sceneBreak))` + `DOMSerializer` into `replaceChildren`, no innerHTML, a document that fails to parse renders nothing). `manuscriptSchema` in `extensions.ts` is `getSchema(buildExtensions(...))` with an inline-tag node id, schema only, so `inlineTag` and `aiOrigin` parse and no plugin runs. Test ids `compile-preview`, `compile-heading` (`data-level`), `compile-scene-meta`, `compile-scene-break`, `compile-text`. Known gaps: not live while open; inline tag colours are read when it renders.
- Manuscript export (F-12.1): no migration, no AI, no new dependency. `src/shared/bookExport.ts` owns `ExportFormat` (`pdf | docx | epub | md`, `EXPORT_EXTENSIONS`, `EXPORT_FORMAT_LABELS`), `ExportScope` (`manuscript` | `chapters { ids }` | `document { id }`), `ExportFormatting` (font serif/sans, size 9–16, spacing 1/1.5/2, letter/A4, `chapterNewPage`, `indentParagraphs`, `sceneBreak`), `ExportOptions`, `ExportProgress` (`collect | render | write`, done/total), `ExportResult`. `compiledBlocks` moved to `src/shared/compiledBlocks.ts` so the preview (F-3.12) and the export share the reading-order rules; `compileStore.ts` grew `compileSection(db, section, { only })` (a chosen node keeps its ancestors and descendants; `compileManuscript` wraps it). Main `src/main/export/`: `collect.ts` `collectBook` (matter units = the top-level children of front/end, text only, empty dropped; NOT_FOUND / VALIDATION for a missing or folder document; `Nothing to export.`), `model.ts` (Tiptap → `BookUnit`s of title/paragraph/heading/quote/sceneBreak blocks; `startsPage` is the one page-break rule; aiOrigin and the import origin are dropped, inline tags print their name), `xml.ts` (escaping, XML-1.0-invalid characters stripped), pure renderers `markdown.ts`, `html.ts` (print page + EPUB XHTML), `docx.ts` (hand-written OOXML), `epub.ts` (EPUB 3, `mimetype` first and stored), `pdf.ts` (hidden sandboxed window with JS off over a temp file, removed in `finally`), `run.ts` `exportBook` (renderPdf injected for tests); `fs.ts` `writeBufferAtomic`. Handler `export:run` picks `<project>.<ext>` beside the project folder via `chooseExportPath`, then collects (so `Nothing to export.` comes after the save dialog). Renderer `src/renderer/features/export/`: `exportStore.ts` (choices in memory per session, `chapterGroups`, `buildExportOptions`, flush then run with a `requestId`-filtered progress subscription; cleared on project close), `ExportDialog.tsx` on `StatsFrame`, which now takes an `action` (the primary button beside a secondary Close). `useOpenDocument` moved to `features/manuscript/` (snapshots and export share it). Since Compile v2 CV2 (2026-10-07) `model.ts`, `html.ts`, and the `BookUnit` writers are gone: the dialog's options become a compile format (`exportDialogFormat`) and print through the CV2 writers (next bullets), and PDF is laid out by Paged.js with script on in a locked-down window. Since CV3 (2026-10-07) the dialog, `exportStore.ts`, `exportDialogFormat`, `exportBook`, `export:run`, and every `bookExport.ts` export but `ExportScope` and `ExportProgress` are gone too: the compile window replaced them (File › Export… opens it).
- Compile v2 model (F-12.4, CV1): no migration. `src/shared/compileFormat.ts` owns the format: `CompileOutput` (`pdf docx epub rtf odt html txt md`, labels, extensions, `FINAL_OUTPUTS` pdf/epub), `BookFont` (`BOOK_FONT_INFO` family names; files ship in CV2), `PageSetup` (`PAGE_SIZE_INFO` in inches, `pageDimensions`), `Typography`, `SectionLayout` per `SectionLevel` (`part chapter chapterScene scene`), `Separator`, `HeadersFooters` (recto/verso slots, `facing`, `hideOnOpeners`), `MatterSettings`, `ContentSettings`, `Replacement` (regex refined), `MetadataSettings`, `CompileFormat` (version 1), `BUILTIN_COMPILE_FORMATS` (ids `builtin:*`), library helpers (`findCompileFormat`, `sortFormats`, `formatNameTaken`, `copyName`, `duplicateFormat`, lenient `CompileFormatLibrary`), and the per-project `CompileProjectState` (settings key `compile.state`: formatId, output, scope, `excluded` ids; `setIncluded`). `src/shared/bookDetails.ts`: `BookDetails` (settings key `bookDetails`, every field defaulted, cover in `assets/covers/`), `bookSurname`, `bookTitle`, `isbnFor`. `src/shared/compileModel.ts`: `CompileSource` (front/manuscript/end `CompileNode`s = `CompiledEntry` + synopsis + notes), `compileBook(input) → CompiledBook { output, format, metadata, items, toc, words }` with `BookItem`s (`page` generated, `matter`, `section` with resolved `heading`/`layout`/`break`/`inPart`/`runningHead`, `separator`, `text` of `ContentBlock`s, `synopsis`, `note`), plus `selectEntries`, `contentBlocks`, `applyOpenings`, `compileReplacements`, `sectionHeading`, number formatting, `furnitureSegments`/`fillFurniture`/`furnitureValues`, `approximateWords`. Main: `compileStore.ts` `compileSource(db)` (one tree and tag read; `compileSection` lost its `only` option to `selectEntries`), `settingsStore.ts` `get/setBookDetails`, `get/setCompileState`, `appStateStore.ts` `compileFormats`, `export/formatLibrary.ts` (`createFormat`, `saveFormat`, `deleteFormat`: built-ins read-only, names unique ignoring case, `COMPILE_FORMATS_MAX`), handlers for the twelve channels (cover via `chooseEntityImage` + `addImageAsset('covers')`). The compile window (CV3) is the only caller in the renderer. Known gaps: replacements run per text run (a match split across marks is missed).
- Compile v2 writers (F-12.4, CV2): no migration; one new dev dependency (`pagedjs` 0.4.3, MIT, its polyfill text bundled into main via `?raw`). Shared: `bookFonts.ts` (the bundled faces per `BookFont` under `resources/fonts/<dir>/` with `OFL.txt`; Fontsource 5.3.0 Latin + Latin Extended WOFF2 by `unicode-range`, Liberation Serif 2.1.5 TTF; `fontFaceCss(fonts, urlFor)`, `fontStack`, `officeFontName`, `formatFonts`), `compilePages.ts` (`pageRuns(book)`: the one page-start rule for every paged writer and the preview — first item and every item with a break; front division bare; openers bare-first under `hideOnOpeners`; the body's first run restarts numbering and, with sides, goes recto; `itemBreak`, `hasSides`), `compileHtml.ts` (items → HTML for `print`/`web`/`epub` flavours, `bookCss` per format, `printDocument`, `webDocument`, `PAGED_FURNITURE_HANDLER`: after layout marks bare pages and writes the folio into `--ms-folio` and contents links, because Paged.js's `counter-reset: page` does not carry), `xmlEscape.ts` (moved from main).
